We rank feasible designs against a target design objective, viz., maximum speed, minimum Cost of Transport (CoT), or max Payload Margin. Because body and locomotion are coupled, the optimal morphology dictates how a robot is driven, while optimal gait depends on the physical body. We train a small number of CPG policies by reinforcement learning across the space of candidate morphologies, co-learning the gait with the underlying robot hardware. Link lengths and actuators are then resolved post-hoc from the policy's logged operating envelope, reducing synthesis cost to a small, fixed number of reinforcement-learning runs instead of one per candidate. Our experiments show three key findings: co-designing body and gait is necessary to satisfy locomotion constraints; actuator-envelope feasibility, rather than locomotion success alone, determines realizable payload capacity; and canonical animal gaits emerge naturally in most designs from morphology and constraints alone. A real-world demonstration further highlights the efficacy of our work.

</details>


### [46] [Distributed Model Predictive Control with Connectivity-based Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19912)
*Jorit Geurts,Danilo Saccani,Melanie N. Zeilinger,Andrea Carron*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种 DMPC 框架，通过为每个智能体分配局部"连通性契约"区域，实现可扩展的分布式实时控制，同时保证通信网络连通性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数分布式模型预测控制（DMPC）方案假设通信网络保持连通，而非主动维持连通性。通常的连通性数学条件是非凸的，且将每个智能体与其他所有智能体耦合，这与可扩展的分布式实时控制器不兼容，因此主动保证连通性十分困难。

Method: 为每个智能体分配一个"连通性契约"，即局部区域，规定其在预测时域内预测位置的允许范围；契约的设计保证只要每个智能体留在自身契约内，整个团队就保持连通。在给定的契约图下，智能体仅需与直接邻居进行一次信息交换即可构造其契约，随后各自独立求解本地优化问题。

Result: 证明了所得闭环系统能够保持连通性、避免碰撞并满足局部状态与输入约束。在微型自主车式机器人上的仿真与硬件实验验证了该方法。

Conclusion: 契约机制将非凸、全局耦合的连通性约束转化为局部可验证条件，实现可扩展的分布式实时控制，并兼具连通保持、避碰与约束满足的理论保证及实验验证。

Abstract: Teams of mobile robots rely on continuous communication with their neighbors for coordination, yet most distributed model predictive control (DMPC) schemes assume the communication network stays connected rather than actively enforcing it. Adding such a guarantee is hard since the usual mathematical condition for connectivity is nonconvex and links every agent to every other, which is incompatible with a scalable distributed real-time controller. We propose a DMPC framework in which each agent is assigned a connectivity contract: a local region prescribing where its predicted positions may lie over the prediction horizon. The contracts are designed so that, as long as every agent stays within its own contract, the team is guaranteed to remain connected. Given the maintained contract graph, an agent builds its contract from a single exchange with its immediate neighbors, after which every agent solves its own optimization problem independently. We prove that the resulting closed-loop system maintains connectivity, avoids collisions, and respects local state and input constraints. Simulation and hardware experiments on miniature autonomous car-like robots demonstrate the approach.

</details>


### [47] [Pose-aware Legged Robot Semantic Exploration with Omnidirectional Perception in Confined Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19460)
*Xiaoyang Zhan,Shiyu Chen,Kenji Shimada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: POSE：一种姿态感知的语义探索系统，利用腿足机器人自身的俯仰/横滚与全向相机-LiDAR感知，在受限环境中权衡目标表面覆盖与探索时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限环境中，地面机器人受限于传感器垂直视场和有限站位距离，从平面视角难以观测到目标物体的上表面；通过机体倾斜可提升覆盖，但会带来额外观测与姿态转换从而增加任务时间，因此需要解决覆盖与效率之间的权衡。

Method: 提出姿态感知的视点采样模块，依据期望覆盖增益从部分物体地图中选择机体姿态；采用对准目标（aim-aligned）的执行方式减少不必要的机体重新定向；引入由视觉语言模型（VLM）辅助的以物体为中心的视点剪枝策略，利用持续观测历史与鸟瞰图（BEV）地图减少冗余巡检；将得到的语义视点与几何探索视点在全局探索规划器中结合。

Result: 仿真显示最终目标表面覆盖率较平面规划基线提升8-10个百分点，探索时间减少17-32%，并在所评估基线中取得最高的平均物体覆盖AUC；在机械车间的腿足机器人（搭载全向相机-LiDAR套件）真实实验中验证了系统的适用性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，自适应机体姿态规划能够改善腿足机器人语义探索中覆盖与效率的权衡；作者计划未来公开代码以惠及社区。

Abstract: Semantic exploration in confined environments requires both environment mapping and detailed observation of target objects. For ground robots, limited sensor vertical fields of view and restricted standoff distances can leave upper object surfaces unobserved from planar viewpoints. Body tilting can improve coverage, but additional observations and posture transitions increase mission time. To address this trade-off, we present POSE, a pose-aware semantic exploration system that exploits a legged robot's intrinsic body pitch and roll with omnidirectional camera-LiDAR perception. The proposed pose-aware viewpoint sampling module selects body postures from partial object maps according to expected coverage gain, while aim-aligned execution reduces unnecessary body reorientation. Further, we introduce an object-centric viewpoint pruning strategy assisted by a vision-language model (VLM), which uses persistent observation history and bird's-eye-view (BEV) maps to reduce redundant inspection visits. The resulting semantic viewpoints are combined with geometric exploration viewpoints in a global exploration planner. Simulations show that POSE improves final target-surface coverage by 8-10 percentage points over the planar planning baseline while reducing exploration time by 17-32%, and achieves the highest mean object coverage AUC among the evaluated baselines. Real-world experiments with a legged robot carrying an omnidirectional camera-LiDAR suite in a machine shop further demonstrate the system's applicability. These results support adaptive body-posture planning for improving the coverage-efficiency trade-off in legged robot semantic exploration. We plan to release the code for community benefit in the future.

</details>


### [48] [FASA: Feedback-Aware Sampling Adaptation for Efficient Diffusion-Based VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19475)
*Yuchen Han,Jianhan Wu,Xiaoyang Qu,Lingwei Kong,Shiyi Li,Jianzong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练的运行时框架FASA，利用多模态反馈动态调节扩散式VLA模型的采样步预算与优化步，实现边缘平台上最高1.45倍的推理加速并保持竞争性成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在具身任务中表现优异，但迭代采样带来沉重的计算与内存访问开销，难以在边缘平台实时部署。现有加速方法要么需要昂贵的训练（如蒸馏、流匹配），要么通过静态调度的剪枝与缓存损害感知能力，忽视了机器人交互负载的动态变化。

Method: FASA是一个免训练的运行时框架，将实时多模态反馈作为去噪管线的控制信号：交互驱动的范围适配器依据视觉与夹爪力反馈调节全局采样步预算；本体感知感知的步适配器在调节后的范围内定位优化步。二者协同设计使底层硬件架构自适应匹配不同执行阶段的负载需求。

Result: 在多个基准上的对比评估表明，推理速度最高提升1.45倍，同时保持具有竞争力的成功率。

Conclusion: 该工作为在资源受限计算平台上部署重型生成式具身AI负载提供了一种新颖的动态运行时架构范式。

Abstract: Diffusion-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve strong performance in embodied tasks, but their iterative sampling imposes heavy computational and memory-access cost, blocking real-time deployment on edge platforms. Existing acceleration methods either require expensive training (e.g., distillation, flow matching) or degrade perception via statically scheduled pruning and caching, ignoring the dynamic workload variance of robotic interactions. This paper presents FASA (Feedback-Aware Sampling Adaptation), a training-free runtime framework that treats real-time multimodal feedback as a control signal for the denoising pipeline: an interaction-driven range adaptor modulates the global sampling-step budget based on visual and gripper-force feedback, and a proprioception-aware step adaptor pinpoints the optimized step within the adapted range. This co-designed framework allows the underlying hardware architecture to adaptively match the workload demands of different execution phases. Comparative evaluations across several benchmarks show that the inference speed can be increased by up to 1.45$\times$ while maintaining competitive success rates, providing a novel dynamic runtime architecture paradigm for deploying heavy generative embodied AI workloads onto resource-constrained computing platforms.

</details>


### [49] [Self-excited actuation enables adaptive and resilient flapping-wing flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19480)
*Rundong Yang,Ethan S. Wold,Ellen Liu,James Lynch,Wei Zhou,Mark Jankauski,Simon Sponberg,Nick Gravish*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次研制出采用异步驱动（模仿昆虫异步飞行肌）的飞行扑翼机器人，证明其翅膀可在无控制输入下响应身体共振力学变化、无需外部传感即可瞬时响应碰撞，并在杂乱环境中表现出优于同步驱动的稳定性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 昆虫飞行肌分为同步肌与异步肌，异步肌具有内在的拉伸激活特性，可在无脑部信号情况下自发产生振翅，被认为赋予飞行控制以自适应与快速响应能力。然而，迄今大多数飞行机器人仍采用同步驱动，异步驱动在飞行机器人中的潜力尚未被验证。

Method: 研制了首个具备飞行能力的异步驱动扑翼机器人，通过飞行实验对比异步驱动与同步驱动，测试翅膀对身体共振力学变化的响应、对障碍物碰撞的瞬时反应，并在杂乱环境中评估稳定性与性能。

Result: 异步驱动使翅膀无需控制输入即可响应身体共振力学变化，且无需外部传感即可瞬时响应障碍物碰撞；在杂乱环境飞行测试中，异步驱动相较同步驱动显著提升了稳定性与性能。

Conclusion: 模仿飞行昆虫异步肌的扑翼机器人驱动策略可提供快速、反应式的驱动响应，使反应先于控制系统介入；这种将具身控制分配至低层驱动动力学与高层感知运动系统的方案，为新型飞行机器人提供了有吸引力的设计蓝图。

Abstract: The muscles that power insect flight fall into one of two categories: 1) synchronous muscles that contract under direct control from the nervous system, and 2) asynchronous muscles which have an intrinsic stretch activation response that spontaneously generates wingbeats without the need for signaling from the brain. It is thought that the emergent nature of asynchronous wingbeats provides both adaptive and responsive capabilities for flight control. To date, most flying robots use synchronous actuation. In this paper we develop the first flight-capable flapping wing robot that uses asynchronous actuation. We demonstrate that asynchronous actuation allows wings to respond to changes in the resonant mechanics of the body without control input, and wings can react instantaneously to collisions with obstacles with no extrinsic sensing needed. Flight tests within cluttered environments demonstrate that asynchronous actuation significantly improves stability and performance when compared to synchronous actuation. In total this work demonstrates that a flapping wing robot actuation strategy that emulates the asynchronous muscles of flying insects can provide fast, reactive actuation responses before a control system would need to intervene. This partitioning of embodied control to both the low-level actuation dynamics and and high-level sensorimotor system provides a compelling blueprint for new flying robots.

</details>


### [50] [PIVOT: Perception-aware Independent Viewpoint Online Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19510)
*Yuyang Chen,Shekoufeh Sadeghi,Charuvahan Adhivarahan,Elton Lemos,Chen Wang,Sanjeev J. Koppal,Karthik Dantu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PIVOT：一种轻量迭代方法，在固定平移轨迹下优化运动解耦传感器的视轴方向以最大化特征可见性，在S²上做两自由度连续优化，蒙特卡洛评估保留暴力搜索98.1–99.6%可见性并提速76–85倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人感知普遍假设传感器视场相对机体固定，但云台相机、MEMS激光雷达等运动解耦传感器允许运行时独立控制感知方向。这一自由度带来计算挑战：在特征密集环境中在线高效选择有用的观察方向。

Method: 提出PIVOT轻量迭代方法，沿固定平移轨迹优化传感器观察方向以最大化特征可见性。在圆锥形FoV模型下，可见性仅依赖光轴，从而将问题化为视球S²上的两自由度优化；采用无坐标的SO(3)指数映射更新，实现高效连续优化，无需显式角度参数化或遍历式视球搜索。

Result: 蒙特卡洛评估中，PIVOT保留暴力搜索98.1–99.6%的可见性，同时获得76–85倍加速。逼真仿真与真实世界实验进一步表明视觉定位鲁棒性提升，并在四足机器人上实现可用的视角控制。

Conclusion: PIVOT为运动解耦传感器提供了一种高效、免参数化的在线视角优化方案，兼顾可见性质量与计算效率，并具备仿真与实机验证的实用性。

Abstract: A fundamental assumption in robotic perception is that the sensor's field of view (FoV) is fixed relative to the robot body. Motion-decoupled sensors, such as gimbal-mounted cameras and MEMS-based LiDARs, instead allow sensing direction to be controlled independently at runtime. This freedom creates a computational challenge: efficiently selecting useful viewing directions online in feature-dense environments. We propose PIVOT, a lightweight iterative method that optimizes sensor viewing direction along a fixed translation trajectory to maximize feature visibility. Under a conical FoV model, visibility depends only on the optical axis, yielding a two-degree-of-freedom optimization on the viewing sphere $S^2$. Coordinate-free $SO(3)$ exponential-map updates enable efficient continuous optimization without explicit angular parameterizations or exhaustive viewing-sphere search. Monte Carlo evaluations retain 98.1--99.6% of brute-force visibility with a 76--85x speedup. Photorealistic simulation and real-world experiments further demonstrate improved visual localization robustness and practical viewpoint control on a quadruped robot.

</details>


### [51] [CoreSense: Traceable Failure Recall and Conflict-Aware Belief Gating for Auditable Robot Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19512)
*Zoe Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoreSense机器人系统集成架构，通过可追踪情景证据与冲突感知信念门控实现可审计的决策（继续/再观测/弃权/上报），在多源评测中显著降低不安全继续率，但尚不构成自主恢复或认证安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在回忆以往失败经验时，无法判断所召回的证据是否仍然有效、是否与当前观测冲突、或是否足以支撑决策，缺乏对证据可信度的把关机制，容易导致不安全地继续执行。

Method: CoreSense集成架构：结合可追踪的情景证据、冲突感知的信念门控（belief gate）与有界可审计建议。门控在放行前检查范围、来源、时间、矛盾与支持度五项条件，据输出PROCEED、请求再观测、弃权或升级。评测采用三层互补方式且不下达物理机器人指令：离线公开真实机器人数据、冻结的信号级仿真、以及实时云部署路径。

Result: 在CableTrace-120与BotFails-200上，信念门控将协议定义的不安全继续率从20%和40%降至0%；独立校准的原始视频策略同样达到0%不安全继续，但对所有正常回合过度阻塞。ViFailback-BotFails视觉检测器达0.778 AUROC但仍全阻塞；循环不相交的UR3遥测在保护性停机上取得0%不安全继续、36.1%过度阻塞、61.9%覆盖率；抓取丢失迁移为负结果。受控物理验证为3.3%、0%、42.0%，冲突感知融合为4.7%、0%、42.8%。20/20云召回验证了CockroachDB Cloud与Amazon Bedrock部署路径。

Conclusion: 该证据支持一种可审计的集成模式，而非自主恢复能力或经认证的安全性。

Abstract: Robots can recall prior failures without knowing whether recalled evidence remains valid, conflicts with current observations, or is sufficient to guide a decision. We present CoreSense, a robot-system integration architecture that combines traceable episodic evidence with a conflict-aware belief gate and bounded, auditable recommendations. The gate checks scope, provenance, time, contradiction, and support before it permits PROCEED, requests re-observation, abstains, or escalates. Evaluation follows three complementary layers without commanding a physical robot: offline public real-robot data, a frozen signal-level simulation, and a live cloud deployment path. On CableTrace-120 and BotFails-200, belief gating reduces protocol-defined unsafe proceeds from 20% and 40% to 0%. A disjointly calibrated raw-video policy also reaches 0% unsafe proceed, but overblocks every nominal episode. On public data, a ViFailback-BotFails visual detector reaches 0.778 AUROC yet remains all-blocking, whereas cycle-disjoint UR3 telemetry for protective stops yields 0% unsafe proceed, 36.1% overblocking, and 61.9% coverage; grip-loss transfer remains a negative result. Controlled physical corroboration yields 3.3%, 0%, and 42.0%, while conflict-aware fusion yields 4.7%, 0%, and 42.8%. Finally, 20/20 cloud recalls validate a CockroachDB Cloud-Amazon Bedrock deployment path. The evidence supports an auditable integration pattern, not autonomous recovery or certified safety.

</details>


### [52] [AURORA: A Natural Language-Driven Agentic Framework for Understanding, Reasoning, and Orchestrating Reliable Air-Ground Co-Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19527)
*Keshu Wu,Hao Zhang,Rui Gan,Xiangbo Gao,Xiaopeng Li,Zhengzhong Tu,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 AURORA——一个自然语言驱动的智能体框架，将空地对场景生成视为"带验证的编译"过程；其核心是类型化中间表示 AGSG，统一支持解析、跨域接地、时序规划、可行性检查、运行时验证、失败定位与有界修复，并配套 AURORA-Bench 评测场景是否忠实实现所请求的交互关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空地对协同仿真研究日益依赖 co-simulation，但场景构建仍依赖人工、耗时且难以验证。更关键的问题是：一个生成场景可能顺利执行，却并未实现用户所要求的空间、时间、通信或行为关系。因此需要从"仅可执行"转向"忠实实现所请求行为"的可信场景生成。

Method: 提出 AURORA 框架，以自然语言为输入，将空地对场景生成建模为"编译+验证"流程。核心构件为 Air-Ground Scenario Graph (AGSG)：一种类型化中间表示，显式连接 agent、空中任务、事件、通信链路、成功条件及其跨域依赖。该共享表示在统一工作流内支撑：仿真器接地解析、道路—空域联合接地、时序规划、执行前可行性检查、基于 trace 的运行时验证、失败定位与有界修复。同时构建 AURORA-Bench 基准。

Result: 在多个语言模型上的实验表明：结构化执行显著提升可靠性；运行时验证能够暴露"基于完成度评测"所忽略的静默失败；局部化修复可在不重新生成整个场景的前提下消解大量违规。

Conclusion: 可靠的场景生成必须验证真正实现的行为，而非仅验证可执行代码；显式中间表示对于可验证、可修复的语言驱动协同仿具有重要价值。

Abstract: Air-ground transportation research increasingly relies on co-simulation, yet constructing scenarios remains labor-intensive and difficult to validate. More importantly, a generated scenario may execute successfully while failing to realize the spatial, temporal, communication, or behavioral relationships requested by the user. This paper presents AURORA, a natural-language-driven agentic framework that treats air-ground scenario generation as a process of compilation with verification. Central to AURORA is the Air-Ground Scenario Graph (AGSG), a typed intermediate representation that explicitly connects agents, aerial missions, events, communication links, success conditions, and their cross-domain dependencies. This shared representation enables simulator-grounded parsing, joint road-airspace grounding, temporal planning, pre-execution feasibility checking, trace-based runtime verification, failure localization, and bounded repair within a unified workflow. We further introduce AURORA-Bench to evaluate not only whether generated scenarios execute, but whether they faithfully realize the requested interactions. Experiments across multiple language models show that structured execution substantially improves reliability, while runtime verification exposes silent failures that completion-based evaluation overlooks. Localized repair further resolves many violations without regenerating the entire scenario. The results show that reliable scenario generation requires verifying realized behavior, not merely executable code, and demonstrate the value of explicit intermediate representations for verifiable and repairable language-driven co-simulation.

</details>


### [53] [SLAMSqueezeBench: Comparing SLAM Systems under Resource Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19533)
*Mohamed Hefny,Karthik Dantu,Steven Y. Ko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 SLAMSqueezeBench 框架，用于在边缘硬件的真实资源约束下测试与比较 SLAM 系统，并对九种 SLAM 系统进行了评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SLAM 系统多在孤立环境下构建与测试，性能报告假设其为系统上唯一运行的任务，而实际边缘硬件面临严重资源约束且需与其他任务共存；现有基准缺少在真实资源约束下比较 SLAM 系统的通用机制。

Method: 开发 SLAMSqueezeBench 框架：在执行期间对 SLAM 系统可用的计算与内存资源施加约束，并通过有限缓冲区满时丢帧的方式模拟真实相机帧采集过程。

Result: 利用该框架比较了九种 SLAM 系统，涵盖经典方法、基于学习的方法以及高斯泼溅（Gaussian splatting）方法。

Conclusion: SLAMSqueezeBench 为社区提供了在真实资源约束下评测 SLAM 系统的统一手段，框架将在论文发表后开放使用。

Abstract: Simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) is one of the services running on an autonomous robot. It is typically run to assist other tasks such as planning, manipulation, etc. All these tasks are run on edge hardware and are subject to severe resource constraints. However, most SLAM systems are built and tested in isolation, and their performance is reported as if they are the only task running on a system. We observe that existing benchmarks lack a common mechanism for comparing SLAM systems under realistic resource constraints. To address this limitation, we have developed SLAMSqueezeBench, a framework that allows testing of SLAM systems under realistic workloads on edge hardware. It does so by imposing constraints on compute and memory resources available for the SLAM system during execution. It also simulates realistic camera frame acquisition with frame drops when a finite buffer is full. Using SLAMSqueezeBench, we compare nine SLAM systems spanning classical systems, learning-based systems, and approaches for Gaussian splatting. Our testing framework will be available for use by the community upon publication.

</details>


### [54] [Navigate or Relocate? Planning Among Movable Obstacles in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19541)
*Yuqing Zhang,Haoyu Zhu,Yiannis Kantaros*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究未知环境下的可移动障碍物导航（NAMO），提出在线框架，通过比较“将可移动物体视为障碍”与“视为可移除”的最短路径来决定导航还是搬移，并利用基于采样的方法搜索相互依赖的搬移序列，还用LLM引导采样。实验表明该方法可扩展至需要相互依赖搬移的杂乱环境，且规划质量优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人规划方法在所有路径被阻塞时失效，此时机器人需决定搬移哪些物体、以何种顺序、放到何处来清出通路，即NAMO问题。现有NAMO规划器多假设环境已知，而针对未知环境的方法通常只做局部搬移推理，无法规划相互依赖的搬移序列。此外，在未知环境中机器人还需判断被阻塞的路线究竟需要搬移，还是通过未探索空间可能存在可行路径。

Method: 提出一个在线框架：通过比较两类最短路径来消解上述歧义——一类将已发现的可移动物体视为障碍，另一类将其视为可移除，据此在导航与搬移之间进行选择。导航复用现有运动规划器；搬移采用基于采样的方法，与现有未知环境方法不同，该方法在相互依赖的搬移序列空间上搜索，并使用LLM来偏置采样。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法可扩展至需要相互依赖搬移的杂乱环境，并且规划质量优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 该在线框架能够在未知环境中有效处理导航与搬移之间的歧义，并支持相互依赖的搬移序列规划，在杂乱场景下展现出良好的可扩展性与优于基线的规划质量。

Abstract: Conventional robot planning methods seek collision-free paths to a goal but fail when all paths are blocked. In these cases, the robot must determine which objects to relocate, in what order, and where to place them to clear a path---a problem known as Navigation Among Movable Obstacles (NAMO). Most NAMO planners assume a known environment, while existing approaches for unknown environments typically reason locally about relocations and cannot plan interdependent relocation sequences. We consider NAMO in unknown environments revealed through onboard sensing, where the robot must decide whether a blocked route requires relocation or a feasible path may exist through unexplored space. We propose an online framework that addresses this ambiguity by selecting between navigation and relocation using shortest paths that treat discovered movable objects as obstacles or as removable. Navigation relies on existing motion planners, while relocation uses a sampling-based approach that, unlike existing approaches for unknown environments, searches over \textit{interdependent} relocation sequences and uses an LLM to bias sampling. Numerical experiments demonstrate scalability to cluttered environments requiring interdependent relocations and improved plan quality over existing baselines.

</details>


### [55] [VABench: Measuring Embodied Spatial Intelligence through Visual Demonstrations, Active Perception, and Metric Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19554)
*Zhongbo Zhang,Jiayi Jin,Yifan Wang,Zaibin Zhang,Haiwen Diao,Lijun Wang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VA-Bench基准，用于评估多模态大模型在空间智能中完整的“观察—推理—行动—修正”闭环能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间智能不应仅停留在描述物体位置。在不完整观测下，模型必须识别并主动获取缺失证据、在统一空间框架中解释证据并据此行动。现有基准难以覆盖这一完整闭环，因此需要新的评测方法。

Method: 构建VA-Bench：通用MLLM仅从RGB演示中学习程序性上下文，主动选择相机视角、下达度量笛卡尔指令，并根据执行反馈修正；模型不获得对象位姿、专家轨迹或学习到的动作头，由固定的模型无关控制器执行模型指定目标。基准包含14个基础任务族（11个单臂、3个双臂）、7个留出几何/布局变体，以及一个长时程五物体组合轨道；在每基础任务20个物理验证种子上，对12个主要模型条件进行三次独立运行，报告最终成功率、9项轨迹级行为诊断和子任务进展。

Result: 最佳模型在标注运行中目标定位达100.0%、空间关系达78.9%，但三次运行宏观平均任务成功率仅53.93±3.17%。主动相机控制显著优于被动多视角观察，匹配比较中成功率由27.86%提升至57.50%。留出几何迁移可使成功率下降超过30个百分点。尽管有显著部分进展，没有模型能完成严格的长时程完整回合。

Conclusion: VA-Bench检验了通用MLLM能否将视觉演示与主动获取的证据转化为成功的具身行动，揭示了当前模型在完整观察—推理—行动—修正闭环上的明显不足。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence requires more than describing object locations. Under incomplete observation, models must identify and acquire missing evidence, interpret it in a common spatial frame, and act on it. We introduce VA-Bench to evaluate the complete observe-reason-act-revise loop. General-purpose MLLMs learn procedural context from RGB-only demonstrations, actively select camera viewpoints, issue metric Cartesian commands, and revise them from execution feedback. Models receive no privileged object poses, oracle trajectories, or learned action heads. A fixed model-agnostic controller executes only model-specified targets. VA-Bench contains 14 base task families (11 single-arm and three dual-arm), seven held-out geometry/layout variants, and a long-horizon five-object composition track. We evaluate 12 primary model conditions in three independent runs over the same 20 physically verified seeds per base task, reporting terminal success, nine trajectory-level behavioral diagnostics, and subtask progress. First, the best-performing model scores 100.0% on target localization and 78.9% on spatial relations in the annotated run. Its three-run macro-average task success is only 53.93+/-3.17%. Second, active camera control significantly improves task success over passive multi-view observation. In one matched comparison, success rises from 27.86% to 57.50%. Third, held-out geometric transfer can reduce task success by over 30 percentage points. No model completes a strict long-horizon episode, despite substantial partial progress. VA-Bench thus tests whether general-purpose MLLMs can turn visual demonstrations and actively acquired evidence into successful embodied action.

</details>


### [56] [Tele-Traversability: Rethinking Traversability for Teleoperated Ground Robots in Terrain Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19577)
*Lewei Feng,Qi Chen,Wenshuo Wang,Xianghao Meng,Minh Kieu,Haijie Guan,Jin Wu,Fuchun Sun,Junqiang Xi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将"可通行性"概念从以机器人为中心扩展到以人为中心，提出"遥操作可通行性（tele-traversability）"概念，纳入操作员的注意力、工作负荷和风险容忍/意识等认知状态，并重新审视其定义、角色及未来趋势与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作中，操作员通过人机界面依赖非现场、不完整且有延迟的反馈判断可通行性，而机器人依据现场车载传感信息评估，二者容易产生分歧，导致决策与动作不匹配。现有可通行性建模、估计与预测方法多从机器人视角出发（robot-centric），适用于全自主移动机器人，却忽视了人类操作员的影响。为弥合人机之间的可通行性评估差异，实现流畅、易用的遥导航，需要引入以人为中心（human-centric）的视角。

Method: 这是一篇概念性/综述性工作。作者首先重新审视机器人学中可通行性（traversability）的定义与角色，然后将其扩展到遥操作场景，提出"遥操作可通行性（tele-traversability）"概念，通过纳入操作员的认知状态（注意力、工作负荷、风险容忍或风险意识）把可通行性从以机器人为中心转向以人为中心；最后指出面向以人为中心遥操作系统的未来趋势与开放挑战。

Result: 论文提出了tele-traversability这一扩展概念，明确了其在机器人学与遥操作系统中的定义与角色，并识别出若干未来趋势与开放挑战。文中未报告实验或量化结果。

Conclusion: 可通行性研究应从以机器人为中心扩展到以人为中心，将操作员认知状态纳入考量，以缓解人机可通行性评估分歧、减少不匹配的决策与动作；tele-traversability是构建面向人类操作员的遥操作系统的关键方向。

Abstract: Teleoperation, a human-in-the-loop control scheme, allows a human operator to remotely command and guide a mobile robot to navigate in off-road environments, yet fluent and user-friendly tele-navigation requires an alignment of traversability evaluation between human and robot. In the teleoperation system, the human operator typically utilizes off-site incomplete and delayed feedback via a human-machine interface to make a judgment of traversability, while the robot makes such an evaluation based on in situ onboard sensory information, which could cause divergent traversability estimation and thus generate mismatched decisions and actions. Existing approaches for traversability modeling, estimation, and prediction are mainly derived from the view of robots, i.e., robot-centric, and are practically suitable for fully autonomous mobile robots, but neglect the influence of human operators. To address this problem, this paper extends the concept of traversability from robot-centric to human-centric by accounting for the operator's cognitive states, such as attention, workload, and risk tolerance or awareness, termed tele-traversability. We first revisit the definitions and roles of traversability in robotics and then extend them to teleoperation settings. Finally, we highlight future trends and open challenges of tele-traversability toward human-centric teleoperation systems.

</details>


### [57] [Recovering Aggressively Pruned Vision-Language-Action Models with Offline Hidden-State Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19579)
*Chiyoung Kim,Sanghyuk Roy Choi,Minhyeok Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种全离线恢复宽度剪枝VLA模型性能的方法（隐藏态知识蒸馏），无需在线rollout，约8 GPU小时即可将63%压缩的OpenVLA-OFT恢复到接近教师水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型语言主干参数量达数十亿，难以部署在机器人硬件上。结构化剪枝可压缩主干，但OpenVLA-OFT剪掉63%后LIBERO-Long成功率从93.2%暴降至0.8%。现有恢复方法需监督微调加强化学习，依赖在线rollout与数百GPU小时，成本高昂。

Method: 采用宽度剪枝：缩小块宽度但保持残差流原始尺寸，使教师与学生隐状态形状一致，可直接匹配而无需投影器。以教师单次前向构建的缓存为目标进行离线训练。在九个压缩比上扫描，对比隐藏态蒸馏与监督恢复；并在CogACT上验证，另比较宽度剪枝与深度剪枝。

Result: 约8 GPU小时即将63%压缩学生恢复到距教师3.5个点内。45%以内两种恢复无显著差异；63%–87%区间隐藏态蒸馏提升+2.1至+4.5点，CogACT上63%起提升+9.4至+22.1点。CogACT 81%时三倍预算将差距缩至平均3.9点，监督恢复仍低20点以上。6-DoF机械臂上72%压缩学生达77.5%成功率（监督为59.5%），比教师快2.23倍，内存少62%。

Conclusion: 宽度剪枝配合隐藏态蒸馏可在完全离线、低算力条件下高效恢复压缩VLA模型性能；同等压缩下宽度剪枝成功率更高，深度剪枝延迟更低，蒸馏显著优于监督恢复。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models let robots follow language instructions, but their language backbones of several billion parameters are the main obstacle to running them on robot hardware. Structured pruning reduces that backbone, and removing 63% of it from OpenVLA-OFT drops LIBERO-Long success from 93.2% to 0.8%. A recent approach restores such a model with supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning, which needs online rollouts and hundreds of GPU-hours. We recover most of the lost success entirely offline. Width pruning narrows the blocks but keeps the residual stream at its original size, so teacher and student hidden states have the same shape and are matched directly, without a projector. Training against a cache built in one teacher pass lifts the 63%-reduced student to within 3.5 points of the teacher in about 8 GPU-hours. A sweep over nine ratios locates where the recovery objective starts to matter. Up to 45% reduction the two do not differ significantly on OpenVLA-OFT. Hidden-state distillation then adds +2.1 to +4.5 points there between 63% and 87%, and +9.4 to +22.1 points on CogACT from 63% onward. At 81% on CogACT, a tripled recovery budget narrows the distilled student's gap to the teacher to 3.9 points on average, while supervised recovery stays more than 20 points below. At matched compression, width pruning yields higher success and depth pruning lower latency. On a 6-DoF manipulator, the distilled student at 72% reduction reaches 77.5% success against 59.5% for supervised recovery, runs 2.23x faster on-board than the teacher, and uses 62% less memory.

</details>


### [58] [OmniCalib: Target-Free, Task-Structured Self-Calibration for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19582)
*Kaixiang Lu,Haiyu Lan,Chunxiao Qiao,You Li,Enyu Li,Yehao Lu,Jiarui Yang,Peiwen Lin,Chuang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniCalib：仅用机器人自身运动与板载传感、无需标定靶标的全身多模块外参与关节零位标定流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 装配、磨损与部件更换会破坏人形CAD模型中的传感器外参与关节零位；现有方法通常只标定单一传感器对，或依赖外部基准物（fiducials）。

Method: 提出OmniCalib无靶标工作流：每个模块将机器人原生任务与一个参数块匹配，进行可观测性检验，并仅把受支持的修正量写回CAD模型。核心为深度ICP方法恢复全部14个手臂关节零位并标定所有RGB-D相机外参；下肢用四个静态双支撑站姿恢复12个关节零位；头部模块结合多相机视觉里程计、腿式里程计与通过实时ROS变换树的动态补偿。

Result: 相对CAD，左腕外参修正10.56 mm/1.74°，右腕6.33 mm/1.25°，胸腔RGB-D相机9.81 mm/0.929°；ICP点对面残差2.09 mm。注入偏移下ICP与ArUco均将14个关节零位恢复到0.1°编码器分辨率以下。AGIBOT A3 Ultra上四个站姿恢复12个下肢零位，RMS误差0.063°。头部模块仅用平面行走，三序列平均SO(3)误差1.061°，最优0.775°，与iKalibr在丰富6-DOF激励下的0.902°相当；机构相对角重复性0.140°。

Conclusion: 注入恢复与留出测试验证了各可观测参数块的有效性，表明无需靶标即可完成全身关联标定。

Abstract: Assembly, wear, and component replacement perturb the sensor extrinsics and joint zeros encoded by a humanoid CAD model. Existing procedures calibrate one sensor pair or require external fiducials. Using only robot-native motion and onboard sensing, we present OmniCalib, a target-free workflow that calibrates the full upper limbs---all 14 arm joint zeros and the extrinsics of both wrist and chest cameras---as well as lower limbs and the multi-camera head rig. Each module matches a robot-native task to a parameter block, checks observability, and writes only supported corrections to the CAD model. Our depth ICP method recovers all 14 arm joint zeros and calibrates all RGB-D camera extrinsics without any calibration target. Relative to CAD, the estimated extrinsic corrections are 10.56 mm and 1.74 degrees for the left wrist, 6.33 mm and 1.25 degrees for the right wrist, and 9.81 mm and 0.929 degrees for the chest RGB-D camera. ICP point-to-plane residual is 2.09 mm. On the same injected offsets, ICP and ArUco recover all 14 joint zeros below the 0.1-degree encoder-resolution reference. On an AGIBOT A3 Ultra humanoid, four static double-support stances recover all 12 lower-limb joint-zero offsets injected with an RMS error of 0.063 degrees. The head module combines multi-camera visual odometry with legged odometry and dynamic compensation through the live ROS transform tree. Using only planar walking, it attains a mean SO(3) error of 1.061 degrees across three sequences. The best sequence reaches 0.775 degrees, competitive with iKalibr at 0.902 degrees from rich 6-DOF excitation. Rig-relative angles repeat within 0.140 degrees. Injection recovery and held-out tests validate each observable block.

</details>


### [59] [Quantifying Mechanical Intelligence in Legged Robots with Information Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19588)
*Zach J. Patterson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出以信息论度量"机械智能"，用现代足式机器人作为基准，把机体动力学视为计算过程与通信信道，量化机械模态与机器人坐标间的信息比特处理，并研究串联弹性驱动与低减速比本体感受传动在运动控制中的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械智能（由机器人物理形态减轻控制负担的能力）已成为机器人学热点，在仿生、软体、集群机器人等领域均有体现，但其严格的理论理解与定量度量落后于已开发的工程系统。作者希望为机械智能建立可量化的理论刻画，并统一解释足式机器人工程中的既有洞见。

Method: 将机体动力学同时建模为计算过程与通信信道，提出若干信息论指标来量化机械智能；以比特为单位度量机械模态处理的信息量及其在各机器人坐标间的传递。围绕"通过串联弹性驱动显式引入柔顺性"与"采用低减速比本体感受传动"这两种方案的权衡展开分析，并考察它们与运动控制策略的交互。研究在三类复杂度递增的系统上进行：简化的机器人腿部传动线性模型、非线性单腿仿真、以及在复杂地形上由学习策略控制的仿真四足机器人。

Result: 表明若干先前的足式机器人工程洞见可用信息论加以描述；实现了对机械模态处理比特量及机器人坐标间信息流动的定量刻画；刻画了串联弹性驱动与低减速比本体感受传动之间的权衡，以及这些机制在运动过程中与控制策略的相互作用。

Conclusion: 该工作为机械智能提供了信息论度量框架，并为更广泛地研究机器人机构及其在具身计算中的作用奠定基础。

Abstract: Mechanical intelligence, loosely defined as the reduction in control burden afforded by a robot's physical form, has become a prominent concept in robotics, with instantiations in bioinspired robotics, soft robotics, robotic swarms, and many other areas. However, rigorous theoretical understanding and quantitative measures of mechanical intelligence have lagged behind the engineering systems that the community has developed. In this work, using modern legged robots as a benchmark and exemplar, we propose several information-theoretic metrics for quantifying mechanical intelligence. By viewing body dynamics as both a computational process and a communication channel, we show that several prior insights in legged-robot engineering can be described using information theory, and we quantify how bits are processed by mechanical modes and across robot coordinates. Specifically, we examine the trade-off between explicitly incorporating compliance through series-elastic actuation and using so-called proprioceptive, low-gear-ratio transmissions, and we explore how these mechanisms interact with control policies during locomotion. We develop these results on systems of increasing complexity: a simplified linear model of a robot-leg transmission, a nonlinear single-leg simulation, and simulated quadruped robots controlled by a learned policy while navigating challenging terrain. These results lay the groundwork for broader study of robot mechanisms and their role in embodied computation.

</details>


### [60] [WorldContact: A Contact-Centric World Model for Scalable Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19600)
*Caoliwen Wang,Mengdi Wang,Heng Zhang,Shixun Huang,Siyuan Chen,Chao Liu,Anpei Chen,Zhendong Wang,Peter Yichen Chen,Huamin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WorldContact——一种以接触为中心的可变形物体操作世界模型，用少量高质量轨迹高效生成额外训练数据，速度比原数值模拟器快10倍，扩充数据后微调VLA策略可将提袋任务成功率从65%提升至95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人适应新物体和新任务需要交互经验，但这类经验获取成本高昂。原数值模拟器为解析快速运动、防止穿透需采用极小积分步长，效率受限，因此需要一种能高效生成训练数据的方法。

Method: 构建WorldContact，一种以接触为中心（contact-centric）的可变形物体操作世界模型。模型从有限的高质量轨迹中学习，并以比源数值模拟器更大的时间步预测物体动力学，从而高效生成额外训练数据。在16项购物袋操作任务上评估，并用于微调现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，再直接部署到真实机器人。

Result: 在单张H100 GPU上，状态推演（不含渲染与磁盘I/O）实现相对源模拟器10倍加速。提袋任务中，仅用源仿真数据微调的策略单次尝试成功率为65%，而使用WorldContact扩充数据集微调后达到95%。

Conclusion: 结果表明WorldContact能实现高效数据生成，支持机器人策略的适应与迁移。

Abstract: Adapting robots to new objects and tasks requires interaction experience that can be costly to obtain. We present WorldContact, a contact-centric world model for deformable-object manipulation, constructed from a limited set of high-quality trajectories to generate additional training data efficiently. It predicts object dynamics using larger time steps than the source numerical simulator, which requires small integration steps to resolve rapid motion and prevent interpenetration. We evaluate WorldContact across 16 shopping-bag manipulation tasks. State-rollout measurements on a single H100 GPU show a $10\times$ speedup over the source simulator, excluding rendering and disk I/O. We use the generated data to fine-tune an existing vision-language-action policy and deploy it directly on a real robot. In bag lifting, the same policy achieves 65% single-attempt success when fine-tuned on source simulation data alone, compared with 95% when fine-tuned on the dataset expanded with WorldContact. These results support efficient data generation with WorldContact for robot policy adaptation.

</details>


### [61] [TacSushi: Tactile-Grounded World-Action Modeling for Dexterous Sushi Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19613)
*Haodi Hu,Kaen Kogashi,Toshiaki Koike-Akino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacSushi：一种基于Cosmos3、以触觉为依托的世界-动作策略，在训练中利用未来后果监督，在部署时去除预测解码器，实现灵巧食物操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧食物操作面临形变、遮挡和接触不确定性带来的控制难题，需要触觉接地且能利用失败经验的控制策略。

Method: 主干编码当前RGB、语言与手部状态；逐特征门控融合将指尖触觉特征并入动作表示。训练时，以示范动作块为条件的解码器预测记录的未来视觉观测、任务进度、相对接触风险与触觉摘要，部署时移除该解码器。失败试验仅用于提供后果监督，其动作不纳入模仿学习。在340次成功与50次失败的真实机器人试验上训练。

Result: 在3个分布内任务和2个分布外食材变体上进行600次独立rollout，与6种方法对比；终端结果采用锚定视觉质量协议评分（5项人类评分与3项视觉语言模型评分等权）。完整TacSushi分布内平均成功率68.3%、分布外37.5%；去除未来后果监督为36.7%/10.0%；以直接触觉拼接替代门控融合为25.0%/17.5%。

Conclusion: 逐特征门控触觉融合与仅训练期预测监督带来互补收益，验证了触觉接地世界-动作策略在灵巧食物操作中的有效性。

Abstract: Dexterous food manipulation requires control under deformation, occlusion, and uncertain contact. We present TacSushi, a tactile-grounded, Cosmos3-based world-action policy that learns from recorded future consequences while acting on current observations. The backbone encodes current RGB, language, and hand state, and feature-wise gated fusion incorporates fingertip tactile features into the action representation. During training, a decoder conditioned on demonstrated action chunks predicts logged future visual observations, task progress, relative contact risk, and tactile summaries; this decoder is removed at deployment. Failed trials provide consequence supervision, but their actions are excluded from imitation. We train TacSushi on 340 successful and 50 failed real-robot trials and compare six methods in 600 separate rollouts across three in-distribution tasks and two out-of-distribution ingredient variants. To assess food quality beyond a single geometric threshold, we score terminal outcomes using an anchored visual-quality protocol that equally weights five human ratings and three vision-language-model ratings per rollout. Full TacSushi achieves 68.3% average in-distribution success and 37.5% out-of-distribution success, compared with 36.7%/10.0% without future-consequence supervision and 25.0%/17.5% with direct tactile concatenation in place of gated fusion. These comparisons support complementary benefits of feature-wise gated tactile fusion and training-only predictive supervision.

</details>


### [62] [EmbodiedMind: Adaptive Data Curation and Prefix-Tree Reinforcement Learning for Efficient Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19659)
*Feifan Wang,Zongbing Zhang,Yu Zhang,Lingfeng Wang,Yurui Zhu,Jin Deng,Mingliang Zhang,Zhengguang Gao,Yongcheng Wang,Jin Xu,Ri Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出高效具身基础模型训练范式EmbodiedMind，通过三阶段协同设计解决样本利用低效、梯度贡献失衡与长程信用分配问题，在18个基准上取得70.02%的平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身基础模型训练通常依赖大规模数据与算力，但存在三个关键局限：(1)低信息量样本导致样本利用效率低；(2)异构任务间梯度贡献不平衡；(3)长程规划中轨迹级奖励无差别惩罚所有token，导致严重的信用分配问题。

Method: 提出三阶段协同训练范式：(1)基于拒绝采样的微调(RSFT)，过滤低信息量样本，建立稳健行为先验并防止分布坍缩；(2)迭代拒绝GRPO(IR-GRPO)，采用按难度分层的任务专属队列，在强化学习迭代中保持数据集均衡，并配合混合奖励机制实现精确的跨任务反馈；(3)提出基于动作前缀树的强化学习算法Trie-GRPO，实现步级优势估计，通过将中间正确决策与下游错误隔离来解决信用分配问题，并在探索效率与深度间取得平衡。

Result: EmbodiedMind在18个基准上取得70.02%的最优平均性能，在长程任务规划准确率上显著优于其他具身基础模型。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了数据选择与分层策略优化相结合的高效训练范式在具身基础模型中的有效性，项目将开源以支持可复现性。

Abstract: Training embodied foundation models typically requires massive-scale datasets and extensive computational resources, yet often suffers from three critical limitations: (1) inefficient sample utilization due to low-informative samples; (2) imbalanced gradient contributions across heterogeneous tasks; and (3) severe credit assignment problem in long-horizon planning, where trajectory-level rewards indiscriminately penalize all tokens. To address these issues, we propose an efficient training paradigm that achieves state-of-the-art average performance through strategic data selection and hierarchical policy optimization. Our approach consists of three synergistic stages. First, Rejection Sampling-based Fine-Tuning (RSFT) filters out low-informative samples to establish robust behavioral priors while preventing distributional collapse. Second, Iterative Rejection GRPO (IR-GRPO) employs task-specific queues stratified by difficulty to keep datasets balanced across reinforcement learning iterations, coupled with a hybrid reward mechanism for precise cross-task feedback. Third, to enhance long-horizon task planning, we introduce Trie-GRPO, a novel reinforcement learning algorithm based on action prefix trees, which enables step-level advantage estimation. This resolves the credit assignment problem by isolating intermediate correct decisions from downstream errors, while effectively balancing exploration efficiency and depth compared to conventional search trees. As a result, EmbodiedMind achieves a state-of-the-art average performance of 70.02% across 18 benchmarks, and significantly outperforms other embodied foundation models in long-horizon task planning accuracy. Our project will be released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [63] [ReShoot: Generative Visual Domain Randomization of Recorded Robot Demonstrations for Visuomotor Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19661)
*Chiyoung Kim,Min Sung Choi,Jinho Ju,Chanhoe Gu,Donghwan Hwang,Wonseok Choi,Woongsun Jeon,Minhyeok Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ReShoot框架：通过视觉语言模型编辑属性并重渲染已有演示视频来合成视觉多样性数据，缓解模仿学习策略的视觉过拟合，无需额外真实采集即可提升对新外观的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习策略常过拟合训练演示中的视觉条件，物体颜色或背景变化会导致性能大幅下降。常规缓解方法是在每个新视觉场景中重新采集演示，但这一过程资源消耗大，需反复使用机器人、受控环境与人工操作。

Method: 将负担从数据采集转移到数据生成：用视觉语言模型对场景进行描述并编辑目标属性（背景、物体颜色或材质），再用边缘条件视频生成器重渲染两个相机视角，并相应更新指令。动作序列与本体感觉轨迹原样复制、不重新标注，因此每个生成回合保留原始的动作与本体感觉标签。

Result: 在LIBERO上，用真实与重渲染演示等量混合训练的策略性能与仅用真实数据训练相当（96.5% vs. 96.9%）；该混合训练集在LIBERO-Plus上提升了对场景扰动的鲁棒性（85.5% vs. 82.3%）。在两个物理机器人平台上，分别使用43和100条预采集演示部署ReShoot，使重着色物体的成功率从0.0%提升至42.9%和47.5%，同时保持原始外观下的性能。

Conclusion: ReShoot通过重渲染已有演示来合成视觉多样性，在不牺牲原始性能的前提下有效提升策略对新视觉场景的鲁棒性，且显著降低数据采集成本。

Abstract: Imitation-learned robot policies are frequently overfit to the visual conditions present in their training demonstrations. Consequently, variations in object color or background appearance often induce substantial performance degradation. A common mitigation strategy is to acquire additional demonstrations in each novel visual context; however, this approach is resource-intensive, requiring repeated access to a robot, a controlled environment, and human operation for every appearance condition to be covered. We introduce ReShoot, a framework that synthesizes visual diversity by re-rendering previously recorded demonstrations under altered appearances, thereby shifting the burden from data collection to generation. A vision-language model captions the scene, edits a targeted attribute (e.g., background, object color, or material), and an edge-conditioned video generator re-renders both camera views to match. The instruction is updated accordingly. The action sequence and proprioceptive trajectory are copied verbatim without relabeling, so each generated episode retains the recorded action and proprioceptive labels. On LIBERO, a policy trained on an equal mixture of recorded and re-rendered demonstrations matches the performance of recorded-only training (96.5% vs. 96.9%). Moreover, the mixed training set improves robustness to scene perturbations on LIBERO-Plus (85.5% vs. 82.3%). Across two physical robotic platforms, deploying ReShoot with 43 and 100 pre-collected demonstrations increased the success rate on recolored objects from 0.0% to 42.9% and 47.5%, respectively, while maintaining performance under the original recorded appearance.

</details>


### [64] [Runtime Safety Filtering for Two-Terminal Hazards in Robotic Battery Recycling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19665)
*Yuxin Cao,Wei Song,Xianglin Yang,Fusen Guo,Lin Li,Xiao Cheng,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在LIBERO中用冻结的OpenVLA策略研究"双端子"接近型操作风险（如导电物体同时靠近电池两极）的运行时安全过滤，发现谓词结构对安全—效用前沿影响甚微，而回退动作策略的影响显著更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运行时安全过滤将不安全状态定义为逐物体keep-out区域的并集，这种表示对依赖联合空间关系的风险过于保守：例如电池回收场景中，导电载荷只有在同时接近两个端子时才会造成短路。因此需要研究如何针对这类双端子风险设计运行时过滤器。

Method: 将运行时过滤器分解为三个设计维度：谓词结构、几何裕度、以及指令被拒绝时的回退动作。比较合取谓词、传统双位点keep-out及其复合三种谓词族，并在各自裕度扫描下刻画安全—效用前沿；在匹配工况下比较四种回退策略（保持、撤退、采样搜索、连续动作障碍投影），并在三个工作单元、第二种策略与任务套件、以及存在的间隙估计误差下进行验证。

Result: 三类谓词族在各自最优裕度下给出几乎相同的安全—效用前沿。回退策略影响显著更大：相比撤退，保持策略使任务成功率最多下降0.302，且并未减少风险；两种最小侵入式回退则残留更多风险。该排序可迁移到第二种策略与任务套件，撤退式过滤在间隙误差下依然有效，但载荷尺寸估计中的相关性误差比端子位置中更大的独立误差更具破坏性。

Conclusion: 对于接近定义的操作风险，裕度选择与回退策略在决定运行时过滤器的安全—效用权衡上比谓词结构更重要。

Abstract: Runtime safety filters for learned manipulation policies typically define unsafe states as unions of object-wise keep-out regions. This representation can be unnecessarily restrictive for hazards that depend on a joint spatial relation, such as battery recycling, where a conductive payload can short a charged cell only when it approaches both terminals simultaneously. We study runtime filtering for this two-terminal hazard in LIBERO using frozen OpenVLA policies. We factor a runtime filter into three design choices: the predicate structure, its geometric margin, and the fallback action applied when a commanded action is rejected. We compare a conjunctive predicate, a conventional two-site keep-out, and a composite of the two. For each predicate, we vary its margin to obtain a frontier between task success and residual hazard. We then compare four fallback strategies at matched operating points: holding, retreat, sampled search, and a continuous-action barrier projection. Across three workcells, the three predicate families trace nearly identical safety--utility frontiers once each is evaluated over its own margin. In contrast, the fallback strategy has a substantially larger effect: holding reduces task success by up to 0.302 relative to retreat without reducing hazard, while both minimally invasive fallbacks leave substantially more residual hazard. This ordering transfers to a second policy and task suite, while retreat-based filtering remains effective under standing errors in the clearances available to the filter, although correlated error in the estimated payload size is more damaging than larger independent errors in terminal position. These results show that, for proximity-defined manipulation hazards, margin selection and fallback strategy can matter more than predicate structure in determining the safety--utility trade-off of a runtime filter.

</details>


### [65] [Towards High-DoF Dexterous Manipulation through VLA Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19666)
*Junlei Zhu,Shenzhe Yao,Chaogui Huang,Wenkai Zhu,Jingwei Peng,Guanqi He,Soren Schwertfeger,Jiahao Chen,Yide Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出四阶段后训练流水线（时序手部动作编解码器、SFT、DAgger、真实世界残差强化学习），使开源VLA基础模型适配高自由度灵巧手，在5项真实任务上均达100%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习得到的VLA基础模型虽具备广泛操作能力，但在特定下游任务与硬件平台上仍需后训练。灵巧手因行为库广、自由度极高，其动作空间大且具有结构性，使适配尤为困难。具体障碍有三：开源VLA不原生提供高自由度手部动作接口；人类接管的DAgger过程中手势不匹配导致指令不连续并污染纠正轨迹；在原始关节空间进行强化学习样本效率低。

Method: 统一的四步后训练流水线：1）学习式时序手部动作编解码器，将预训练VLA适配到绝对式灵巧手指令；2）监督微调（SFT）；3）DAgger，采用缓冲回滚、位姿对齐与平滑指令混合，实现连续且任务相关的纠正；4）真实世界残差强化学习，在编解码器捕获的潜空间中做残差探索，将探索限制在协调的手部动作内。

Result: 在双臂交接、手内重定向与工具使用等5项多样化真实世界任务上评估。在报告的后训练预算内，所得策略在每项任务20次试验中均达到100%成功率。

Conclusion: 该流水线为将VLA基础模型适配至可靠的真实世界灵巧操作提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Imitation-learned vision--language--action (VLA) foundation models acquire broad manipulation capabilities by scaling robot data across tasks and embodiments, but reliable deployment on a specific downstream task and hardware platform still requires post-training. Dexterous hands make this adaptation particularly difficult: their broad behavioural repertoire and high degree of freedom create a large and structured action space. Three obstacles are central: open-source VLAs do not natively provide an action interface for high-DoF hands; gesture mismatch during human-gated DAgger takeover creates command discontinuities and contaminates corrective trajectories; and reinforcement learning in the raw joint space is sample-inefficient. We present a unified four-step post-training pipeline comprising a learned temporal hand-action codec, supervised fine-tuning, DAgger, and real-world residual reinforcement learning. The codec adapts a pretrained VLA to absolute dexterous-hand commands. Buffered rollback, pose alignment, and smooth command blending enable continuous, task-relevant DAgger corrections, while latent residual RL confines exploration to coordinated hand motions captured by the codec. We evaluate the pipeline on five diverse real-world tasks spanning bimanual transfer, in-hand reorientation, and tool use. Within the reported post-training budgets, the resulting policies achieve 100\% success on every evaluated task over 20 trials per task. These results provide a practical path for adapting VLA foundation models to reliable real-world dexterous manipulation.

</details>


### [66] [VAST: V2X/Dynamic Map-Aware Autonomous Driving Systems Validation Toolchain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19681)
*Shunsuke Ito,Takuya Azumi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VAST 是一个面向 V2X/动态地图的协同自动驾驶验证工具链，打通 Scenic、SS2、AWSIM、Autoware 与 SIM-LDM，解决互操作性并量化边缘案例发现与安全性收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT-边缘-云连续体中的协同自动驾驶需要覆盖车辆、路侧传感器、边缘动态地图服务与车载自动驾驶栈的系统级验证，而现有工具链之间存在互操作性鸿沟，难以在统一仿真中联合评估。

Method: 不提出新搜索算法，而是构建 V2X/动态地图感知的验证工具链：实现 Lanelet2-to-Scenic 映射、基于 ROS 2 经 Scenario Simulator v2 的联合仿真、向 Autoware 注入动态地图对象，并采集 TTC、PET、碰撞、超时与性能指标；在遮挡交叉口场景中比较约束采样与动态地图可用性，并做 1-16 个 NPC 的吞吐研究。

Result: 遮挡交叉口场景中，Lanelet2 兼容的约束采样将边缘案例发现率从 40.0% 提升到 80.0%，单例平均耗时从 259.7 s 降至 110.4 s；相同生成场景分布下，动态地图可用使碰撞率从 78.0% 降至 40.0%，非碰撞结果从 22.0% 升至 60.0%，TTC/PET 变化具统计显著性。1-16 NPC 吞吐研究中采样耗时始终低于 0.1 s，运行时间主要受 AWSIM/Autoware 执行与重启开销支配。

Conclusion: VAST 可作为协同自动驾驶信息物理系统的实用验证基础设施，其价值在于集成与度量而非新算法，瓶颈主要在仿真器执行与重启开销。

Abstract: Cooperative autonomous driving in the IoT-to-Edge-to-Cloud continuum requires system-level validation across vehicles, infrastructure sensors, edge-side Dynamic Map services, and in-vehicle autonomous-driving stacks. This paper presents VAST, a V2X/Dynamic Map-aware validation toolchain that connects Scenic, Scenario Simulator v2, AWSIM, Autoware, and SIM-LDM. VAST does not introduce a new search algorithm; instead, it addresses interoperability challenges, including Lanelet2-to-Scenic mapping, ROS 2-based co-simulation through SS2, Dynamic Map object injection into Autoware, and collection of TTC, PET, collision, timeout, and performance measurements. In occluded-intersection scenarios, Lanelet2-compatible constrained sampling increases the edge-case discovery rate from 40.0% to 80.0% and reduces the average time per discovered edge case from 259.7 s to 110.4 s. Under the same generated scenario distribution, Dynamic Map availability reduces the collision rate from 78.0% to 40.0% and increases non-collision outcomes from 22.0% to 60.0%, with statistically significant TTC/PET shifts. A throughput study with 1-16 NPCs shows that sampling remains below 0.1 s, whereas AWSIM/Autoware execution and restart overhead dominate runtime. These results position VAST as a practical validation infrastructure for cooperative autonomous-driving CPSs.

</details>


### [67] [LYRIC: Language-Driven Physics-Based Character Control for Contact-Rich Whole-Body Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19688)
*Zeyu Han,Zichong Meng,Julian Tanke,Minami Matsumoto,Sergey Bashkirov,Yingruo Fan,Selim Engin,Dongseok Shim,Takashi Shibuya,Yuki Mitsufuji,Huaizu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LYRIC 是一种基于生成式流匹配的控制器，可用自由形式语言指令和稀疏终端物体目标驱动仿真人进行接触丰富的全身物体交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从语言和稀疏目标实现接触丰富的全身物体交互控制困难，动作捕捉参考不完美，且完整人体运动学参考约束过强。

Method: 用几何条件交互奖励和接触附近松弛参考跟踪训练单一跟踪策略；将控制器分解为任务级规划器（预测短时域物体与人体根轨迹）和动作生成器（闭环求解全身运动与接触）；行为克隆后冻结规划器，用其预测作为稳定监督对动作生成器进行策略后调优。

Result: OMOMO 受控评测中跟踪器成功率 64.3%，对比 InterMimic 复现的 53.2%；统一策略在完整 OMOMO 数据集达 76.5%；留出集上任务成功率 90.3%，对比最强匹配运动学规划基线 74.2%，语义对齐和运动质量更好；无需重训练支持测试时物体路径点引导，并可零样本迁移到新物体形状。

Conclusion: LYRIC 通过规划-生成分解与策略后调优，实现语言驱动的接触丰富全身交互，在成功率、语义对齐和泛化性上优于匹配基线。

Abstract: We present LYRIC, a generative flow-matching controller for language-driven physics-based contact-rich interaction control, that enables simulated characters to perform contact-rich whole-body object interactions from a free-form language instruction and a sparse terminal object goal. To obtain reliable expert trajectories from imperfect motion-capture references, a single tracking policy is trained using geometry-conditioned interaction rewards and relaxed reference tracking near hand-object contact. To guide interaction progress without prescribing a full-body kinematic reference, we factorize the controller into a task-level planner that predicts short-horizon object and humanoid-root trajectories, and an action generator that resolves whole-body motion and contacts in closed loop. After behavior cloning, we freeze the planner and post-tune the action generator on policy using the planner's predictions as stable supervision for intermediate task progression. In a controlled OMOMO evaluation, our tracker achieves 64.3% success compared with 53.2% for an InterMimic reimplementation, while a unified policy achieves 76.5% on the full OMOMO dataset. On the held-out split, LYRIC achieves 90.3% task success, compared with 74.2% for the strongest matched kinematic-planner baseline, with better semantic alignment and motion quality. Without retraining, the controller also supports test-time object-waypoint guidance. Qualitative results further demonstrate robust, natural contact-rich interactions and zero-shot transfer to novel object shapes. The webpage is available at https://neu-vi.github.io/LYRIC/

</details>


### [68] [UniExo: Unified Multi-Skill Policies for Musculoskeletal Locomotion and Co-Adaptive Exoskeleton Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19690)
*Yifei Yuan,Jakob Wolf,Ghaith Androwis,Xianlian Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniExo：先构建多技能人体运动策略，再与髋关节外骨骼控制策略协同训练，实现无需模式切换、支持行走/转弯/跑步/后退行走及其转换的统一控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常运动包含多种活动及其频繁转换，但多数外骨骼控制器仅针对单一活动或少量相关动作设计，活动切换需显式模式切换并分别调参或重新训练；基于仿真的学习虽减少硬件调参，但仍保留该局限。

Method: 将行走、转弯、跑步、后退行走四个单技能模仿专家蒸馏为带技能隐变量的统一网络，并在转换序列上用强化学习微调，得到统一人体策略；再以人体策略的髋关节力矩预测初始化单个髋外骨骼控制器，通过多智能体强化学习在四种技能上与人体策略协同自适应。

Result: 统一人体策略在四种技能未见片段上平均跟踪成功率94.7%，抗扰动鲁棒性优于各单技能专家；协同自适应改变了助力力矩时序并提高向髋关节提供正功的比例；在定制髋外骨骼上，控制器在6名参与者、4种跑步机速度下泛化良好，并协助1名参与者连续完成四种技能及转换，无需技能标签或显式模式切换。

Conclusion: UniExo 为以统一、用户特定控制器替代活动特定控制器迈进一步，能够支持多样运动活动及其之间的转换。

Abstract: Daily locomotion encompasses diverse activities and frequent transitions between them, yet most exoskeleton controllers are designed for a single activity or a narrow set of related movements. Changes in activity therefore typically require explicit mode switching and separately tuned or retrained controllers. Simulation-based learning reduces the need for hardware-based tuning but generally retains this limitation. Here we present UniExo, a framework that first constructs a multi-skill musculoskeletal human policy and then jointly trains an exoskeleton control policy with it. Four single-skill imitation experts for walking, turning, running and backward walking are distilled into a single network structured by a skill latent and subsequently fine-tuned through reinforcement learning on transition sequences. The resultant unified human policy achieves a mean tracking success rate of 94.7% on unseen clips of the four skills and exhibits greater robustness to perturbations than its constituent experts. A single hip exoskeleton controller (UniExo) is initialized from hip moment prediction of the human policy and co-adapted with it through multi-agent reinforcement learning across the four skills. This co-adaptation shifts the timing of the assistance torque and raises the fraction of positive work delivered to the hip. When deployed on a custom hip exoskeleton, the controller generalizes across four treadmill speeds in six participants and assists one participant through a continuous route of all four skills and their transitions, without skill labels or explicit mode switching. UniExo thus provides a step towards replacing activity-specific controllers with unified, user-specific controllers that support diverse locomotor activities and the transitions between them.

</details>


### [69] [Decoupling Physical Speed from Path Parameterization in Singularity-Free Guiding Vector Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19726)
*Zhouru Xiao,Sha Luo,Yang Lu,Mingliang Xiao,Weijia Yao,Bohuan Lin,Xianzhe Cheng,Yaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种具有指定物理速度（PPS）的新型无奇异引导矢量场，实现全局收敛且物理速度可任意设定，并给出二阶模型饱和加速度控制律，经仿真与四旋翼3D实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无奇异引导矢量场（SF-GVF）借助附加虚拟坐标消除了奇异点并保证全局收敛，但在原始低维空间中沿期望路径的速度由路径参数化决定，无法任意指定；而部分归一化物理投影的变通方法可能导致归一化分母为零，引入新的奇异点。

Method: 提出新的具有指定物理速度（PPS）的SF-GVF：在高维空间（含虚拟维）中构造矢量场，使其积分曲线从任意初始条件指数收敛至期望路径；设计使机器人物理速度收敛到PPS、且路径误差动态在期望路径正则重参数化下保持不变的结构；并针对二阶运动学模型开发饱和加速度控制律。

Result: 理论分析证明了积分曲线的指数收敛性与物理速度的PPS收敛性；对比仿真与不同PPS曲线下的四旋翼3D路径跟踪实验验证了理论结果，并展示了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该PPS-SF-GVF消除了原有变通方法引入的奇异点问题，兼顾全局收敛与物理速度的自由指定，且对路径参数化具有不变性，配合饱和加速度控制律可用于实际机器人（如四旋翼）的路径跟踪。

Abstract: The existing singularity-free guiding vector field (SF-GVF) with an additional virtual coordinate can eliminate singular points (i.e., points where the vector field vanishes) inherent in conventional GVFs and guarantee global convergence of robot trajectories to closed and self-intersecting desired paths. However, the desired speed given by the GVF along the desired path in the original lower-dimensional space cannot be arbitrarily specified but depends on path parameterizations. One possible workaround is to partially normalize the physical projection of the SF-GVF and assign a user-designed speed. However, we show that this workaround may introduce new singularities since the normalization denominator can become zero. To address this issue, we propose a new SF-GVF with prescribed physical speed (PPS). The integral curves of the new SF-GVF converge exponentially to the desired path from any initial condition in the higher-dimensional space (including virtual dimension); more importantly, the robot's physical speed converges to the PPS, while the path-error dynamics remain invariant under regular reparameterizations of the desired path. We further develop a saturated acceleration control law for second-order kinematic models. Finally, comparative simulations and 3D path-following experiments with a quadrotor under different PPS profiles validate the theoretical results and demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [70] [Equivariant Filter Design for Acoustic and Depth Aided Inertial Navigation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19742)
*Arihant Lunawat,Pieter van Goor,Frank Dellaert,Stefan B. Williams*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对AUV无GPS导航，利用切丛群对称性构造等变滤波器，将IMU偏置纳入状态空间几何，消除导航状态线性化误差，仿真与实测均优于IEKF与MEKF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV无GPS导航通常融合IMU、DVL速度与压力深度，基于李群的状态表示可提升精度与一致性。但现有IEKF类滤波器将IMU偏置作为欧氏扩展附加，破坏了群仿射结构，使精确对数线性误差动力学失效，导致协方差与估计同时退化。

Method: 采用切丛群（Tangent-Group, TG）对称性，使偏置内蕴于状态空间几何中，为该导航系统推导等变滤波器（EqF）；导航状态线性化误差为零，仅偏置存在二阶误差。同时构建DVL的等变输出模型（更新仅引入三阶线性化误差），并给出直接压力输出模型。

Result: 蒙特卡洛仿真将TG-EqF与双框架群IEKF及乘性EKF对比：在姿态、速度、位置上，TG-EqF误差较两种方法均降低18%–25%；协方差质量上，其ANEES更接近名义值1。基于AUV实测数据的离线分析验证了仿真结论，位置漂移减小。

Conclusion: 将偏置嵌入群几何而非欧氏扩展的切丛群等变滤波，可保持精确的误差动力学与一致的协方差估计，在AUV组合导航中同时提升估计精度与滤波一致性。

Abstract: Autonomous Underwater Vehicles (AUVs) navigating without GPS typically fuse inertial measurements with acoustic Doppler Velocity Log (DVL) velocities and pressure-derived depth. Posing the navigation state on a Lie group improves accuracy and consistency. However, state-of-the-art filters based on the Invariant Extended Kalman Filter (IEKF) append the Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) biases as a Euclidean extension, which breaks the group-affine structure required for exact log-linear error dynamics, causing the reported covariance to degrade alongside the estimate. We apply the Tangent-Group (TG) symmetry, which carries the biases within the geometry of the state space, to derive an Equivariant Filter (EqF) for this system, leaving zero linearization error in the navigation states and second-order error only in the biases. We develop an equivariant output model for the DVL, whose update incurs only third-order linearization error, together with a direct pressure output. Monte Carlo simulations benchmark the TG-EqF against a Two-Frame-Group IEKF and a Multiplicative EKF. The TG-EqF reduces error by 18--25\% against both alternatives in each of attitude, velocity, and position. The main benefit is in the covariance it estimates: its Average Normalized Estimation Error Squared (ANEES) stays closer to its nominal value of one than that of the others. Offline analysis on AUV field data corroborates the findings of the simulations, demonstrating reduced position drift.

</details>


### [71] [LIFD: Anchored Diffusion for 3D-Aware Scene Memory in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19796)
*Wenbo Li,Yiteng Chen,Wenhao Li,Qingyao Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIFD是一个面向部分可观测机器人操作的持久化3D感知场景记忆框架，通过场景token表示与生成式补全实现记忆保持，在LIBERO、MetaWorld及真实UR5e任务上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测下的机器人操作需要超出当前视野的空间信息。几何感知的RGB特征虽能描述可见结构，但当机器人或场景移动后，先前观测区域会消失。因此需要一种既能保留观测历史、又能推断缺失内容且不脱离可见证据的场景表示。

Method: 提出LIFD（Look, Imagine, Focus, and Do）框架，构建持久化、3D感知的场景记忆。方法从多视角一致性学习场景token表示，并借助单张RGB视图与循环记忆进行补全；采用rectified-flow模型生成token，以Anchor-Guided Cross-Attention使补全以当前几何特征为条件；用紧凑slot特征连接该表示与操作策略。表示学习阶段使用多视角与几何监督，部署仅需单目RGB相机、本体感知和任务指令。

Result: LIFD（Staged）在LIBERO上平均成功率91.6%，MetaWorld上79.8%，较Joint training在LIBERO平均成功率提升3.1个百分点。在四个UR5e任务族（每族10个演示）上平均成功率56.0%，而OpenVLA-7B为40.5%。

Conclusion: LIFD通过生成式场景token记忆与锚点引导条件补全，在保持与可见证据联系的同时实现持久3D场景表示，并有效迁移到操作策略，在仿真与真实任务中均优于基线。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation under partial observability requires spatial information that extends beyond the current view. Geometry-aware RGB features describe visible structure, but previously observed regions may disappear as the robot or scene moves. Maintaining a useful scene representation therefore requires retaining observation history while inferring missing content without losing its connection to visible evidence. We introduce LIFD (Look, Imagine, Focus, and Do), a framework for persistent, 3D-aware scene memory. LIFD learns a scene-token representation from multi-view agreement and completes it from a single RGB view and recurrent memory. A rectified-flow model generates the tokens while Anchor-Guided Cross-Attention conditions completion on current geometric features. Compact slot features connect this representation to a manipulation policy. Multi-view and geometric supervision are used during representation learning; deployment requires one RGB camera, proprioception, and a task instruction. LIFD (Staged) reaches 91.6% average success on LIBERO and 79.8% on MetaWorld, improving LIBERO average success by 3.1 percentage points over Joint training. On four UR5e task families with ten demonstrations per family, it achieves 56.0% mean success, compared with 40.5% for OpenVLA-7B.

</details>


### [72] [Affective Shared Autonomy: Temporal Affect Dynamics and Subjective Evaluation in Bimanual Teleoperation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19802)
*Zhengji Liang,Guiyin Tian,Sijin Qu,Hainan Liu,Shiyan Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出情感感知共享自主遥操作框架，基于面部视频、心电信号与双臂运动学实时估计操作者情感状态，仅在检测到持续不利状态时触发辅助，用户研究（N=30）显示有效状态提升最高39.7%且不损害用户自主性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理遥操作中高负荷任务易引发严重认知负荷、挫败感与执行崩溃，而传统共享自主范式主要依赖空间误差边界等任务规则，忽略操作者瞬态情感状态，导致控制干预错位。

Method: 构建情感感知共享自主框架：从同步的面部视频、心脏信号与双臂运动学中估计操作者情感状态，输出七类情感分布与三类别操作抽象（中性、有效、不利）；仅当检测到持续不利状态时选择性触发辅助，以保留任务正向投入。同时采集首个涵盖视觉、生理与双臂运动追踪的多模态时序情感状态数据集，并构建多模态融合模型。

Result: 用户研究（N=30）表明情感辅助将有效状态提升最多39.7%，且不损害用户自主性；多模态融合模型在追踪时序状态动态方面优于零样本基线（Qwen、MiniCPM-V）。

Conclusion: 该真实世界部署为物理人机交互提供了一种以人为中心的新框架，整合视觉、生理与运动追踪信息，实现基于情感状态的共享自主遥操作。

Abstract: Physical teleoperation integrates human cognitive flexibility with robotic precision, yet demanding manipulation tasks frequently induce severe cognitive workload, acute frustration, and execution breakdown. Conventional shared autonomy paradigms rely primarily on task-based rules, such as spatial error boundaries, which disregard the operator's transient affective state and risk misaligned control interventions. To address this limitation, we propose an affect-aware shared autonomy teleoperation framework that dynamically modulates robotic assistance based on real-time operator state estimation. The system estimates operator affective states from synchronized facial video, cardiac signals, and bilateral arm kinematics, outputting a seven-state affective distribution and a three-category operational abstraction (neutral, productive, adverse). Affect-aware assistance is selectively triggered when the user is detected in a continuous adverse state, preserving task-positive engagement without unnecessary disruption. The empirical user study ($N = 30$) confirms that the proposed affective assistance increases the productive states by up to 39.7% without compromising user agency. The collected dataset represents the first multimodal dataset that provides continuous visual, physiological, and operator's bilateral motion tracking of temporal affective state shifts during bimanual teleoperation. Our multimodal fusion model outperforms zero-shot baselines (Qwen, MiniCPM-V) in tracking temporal state dynamics. This real-world deployment offers a new human-centric framework that integrates visual, physiological, and motion tracking for physical human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [73] [HEROIC: Heterogeneous Evidential Reasoning for Open-Vocabulary Identification and Cross-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19803)
*Mihir Chauhan,Aarav Jain,Addison Zucek,Manmeet Dang,Damon Conover,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HEROIC——一个仅用自然语言通信的分散式异构空地多智能体开放词汇搜索协调框架，通过传感器属性与尺度律自动分配角色，并在地形/高度受限时让空中智能体从搜索转为空中分诊、护航与路径引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空地异构机器人团队适合开放世界搜索（侦察、城市搜救、灾后响应、危险环境）。两类平台失效模式不同：空中覆盖快但难辨小/遮挡目标，地面可近距离识别感兴趣目标但覆盖面积小。现有语言任务团队要么预先固定角色，要么由语言模型基于手工能力标签分配角色，导致团队无法在任务中途判断某资产何时不再有用。

Method: HEROIC为分散式异构多智能体开放词汇搜索协调框架，智能体仅以自然语言通信。初始角色分配由传感器属性与尺度律推导，判断目标能否高置信检测；仅凭任务自然语言提示，该律即给出空中飞行高度与扫描间距。当计算高度低于安全飞行高度，空中智能体自我重任务，从搜索者转为空中分诊、护航与地面智能体的路径引导。两类机器人均维护对搜索区域的证据信念（正证据为方位射线，负证据为对数几率后验），并以近距离验证作为到达的门控条件。

Result: 全栈实验中，HEROIC在全部6个场景中84%到达目标，而视觉-语言前沿基线、前沿搜索、割草机式与随机游走在相同感知下为35-54%，且HEROIC到达目标快2-4倍。

Conclusion: HEROIC表明，仅靠自然语言通信的分散式异构空地团队，借助基于传感器属性与尺度律的动态角色分配及证据信念门控，可在开放词汇搜索中显著提升到达成功率与效率，优于固定角色或手工标签分配的方法。

Abstract: Multi-agent heterogeneous air-ground robot teams are attractive for open world search, with applications for reconnaissance, urban search and rescue missions (USAR), disaster response and recovery, and hazardous environments. These two platforms have different failure modes: aerial robots cover ground quickly but cannot resolve small or occluded targets from altitude, while ground robots can identify objects-of-interest, such as people or hazardous objects, at close range but cover less area. Existing language-tasked teams either have roles fixed prior, or have a language model assign them from hand-written capability tags, so the team is unable to know when within a mission an asset is no longer useful. We present HEROIC, a decentralized heterogeneous multi-agent open-vocabulary search coordination framework that requires agents to communicate in natural language only. HEROIC's initial agent role assignment is derived from sensor properties and a scale law to determine whether targets can be detected with a high confidence. From the mission's natural language prompt alone, this law assigns aerial flight altitudes and sweep spacing. When this calculated height falls below the altitude for safe flight, aerial agents re-task themselves from searcher to aerial triage, escort, and route guide for ground agents. Both robots maintain an evidential belief over the search area (bearing rays for positive evidence, a log-odds posterior for negative evidence) and gate any arrival on close-range verification. In full-stack experiments, HEROIC reaches the target 84% of the time across all 6 scenes, compares to 35-54% for vision-language frontier baselines, frontier-based search, lawnmower, and random-walk running the same perception, all while being 2-4x sooner to arrive at the target.

</details>


### [74] [Vehicle Trajectory Prediction via Neural Fusion of Multiple EKF-Based Trajectory Candidates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19813)
*Seong-Jun Kim,Seung-Hyun Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出将Trajectron++神经网络预测器与扩展卡尔曼滤波(EKF)多轨迹候选进行后融合的框架，在nuScenes上无需改动基线架构即降低位移误差约14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需预测周围车辆轨迹以评估碰撞风险并规划安全路径。现有神经网络预测器虽借助历史信息、动态场景图与语义地图取得较优性能，但在加速、减速、转向等特定运动状态下可能输出物理上不可行的轨迹。

Method: 在后期阶段融合Trajectron++（神经网络轨迹预测器）的输出与基于扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）生成的多条轨迹候选，通过可学习的融合策略结合两者，且不修改基线网络架构。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，相比Trajectron++基线，平均位移误差(ADE)降低13.7%，最终位移误差(FDE)降低14.6%。

Conclusion: 基于EKF的轨迹候选可通过学习式融合有效补充神经轨迹预测，提升物理可行性与预测精度。

Abstract: Predicting the future trajectories of surrounding vehicles in autonomous driving is important for collision risk assessment and safe ego-vehicle path planning. Conventional neural network-based trajectory predictors typically achieve strong prediction performance by exploiting agent history, dynamic scene graphs, and semantic maps. However, in specific motion regimes such as acceleration, deceleration, and turning, these predictors may fail to reflect physically feasible trajectories. To address this issue, this study proposes a framework that fuses the output of Trajectron++, a neural network-based trajectory predictor, with extended Kalman filter (EKF)-based multiple trajectory candidates at a late stage. On the nuScenes dataset, the proposed method reduces the average displacement error and final displacement error of the Trajectron++ robot baseline by 13.7% and 14.6%, respectively, without modifying the baseline architecture. These results indicate that EKF-based trajectory candidates can effectively complement neural trajectory prediction through learned fusion.

</details>


### [75] [RotateIt! Fast and Reliable Single-Arm Garment Unfolding via Online-Adaptive Dynamic Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19817)
*Zeqing Zhang,Zuokun Xie,Ao Fang,Bin Dai,Zhengjie Shu,Yifeng Tang,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RotateIt!——首个以动态轴旋转为核心操作原语的单臂衣物展开框架，在仿真与真实场景中显著优于准静态方法，并可衔接自主折叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 衣物展开是机器人下游任务的关键前提：准静态方法需重复动作、效率低，而现有动态方法多依赖双臂甩动，硬件与协调要求高。因此需要一种单臂、紧凑、高效且能利用动态特性的展开方案。

Method: 从桌面随机初始构型出发，机器人选取"旋转有效"的抓取点，将提起的衣物绕近似固定的锚点做自适应轴向旋转，借助惯性张力在紧凑工作空间内分离重叠衣层。用抓取排序器（grasp ranker）选择锚点，并用在线残差策略自适应调节旋转幅度与速度，从而决定释放时机。仿真训练的策略零样本迁移至真实世界。

Result: 在已见与未见的仿真衣物及8件未见真实衣物上，三次尝试内的成功率比准静态抓放方法提升44.0–61.0个百分点；仿真策略零样本迁移真实世界取得成功率75.6%，首次尝试覆盖率提升41%，最终覆盖率提升26%。

Conclusion: 动态轴向旋转可作为单臂衣物展开的有效主操作原语，且所获展开状态无需人工重整即可支撑后续自主折叠，展示了动态操作原语在可变形物体操控中的潜力。

Abstract: Robotic garment unfolding is essential for downstream tasks, yet quasi-static methods require repeated actions, while existing dynamic approaches predominantly rely on bimanual flinging. We present RotateIt!, a single-arm framework that uses adaptive axial rotation for dynamic garment unfolding. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first unfolding framework to employ dynamic axial rotation as its primary manipulation primitive. From a randomly initialized tabletop configuration, the robot selects a rotation-effective grasp and rotates the lifted garment about an approximately fixed anchor, generating inertial tension that separates overlapping layers within a compact workspace. A grasp ranker selects the anchor, while an online residual policy adapts the rotation extent and speed, thereby determining the release timing. Across seen and unseen simulated garments and eight unseen real garments, RotateIt! improves success within three attempts by 44.0-61.0 percentage points over quasi-static pick-and-place. The simulation-trained policies transfer zero-shot to the real world, achieving 75.6% success, 41% higher first-attempt coverage, and 26% higher final coverage. The resulting states further enable autonomous robotic folding without manual rearrangement.

</details>


### [76] [TADreamer: Zero-Shot Language-Guided 3D Navigation for Terrestrial-Aerial Bimodal Robots via Video Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19824)
*Xiangyu Li,Tiancheng Lai,Xijie Huang,Ruitian Pang,Siqi Shen,Juncheng Chen,Zaisheng Pan,Chao Xu,Fei Gao,Yanjun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出零样本框架 TADreamer，将视频想象导航落地到实测几何，实现陆空双模态机器人导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 陆空双模态机器人的语言引导导航需要选择与场景语境和任务意图匹配的路线与运动模式。生成视频可表达此类运动序列，但受尺度模糊和轴向相关几何畸变影响，难以从中恢复度量一致的导航参考。

Method: 无需任务特定训练或微调的零样本框架：视觉语言模型将机载观测与指令转为导航提示，筛选有效生成视频并在需重生成时提供纠正反馈；将选中视频重建为带陆地/空中模式标注的3D路点；两阶段标定先用视场约束初始化尺度估计，再通过将重建点云配准到实测几何来精化轴向尺度、旋转与平移；标定后的路点与模式标签引导融入实测几何的规划器驱动机器人执行。

Result: 在七个室内外场景完成真实世界导航实验；每轮五个候选时，七个场景均在两轮内获得可用视频；标定观测上，相比 NavDreamer 平均绝对深度误差降低87.7%，平均绝对相对深度误差降低86.3%。

Conclusion: TADreamer 证明可通过零样本的视频想象加实测几何标定，获得度量一致的导航参考，支撑陆空双模态机器人在真实环境中的可靠导航。

Abstract: Language-guided navigation for terrestrial-aerial bimodal robots requires selecting routes and locomotion modes that match scene context and task intent. Generated videos can represent such motion sequences, but recovering metrically consistent navigation references from them is challenging because of scale ambiguity and axis-dependent geometric distortions. We present TADreamer, a zero-shot framework that grounds video-imagined navigation in measured geometry without task-specific training or fine-tuning. A vision-language model translates onboard observations and instructions into navigation prompts, selects valid generated videos, and provides corrective feedback when regeneration is needed. The selected video is reconstructed into 3D waypoints annotated with terrestrial or aerial modes. A two-stage calibration procedure uses field-of-view constraints to initialize scale estimation, then refines axis-dependent scales, rotation, and translation by registering the reconstructed point cloud to measured geometry. The calibrated waypoints and mode labels guide a planner that incorporates measured geometry for robot execution. Real-world experiments demonstrate navigation across seven indoor and outdoor scenarios. With five candidates per round, usable videos are obtained within two rounds in all seven scenarios. On the calibration observations, our method reduces mean absolute depth error by 87.7% and mean absolute relative depth error by 86.3% compared with NavDreamer.

</details>


### [77] [Improving Cross-embodiment Transfer in Latent Action Models with Action-Similarity Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19846)
*Maxime Alvarez,Renzo Caballero,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出用动作相似性监督替代辅助动作预测损失来训练潜在动作模型（LAM），使潜在动作编码与具体本体无关，在RoboTwin 2.0双臂跨本体迁移实验中使成功率提升一倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略可借助网络规模预训练获得视觉与语言能力，但演示数据采集昂贵且与录制机器人强绑定。潜在动作模型（LAM）可从无动作视频中学习可跨本体共享的潜在动作，然而实际中存在两个问题：对背景视觉噪声敏感；同一运动在不同机器人上可能被编码为不同潜在动作。已有方案通过辅助损失从潜在动作预测机器人动作，将潜在动作空间与特定本体的动作空间绑定，本文希望探索同一标签的另一种用法以摆脱本体特异性。

Method: 采用动作相似性监督：训练任意两个潜在动作之间的相似度，使其匹配两段真实机器人动作序列的相似度。真实动作从不被LAM直接预测，因此潜在动作无需编码本体特定信息。在RoboTwin 2.0上构建受控实验：两台双臂机器人演示互不相交的任务集，策略在所有演示上训练，每台机器人在闭环中仅评估对方演示过的任务。对比LAM训练中的辅助动作预测损失，并考察相似度计算空间（末端执行器运动 vs 关节空间运动）以及是否允许损失跨两台机器人比较潜在动作。

Result: 在策略架构、超参数、数据集与评估协议均固定的条件下，预测潜在动作相比预测真实动作使跨本体成功率提升超过一倍。在给定相同真实动作的前提下，相似性监督的迁移效果好于在LAM训练中预测真实动作的辅助损失。使用末端执行器运动而非关节空间运动计算相似度，并允许损失跨两台机器人比较潜在动作，是本研究中最优的方案。

Conclusion: 相似性监督是一种比动作预测辅助损失更有效的LAM训练方式，可使潜在动作空间与本体解耦并提升跨本体迁移性能；相似度定义在末端执行器空间且跨本体比较时效果最佳。

Abstract: As generalist robot policies gain vision and language from web-scale pretraining, demonstrations remain costly to collect and tied to the robot that recorded them. Latent action models (LAMs) address both by learning latent actions from action-free videos that can be shared across embodiments, however, in practice, LAMs are sensitive to background visual noise, and the same motion from two different robots may be encoded with different latents. One solution to the background visual noise is to add an auxiliary loss predicting the robot action from the latent action, further associating the latent action space to the embodiment specific robot action space. We study a different use of the same labels, through action-similarity supervision. The similarity between any two latent actions is trained to match the similarity of the two ground-truth robot action sequences. The ground-truth actions are never predicted by the LAM, so the latent action does not need to encode embodiment specifics. We evaluate cross-embodiment transfer on RoboTwin 2.0 in a controlled setup, two bimanual robots demonstrate disjoint task sets, a policy is trained on all the demonstrations, and each robot is evaluated closed-loop on the tasks only the other demonstrated. With the policy architecture and its hyperparameters, the dataset, and the evaluation protocol fixed, predicting latent actions instead of ground-truth actions more than doubles cross-embodiment success. Given the same ground-truth actions, similarity supervision transfers better than an auxiliary loss that predicts the ground-truth action during the LAM training. Computing the similarities on end-effector motion rather than joint-space motion, and letting the loss compare latent actions across the two robots, gives the best approach of the study.

</details>


### [78] [GR2PO: Group Relative Return Policy Optimization for Continuous Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19850)
*Pengqin Wang,Qiming Zhang,Shaojie Shen,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无评论家强化学习框架GR2PO，用于连续机器人控制，性能超越无评论家基线并媲美主流Actor-Critic方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Actor-Critic方法依赖价值网络，带来额外训练开销且价值估计误差会影响策略学习；而现有的无评论家组相对策略优化方法在稠密奖励环境中直接使用即时奖励，无法学习长期动作结果。

Method: GR2PO从并行采集的轨迹中估计折扣回报，在每个rollout时间索引处进行组归一化，并利用相对优势与裁剪目标更新策略。

Result: 在机器人控制仿真环境中验证，并部署到真实边缘设备。GR2PO显著优于使用即时奖励的无评论家基线，与最先进Actor-Critic方法竞争力相当；训练效率具有竞争力；在NVIDIA Jetson TX2上的推理测试验证了在边缘平台部署的可行性。

Conclusion: GR2PO是一种有效的无评论家强化学习框架，消融实验分析了并行组大小、回报估计方法和目标裁剪比例对学习性能的影响，代码将在论文接收后公开。

Abstract: Actor-critic architecture has been widely used in continuous robot control. However, they rely on learning a value network, introducing additional computational overhead during training. Moreover, policy learning may also be affected by the approximation error of value estimation. Critic-free group relative policy optimization methods provide a simpler training approach by removing the need for a critic. However, they fail to learn long-term action outcomes when directly applying immediate rewards to policy optimization in dense-reward environments. To address these problems, we propose Group Relative Return Policy Optimization (GR2PO), a critic-free reinforcement learning framework for continuous robot control. GR2PO estimates the discounted returns from the parallelly collected trajectories, performs group normalization at each rollout time index, and uses relative advantages and clipped targets to update the policy. To evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed framework, we instantiate it on robot control simulation environments and deploy the model to a real-world edge device. The results show that GR2PO significantly outperforms critic-free baselines that use immediate rewards and performs competitively against state-of-the-art actor-critic methods. Furthermore, GR2PO demonstrates competitive training efficiency. Inference tests on NVIDIA Jetson TX2 demonstrate the feasibility of deploying the learned policies on edge platforms. Further ablation experiments analyze the effects of parallel group size, return estimation methods, and target clipping ratio on learning performance. To support follow-up research, we will make the complete code publicly available after the paper is accepted, including the framework implementation, experimental configuration, and training and evaluation scripts.

</details>


### [79] [Feeling Terrain Before Crossing: World Models for Off-Road Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19863)
*E-In Son,Dong-Wook Kim,Ji-Hoon Hwang,Kangsun Lee,Jisung Bae,Jung-Taak Kim,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO
