TL;DR: 提出首个以本体感觉为条件的越野导航世界模型 Feel-WM，同时预测视觉场景与机器人自身的物理未来（本体感觉状态与失败风险），并在真实山地环境中实现闭环越野导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野导航因依赖机器人-地形交互，仅预测视觉场景不足，需同时预测机器人将感受到的物理动态（打滑、倾斜、震动等），而现有以场景为中心的世界模型无法做到。

Method: Feel-WM 以本体感觉为输入条件，学习预测未来本体感觉状态与失败风险（无需人工标签，直接从机器人自身经验学习）；规划器同时推演物理未来与场景，并以可分离的评分将预测失败风险与目标相似度加权。

Result: 在真实越野数据与仿真中，Feel-WM 在开环规划与闭环崎岖地形导航上均优于纯视觉导航世界模型，覆盖轮式与足式平台；部署于 Husky 山地小径，可车载实时规划、预判崎岖路面并绕行，完成端到端策略失败的路线。

Conclusion: 将本体感觉引入世界模型，使预测覆盖“看到什么”与“感受到什么”，显著提升越野导航的规划与执行能力，优于纯视觉方案。

Abstract: Navigation world models plan by foresight, predicting the future that each candidate action sequence produces and selecting the best, rather than mapping observations to actions directly. Unlike urban settings where a predicted scene is a sufficient proxy, off-road navigation hinges on the robot--terrain interaction, so the prediction must cover not only what the camera will see but what the robot will feel. However, existing scene-focused models do not predict how much the robot will slip, tilt or shake along a planned trajectory. Proprioception captures these dynamics directly and, when used as input, improves the prediction of the physical future. We present Feel-WM, the first off-road navigation world model that conditions on proprioception and predicts what the robot will feel alongside what the camera will see. The physical future takes the form of a future proprioceptive state and a failure risk, both learned from the robot's own experience without human labels. The planner rolls out the physical future alongside the scene and weighs the predicted failure risk against goal similarity in a separable score. Experiments on real off-road data and in simulation demonstrate that Feel-WM outperforms visual-only navigation world models in open-loop planning and closed-loop rough-terrain navigation across wheeled and legged platforms. Deployed on a Husky on mountain trails, Feel-WM plans onboard, predicts rough ground ahead and steers around it, completing courses that an end-to-end policy fails.

</details>


### [80] [Learning Reliable Parking Policies via Offline Reinforcement Learning with Quantized Action Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19894)
*Zewei Yang,Zengqi Peng,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于路点级离线强化学习的交互感知自动泊车框架，在CARLA中取得最高泊车成功率并可迁移到未见车位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市环境中的自动泊车是常规但安全攸关的任务。杂乱且弱结构化的停车空间，以及周围车辆带来的交互不确定性，导致可靠的机动生成十分困难。

Method: 构建了来自分层专家轨迹并带旋转路点增强的专用泊车数据集，涵盖非交互与交互场景；策略以紧凑状态表示为条件，其中LiDAR障碍特征经特征级线性调制（FiLM）适配到目标位姿；使用状态条件化tokenizer将连续路点序列量化为离散动作token，并在其上执行保守Q学习（CQL）以抑制对支持不足动作的价值高估。

Result: 在高保真CARLA模拟器中进行大量闭环实验，所提框架在所有基线上取得最高泊车成功率，并能可靠迁移到未见过的泊车位。

Conclusion: 路点级离线强化学习结合交互感知状态表示与保守价值学习，可有效提升自动泊车的成功率与泛化能力。

Abstract: Parking is a routine yet safety-critical task for autonomous vehicles operating in urban environments. However, cluttered and weakly structured parking spaces, compounded by the interactive uncertainty from surrounding vehicles, hinder reliable maneuver generation. To address these challenges, we develop a waypoint-level offline reinforcement learning framework for interaction-aware autonomous parking. Specifically, a dedicated parking dataset is constructed from hierarchical expert rollouts with rotational waypoint augmentation, covering both non-interactive scenarios and interactive ones. The policy is then conditioned on a compact state representation, in which LiDAR-based obstacle features are adapted to the target pose via feature-wise linear modulation. A state-conditioned tokenizer further quantizes continuous waypoint sequences into discrete action tokens, over which conservative Q-learning is performed to suppress value overestimation on poorly supported actions. Extensive closed-loop experiments are conducted in the high-fidelity CARLA simulator. The proposed framework attains the highest parking success rate among all baselines and transfers reliably to unseen parking slots.

</details>


### [81] [Learning and Transferring Closed-Loop Robot Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19906)
*So Kuroki,Yujin Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将完整闭环策略实现视为可复用的执行经验：编码智能体在源任务上生成并迭代优化策略代码存入软件archive，新任务据此生成并优化策略后冻结执行。源任务成功率28.3%→64.2%；九个目标任务三次运行平均成功率：无参考45.2%、初始源码41.5%、优化源码57.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环机器人策略需观测处理、状态管理与情境分支，人工设计与调参成本高。编码智能体虽已支持控制代码生成与优化，但"在源任务上被改进的实现是否也能帮助新任务的策略获取"尚不明确。

Method: 把完整闭环实现当作可复用的执行经验。源任务：编码智能体基于少量成功示范生成策略代码，并用仿真反馈迭代改进，验证集选出的实现存入软件archive。新任务：智能体结合archive中的实现、目标任务示范与执行反馈生成并改进策略，最终策略冻结、执行时不再调用模型。在RoboCasa的4个源任务与9个目标任务上，跨3次独立运行比较无参考、初始源码参考、优化后源码参考三种条件。

Result: 源任务经迭代优化，平均成功率由28.3%提升至64.2%。九个目标任务三次运行的平均成功率：无参考45.2%，初始源码参考41.5%，优化源码参考57.0%。优化参考在三次运行中均优于初始参考，平均提升15.6个百分点；但在两个目标任务上按运行平均时初始参考更优。

Conclusion: 在该设定下，经执行优化的软件可作为获取新策略的有效资源；初始参考并非普遍有效，其价值取决于任务。

Abstract: Closed-loop robot policies require observation processing, state management, and situation-dependent branching, making them costly to design and tune manually. Although coding agents increasingly support control-code generation and optimization, it remains unclear whether implementations improved on source tasks also support policy acquisition for new tasks. We study this question by treating complete closed-loop implementations as reusable execution experience. For each source task, a coding agent generates policy code from a few successful demonstrations and iteratively improves it using simulation feedback. The validation-selected implementations are retained in a software archive. For new tasks, the agent generates and improves policies using archived implementations, target demonstrations, and execution feedback. The resulting policy is then frozen and executes without further model calls. Across four source tasks in RoboCasa, iterative optimization increases mean success from 28.3% to 64.2%. Across nine target tasks and three independent runs, mean success is 45.2% without references, 41.5% with initial source code, and 57.0% with optimized source code. Optimized references outperform initial references in all three runs on the nine-task average, with a mean gain of 15.6 percentage points. These results demonstrate the value of execution-improved software as a resource for acquiring new policies in this setting, although initial references remain better on two target tasks when averaged across runs.

</details>


### [82] [Co-VLA: Consensus-based Federated Training for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19923)
*Haolong Li,Guner Dilsad Er,Michael Muehlebach,Joerg Stueckler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Co-VLA，将ADMM共识优化引入联邦VLA训练，支持全模型训练与参数高效微调，性能可比拟集中式训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型随规模扩大而性能提升，但机器人数据天然分布在异构的机器人、任务与地点中，集中式收集成本高或不现实；联邦学习虽可去中心化训练，但需应对客户端数据分布异构问题。

Method: 采用交替方向乘子法（ADMM）进行共识优化，实现联邦式VLA训练；同一算法同时支持全模型训练与参数高效微调，并兼容固定秩与秩自适应适配器；各客户端在不共享本地数据的前提下协同训练共享模型。

Result: 实验表明，在全模型训练与参数高效微调两种设定下，Co-VLA均取得与集中式训练相当的性能。

Conclusion: ADMM共识优化可作为联邦VLA训练的通用框架，兼顾异构客户端协作、数据隐私与集中式级别的训练效果，为去中心化机器人数据下的规模化VLA学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose robot learning, with performance improving as models and datasets scale. Scaling robot data collection, however, remains challenging because data are naturally distributed across robots, tasks, and locations, making centralization costly or impractical. Federated learning offers a way to train on decentralized robot data, but applying it to VLAs requires accounting for heterogeneous robot client data distributions. We present Co-VLA, which applies consensus optimization using the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers~(ADMM) to federated VLA training. We show that the same algorithm supports both full-model training and parameter-efficient fine-tuning with both fixed-rank and rank-adaptive adapters. The name Co-VLA reflects both consensus and collaboration: clients with different local robot datasets collaboratively train a shared model without sharing their data. Our experiments demonstrate that Co-VLA achieves performance comparable to centralized training in both full-model training and parameter-efficient fine-tuning settings.

</details>


### [83] [Execution-Aware Pre-Execution Ranking for Grasp-Conditioned Robotic Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19946)
*Tianyuan Liu,Rutherford Agbeshi Patamia,Benjamin Champion,Richard Dazeley,Akansel Cosgun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将抓取-放置候选的放置问题建模为执行前的排序问题，用点云与位姿描述符预测规划及执行成功率，在自建数据集和跨平台迁移上均显著优于cuMotion基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何上可行的放置位置在实际执行中仍可能失败，因为所选抓取会改变末端执行器位姿、碰撞几何与运输运动，因此需要在规划前对候选进行可行性评估与排序。

Method: 将放置建模为执行前排序问题，对给定的抓取-放置候选打分。模型输入为带类型的目标条件点云与三种位姿描述符，采用分层预测头，先预测规划成功、再以规划成功为条件预测执行成功；并支持联合选择与固定目标两种排序设置。

Result: 在30物体、1235场景、按场景组留出划分的数据集上，三随机种子在覆盖测试组上的top-1成功率为：联合选择85.63±1.08%，固定目标排序79.84±0.16%。冻结的seed-42检查点上，联合排序top-1从72.84%提升至85.78%（对比全池cuMotion），固定目标排序从59.65%提升至79.67%。冻结迁移到xArm7/MoveIt无需xArm专用重训练。27个锁定案例中13个端到端完成（48.15%）；通过Top-5预检并开始执行的16个案例中13个成功（81.25%）；候选级部署可行性预测召回81.25%、特异度85.20%、平衡准确率83.23%。

Conclusion: 通过学习式执行前排序，可有效预测抓取-放置候选的规划与执行可行性，显著提升放置成功率，并具备跨机械臂平台的零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: A geometrically valid placement can still be difficult to execute because the selected grasp changes the required end-effector pose, collision geometry, and transport motion. Placement is formulated as a pre-execution ranking problem in which supplied grasp-placement candidates are scored before planning. The model combines a typed target-conditioned point cloud with three pose descriptors and hierarchical heads for planning success and execution success conditioned on planning. On a 30-object, 1,235-scene dataset with scene-group-held-out splits, three-seed top-1 success on covered test groups reaches 85.63 +/- 1.08% for joint selection and 79.84 +/- 0.16% for fixed-target ranking. For the designated frozen seed-42 checkpoint, top-1 success improves from 72.84% to 85.78% over full-pool cuMotion for joint ranking and from 59.65% to 79.67% for fixed-target ranking. Frozen transfer to xArm7/MoveIt requires no xArm-specific retraining. Across 27 locked cases, 13 complete end to end (48.15%). Of the 16 cases that pass Top-5 preflight and begin execution, 13 succeed (81.25%). Candidate-level deployment-feasibility prediction reaches 81.25% recall, 85.20% specificity, and 83.23% balanced accuracy.

</details>


### [84] [LapaTrack-3D: 6 DoF pre-operative shape tracking for laparoscopic surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19954)
*Jingwei Song,Javid Hussain Jakir,Ray Zhang,Wenwei Zhang,Hao Zhou,Xiaomeng Xian,Maani Ghaffari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LapaTrack-3D，一种面向单目腹腔镜手术的实时6自由度跟踪算法，通过改进ORB-SLAM2实现术中视频与术前CT数据的对齐，处理速率达13 Hz（1280×720）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腹腔镜手术缺乏触觉反馈且视野透明度不足，难以准确定位目标器官内部解剖结构。需要将术中视频与术前数据（如CT）对齐，以实现实时6自由度跟踪。

Method: 基于ORB-SLAM2框架进行四项改进：(1)利用原始3D形状快速初始化单目模式；(2)采用伪分割策略分离目标器官与背景；(3)在位姿图优化中引入3D形状作为几何先验；(4)改进多尺度Retinex彩色保持（MSRCP）算法用于恶劣光照下的图像增强。

Result: 在体与离体实验验证LapaTrack-3D提供稳健的3D跟踪，有效应对光照差、快速运动、视野外、部分可见及“器官-背景”相对运动等典型挑战，1280×720像素视频处理速率达13 Hz。

Conclusion: LapaTrack-3D是适用于单目腹腔镜手术的实时6自由度跟踪方案，结合术前3D先验与图像增强有效提升跟踪鲁棒性，满足实时性要求。

Abstract: This work proposes a real-time 6 Degree-of-Freedom (6 DoF) tracking algorithm for monocular laparoscopic surgery. It provides alignment between intra-operative video and pre-operative data (e.g., CT). The 6 DoF tracking offers a solution for accurately locating the internal anatomy of the target organ despite the lack of tactile feedback and transparency. The ORB-SLAM2 framework is adopted and modified for prior-based 3D tracking with four major modifications. First, the primitive 3D shape is used for fast initialization of the ORB-SLAM2 monocular mode. Second, a pseudo-segmentation strategy is employed to separate the target organ from the background for tracking. Third, the 3D shape is incorporated as a geometric prior in its pose graph optimization. Fourth, the Multi-Scale Retinex with Chromaticity Preservation (MSRCP) algorithm is leveraged and modified for image enhancement in challenging illumination scenarios. In-vivo and ex-vivo experiments validate that LapaTrack-3D provides robust 3D tracking and effectively handles typical challenges such as poor illumination, fast motion, out-of-field-of-view scenarios, partial visibility, and ``organ-background'' relative motion. LapaTrack-3D achieves a processing rate of 13 Hz for 1280*720 pixel video.

</details>


### [85] [Hybrid Residual Reinforcement Learning for Contact-Rich Robotic Book Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19962)
*Tianyuan Liu,Rutherford Agbeshi Patamia,Benjamin Champion,Akansel Cosgun,Richard Dazeley*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究抓取后书本插入紧凑书架的接触丰富控制问题，提出保留标称任务空间控制器负责结构化插入与就位、用残差PPO提供有界局部修正并决定释放时机的混合策略，仅对开-退-合的过渡阶段进行脚本化。仿真中512个固定条件下成功率达98.50%，远超标称控制的37.89%；实体xArm7上成功率从26.7%提升至63.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将已抓取的书本放入紧凑书架是一个紧凑但困难的接触丰富控制问题：毫米级位姿误差即可把几何上可行的接近变为卡阻、释放失败或未完全就位。论文聚焦抓取获取与全局接近之后的最终阶段，探讨控制权限应如何在已知几何与学习行为之间分配。

Method: 方法采用混合控制架构：保留标称任务空间控制器用于结构化插入与就位；残差PPO提供有界局部修正并决定释放时机；仅对短暂的开-退-合（open-retreat-reclose）过渡进行脚本化。最终硬件部署策略通过在匹配部署条件的仿真中进行评估，覆盖512个固定条件、三次独立训练运行。

Result: 仿真中三次独立训练运行在512个固定条件下平均成功率为98.50%（样本标准差0.23个百分点），标称控制为37.89%。实体xArm7上30个匹配条件共60次试验显示同样的定性优势：残差控制将成功率从26.7%提升至63.3%，失败次数从22降至11，并在两种控制器表现不同的15个匹配条件中赢下13个。鲁棒性测试表明，在最高1.5倍的初始化扰动下性能仍高于87%，而极小间隙暴露了局部修正的几何极限。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种混合设计：几何负责维持可靠的任务结构，而将学习集中于固定规则难以处理的接触敏感行为。

Abstract: Placing a grasped book into a tight shelf is a compact but difficult contact-rich control problem: millimetre-scale pose error can turn a geometrically valid approach into jamming, failed release, or incomplete seating. We study this final phase after grasp acquisition and global approach, and ask how control authority should be divided between known geometry and learned behaviour. Our method retains a nominal task-space controller for structured insertion and seating, while residual PPO supplies bounded local corrections and decides when to release. Only the brief open-retreat-reclose transition is scripted. For the final policy used on hardware, a deployment-matched simulation evaluation over 512 fixed conditions yields 98.50 percent mean success (0.23 percentage-point sample SD) across three independent training runs, compared with 37.89 percent for nominal control. On the physical xArm7, 60 trials over 30 matched conditions show the same qualitative advantage: residual control raises success from 26.7 percent to 63.3 percent, reduces failures from 22 to 11, and wins 13 of the 15 matched conditions in which the two controllers differ. Robustness tests show that performance remains above 87 percent under initialization perturbations up to 1.5x, while very tight clearances expose the geometric limit of local correction. These results support a hybrid design in which geometry preserves reliable task structure and learning is concentrated on the contact-sensitive behaviour that fixed rules handle poorly.

</details>


### [86] [MaskHarness-WAM: Instance-Grounded Harnessing for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19974)
*Zitai Huang,Taiyi Su,Jian Zhu,Jianjun Zhang,Chong Ma,Tianbin Liu,Weiyi Lu,Yi Xu,Hanli Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MaskHarness-WAM，通过目标掩码连接高层规划与底层策略，实现长时序多物体顺序操作，实验证明其显著优于有限时序策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序机器人操作需在多个外观相同的物体间进行连续目标跟踪，并按指定顺序操作；仅依赖有限时序的操作策略难以判断应操作哪个实例以及何时进入下一阶段。

Method: 提出MaskHarness-WAM——一种实例化（instance-grounded）的长时序操作框架。用目标掩码连接高层任务规划与底层操作策略，利用视觉反馈进行子任务调度与连续执行；每个子任务对应不同目标实例，故在子任务切换时需在新场景下重新建立初始目标掩码。系统在子任务边界持续重观测环境、生成并验证目标掩码，更新提供给底层策略的实例级空间条件；并依据子任务完成状态的验证结果切换目标实例以推进任务。

Result: 在真实机器人平台上的实验表明，MaskHarness-WAM在顺序多物体操作任务上大幅优于有限时序策略。

Conclusion: MaskHarness-WAM能有效将局部操作技能扩展为可靠的长时序执行，验证了实例级目标掩码与视觉反馈调度在长时序操作中的有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation requires not only stable local visuomotor control, but also continuous target tracking and reliable task progress assessment throughout execution. This challenge becomes particularly critical when multiple objects share identical appearances and must be manipulated in a prescribed order. In such scenarios, relying solely on a limited-horizon manipulation policy is often insufficient to determine which instance should be operated on and when the task should transition to the next stage. To address this challenge, we propose MaskHarness-WAM, an instance-grounded harness for long-horizon manipulation. The proposed system connects high-level task planning with low-level manipulation policies through target masks, while leveraging visual feedback for subtask scheduling and continuous execution. Since each subtask corresponds to a different target instance, the low-level policy requires a newly established initial target mask under the updated scene at each subtask transition. The harness continuously re-observes the environment, generates, and verifies the target mask at subtask boundaries, thereby updating the instance-level spatial condition provided to the low-level policy. Furthermore, the system advances the manipulation process by switching target instances according to the verified completion status of each subtask. Experiments on a real robot platform demonstrate that MaskHarness-WAM substantially outperforms limited-horizon policies on sequential multi-object manipulation, showing its effectiveness in extending local manipulation skills to reliable long-horizon execution.

</details>


### [87] [Compliance for Free: Learning Identifiable Impedance via Bilateral Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19976)
*Harsha Guda,Adrià Colomé,Carme Torras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出利用四通道双边遥操作消除操作者预期平衡位置与刚度之间的不可辨识性，以零标注成本生成逐步、方向相关的柔顺标签，微调VLA使其在输出位姿的同时输出刚度；在Franka Research 3擦拭任务上，该方法是指令要求"用力擦"时接触力唯一发生显著变化的策略（6.4N→9.1N RMS，Cohen's d=0.89，p=0.023）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作模型只告诉机器人往哪移动，却不告诉它用多大力推。接触密集任务依赖"柔顺性"这一量，但现有示范接口无法记录它。根本障碍在于可辨识性：实际位姿与测量力无法区分操作者的预期平衡位置与刚度，因此VR控制器、SpaceMouse和手持夹爪在原理上就无法提供柔顺监督。已有输出柔顺策略的方法需依赖人工指定任务结构、特权仿真接触状态或专用力/触觉硬件。

Method: 采用四通道双边遥操作，将leader臂作为预期平衡位置的独立测量，仅利用机械臂已有的关节力矩传感，通过回归实现逐轴刚度的可辨识性，从而以零标注成本获得逐时间步、方向相关的柔顺标签；用这些标签微调VLA，使其在输出位姿的同时输出刚度。

Result: 在Franka Research 3擦拭任务上，对比五种策略，本文方法是唯一在指令要求"用力擦"而非普通擦拭时接触力发生变化的策略：RMS力从6.4N（普通）升至9.1N（用力），Cohen's d = 0.89，p = 0.023。

Conclusion: 四通道双边遥操作可从根本上解决柔顺监督的不可辨识性难题，无需专用力/触觉硬件或特权信息即可生成高质量柔顺标签，使VLA具备指令驱动的力调制能力，为接触密集任务的柔顺策略学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models tell a robot where to move, but not how hard to push. Contact-rich tasks depend on that second quantity, compliance, yet no widely used demonstration interface records it. The obstacle is identifiability as realized pose and measured force cannot separate the operator's intended equilibrium from their stiffness, so VR controllers, SpaceMouse and handheld grippers cannot supply compliance supervision even in principle. Prior compliance-output policies work around this with hand-specified task structure, privileged simulation contact state, or dedicated force and tactile hardware. Four-channel bilateral teleoperation removes the ambiguity directly by using the leader arm as a separate measurement of the intended equilibrium, making per-axis stiffness identifiable by regression using only the joint-torque sensing already on the manipulator. This yields per-timestep, direction-dependent compliance labels at zero annotation cost, which we use to fine-tune a VLA to emit stiffness alongside pose. On a Franka Research 3 wiping task, ours is the only policy of five whose contact force changes when the instruction asks for a firm wipe rather than a normal one (6.4N (normal) to 9.1N (firm) RMS, Cohen's d = 0.89, p = 0.023

</details>


### [88] [DR-MPC: Fast and Feasible Dynamics-Relaxed Model-Predictive Control for Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20035)
*Run Wang,Alapati Tuerxun,Shuo Liu,Wei Xiao,Ján Drgoňa,Yilin Mo,Liang Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DR-MPC（dynamics-relaxed MPC）腿足机器人运动控制框架及配套内点法求解器，通过将动力学等式与仿射输入约束转为二次惩罚、仅保留箱约束，构建块箭头Hessian的箱约束QP，并用Schur补消除状态与仿射输出方向，实现高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿足机器人运动控制中，传统MPC需在线求解带动力学等式约束与接触相关仿射约束的优化问题，求解代价高、难以满足机载实时性要求，亟需兼顾可行性与计算效率的MPC形式及专用求解器。

Method: 提出DR-MPC：将动力学等式约束与仿射输入约束移入二次惩罚项，仅保留非空箱约束，并结合接触感知输入参数化，使可行性与接触约束在构造层面得到保证。所得箱约束QP具有块箭头Hessian结构，通过Schur补消去状态与仿射输出方向；求解器仅对经摆动腿力消除与接触对齐的移动分块（move blocking）后的降维控制系统进行分解。配合定制内点法（IPM）求解。

Result: 在相同DR-MPC公式的对比实现中，本方法端到端MPC中位加速比达HPIPM的16.0倍、OSQP的4.4倍，仿真中运动性能相当。机载MPC端到端中位时间为4.4 ms，并在Unitree Go1四足机器人上完成验证。

Conclusion: DR-MPC通过约束松弛与结构化QP设计，在保持可行性与运动性能的同时显著提升腿足MPC求解效率，具备机载实时部署能力，代码将于发表后开源。

Abstract: This paper presents dynamics-relaxed model predictive control (DR-MPC), a novel MPC formulation for legged locomotion, and a tailored interior-point method (IPM) solver. The formulation combines online optimization feasibility by construction with a contact-aware input parameterization. DR-MPC moves the dynamics equality and affine input constraints into quadratic penalties and retains only nonempty box constraints. The resulting box-constrained quadratic program (QP) has a block-arrow Hessian that enables the state and affine-output directions to be eliminated through a Schur complement. The solver factors only the reduced control system after swing-force elimination and contact-aligned move blocking. For the evaluated implementations using the same DR-MPC formulation, our method achieves median end-to-end MPC speedups of $16.0\times$ over HPIPM and $4.4\times$ over OSQP, with comparable locomotion performance in simulation. DR-MPC achieves a median onboard MPC end-to-end time of $4.4$ ms and is validated on a Unitree Go1 quadruped. Open-source code will be made available after publication.

</details>


### [89] [Mechanical Precision Weeding with a Quadruped Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20048)
*Ruben Beumer,Tom Janssen,René van de Molengraft,Duarte Antunes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于四足机器人（Boston Dynamics Spot）的机械除草新方案，通过在足端固定不动时驱动腿部实现工具运动，降低土壤压实、简化自动化并提升可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学除草因杂草抗性上升和环境影响而日益不可持续；机械除草虽避免了化学弊端，但通常依赖大型机械，易造成土壤压实。

Method: 采用Boston Dynamics Spot四足机器人，搭载定制除草工具（末端带铣削钻头）；工具与机器人刚性连接，通过驱动腿部、保持足端静止来利用机器人基座的自由度。开发了支持自主除草的软件架构，并整合所需各组件。

Result: 在室内和室外环境中对当前概念验证的精度与效率进行了分析。

Conclusion: 该方案相较现有自动化机械除草方法具有减少土壤压实、自动化更简单、可扩展性更好的优势，并为提升系统精度与效率提出了未来工作建议。

Abstract: Herbicide-based weed control is increasingly unsustainable due to rising weed resistance and the adverse environmental impacts of chemical use. While mechanical weed control avoids these drawbacks, it is typically implemented using large machines that cause soil compaction. We propose a novel alternative based on small mobile robots for mechanical weeding. Compared with existing automated mechanical weeding approaches, the proposed method offers reduced soil compaction, simpler automation, and improved scalability. Our solution involves a Boston Dynamics Spot quadruped robot equipped with a custom weed removal tool featuring a milling bit at its end. The tool is rigidly attached to the robot and uses the degrees of freedom of the robot base by actuating the legs, while keeping the feet stationary. We develop a software architecture that enables autonomous weed removal and integrate this system with all other required components. We analyze the accuracy and efficiency of the current proof of concept both in an indoor and outdoor environment and provide recommendations for future work to make the system more accurate and efficient.

</details>


### [90] [FlipToSee: A Probabilistic Stable Placement Prior for Active Visual Exploration via Regrasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20078)
*Chang Shu,Sushil Samuel Dinesh,Shinkyu Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FlipToSee框架，通过将放置姿态参数化为S²上的单位支撑法向量并以von Mises–Fisher混合密度网络建模多模态分布，结合确定性候选提取与鲁棒性重排序，实现单视图点云下未知物体的稳定放置预测，支撑主动视觉探索中的重抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动视觉探索常需将未知静置物体翻转至另一个稳定支撑面以暴露被遮挡表面，而穷举式物理搜索代价高。稳定放置预测本身具有多模态性，传统6-DoF回归还因同时建模平移与平面内偏航而引入额外歧义，因此需要一种消除表示歧义、能刻画多模态分布的预测方法。

Method: FlipToSee从单视图点云学习概率放置先验：将放置参数化为S²球面上的单位支撑法向量，避免6-DoF回归中平移与偏航带来的表示歧义；用von Mises–Fisher混合密度网络建模其多模态条件分布；为解耦模态多样性与物理鲁棒性，从混合分量中确定性地提取紧凑候选集，并用候选对齐监督训练的辅助头进行鲁棒性感知重排序。

Result: 仿真中，首个候选放置成功率在同分布物体上达98.4%，分布外形状上达95.3%，零样本迁移到家用YCB物体上达90.0%；并在真实机器人上将该放置先验与抓取及运动规划集成，用于探索性重抓取。

Conclusion: 将放置表示为S²上的单位支撑法向量并用vMF混合密度网络建模，可有效消除6-DoF回归的表示歧义并捕捉多模态性；确定性候选提取与鲁棒性重排序的结合在分布内外及零样本迁移场景下均取得高成功率，且能落地于真实机器人探索性重抓取任务。

Abstract: Active visual exploration of tabletop objects often requires reorienting an unknown resting object onto a different stable support face to expose occluded surfaces. To identify such placements without exhaustive physical search, we learn a probabilistic placement prior from a single-view point cloud. Stable placement prediction is inherently multimodal, and conventional 6-DoF regression introduces further ambiguity by modeling translation and in-plane yaw. We therefore propose FlipToSee, a probabilistic framework that removes this representational ambiguity by parameterizing placements as unit support normals on $S^2$ while modeling their multimodal conditional distribution via a von Mises--Fisher mixture density network. To decouple mode diversity from physical robustness, FlipToSee deterministically extracts a compact candidate set from the mixture components and applies robustness-aware reranking using an auxiliary head trained with candidate-aligned supervision. In simulation, FlipToSee achieves $98.4\%$ first-proposal success on in-distribution objects, $95.3\%$ on out-of-distribution shapes, and $90.0\%$ under zero-shot transfer to household YCB objects. We further demonstrate the learned placement prior on a physical robot by integrating it with grasp and motion planning for exploratory regrasping.

</details>


### [91] [Graph-Based Design of Soft Grippers with Multi-Objective Quality-Diversity Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20087)
*Andre Farinha,Ge Shi,Harry Bowman,Brendan Tidd,David Howard,Josh Pinskier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于图的设计空间表示与多目标、多样性驱动的遗传优化框架，用于设计软体抓取器，并研究任务多样性对泛化能力的影响。结果表明，在足够多样的抓取案例上优化可产生具有涌现泛化能力的设计，比任务专用方案在新场景下更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨多种物体的有效操作对农业收获、实验室及家庭自动化等应用至关重要。软体机器人固有柔顺性适合此挑战，但设计能跨任务泛化的抓取器仍很困难：一是连续介质力学设计空间巨大，二是存在对特定场景过拟合的风险。

Method: 提出基于图（graph-based）的设计空间来表示软体结构与机构；结合多目标、多样性驱动的遗传优化框架，在设计过程中显式促进解的多样性。优化时使用多个抓取场景，研究任务多样性如何影响对未见物体与接触条件的泛化能力涌现。

Result: 在足够多样的抓取案例集合上进行优化，可得到具有涌现泛化能力的设计；相比任务专用解，这些设计在新场景下表现出更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 多样性驱动优化为通用软体抓取器提供了一条有原则的设计路径，与软体机器人可适应的本质相契合。

Abstract: Effective manipulation across diverse objects is critical for applications ranging from agricultural harvesting to laboratory and domestic automation. While the inherent compliance of soft robotics is well suited to this challenge, designing grippers that generalize across tasks remains difficult due to the vast design space of continuum mechanics and the risk of overfitting to specific scenarios.
  We propose a graph-based design space for representing soft structures and mechanisms, coupled with a multi-objective, diversity-driven genetic optimization framework that explicitly promotes solution variety throughout the design process. Using multiple grasping scenarios during optimization, we study how task diversity influences the emergence of generalization to unseen objects and contact conditions.
  Our results show that optimization over a sufficiently diverse set of grasping cases leads to designs with emergent generalization, exhibiting improved robustness compared to task-specific solutions on novel scenarios. These findings suggest that diversity-driven optimization offers a principled pathway toward general-purpose soft grippers, aligned with the adaptable nature of soft robotics.

</details>


### [92] [Safety-Critical Scenanrio Emerges from Initial Scene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20103)
*Yin Wu,Jiarong Wei,Carl Esselborn,Shubham Phoolari,Ahmed Abouelazm,Daniel Slieter,J. Marius Zöllner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 AdvScene，将安全关键驾驶场景生成视为初始化问题：在给定黑盒驾驶策略下，学习生成更易演化出关键交互的真实初始场景。方法为两阶段条件潜扩散模型，先在自然驾驶数据上预训练，再用闭环仿真反馈通过强化学习后训练对抗智能体生成分支。在 Waymo 数据集、12 组自我车与交通策略组合上，方法显著提升了自我车责任碰撞率与 TTC<3s 事件率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全关键场景生成多从驾驶数据的初始场景出发去操纵周围智能体行为，这限制了可发现的失效空间：驾驶数据本身缺乏有意义的交互机会。例如 Waymo Open Motion Dataset 中 20.44% 的片段自我车始终静止，30.39% 的初始帧 10 米内无邻近交通参与者。因此需要把问题重新定义为初始场景的生成。

Method: AdvScene 是一个条件潜扩散模型，采用两阶段训练：第一阶段在自然驾驶数据上预训练；第二阶段针对对抗智能体生成分支，使用闭环仿真器 rollout 的反馈、以强化学习进行后训练。模型以自我车行驶位移为条件，避免自我车保持静止；RL 微调则直接利用不可微的安全关键指标来诱导关键性。

Result: 在 Waymo 数据集上覆盖 12 组自我车与交通策略组合的实验表明，AdvScene 显著提高了自我车责任碰撞事件的发生率以及 TTC<3s 事件的发生率。

Conclusion: 将安全关键场景生成作为初始化问题、并结合条件潜扩散与基于闭环仿真反馈的强化学习微调，可有效生成更易演化为关键交互的真实初始场景，从而拓展对黑盒驾驶策略失效模式的发现能力。

Abstract: Safety-critical driving scenario generation has largely focused on manipulating the behavior of surrounding agents while starting from an initial scene from driving data. This assumption can limit the space of discoverable failures, since driving data can provide little opportunity for meaningful interaction. For example, in the Waymo Open Motion Dataset, 20.44% of recorded slices feature a stationary ego vehicle that never moves, and 30.39% of initial frames contain no nearby traffic participants within 10 meters. We instead study safety-critical scenario generation as an initialization problem: given agnostic black-box driving policies, we learn to generate realistic initial scenes that are more likely to evolve into critical interactions. We propose AdvScene, a conditional latent diffusion model that is trained in two stages. Starting from pretraining on naturalistic driving data, we post-train the adversarial-agent generation branch using reinforcement learning with feedback from closed-loop simulator rollouts. Conditioning on ego driving displacement prevents the ego from remaining static, and RL finetuning induces criticality directly with non-differentiable safety-critical metrics. Experiments on the Waymo dataset across 12 combinations of ego and traffic policies show that our AdvScene substantially increases the rate of ego-fault collision events and TTC<3s events.

</details>


### [93] [AnyViewDex: View-Invariant Dexterous Manipulation from RGB Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20107)
*Soham Patil,Om Sanjay Gunjal,Sourabh Bhosale,Arhan Chavare,Ramandeep Singh Hora,Spandan Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnyViewDex通过非对称训练将几何知识编码进视觉表示，仅用未标定单目RGB实现视角不变的灵巧操作，无需测试时3D传感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧操作的视觉运动策略对相机视角偏移高度敏感；现有实现视角不变性的方法依赖RGB-D或点云等显式3D模态，带来硬件依赖、标定要求和传感器噪声脆弱性等部署问题。

Method: 提出非对称训练管线：结合多视角对比对齐与特权3D几何监督。在仿真训练中回归物体的绝对3D坐标，以此辅助目标提供几何接地信号，缓解全局池化对比嵌入的空间坍缩。部署时策略零样本运行，仅使用未标定单目RGB与本体感知。方法在强化学习和学生-教师蒸馏两种范式中验证。

Result: 在xArm7配16自由度LEAP Hand的硬件评测中，AnyViewDex在8个未见物体、6个未标定视角下取得76.7%抓取成功率（480次试验；含全部消融条件共2,400次），表明几何接地的单目策略可零样本迁移，无需测试时深度。

Conclusion: 无需显式测试时3D传感，通过在仿真中把几何知识编码进视觉表示即可实现视角不变控制；几何接地的单目策略能够零样本跨视角、跨物体迁移到真实硬件。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies for multi-fingered dexterous manipulation are highly sensitive to camera viewpoint shifts. To achieve view invariance, recent methods increasingly rely on explicit 3D modalities like RGB-D or point clouds, which can introduce hardware dependencies, calibration requirements, and vulnerability to sensor noise during real-world deployment. In this work, we show that view-invariant control can be achieved without explicit test-time 3D sensing by encoding geometric knowledge into the visual representation during simulation. We present AnyViewDex, an asymmetric training pipeline that combines multi-view contrastive alignment with privileged 3D geometric supervision. By regressing absolute 3D object coordinates during simulated training, this auxiliary objective provides a geometric grounding signal that mitigates the spatial collapse of the globally pooled contrastive embedding. At deployment, the policy operates zero-shot using only uncalibrated monocular RGB and proprioception. We validate this approach across both reinforcement learning and student-teacher distillation. In hardware evaluation on an xArm7 with a 16-DoF LEAP Hand, AnyViewDex reaches 76.7% grasping success across eight unseen objects and six uncalibrated viewpoints (480 trials; 2,400 across all ablation conditions), indicating that geometrically grounded monocular policies transfer zero-shot without test-time depth. Project Page: https://anyviewdex.github.io/

</details>


### [94] [Universal Navigation Interface: Robot-Free Data for Wheeled Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20114)
*Sarvesh Prajapati,Ananya Trivedi,Lorena Maria Genua,Drake Moore,Bruce Maxwell,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出UNI，一种无需机器人本体的低成本导航数据采集范式：用四轮助行器与智能手机采集受物理约束的人类演示，采集37.2 km真实数据并恢复度量轨迹，用于监督目标条件导航模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界移动机器人导航数据通常依赖平台专属的遥操作，采集成本高、难以规模化。因此需要一种不依赖目标机器人本体的低成本数据采集方式。

Method: 使用四轮助行器（rollator）加智能手机的UNI范式，采集受物理约束的人类演示数据；由于助行器无法上楼梯、跨越未切坡的路缘或通过狭窄间隙，演示天然偏向轮式可行路线。基于UNI采集37.2 km真实导航数据并恢复度量轨迹，直接监督目标条件导航模型，并将视觉导航模型微调后闭环迁移至电动轮椅。

Result: 在留出的UNI演示上，轨迹预测误差降低17.4–24.8%；在其他导航数据集上的收益因数据集与指标而异；在路缘、楼梯与路缘切坡场景中实现了向电动轮椅的闭环迁移。

Conclusion: 低成本物理代理可作为不依赖目标机器人本体的实用导航监督数据来源。

Abstract: Collecting real-world navigation data for mobile robots typically requires platform-specific teleoperation, making large-scale collection expensive and difficult to scale. We introduce Universal Navigation Interface (UNI), a robot-free data collection paradigm that uses a four-wheeled rollator walker (rollator) and smartphone to collect physically constrained human demonstrations. Because the rollator cannot climb stairs, negotiate uncut curbs, or pass through narrow gaps, demonstrations are naturally biased toward wheeled-feasible routes. Using UNI, we collect 37.2 km of real-world navigation data and recover metric trajectories that directly supervise goal-conditioned navigation models. Fine-tuning visual-navigation models on UNI reduces trajectory prediction error by 17.4-24.8% on held-out UNI demonstrations. Evaluation on other navigation datasets shows benefits that vary by dataset and metric. We further demonstrate closed-loop transfer to a powered wheelchair in curb, staircase, and curb-cut scenarios. These results support low-cost physical proxies as a practical source of navigation supervision collected without the target robot.

</details>


### [95] [GPT-6-Astra in a Navigation Workflow: Behavioral Analysis in Zero-Shot Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20116)
*Guangzhao Dai,Qi Wu,Bin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文以通用大模型GPT-6-Astra在零样本VLN-CE系统中进行评测，采用观察—决策—执行流程与直接API调用，无需导航专用微调；在50个R2R-CE验证未见episode上取得52.0%成功率、48.9% SPL与70.8% nDTW，并指出"局部判断正确"难以转化为持续进展与恰当停止是核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究通用多模态大模型能否在不依赖导航专用微调、不封装专用智能体框架的条件下，仅靠直接模型API调用完成连续环境中的零样本视觉语言导航，并评估包含上下文管理与动作控制的完整系统表现。

Method: 采用统一的观察—决策—执行（observation–decision–execution）工作流：每次请求接收筛选后的观测、执行反馈与保留的进度记录，直接调用模型API。评测覆盖完整系统（含上下文管理与动作控制）。在Open-Nav使用的100个R2R-CE验证未见（val-unseen）episode中选取50个进行评测。指标为成功率、SPL与nDTW，并辅以对记录响应、复检行为与终止方式的定性/定量分析。

Result: 成功率52.0%，SPL 48.9%，nDTW 70.8%。三项发现：其一，记录响应借助观测与所提供的history，将地标与早前动作关联到指令；其二，复检中包含请求补充视角与修正不确定判断；其三，任务理解与自主完成之间存在差距——能识别未完成的穿越却仍在继续旋转。终止情形上，36.0%的episode以工作流接受的STOP成功结束，另有16.0%在步数上限时满足距离判据。

Conclusion: 通用大模型零样本VLN-CE在无专用微调与无封装智能体框架下具备一定可行性，但核心瓶颈在于将正确的局部判断转化为持续进展与恰当停止；终止质量的差异表明停止决策与进度维持是后续改进的关键方向。

Abstract: We study GPT-6-Astra in a zero-shot Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) system, where it interprets instructions, assesses its surroundings, and proposes actions. The system uses a common observation--decision--execution workflow with direct model API calls, without a packaged agent harness or navigation-specific fine-tuning. In this workflow, each request receives selected observations, execution feedback, and retained progress records. Evaluation covers the complete system, including context management and action control. We evaluate the system on 50 of the 100 R2R-CE val-unseen episodes used by Open-Nav. It achieves a success rate of 52.0\%, an SPL of 48.9\%, and an nDTW of 70.8\%. Our analysis highlights three findings. First, recorded responses link landmarks and earlier actions to instructions using observations and supplied history. Second, reviews include requests for additional views and revisions of uncertain judgments. Third, the results suggest a gap between task understanding and autonomous completion: an unfinished crossing is recognized while rotation continues. At termination, 36.0\% of episodes succeed with a workflow-accepted STOP, while another 16.0\% meet the distance criterion at the step limit. These results highlight a central challenge: translating correct local judgments into sustained progress and appropriate stopping.

</details>


### [96] [VLN on the Fly: An Onboard Vision-Language Navigation Stack for Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20191)
*Marco S. Tayar,Felipe Tommaselli,Gianluca Capezutto,Pedro Antonio Rabelo Saraiva,Pedro H. V. de Freitas,Lucas Kido,Guilherme Sonego,Ricardo V. Godoy,Marcelo Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 VLN on the Fly：一种模块化、可机载运行的视觉语言导航栈，将 grounding、规划与控制分离，在四旋翼上实现厘米级目标到达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在飞行机器人上全机载运行视觉语言导航很困难：grounding、规划与控制需共享有限算力，且端到端策略将各阶段融合为单一网络，导致单阶段错误难以隔离，丧失了模块化栈的可观测性与安全检查能力。

Method: 保持 grounding、规划、控制为独立的可检查阶段：量化 VLM 将指令 grounding 到粗粒度图像单元，深度信息将其提升为 3D 目标，快速 B 样条规划器生成可行轨迹，预训练强化学习策略将其跟踪为电机指令，并可跨多种四旋翼迁移。

Result: 在受控室内空间中，针对三种日常参照物完成 15 次机载飞行，13/15 次到达目标，平均目标误差 5.72 cm，平均 GPU 利用率 39.3%；另在 6 次杂乱环境试验中，栈在机载感知门控下跟踪无碰撞轨迹。

Conclusion: 模块化、可检查的机载栈能在有限算力下实现可靠的空中视觉语言导航，兼顾精度与安全性，优于放弃可观测性的端到端方案。

Abstract: Running vision-language navigation fully onboard an aerial robot is hard, since grounding, planning, and control must share limited compute and a single-stage error is difficult to isolate in flight. End-to-end aerial policies fuse these stages into one network, giving up the observability and safety checks a modular stack keeps available. We propose VLN on the Fly, an onboard stack that keeps grounding, planning, and control as separate, inspectable stages. A quantized VLM grounds an instruction to a coarse image cell, depth lifts it to a 3D goal, a fast B-spline planner returns a feasible trajectory, and a pretrained reinforcement learning policy tracks it to motor commands across quadrotors. Across 15 onboard flights over three everyday referents in a controlled indoor volume, the stack reaches the target in 13 of 15 trials with 5.72 cm mean goal error and 39.3% average GPU utilization. In 6 additional cluttered-environment trials, the stack tracks collision-free trajectories under onboard perception gating.

</details>


### [97] [Strategic Transformer for Resource-Constrained Multi-Object Navigation in Ultra-Large-Scale Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20227)
*Daiki Iwata,Kanji Tanaka,Senta Hishida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将大规模室内多目标导航重构为集合定向问题（SOP），提出轻量级Strategic Transformer作为优先级规划器，并结合有界2-opt局部优化，实现资源受限下的高效探索-利用平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超过2000平方米的大型室内环境中，资源受限的多目标导航在效率和策略规划方面面临巨大挑战。现有基线方法存在规划僵化或行为短视的问题，且SOP的NP-hard特性使实时导航计算困难。

Method: 将任务重构为集合定向问题（SOP）：利用由SOP模型支配、由独立启发式切换器管理探索。开发Strategic Transformer作为优先级规划器，将专家组合逻辑内化为41.03ms的前向推理，缓解师生信息不对称；引入几何注意力偏置以建模长程结构依赖。将Transformer的宏观规划与基于受限候选图的有界迭代2-opt局部精化相结合。

Result: 相比重型元启发式方法实现94倍加速，保证适合机载部署的有界延迟推理。在ProcTHOR上的实验表明，该方法成功弥合探索与利用之间的差距，在按路径长度加权的进度（PPL）指标上优于精心重实现的基线，为可扩展、资源受限的导航建立了新基准。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架在资源受限的大规模室内多目标导航中实现了高效且具策略性的规划，兼顾实时性与规划质量，树立了新的性能基准。

Abstract: Resource-constrained multi-object navigation in vast indoor environments ($>2,000\text{ m}^2$) poses significant challenges for efficiency and strategic planning. To tackle this, we reformulate the task as a Set Orienteering Problem (SOP), providing an optimization framework under resource constraints where exploitation is governed by the SOP model and exploration is managed by a separate heuristic switcher. Conventional baselines suffer from either rigid planning or myopic behaviors. To overcome these limitations and resolve the NP-hard computational challenges of SOP for real-time navigation, we develop the Strategic Transformer. This lightweight architecture functions as a priority planner that internalizes expert combinatorial logic into a predictable $41.03\text{ ms}$ forward pass while reducing teacher-student information asymmetry. Incorporating geometric attention biases allows the network to effectively model long-range structural dependencies. By coupling the Transformer's macro-plan with a bounded iterative 2-opt local refinement on a capped candidate graph, our framework achieves a $94\times$ speedup compared to heavy meta-heuristics, ensuring bounded-latency inference suitable for onboard deployment. Experiments on ProcTHOR validate that our method successfully bridges the gap between exploration and exploitation, outperforming carefully re-implemented baselines under Progress weighted by Path Length (PPL) and establishing a new benchmark for scalable, resource-constrained navigation.

</details>


### [98] [LLM-Guided Transformation of Non-Critical Driving Scenes into Safety-Critical Scenarios Using Augmented Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20318)
*Noura Fady,Farah Khaled,Catherine M. Elias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种自动化流水线，融合计算机视觉、大语言模型（LLM）与增强现实（AR），将真实安全驾驶场景转化为安全关键场景，用于自动驾驶系统（ADS）测试。在nuScenes数据集上，安全分类准确率达97.52%，并成功生成行人横穿、后方超车、急刹等真实场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试自动驾驶系统需要真实的安全关键场景，但从真实驾驶中采集此类数据成本高昂且存在安全风险，现有数据难以满足测试需求。

Method: 构建自动化流水线：通过计算机视觉检测并跟踪道路使用者，提取距离、速度、运动方向、碰撞时间（TTC）等安全特征并评估场景关键性；对安全场景由LLM生成逼真的致碰撞对象与行为，再借助AR将其集成到原始场景中。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上安全分类准确率达到97.52%，成功生成行人横穿、后方超车车辆、急停事件等真实安全关键场景。

Conclusion: 该方法为自动驾驶系统的测试与验证提供了一种高效、灵活的自动化安全关键场景生成方案。

Abstract: Testing Autonomous Driving Systems (ADS) requires realistic safety-critical scenarios, but collecting such data from real-world driving is costly and unsafe. This paper presents an automated pipeline that transforms safe driving scenes into safety-critical scenarios by combining computer vision, Large Language Models (LLMs), and Augmented Reality (AR). The system detects and tracks road users, extracts safety features including distance, velocity, motion direction, and Time-to-Collision (TTC), and assesses scene criticality. Safe scenes are modified by an LLM, which generates realistic collision-inducing objects and behaviors that are integrated into the original scene using AR. The proposed pipeline was evaluated on the nuScenes dataset, achieving 97.52% safety classification accuracy and successfully generating realistic scenarios such as pedestrian crossings, rear overtaking vehicles, and sudden-stop events. The results demonstrate an effective and flexible approach for automated generation of safety-critical scenarios to support the testing and validation of autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [99] [Implementation of Tightly-Coupled SLAM Fusion of GPS, IMU, and LiDAR for Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20321)
*Amr O. Elmehrath,Farah Khaled,Rana Nahas,Catherine M. Elias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文实现了一个紧耦合的多传感器融合SLAM架构，集成Velodyne 3D LiDAR、高频IMU与GPS，基于FAST-LIO2与IESKF构建3D前端，并用GTSAM位姿图优化后端处理多模态回环，克服单传感器盲区，生成高精度点云与HD地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶在未知环境中完全依赖SLAM进行安全导航，但单一传感器存在致命失效点：LiDAR在无特征走廊中退化，IMU累积数学漂移，GPS在城市峡谷中频繁失锁。因此需要多模态紧耦合融合以实现连续空间感知。

Method: 采用分阶段开发方法：先建立2D基线以验证硬件同步与变换几何，再升级为基于FAST-LIO2的完整3D架构。该架构使用迭代误差状态卡尔曼滤波（IESKF）将密集3D激光点与连续惯性数据紧耦合处理，消除高速下的运动模糊；后端采用GTSAM位姿图优化执行多模态回环检测，以消除长期漂移。

Result: 在仿真环境与实机部署中评估，结果表明紧耦合3D融合能有效克服各单一传感器的盲区，生成高细节点云，为下游高级自动驾驶规划器提供所需的高精（HD）地图基础。

Conclusion: 紧耦合的多模态（LiDAR-IMU-GPS）3D SLAM方案可弥补单传感器缺陷，实现连续、鲁棒的空间感知，并输出可用于自动驾驶下游任务的高精度HD地图。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles depend entirely on Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) to navigate safely in unknown environments. However, relying on a single sensory modality introduces critical failure points: LiDAR systems degrade in featureless corridors, Inertial Measurement Units (IMUs) accumulate mathematical drift, and GPS drops frequently in urban canyons. This paper presents the implementation of a tightly-coupled SLAM fusion architecture that integrates a Velodyne 3D LiDAR, a high-frequency IMU, and GPS to achieve continuous spatial awareness. Utilizing a phased development methodology, we establish a 2D baseline to validate hardware synchronization and transform geometries before upgrading to a full 3D architecture driven by FAST-LIO2. This advanced approach uses an Iterated Error-State Kalman Filter (IESKF) to process dense 3D laser points alongside continuous inertial data, eliminating motion blur at high speeds. To eradicate long-term drift, a GTSAM pose-graph optimization back-end executes multi-modal loop closures. Evaluated across simulated environments and physical deployments, the results demonstrate that tightly-coupled 3D fusion effectively overcomes individual sensor blind spots to generate highly detailed point clouds, providing the foundational High-Definition (HD) maps required for advanced downstream autonomous planners.

</details>


### [100] [RoboFind: Multi-Agent Personalized Object Search for People Who Are Blind or Have Low Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20330)
*Ruiping Liu,Shaofang Quan,Qian Yin,Jingqi Zhang,Junwei Zheng,Yufan Chen,Di Wen,Weijia Fan,Kailun Yang,M. Saquib Sarfraz,Tamim Asfour,Kunyu Peng,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboFind——一个面向盲人与低视力用户的多智能体个性化物体搜索框架：由智能手机完成"目标教学"，四足机器人执行"空间搜索"，通过目标教学、导航、验证与协调恢复四个智能体的协同，实现"先确认身份、再宣告完成"的可信赖搜索。32 次实机任务成功率为 85.0%，且将误报成功从 75.0% 降至 5.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲人与低视力用户常需寻找"属于自己的那一件特定物品"，而非同类别中的任意一个实例。该任务要求机器人能进入用户无法到达的视角，并配合可访问的交互界面让用户说明目标、获知是否找对。现有单步、首次停靠式的搜索范式缺乏对"物体身份"的事后核验，容易在找到同类但非目标的物体时误报成功，使结果对用户不可依赖。

Method: RoboFind 采用多智能体架构，由智能手机负责"教"目标、四足机器人负责"找"目标。目标教学智能体（Target Teaching Agent）通过具备 AR 引导、语音与触觉反馈、屏幕阅读器支持的可访问采集流程，将用户引导下的手机录制内容转化为语义目标画像与可复用的多视角参考库，使后续任务无需重复教学即可引用已存物体。运行时，导航智能体（Navigation Agent）探索环境并生成候选目标，验证智能体（Verification Agent）将每个候选与存储参考进行身份比对，协调与恢复智能体（Coordination and Recovery Agent）据此完成任务或触发恢复与继续搜索。

Result: 在 32 次真实机器人任务中，RoboFind 成功率达 85.0%，而重建的顺序式"首次停靠"基线在 10 个目标、20 次试验中仅为 25.0%；误报成功由 75.0% 降至 5.0%。在 6 个共享目标上，RoboFind 为 10/12 次成功，而 12 次独立执行的"仅 GPT-6 Astra"试验仅为 5/12。

Conclusion: 该多智能体设计契合个性化物体搜索的需求：在宣告任务完成之前先行验证物体身份，是让搜索结果对用户真正可靠的关键。语义画像加多视角参考库的"一次教学、多次复用"机制，也降低了长期使用中的用户负担。

Abstract: Blind and low-vision users often need to locate a specific personal object rather than an arbitrary instance of the same category. The task calls for a robot that can move through the space and reach viewpoints the user cannot, and for an accessible interface where the user says which object is meant and learns whether the right one was found. We present RoboFind, a multi-agent framework in which a smartphone teaches the target and a quadruped robot carries out the search. A Target Teaching Agent converts guided smartphone recordings into a semantic target profile and a reusable multi-view reference bank through an accessible capture flow with AR guidance, speech and haptic feedback, and screen-reader support, so later missions refer to a stored object without repeating the teaching process. At runtime, a Navigation Agent explores the environment and proposes candidate targets, a Verification Agent checks each candidate against the stored references, and a Coordination and Recovery Agent completes the mission or triggers recovery and continued search. Across 32 real-robot missions, RoboFind reaches 85.0% success against 25.0% for a reconstructed sequential first-stop baseline over 20 trials with ten targets, and reduces false success from 75.0% to 5.0%. On six shared targets it succeeds in 10/12 trials, against 5/12 for 12 independently executed GPT-6 Astra-only trials. These results show that the multi-agent design fits the demands of personalized object search, where verifying object identity before declaring completion is what makes the outcome something a user can rely on.

</details>


### [101] [Navi-Agent: Unlocalized Monocular Navigation Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20388)
*Wenyuan Xie,Mengyang Hong,Yongzhong Wang,Yanbiao Ji,Yijin Zhou,Shaokai Wu,Shalayiding Sirejiding,Huayi Zhou,Yi-Chao Chen,Ma Ling,Yue Ding,Hongtao Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Navi-Agent，一个零样本 VLN-CE 智能体，通过视觉观测与运动历史构建无坐标的空间状态（导航拓扑图），实现基于观测的自定位、进度验证与视觉回访恢复，在零样本基准和真实机器人上达到几何约束方法中的 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLN-CE 要求智能体在未知环境中执行长程指令。现有零样本系统多依赖几何定位或坐标表示维持空间状态；近期的几何约束导航虽去除深度和全局一致坐标，却难以持续维持空间感知，从而影响地点确认、进度验证与错误恢复。

Method: Navi-Agent 从视觉观测与已执行运动历史构建无坐标空间状态，组织为导航拓扑：节点表示视觉地点，边表示运动转移。该表示支持基于观测的近似自定位、任务进度验证，以及基于视觉回访的恢复。导航采用闭环方式：将指令分解为子目标、执行局部视觉导航、并通过空间状态验证已访问地点。

Result: 在零样本 VLN-CE 基准与真实机器人平台上，Navi-Agent 在几何约束方法中取得 SOTA 性能，并与依赖几何定位的方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 无坐标的拓扑空间状态可有效支撑长程零样本 VLN-CE 的空间感知与恢复能力，在无需深度与全局一致坐标的条件下实现强性能，并在真实平台上得到验证。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires an embodied agent to execute long-horizon instructions in unknown environments. Existing zero-shot VLN-CE systems typically maintain spatial states through geometric localization or coordinate-based representations. Recent geometry-constrained navigation removes depth and globally consistent coordinates, but maintaining persistent spatial awareness for place confirmation, progress verification, and recovery remains challenging. We present Navi-Agent, a zero-shot VLN-CE agent that constructs a coordinate-free spatial state from visual observations and executed motion histories. Navi-Agent organizes this state as a navigation topology, where nodes represent visual places and edges represent motion transitions. This representation enables observation-based approximate self-localization, task progress verification, and visual revisitation-based recovery. Navi-Agent performs closed-loop navigation by decomposing instructions into sub-goals, executing local visual navigation, and verifying visited places through the constructed spatial state. Experiments on zero-shot VLN-CE benchmark and real-world robot platforms show that Navi-Agent achieves state-of-the-art performance among geometry-constrained methods while remaining competitive with approaches relying on geometric localization.

</details>


### [102] [Imagine-TAMP: Imagination-Guided Task and Motion Planning in Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20396)
*Antareep Singha,Shivaram Kumar,Yoonwoo Kim,Yoonchang Sung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Imagine-TAMP：在部分可观测下用语义与几何"想象"评估任务级策略，将观察与操作的取舍纳入TAMP，先比较再运动规划，显著提升成功率并缩短规划时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在杂乱环境中常需操作位置仅部分可观测的物体。核心难点在于：应当先去获取新的观测，还是先移开可能遮挡目标的物体。传统任务与运动规划（TAMP）依赖符号动作代价或昂贵的几何规划来做此决策，二者都无法充分刻画"一次观测在多大程度上能揭示被遮挡目标"。

Method: Imagine-TAMP是一个交错式规划与执行框架，在投入昂贵运动规划之前，先用语义与几何想象比较部分可观测下的备选任务级策略。视觉语言模型利用目标与可见物体间的常识关系塑造目标位置的粒子信念；生成式场景模型估计未观测区域的合理几何。给定目标假设与想象场景后，生成多个符号规划骨架，并赋予非单位代价，该代价近似操作代价与感知动作带来的目标可见性，从而区分"短但信息量低的观测策略"与"先操作遮挡物以更好暴露目标的长策略"。选定骨架再细化为可行的连续规划并执行，新观测更新信念并在必要时触发重规划。

Result: 想象引导的评估改善了"观察还是操作"的决策：在视角受限的货架场景中，非单位几何评估将成功率从46.0%提升至84.0%；语义信念塑造进一步减少操作与重规划。在真实机器人上，完整系统相比仅用几何的消融版本减少规划时间32%。

Conclusion: 将语义与几何想象引入TAMP的代价评估，可在部分可观测下更准确地权衡观测与操作，既提升任务成功率，又降低操作、重规划与规划时间。

Abstract: Robots operating in cluttered environments must often manipulate objects whose locations are only partially observable. A central challenge is deciding whether to acquire another observation or to first manipulate objects that may occlude the target. Conventional task and motion planning (TAMP) approaches typically make this decision using symbolic action costs or expensive geometric planning, neither of which adequately captures how likely an observation is to reveal an occluded target. We introduce Imagine-TAMP, an interleaved planning and execution framework that uses semantic and geometric imagination to compare alternative task-level strategies under partial observability before committing to expensive motion planning. A vision-language model shapes a particle belief over target locations using commonsense relationships between the target and visible objects, while a generative scene model estimates plausible geometry in unobserved regions. Given a target hypothesis and imagined scene, Imagine-TAMP generates multiple symbolic plan skeletons and assigns non-unit costs that approximate both manipulation effort and target visibility from sensing actions, distinguishing a short but poorly informative observation strategy from a longer strategy that first manipulates an occluder to better expose the target. The selected skeleton is then refined into a feasible continuous plan and executed, with new observations updating the belief and triggering replanning when necessary. Experiments show that imagination-guided evaluation improves observation-versus-manipulation decisions: in viewpoint-constrained shelf scenes, non-unit geometric evaluation increases success from 46.0% to 84.0%, while semantic belief shaping further reduces manipulation and replanning. On a real robot, the complete system reduces planning time by 32% relative to a geometry-only ablation.

</details>


### [103] [Resilient Motion Planning for Free-Flying Space Robots under Actuator Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20407)
*Nicolas de Maddalena,Joris Verhagen,Jana Tumova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种概率性、主动式的运动规划框架，将执行器失效建模为马尔可夫链，结合预计算可达集与RRT*规划器，在自由飞行机器人上最大化目标到达概率，实现最大韧性的运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由飞行机器人依赖多个推进器进行空间机动，一旦一个或多个推进器失效，机器人可能失去控制能力并导致任务失败。同时，其自由飞行特性意味着即使没有驱动，机器人仍会沿（局部）直线轨迹继续运动。因此，需要一种在规划中显式考虑执行器失效的运动规划方法，以提升任务韧性。

Method: 该框架将执行器失效模式建模为马尔可夫链，并沿规划时域传播成功到达目标的概率；利用预计算的可达集评估机器人在潜在失效情况下到达各航点的能力；采用基于RRT*的规划器对这些航点进行拼接，从而在整体上最大化目标到达概率，并利用自由飞行特性生成最大韧性的运动规划。

Result: 在物理自由飞行平台上进行了实验验证，并注入了执行器失效情形，结果表明该方法能够有效生成具有韧性的运动规划。

Conclusion: 通过将执行器失效显式纳入概率规划框架，并利用自由飞行的被动运动特性，可在存在执行器失效风险时最大化目标到达概率，提升自由飞行机器人的任务可靠性，并已通过物理平台实验验证。

Abstract: Free-flying robots rely on multiple thrusters to maneuver in space. If one or more of these thrusters fail, the robot may lose control authority and risk mission failure. At the same time, their free-flying nature implies that, even in the absence of actuation, they continue along (locally) straight-line trajectories. In this work we present a probabilistic, proactive, motion planning framework that explicitly accounts for actuator failures in space. We model actuator failure modes as a Markov chain and propagate the probability of successfully reaching the goal along the planning horizon. Precomputed reachable sets evaluate the robot's capabilities of reaching waypoints under potential failures and an RRT$^*$-based planner concatenates these waypoints. The resulting algorithm maximizes the overall target-reaching probability, providing maximally resilient motion plans utilizing free-flying properties. We validate our approach experimentally on a physical free-flyer platform with injected actuator failures.

</details>


### [104] [Time-Efficient Iterative Learning Planning for Safety-Critical Dynamic Obstacle Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20435)
*Zhiyi Chen,Shuli Lv,Chen Min,Yong Xu,Jian Sun,Quan Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文扩展迭代学习规划（ILP），通过引入预见性风险融合控制屏障函数（ARB-CBF），实现动态环境下安全关键的自主导航，兼具时间效率与实时安全保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主移动机器人在受限的机载计算条件下，需要兼具时间高效的规划能力与安全关键的动态避障能力。ILP 虽能提供轻量高效的路径规划，但缺乏对动态障碍物的感知与避障的显式机制，因此需要对其进行扩展以应对动态环境中的安全需求。

Method: 将 ILP 与预见性风险融合控制屏障函数（ARB-CBF）相结合。扩展后的 ILP 基于局部障碍风险，通过分数幂更新规则学习遍历速度与转向偏置曲线，生成标称控制指令；ARB-CBF 在运行时对这些指令进行修正，以提供实时安全保障。算法分析表明，ILP 重规划阶段的复杂度为 O(kN)（k 为迭代次数，N 为路径点数量），ARB-CBF 则以线性复杂度执行。

Result: 通过全面的仿真与真实世界实验验证了该框架，结果表明相于基于优化的基线方法，该框架在时间效率与安全性上表现更优，且计算开销更低。

Conclusion: 该扩展框架适用于资源受限平台，能够在有限机载计算条件下兼顾实时安全保证与时间高效的动态避障导航。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile robots require timeefficient planning and safety-critical dynamic obstacle avoidance under constrained onboard computation. While Iterative Learning Planning (ILP) offers lightweight and efficient traversal planning, it lacks explicit mechanisms for dynamic obstacle perception and avoidance. This article extends ILP to safety-critical navigation in dynamic environments by integrating an anticipatory risk-blended control barrier function (ARB-CBF). The extended ILP learns traversal-speed and steering-bias profiles via a fractionalpower update based on local obstacle risk, generating nominal control commands that ARB-CBF modifies at runtime for real-time safety guarantees. Algorithmic analysis demonstrates that the ILP replanning stage scales at O(kN) for k iterations and N waypoints, while ARB-CBF executes with linear complexity. Comprehensive simulations and real-world experiments validate the framework, demonstrating superior temporal efficiency and safety with lower computational overhead compared to optimizationbased baselines, making it highly suitable for resourceconstrained platforms.

</details>


### [105] [Spatial-Semantic Uncertainty in VLM-Based Target Search: Balancing Exploration and Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20443)
*Alkesh K. Srivastava,Jonathan Diller,Vijay Kumar,Philip Dames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出空间-语义不确定性分离的建模框架，将VLM概率证据融入全局目标身份后验，并用信息论规划器通过空间/语义期望信息增益（EIG）分别评估候选发现与目标消歧，在退化观测的搜索-识别任务中显著优于随机搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人依据自然语言描述搜索目标时，需同时解决"去哪里搜"（空间不确定性）和"哪个候选是目标"（语义不确定性）两个问题。现有基于VLM的搜索系统常将这两类不确定性混为一谈，缺乏显式区分，导致无法有效权衡探索广度与识别速度。

Method: 提出空间-语义不确定性分解式建模：对空间与语义各自维护独立信念，将VLM给出的概率证据整合进全局目标身份后验（并为未发现目标保留概率质量）；据此设计信息论规划器，用空间EIG衡量候选发现的收益、语义EIG衡量目标消歧的收益，形成可调节的探索-识别权衡机制；同时对比6种VLM不确定性引出接口。

Result: 在500个合成目标上的评估显示，6种VLM接口识别准确率相近，但标定质量与虚假置信差异显著。退化观测搜索-识别实验中，基于EIG的规划器在75.0%-92.5%的试验中达成置信决策，随机搜索仅20.0%；不同空间-语义权重在达到置信后识别准确率相当，而提高语义权重可减少无谓探索与VLM查询次数。

Conclusion: 显式规划语义不确定性可在不牺牲决策质量的前提下加速目标确认，说明不确定性表示与不确定性驱动规划在具身VLM系统中扮演不同但互补的角色；仅看识别准确率会掩盖标定与置信度层面的关键差异。

Abstract: Robots searching for a target from a natural-language description must determine not only where to search, but also which observed candidate is the desired target. These decisions reflect two distinct sources of uncertainty - spatial uncertainty over candidate locations and semantic uncertainty over target identity - that are often conflated in VLM-based search systems. We introduce a spatial-semantic uncertainty formulation that maintains separate beliefs over each component and integrates probabilistic VLM evidence into a global target-identity posterior, including probability mass for undiscovered targets. This decomposition allows an information-theoretic planner to independently value candidate discovery and target disambiguation through spatial and semantic expected information gain (EIG), providing an explicit mechanism for trading broader exploration against earlier identification. We evaluate six VLM uncertainty-elicitation interfaces on 500 synthetic targets and show that similar recognition accuracy can conceal substantial differences in calibration and false confidence. In degraded-observation search-and-identify experiments, EIG-based planners reach confident decisions in 75.0%-92.5% of trials, compared with 20.0% for Random search, while different spatial-semantic weightings achieve comparable identification accuracy once confidence is attained. Increasing semantic emphasis reduces unnecessary exploration and VLM queries, demonstrating that explicitly planning over semantic uncertainty can accelerate target resolution without sacrificing decision quality. These results highlight the distinct roles of uncertainty representation and uncertainty-driven planning in embodied VLM systems.

</details>


### [106] [Visual Sim-to-Real Learning for Robotic Insertion under Geometric Variations: Application to Rebar Installation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20477)
*Tao Sun,Beining Han,Patrick Yin,Rui Xu,Harry He,Abhishek Gupta,Szymon Rusinkiewicz,Yi Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RebarSim：纯仿真训练的视觉 sim-to-real 系统，采用"特权状态教师 RL + 多视角学生蒸馏"，实现钢筋插入任务零样本真机迁移，91.3% 成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钢筋插入是建筑工地重复性高、体力消耗大的任务，且属于 1.4 mm 间隙的强接触问题。零件存在两层变化：结构件的标称设计与围绕标称设计的制造公差，导致设计或批次改变时需重新采集真实数据，成本高。

Method: 在程序化生成的钢筋几何上，用强化学习训练基于特权状态的教师策略；再通过大规模域随机化，将教师蒸馏为从多视角原始 RGB 与本体感知直接映射到动作的学生策略；训练全程在仿真中完成，无需真实数据。

Result: 学生策略零样本迁移到真实机器人，对真实工厂批次钢筋插入成功率达 91.3%。跨多样标称设计训练可提升教师与学生在未见设计上的零样本成功率，且学生优于单设计专家在其本设计上的表现；预训练学生适应新设计所需蒸馏样本比从头训练少 4–6 倍。消融显示外观随机化与 DAgger 混合缺一不可，否则成功率骤降。

Conclusion: 几何多样性与预训练是零样本迁移的关键支撑，视觉 sim-to-real 依赖外观随机化与 DAgger 混合策略，验证了全仿真训练在接触密集装配任务上的可行性。

Abstract: Rebar insertion is among the most repetitive and physically demanding tasks on construction sites, and a contact-rich problem at 1.4 mm clearance. The parts, however, vary at two levels: a nominal design per structural member, and fabrication tolerance around each nominal design. Real-world data therefore has to be re-collected as designs and batches change. We present RebarSim, a visual sim-to-real system trained entirely in simulation. A privileged state-based teacher is trained with reinforcement learning over procedurally generated rebar geometries, then distilled into a multi-view student that maps raw RGB and proprioception directly to actions under extensive domain randomization. The student transfers to the real world zero-shot, seating rebars taken from a real factory production run in 91.3% of real-robot rollouts. Underlying that result, geometry diversity and pretraining both bring benefits. Training across a diverse set of nominal designs rather than one lifts the zero-shot success of both the teacher and the student on unseen designs, and the student policy outperforms a single-design specialist on that specialist's own design. A pretrained student then adapts to a new design with 4--6x fewer distillation samples than one trained from scratch. Visual sim-to-real transfer depends on appearance randomization and the DAgger mixture: removing either one sharply lowers success. Videos, code, and task assets are available at https://rebarsim.github.io.

</details>


### [107] [Worst-Case Hidden-Vehicle Trajectory Search in Spatiotemporal Occlusion Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20480)
*Ruichen Tan,Zengxiang Lei,Satish Ukkusuri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 HC-MTS，将时间遮挡推理与响应感知搜索结合，通过双层极小极大搜索最坏历史一致交互，在 Waymo 数据集上验证了视野记忆扩展可显著减少隐藏种子与反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遮挡给自动驾驶带来根本不确定性；现有方法多传播逐帧假设或针对预设隐藏智能体预测优化自车行为，未探索最坏但历史一致的交互情形。

Method: HC-MTS 先构造有限隐藏状态模式，每个模式由满足多帧可见性、占据、语义地图支持与类别运动学约束的后向见证认证；随后求解双层极小极大问题：内层有限预言机在到达目标与乘坐舒适性上最大化自车驾驶分数，外层搜索选择使该最优响应值最小的合法隐藏车辆轨迹。

Result: 在 8 个 Waymo Open Motion Dataset 场景中，将可见性记忆视野从 K=1 增至 K=20，平均每场景车辆、行人及总保留隐藏种子数分别减少 18.12%、21.67%、18.45%；HC-MTS 识别出 6 个可避免反例，其余 2 个场景在有限搜索预算内未找到产生碰撞的合法攻击者。

Conclusion: 时间遮挡推理与响应感知的极小极大搜索能有效压缩历史一致的隐藏状态空间并暴露可避免的对抗交互，为遮挡下的安全规划提供更严格的评估框架。

Abstract: Occlusion creates fundamental uncertainty in autonomous driving. Existing methods often propagate frame-wise hypotheses or optimize ego behavior against prescribed hidden-agent predictions, leaving the worst history-consistent interaction unexplored. We introduce History-Conditioned Minimax Trajectory Search (HC-MTS), which combines temporal occlusion reasoning with response-aware search. First, HC-MTS constructs finite hidden-state modes, each certified by a backward witness satisfying multi-frame visibility, occupancy, semantic-map support, and class-specific kinematic constraints. It then solves a bilevel minimax problem: an inner finite oracle maximizes the ego driving score over destination attainment and ride comfort, while the outer search selects the legal hidden-vehicle trajectory that minimizes this best-response value. Across eight Waymo Open Motion Dataset scenarios, increasing the visibility-memory horizon from K=1 to K=20 reduces the mean per-scenario vehicle, pedestrian, and total retained hidden-seed counts by 18.12%, 21.67%, and 18.45%, respectively. HC-MTS identifies six avoidable counterexamples, while no legal collision-producing attacker is found in the remaining two scenes within the finite search budget.

</details>


### [108] [Towards AI-enhanced control: a numerical technique for trajectory smoothing of a parallel robot for pancreatic surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20499)
*Iosif Birlescu,Alexandru Pusca,Bogdan Gherman,Calin Vaida,Ionut Zima,Damien Chablat,Doina Pisla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于微创胰腺手术并联机器人末端执行器轨迹平滑的数值方法，基于实时主从控制架构，通过生成S曲线控制加速度以降低组织损伤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微创手术中末端执行器的加速度突变会加剧组织创伤，且主从控制需要满足实时性要求，因此需要一种兼顾平滑性与实时性的轨迹规划方法。

Method: 针对实时主从架构，采用3D空间鼠标进行速度指令输入；通过生成S曲线平滑末端执行器速度场以控制加速度；并依据鼠标指令对S曲线进行分段以实现实时控制。特别考虑了所有指令输入下加速时间恒定的情形。

Result: 数值结果表明，末端执行器参数空间与主动关节参数均能实现无突变的稳定过渡，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法可有效实现并联机器人末端轨迹平滑并支持实时控制；后续将在实验模型上验证，并集成到基于AI的训练模块中。

Abstract: The paper presents a numerical approach for the end-effector trajectory smoothing of a parallel robot designed for minimally invasive pancreatic surgery. The approach is tailored for real-time master-slave control architecture and uses a 3D space mouse for command input for velocity control. The trajectory smoothing is achieved by generating S-curves in the end-effector velocity fields, thus controlling the accelerations, which in turn reduces tissue trauma in the minimally invasive procedures. Real-time control is enabled by segmenting the S-curves based on the command inputs from the 3D space mouse. A special case is considered where the acceleration time is constant for all command inputs. Numeric results demonstrate stable transitions (without abrupt changes) in both the end-effector parameter space and in the active joints parameters, thereby validating the proposed approach. Further work aims to test the approach on an experimental model and integrate it into AI-based training modules.

</details>


### [109] [Integrated Guidance and Control of a Mother-Child UAV-UGV System for Cooperative Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20540)
*Aashish Sahu,R. Prasanth Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并实地验证了一套异构“巡视车-母机-子机”系统的自主空中回收框架，实现子机对悬停多旋翼母机的自主回收，20次室外任务成功17次（85%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对悬停多旋翼母机进行回收不同于地面或舰载平台，因为回收面本身是一台主动控制、推力受限的飞行器，这带来了额外的相对动力学、扰动与安全约束挑战，需要系统级的自主框架来保证回收过程的可行性与安全性。

Method: 回收栈集成了：受限加加速度（jerk-bounded）参考生成、扰动观测器增强的平面跟踪、可行性感知的垂直控制、基于离散时间障碍函数的相对垂直几何安全滤波器，以及通信感知的母机状态预测。在PX4-ROS 2架构上实现，融合RTK GNSS、IMU与气压计，母机侧1D激光雷达仅作为近距接触辅助线索。任务流程涵盖巡视车监督、母机转移、子机部署出击、自主返回、空中回收与同步下降。

Result: 全程保持RTK-fixed定位。20次室外协同任务中17次完成部署、出击与回收，任务成功率85%。成功回收的终端对准平均耗时6.3 s；接受时刻平均平面对准误差0.18 m，0.40 m捕获半径内最大终端平面偏差0.32 m；耦合下降过程中记录的最小相对垂直间距0.41 m；母机平面定点RMS误差0.25 m。3次失败发生在不同任务阶段并单独分析。

Conclusion: 结果证明了在所测试室外作业包线内实现自主空中回收的可行性；主要贡献在于将上述方法协调集成为面向悬停多旋翼母机回收的系统级方案，并完成全尺寸室外验证。

Abstract: Autonomous recovery of a small multirotor onto a hovering multirotor carrier differs from recovery onto ground or shipborne platforms because the recovery surface is itself an actively controlled, thrust-limited aerial vehicle. This paper presents a field-validated autonomy framework for a heterogeneous rover-mothership-child system executing rover supervision, mothership transit, child deployment and sortie, autonomous return, aerial recovery, and synchronized descent. The recovery stack combines jerk-bounded reference generation, disturbance-observer-augmented planar tracking, feasibility-aware vertical control, a discrete-time barrier-based safety filter for relative vertical geometry, and communication-aware carrier-state prediction. The contribution is the coordinated system-level integration of these methods for recovery onto a hovering multirotor and its full-scale outdoor validation. The framework is implemented on a PX4-ROS 2 architecture using RTK-enabled GNSS, IMU, and barometric fusion, with mothership-side 1D lidar used only as an auxiliary near-contact cue. RTK-fixed positioning was maintained throughout testing. Across 20 outdoor cooperative missions, 17 successfully completed deployment, sortie, and recovery, giving an observed mission success rate of 85%. For successful recoveries, mean terminal-alignment time was 6.3 s, mean planar alignment error at acceptance was 0.18 m, maximum terminal planar deviation was 0.32 m within a 0.40 m capture radius, and minimum logged relative vertical separation during coupled descent was 0.41 m. Mothership planar station-keeping RMS error was 0.25 m. The three unsuccessful trials occurred at different mission stages and are analyzed separately. Results demonstrate practical autonomous aerial recovery within the tested outdoor operating envelope.

</details>


### [110] [Learning Slope-Adaptive Whole-Body Locomotion for Humanoid Robots in Roofing Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20558)
*Songyang Liu,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出任务语义场景锚定的全身运动学习框架，使Unitree G1在斜屋顶完成行走、钉枪、锤击等作业动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屋顶作业需在斜面上协调移动、平衡与作业动作，人形机器人应用难度大；直接重定向人类演示虽保留动作外观，却会使机器人手足相对屋顶位置错误，缺乏场景空间约束。

Method: 用跟踪系统采集人类演示并重定向至Unitree G1，用度量屋顶模型提供空间参考；轨迹级优化将推断的支撑接触与标注的作业关系锚定到屋顶；执行感知强化学习使策略在动态跟踪误差下保持这些关系。

Result: 多运动跟踪、屋顶坡度覆盖矩阵、五路钉枪消融、锤击与侧推跨任务实验及与纯强化学习/零样本遥操作对比中，所有种子均满足支撑、作业间隙与非穿透准则；作业间隙误差0.256–0.531 cm，各任务3/3成功；实机复现上坡行走、钉枪、锤击与弯腰，基座坐标系平均运动误差低于80 mm。

Conclusion: 场景锚定的人类运动学习可作为面向建筑作业的人形机器人运动基元的有效基础。

Abstract: Roofing requires workers to coordinate locomotion, balance, and work-related body motions on pitched surfaces, creating a challenging application for humanoid robots. Directly retargeted human demonstrations, however, may preserve motion appearance while placing the robot's feet or hands incorrectly relative to the roof. This study presents a task-semantic scene-grounded framework for learning roofer-style whole-body motions on a Unitree G1. Human demonstrations are captured using a tracking system and retargeted to the robot, while a metric roof model supplies the spatial reference unavailable from the tracking system. A trajectory-level optimization grounds inferred support contacts and annotated work relations to the roof, and execution-aware reinforcement learning encourages the resulting policy to preserve these relations under dynamic tracking errors. The framework is evaluated through a multi-motion tracking study, a roof-pitch coverage matrix, a five-way nailgun ablation, cross-task experiments on hammering and lateral pushing, and comparisons with pure reinforcement learning and zero-shot teleoperation. Our method enables the robot to satisfy support, work-clearance, and nonpenetration criteria across all evaluated seeds. Across nailgun, hammering, and pushing, it achieves work-clearance errors between 0.256 and 0.531 cm and 3/3 successful evaluations per task. Physical experiments reproduce uphill walking, nailgun, hammering, and bending motions with mean base-frame motion errors below 80 mm. These findings establish scene-grounded human motion learning as a promising basis for construction-oriented humanoid motion primitives.

</details>


### [111] [OmniMimic: Dynamics-completed Motion Augmentation for Multi-style Omnidirectional Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20566)
*Sheng Wu,Guoqiang Zhao,Zhe Yang,Fei Teng,Zhikun Zhou,Yanlin Yang,Zheng Fang,Hong Zheng,Yaonan Wang,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniMimic将方向受限的动物演示转化为单一多步态策略，实现四足机器人全向速度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人可通过动物演示获得自然步态风格，难以用人工奖励设计替代。但现有动物演示方向覆盖狭窄，在后退、侧移和转向指令上缺乏风格一致的监督信号。

Method: 提出OmniMimic训练框架：1）结合时间反转、约束动力学补全与矢状面镜像，构建超出观测方向的机器人特异性运动学与物理监督；2）将指令从演示速度分布逐步扩展至目标各轴边界；3）采用共享actor加软门控、步态特化残差专家的结构，兼顾可复用运动技能与步态特定修正。

Result: 在仿真中对四种步态验证：相比匹配的APEX基线，前后向参考速度下的平均足部位置RMSE降低12.9%，均匀笛卡尔指令网格上的速度跟踪RMSE降低63.1%。

Conclusion: 通过数据增强与渐进指令扩展，可在有限动物演示下训练出覆盖全向速度范围的多步态单策略，显著提升足部轨迹精度与速度跟踪性能。

Abstract: Animal demonstrations provide quadruped robots with natural and distinctive gait styles that are difficult to specify through hand-crafted rewards. However, their narrow directional coverage leaves little style-consistent supervision for backward, lateral, and turning commands. We present OmniMimic, a training framework that turns directionally limited animal demonstrations into a single multi-gait policy over target per-axis velocity ranges. OmniMimic first combines temporal reversal, constrained dynamics completion, and sagittal reflection to construct robot-specific kinematic and physical supervision beyond the observed directions. It then expands commands progressively from the demonstrated velocity distribution toward the target per-axis bounds, and uses a shared actor with soft-gated, gait-specialized residual experts to balance reusable locomotion skills with gait-specific corrections. Across four gaits in simulation, OmniMimic reduces mean foot-position RMSE at forward and backward reference velocities by 12.9% and velocity-tracking RMSE on a uniform Cartesian command grid by 63.1%, compared with the matched APEX baseline. The project page is at https://OmniMimic.github.io.

</details>


### [112] [Walking on the Slope: Stable Bipedal Gaits with Genetic-Algorithm-Optimized Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20570)
*Madhav Rijal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对8自由度双足机器人进行了运动学与动力学建模、轨迹生成及稳定性分析，采用GA优化轨迹参数，仿真验证其在平地与斜坡上的ZMP稳定性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究8自由度双足机器人在平地与倾斜地形上行走的建模、轨迹生成与稳定性问题，明确其稳定行走的极限条件及影响因素。

Method: 采用DH参数与齐次变换推导正运动学；用闭式逆运动学将由三次样条生成的髋部与摆动足笛卡尔轨迹映射为关节角；用Newton-Euler迭代算法计算关节力矩；用ZMP准则评估动态稳定性；用遗传算法以关节做功最小为目标、并施加ZMP可行性惩罚，优化髋高、摆动足最大抬升高度与额状面倾斜角。

Result: MATLAB仿真表明，标称8自由度模型在步态完成时间低至0.5 s、坡度至22.5°（给定足部几何）时仍保持ZMP稳定；超出该极限ZMP会离开支撑多边形，需修改足部尺寸或轨迹参数；ZMP稳定性由各连杆的质量分布而非机器人总质量决定。

Conclusion: 该8自由度双足机器人的ZMP稳定行走存在明确的步态时间与坡度上限，超过后需调整足部尺寸或轨迹参数；稳定性主要受质量分布而非总质量支配。

Abstract: This paper presents the kinematic and dynamic modeling, trajectory generation, and stability analysis of an 8-degree-of-freedom (DOF) biped robot walking on flat and inclined terrain. Denavit-Hartenberg (DH) parameters and homogeneous transformations are used to derive the forward kinematics, while closed-form inverse kinematics maps the desired hip and swing-foot Cartesian trajectories, generated with cubic splines, to joint angles. Joint torques are computed using the Newton-Euler iterative algorithm, and dynamic stability is evaluated using the zero moment point (ZMP) criterion. A genetic algorithm (GA) optimizes the hip height, maximum swing-foot lift, and frontal-plane tilt angle by minimizing the work done by the joints subject to a ZMP feasibility penalty. Simulation results in MATLAB show that the nominal 8-DOF model remains ZMP-stable for step completion times down to 0.5 s and for slope inclinations up to 22.5 degrees with the given foot geometry. Beyond these limits, the ZMP leaves the support polygon, and either the foot dimensions or the trajectory parameters must be modified. The results also show that ZMP stability is governed by the mass distribution among the links rather than the total mass of the robot.

</details>


### [113] [Accelerating Visual Policy Learning with Sampling-Based Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20575)
*Yilang Liu,Haoxiang You,Qian Wang,Daniel Rakita,Ian Abraham*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出采样引导策略搜索（SGPS），将采样型模型预测控制与一阶策略梯度结合，在单GPU上高效训练视觉运动策略，并零样本迁移到真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习运动与操作的视觉策略需协调环境接触，计算与GPU显存开销大。一阶策略梯度（FoPG）借助可微仿真降低训练成本，但局部优化易收敛到非预期的接触模式。

Method: SGPS将基于采样的模型预测控制（MPC）的反复动作-目标精化与一阶策略优化耦合：先用行为克隆从采样动作初始化策略；训练在扰动初始状态与随机化动力学下，交替进行采样精化与短时域FoPG更新。视觉训练采用解耦的FoPG形式，将渲染排除在计算图外，从而可直接从深度观测学习，无需状态-策略教师。

Result: 在单GPU上，SGPS为仿真Unitree Go2与G1机器人学习了运动、越障、推箱与双臂搬运策略。实验表明，精化过程在初始化与跟踪之外仍能进一步改进策略学习。硬件部署中，蒸馏后的策略零样本迁移到真实Go2，利用机载深度自主小跑、爬行、越障并在这些行为间切换。

Conclusion: 将采样精化与一阶策略优化耦合，可克服FoPG局部最优导致的非预期接触模式，实现高效视觉策略训练与零样本真机迁移。

Abstract: Learning visual policies for locomotion and manipulation requires coordinating contact with the environment and can incur substantial computation and GPU memory costs. First-order policy gradients (FoPG) reduce training cost through differentiable simulation, but local optimization can converge to unintended contact patterns. To address this shortfall, we propose Sampling-Guided Policy Search (SGPS), which couples recurring action-target refinement by sampling-based model-predictive control with first-order policy optimization. Behavior cloning initializes the policy from sampled actions; training then alternates sampling-based refinement with short-horizon FoPG updates under perturbed initial states and randomized dynamics. For visual policy training, we use a decoupled FoPG formulation that excludes rendering from the computation graph, enabling direct learning from depth observations without a state-policy teacher. On a single GPU, SGPS learns policies for locomotion, obstacle traversal, crate pushing, and bimanual carrying on simulated Unitree Go2 and G1 robots. Our experiments further show that refinement improves policy learning beyond initialization and tracking alone. For hardware deployment, the distilled policy transfers zero-shot to a real Go2 and uses onboard depth to autonomously trot, crawl, clear hurdles, and switch between these behaviors.

</details>


### [114] [V2-STRep: VLM-Grounded Structured Task Representations for Reusable Robot Skills Acquired from Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20582)
*Yexin Hu,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: V2-STRep是一个零样本框架，将人类操作视频生成模型产生的运动转化为可复用机器人技能，通过VLM结构化的任务表示实现跨场景迁移与指令适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类操作视频蕴含丰富的运动与交互线索，可用于无需机器人示范的技能获取。视频生成模型能由初始场景图像和任务指令合成示范，避免逐任务录制示范。然而，恢复出的运动仅捕捉单一场景的特定实现，任务结构、几何关系与约束是隐式的，难以复用和迁移。

Method: 提出V2-STRep零样本框架，将生成视频运动转化为可复用机器人技能。核心是基于VLM的结构化任务表示，指定运动阶段、参考物与任务相关约束；目标以最小几何结构描述：点、点法向、轴、平面及完整6D位姿。将VLM提供的2D图像空间线索借助RGB-D提升到3D，重建任务几何与候选抓取位姿。用几何特定规则将运动迁移到新场景；任务约束轨迹优化将抓取选择与完整机器人运动规划耦合，在满足任务要求的同时利用剩余旋转自由度适应关节限位。更新部署中的grounding与约束即可在新兼容指令下复用，无需重新生成视频。

Result: 在六项真实世界操作任务上，相比基线方法提升了执行成功率；成功获得的技能可可靠地跨场景迁移；能适应部署指令的变化。

Conclusion: V2-STRep通过结构化任务表示将视频运动抽象为可复用技能，实现了零样本、跨场景、指令可更新的机器人操作技能获取，验证了从人类操作视频中提取可迁移技能的有效性。

Abstract: Human manipulation videos provide rich motion and interaction cues for acquiring robot skills without robot demonstrations. Video generation models synthesize such demonstrations from an initial scene image and task instruction, avoiding the need to record demonstrations for each task. However, the recovered motion captures only one scene-specific realization, leaving task structure, geometric relations, and constraints implicit. We present V2-STRep, a zero-shot framework that converts generated video motion into reusable robot skills through VLM-grounded structured task representations. The representation specifies motion phases, references, and task-relevant constraints, with targets described by minimal geometric structures: points, point-normals, axes, planes, and full 6D poses. VLM-provided 2D image-space cues are lifted into 3D using RGB-D observations to reconstruct task geometry and candidate grasp poses. Geometry-specific rules transfer motion to new scenes, while task-constrained trajectory optimization couples grasp selection with complete robot motion planning. It preserves task requirements while using remaining rotational freedom to accommodate joint limits. Updating deployment grounding and constraints enables reuse under new compatible instructions without generating another video. Experiments on six real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate improved execution success over baselines, reliable cross-scene transfer of successfully acquired skills, and adaptation to changed deployment instructions.

</details>


### [115] [CoRef-GS: Cooperative Referring Gaussian Splatting for Multi-Agent Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20586)
*Zhikun Zhou,Kunyu Peng,Runyi Yang,Junhao Cai,Di Wen,Ruiping Liu,Danda Pani Paudel,Yi Zhou,Luc Van Gool,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出协作指代高斯接地问题与CoRef-GS框架，实现融合地图上的视角条件关系推理，并发布双四足机器人基准CoQuad-Ref。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
