Motivation: 具身机器人的指代场景理解需从指定视角接地物体与关系语言查询。单智能体的局部语义高斯地图可支持该任务，但在协作场景中，独立重建的地图经对齐融合后仍需保持接地有效性：被指目标或其上下文地标可能来自其他智能体的观测，而空间关系必须从查询机器人的视角解释。该问题要求几何可对齐性、实例级语义可比性与视角条件关系推理三项能力。现有语言感知高斯方法多针对单地图查询，而高斯配准方法仅优化几何或光度对齐，不保留面向语言接地的语义兼容性。

Method: 提出CoRef-GS协作指代高斯泼溅框架：先构建局部开放词汇、实例感知的高斯地图；再通过跨智能体对齐模块，依据几何与语义一致性对齐部分重叠的地图；最后利用视角条件掩码关系图完成查询接地。同时构建CoQuad-Ref双四足机器人基准，覆盖真实与仿真室内场景。

Result: 仿真场景中，CoRef-GS将旋转误差由粗初始化后的2.58°降至细化后的0.15°；真实场景指代mIoU由ReferSplat的52.6%提升至68.8%。基准与源码将于https://github.com/ruojiruoli17/CoRef-GS.git 公开。

Conclusion: CoRef-GS通过几何-语义联合对齐与视角条件关系推理，有效解决多机融合地图上的指代高斯接地，并建立可复现的协作具身指代基准。

Abstract: Referring scene understanding for embodied robots requires grounding object- and relation-centric language queries from a designated viewpoint. While a local semantic Gaussian map can support such grounding within one agent's observations, cooperative settings require this ability to remain effective after independently reconstructed maps are aligned and fused. In this setting, the referred target or its contextual landmark may come from another agent's observations, while spatial relations must still be interpreted from the querying robot's viewpoint. We formulate this problem as cooperative referring Gaussian grounding over fused maps, which requires geometric alignability, instance-level semantic comparability, and view-conditioned relation reasoning. Existing language-aware Gaussian methods mainly focus on single-map querying, whereas Gaussian registration methods optimize geometric or photometric alignment without preserving language-grounding-oriented semantic compatibility. We propose CoRef-GS, a cooperative referring Gaussian splatting framework. CoRef-GS constructs local open-vocabulary instance-aware Gaussian maps, then aligns partially overlapping maps with a cross-agent alignment module by geometric and semantic consistency, and grounds queries using a view-conditioned mask relation graph. We further introduce CoQuad-Ref, a dual-quadruped benchmark spanning both real-world and simulated indoor scenes. Experiments show that, on simulated scenes, CoRef-GS reduces the rotation error from 2.58° after coarse initialization to 0.15° after refinement, and improves real-world referring mIoU over ReferSplat from 52.6% to 68.8%. The established benchmark and source code will be publicly released at https://github.com/ruojiruoli17/CoRef-GS.git.

</details>


### [116] [Semantic SLAM in Precision Agriculture using Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20604)
*Ruben Beumer,Sander Doodeman,René van de Molengraft,Duarte Antunes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向精准农业的实时语义世界建模框架，将贝叶斯推断的概率语义地图与基于g²o的图优化SLAM结合，在Gazebo仿真及Spot机器人实测中实现对至少400株植物的实时建图与定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准农业中的自主机器人需要在作物田间同时完成建图、定位与任务执行，而仅依赖GPS难以满足精度与鲁棒性需求；同时作物及其属性（种类、尺寸、健康状态）具有明确语义价值，需要被纳入地图表示。

Method: 框架融合两部分：一是对物体及其语义属性进行概率建模，并通过贝叶斯推断持续更新；二是基于g²o图优化实现的图结构SLAM，实现不单纯依赖GPS的定位与建图。机器人利用植物类型、大小、健康等语义信息，在田间边作业边建图与定位。感知端训练YOLOv8n目标检测模型，从深度相机观测中提取物体与语义数据。

Result: 通过Gazebo仿真以及使用Boston Dynamics四足机器人Spot在含人工植物的室内田间的实体实验进行验证，系统能够成功地对至少400株植物实现实时建图。

Conclusion: 将语义概率建图与图优化SLAM集成，可为精准农业机器人提供不依赖GPS的实时语义世界模型，并在仿真与真实机器人平台上验证了其可扩展性（≥400株植物）。

Abstract: This paper presents a real-time semantic world modeling framework specialized for precision agriculture using autonomous robots. The framework combines probabilistic mapping of objects and their semantic attributes, updated through Bayesian inference, with a graph-based Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) approach implemented using $g^2o$, a general framework for graph optimization. This integration enables accurate mapping and localization without relying solely on GPS. By leveraging semantic information such as plant type, size, and health, the robot can perform tasks while mapping and localizing itself within a field of crops. The proposed framework was validated through Gazebo simulations and physical experiments on an indoor field with artificial plants using Boston Dynamics' robot dog Spot. A YOLOv8n object detection model was trained to extract object and semantic data from depth camera observations. These simulations and experiments demonstrate that the system can successfully perform real-time mapping of up to at least 400 plants.

</details>


### [117] [Bayesian Continuum Robot Dynamics and State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20605)
*James M. Ferguson,Tucker Hermans,Alan Kuntz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种连续体机器人动态状态估计的因子图方法，将惯性项与阻尼项等效为外力载荷，从而在保留静力学代数形式的前提下近似Cosserat杆动力学，实现随机前向仿真与联合状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续体机器人因子图状态估计多依赖准静态假设或白噪声运动先验，在惯性效应显著时无法刻画真实物理过程，导致动态运动下精度受限。

Method: 以Cosserat杆动力学为建模基础，将惯性与阻尼写成等效外加载荷，使动态平衡方程沿用先前准静态工作推导出的代数形式。无主干观测时框架退化为机器人运动的随机前向仿真；有观测时则联合优化运动学与动力学状态，并推断外加载荷等状态量。

Result: 在肌腱驱动连续体机器人上完成仿真与实验验证，展示了随机前向仿真与状态估计的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作将因子图连续体机器人估计从准静态扩展至动态场景，在保持静力学求解结构的同时提升了动态运动下的建模精度与状态估计能力。

Abstract: Recent factor graph approaches to continuum robot state estimation have been successful for quasi-static applications and spatiotemporal estimation using white-noise kinematic motion priors. However, when inertial effects are significant, these approximations may fail to capture the underlying physics, limiting accuracy during dynamic motions. In contrast, our approach approximates the Cosserat rod dynamics of continuum robots. We write inertia and damping as equivalent applied loads, so that the dynamic balance retains the algebraic form of the static one from prior work with quasi-static robots. Without backbone observations, the framework reduces to a stochastic forward simulation of the robot's motion. Given observations, it jointly refines kinematic and dynamic states and infers external loads, among other states. We validate the approach through simulation and experiments, demonstrating stochastic forward simulation as well as state estimation on tendon-driven continuum robots.

</details>


### [118] [INSPECT: Learning Robot View Selection from Assistant Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20615)
*Di Wen,Kailun Yang,Wenhao Guo,Yitian Shi,Junwei Zheng,Yufan Chen,Ruiping Liu,Jiale Wei,Rania Rayyes,Kunyu Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出INSPECT系统，使机器人能够从智能眼镜助手的记录中学习视角偏好，用于装配过程中的零件存在性及安装正确性检查，在物理变速箱装配与商用角磨机数据上均取得优于基线的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在装配检查中需判断零件是否存在及是否安装正确。第一视角装配辅助中，头部运动与工件操作蕴含检查证据，而口头状态确认将观察与流程结果关联。然而机器人选视角面临困难：缺乏目标域视角标签用于策略训练、需在只有当前观测与已知位姿（无候选图像）条件下决策，且物体证据的校准需对身份变化保持稳健。

Method: 提出INSPECT，从回答零件查询并提供下一步指导的智能眼镜助手记录中学习机器人视角偏好。关键组件包括：(1) Presence-Invariant TwinSwap（PI-TwinSwap），通过成对身份干预校准物体证据；(2) claim-indexed supervision（声明索引监督），将证据需求与相机可复现的观测变化分离；(3) object-centered calibration（物体中心校准），将相对视角偏好适配到机器人位姿；(4) clause-level screening（子句级筛选），检验预测证据。机器人仅利用当前观测与已知位姿选视角，无需候选图像。评测采用带标注的助手视频回放模拟状态反馈，不使用目标域视角标签训练策略。

Result: 在物理变速箱装配图像上，INSPECT在对比的非oracle策略中取得最高视角效用，将人工评定的完全可验证率从34.8%提升至41.7%（相比保持当前视角）。在IMPACT的商用角磨机记录上，迁移的相对视角选择器在感知头冻结条件下将正确决策率从50.6%提升至54.3%。

Conclusion: INSPECT可从第一视角助手记录中学习视角偏好，在无目标域视角标签、无候选图像条件下实现有效的机器人检查视角选择，显著提升装配检查的可验证性与决策正确率，且具备跨域迁移能力。

Abstract: Robots inspecting an assembly must determine which parts are present and whether they are correctly installed. During egocentric assembly assistance, head motion and workpiece handling reveal evidence for these checks, while spoken state confirmations link observations to procedural outcomes. We introduce INSPECT, which learns robot view preferences from records of a smart-glasses assistant that answers part queries and provides next-step guidance. Presence-Invariant TwinSwap (PI-TwinSwap) calibrates object evidence through paired identity interventions. Claim-indexed supervision separates evidence requirements from camera-reproducible observation changes. Object-centered calibration adapts relative view preferences to robot poses, while clause-level screening checks predicted evidence. The robot selects views using only its current observation and known poses, without candidate images. Evaluation uses annotated assistant-video replay to simulate state feedback, without target-domain view labels for policy training. On images of physical gearbox assemblies, INSPECT achieves the highest view utility among the compared non-oracle policies and raises human-rated full verifiability from 34.8% to 41.7% compared with keeping the current view. On commercial angle-grinder recordings in IMPACT, the transferred relative-view selector increases the correct decision rate from 50.6% to 54.3% with a frozen perception head. The source code is available at https://github.com/Kratos-Wen/INSPECT.

</details>


### [119] [A Simulation Platform for AUV Fault Recovery: Exploring LLM-Based Diagnostic Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20620)
*Khalid Halba,Kylie Cooper,James G. Bellingham*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SPAR闭环仿真平台，将实时C语言AUV控制软件与LLM编排层耦合，通过480次集成试验评估LLM辅助的故障诊断与恢复规划，发现模型选择主导诊断性能，且诊断与决策表现并不耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV在通信不可靠环境下必须自主从故障中恢复。传统确定性分层控制可管理正常运行，但面对未预期故障的恢复能力有限。由于语言模型具有随机性，单次演示无法提供严谨评估，需要集成化测试方法。

Method: 构建SPAR（Simulation Platform for AUV Recovery）闭环仿真架构：耦合实时C语言车载软件与高层编排层，涵盖基于物理的故障注入、结构化提示、语言模型交互、任务文件生成、验证、执行与LLM评判打分。针对质量偏移故障，在480次试验中变化故障实现、提示结构、推理模型与任务条件，评估一个前沿模型和三个可本地部署的现成LLM。

Result: 模型选择主导诊断结果：前沿模型在85-90%试验中将CG偏移机制列入前三假设，最佳本地模型为60-78%。推理分析显示，本地模型成功与遵循完整诊断流程相关，而较弱模型常在执行器仍跟踪指令时过早断言升降舵故障。诊断性能与操作决策性能在该数据集中未见耦合。

Conclusion: 贡献在于：(1)一种将未预期故障恢复从检测扩展到缓解的架构；(2)一种在低功耗AUV上评估LLM辅助任务管理的集成方法。若无可靠通信，该架构可提升AUV的自主恢复能力。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) operating beyond reliable communications must recover from failures without human intervention. We investigate an architecture in which conventional deterministic layered control autonomy manages normal operations, while an invokable large language model (LLM) serves as a diagnostic and recovery planner when onboard anomaly detection identifies performance outside expected limits. Because language models are stochastic, rigorous evaluation requires ensemble testing rather than individual demonstrations. We present a closed-loop simulation architecture that couples real-time C vehicle software with a higher-level orchestration layer for physics-based fault injection, structured prompting, language-model interaction, mission file generation, validation, execution, and LLM-judge scoring. The framework, which we call SPAR (Simulation Platform for AUV Recovery), supports evaluation across fault realizations, prompt structures, reasoning models, and mission conditions. We vary these for a mass-shift fault over 480 SPAR trials, evaluating a frontier model and three off-the-shelf locally deployable LLMs. Model choice dominates diagnosis: the frontier model places the CG-shift mechanism in its top three hypotheses in 85-90% of trials, versus 60-78% for the best local model. Reasoning analysis indicates that local-model success is associated with following the complete diagnostic procedure, whereas weaker models often commit prematurely to elevator failure even though the actuator tracks its command. Diagnosis and operational decision performance do not appear to be coupled in this dataset. The contributions are an architecture extending unanticipated-fault recovery from detection to mitigation and an ensemble methodology for evaluating LLM-assisted mission management on low-power AUVs.

</details>


### [120] [SmellDiffusion: Diffusion-Based Quadruped Navigation with Olfactory Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20624)
*Faith Ogunwoye,Iana Zhura,Hajira Amjad,Timofei Kozlov,Didar Seyidov,Dmitrii Plotnikov,Fedor Fedorov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SmellDiffusion 仿真流程，用开放词汇嗅觉场景图表示气体种类区域，并结合峰位几何门控与扩散规划器完成气源定位与导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人被派往指定气体泄漏点，需要保持气体身份一致、估计气源并导航至目标。现有方法在大流场中浓度峰与真实源点分离时误差较大，且经典规划与学习式规划缺乏共享目标的统一评估。

Method: 在开放词汇嗅觉场景图中表示物种特异的气体区域，并在经典规划器与扩散规划器之间共享所选目标；引入仅在训练集标定的源无关几何门控，在观测峰处选择性触发前向匹配校正；采用基于扩散的气体引导轨迹生成，并与气体引导 A* 及普通 A* 对比。

Result: 在 424 组源-风配置中，28 组浓度峰偏离源点超过 0.5m；几何门控以 0.64 精确率检出 10 个留出偏移中的 9 个。门控校正使偏移案例平均误差从 1.468m 降至 0.592m（降低 60%），仅对 14/204 案例使用匹配；全案例平均误差从 0.205m 降至 0.180m。十次采样扩散的气体暴露均值与气体引导 A* 相当（0.0476 对 0.0455）；单次扩散提议 41.7ms，气体引导 A* 为 72.3ms，但十次串行采样总耗时更长。普通 A* 同样到达目标且为最快、路径最短。六次匹配 Gazebo 运行中机器人到源平均误差：A* 0.39m，扩散 0.31m。

Conclusion: 共享场景图目标估计配合峰位几何门控校正，可在少样本触发下显著降低源定位误差；扩散规划以更短单次推理时间达到与气体引导 A* 相当的暴露水平，但串行采样代价较高，普通 A* 仍是最快最短路径基线。

Abstract: A robot sent to a named gas leak must preserve gas identity, estimate the source, and navigate to the resulting goal. We present SmellDiffusion, a simulation pipeline that represents species-specific gas zones in an open-vocabulary olfactory scene graph and shares the selected goal between classical and diffusion planners. Its key components are a peak-local geometric gate for selective source correction and diffusion-based, gas-guided trajectory generation. Among 424 unique source-wind configurations in solved flow, 28 have a concentration peak displaced more than 0.5m from the source. A source-independent geometric gate, calibrated only on the training split and evaluated at the observed peak, detects 9 of 10 held-out displacements at 0.64 precision. Gating a precomputed forward-matching correction reduces mean error on the displaced cases from 1.468m to 0.592m (60%), using matching for only 14/204 cases. All-case mean error falls from 0.205m to 0.180m. All planners receive the same scene-graph source estimate as their goal. In a controlled comparison, best-of-ten diffusion achieves mean gas exposure comparable to gas-guided A* (0.0476 versus 0.0455). A single diffusion proposal takes 41.7ms, compared with 72.3ms for gas-guided A*, although best-of-ten sequential sampling increases total runtime. Plain A* also reaches the same goal and remains the fastest and shortest-path method. Six matched Gazebo runs give mean robot-to-source errors of 0.39m for A* and 0.31m for diffusion.

</details>


### [121] [RTK-Vision PPO for Autonomous Micro UAV Recovery on an Airborne Carrier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20629)
*Aashish Sahu,R Prasanth Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RTK-视觉引导的强化学习框架，使小型无人机能从运动中的大型载机自主起飞、执行任务、返回并重新对接，14次户外试验成功率92.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现可重复的"部署—任务—回收"循环需要无人机自主降落到运动中的空中载机，但该任务耦合了远距离会合、近距感知、载机运动、气动干扰与不连续接触事件，现有方法难以整体解决。

Method: 采用RTK-视觉引导的强化学习框架：子机由载机携带并空中起飞，两机均配备RTK-GNSS且载机持续共享导航状态；近回收平台时RTK保持工作，下视相机配合基准标记检测器提供相对对齐信息；末端回收阶段由PPO策略控制，在MuJoCo物理仿真中训练，显式引入传感器噪声模型、气动扰动代理和标记延迟随机化，再迁移到硬件；PX4负责底层稳定，确定性安全门独立于学习策略授权下降。

Result: PPO模型在2000个随机末端回合中成功率99.55%，相同条件下调优的PD基线为78.4%，平面末端误差中位数6.62 cm；14次户外试验中完整任务成功13次（92.9%），涵盖近区回收以及载机离开投放点后的回收。

Conclusion: 验证了完整的自主空中部署与回收循环，而非孤立的着陆动作，为巡检、监视和移动物流中的可重复载机—子机作业奠定实用基础。

Abstract: Autonomous recovery of a micro unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) onto a moving airborne carrier enables reusable deploy-mission-recover operation, but couples long-range rendezvous, close-range perception, carrier motion, aerodynamic interaction, and a discontinuous contact event. This paper presents an RTK-vision-guided reinforcement-learning framework in which a child UAV is physically transported by a larger carrier, takes off from the carrier while airborne, executes an independent sortie, returns to the carrier's current position, redocks, and subsequently descends with the carrier. Both vehicles carry RTK-GNSS, and the carrier continuously shares its navigation state with the child. Near the recovery deck, RTK remains active while a downward-facing camera with a fiducial marker detector provides marker-relative alignment cues. A proximal policy optimization (PPO) policy governing the terminal recovery phase is trained in a physics-based MuJoCo simulation environment with explicit sensor noise models, an aerodynamic disturbance surrogate, and marker-latency randomization, then transferred to hardware. PX4 retains low-level stabilization, and a deterministic safety gate authorizes descent independently of the learned policy. The PPO checkpoint achieves 99.55% success over 2,000 held-out randomized terminal episodes, compared with 78.4% for a tuned PD baseline under identical conditions, with a median planar terminal error of 6.62 cm. Across 14 outdoor trials, the full mission succeeds in 13 trials (92.9%), spanning both near-region recovery and recovery after the carrier translates away from the release point. The results demonstrate a complete autonomous aerial deployment-and-recovery cycle rather than an isolated landing maneuver, establishing a practical basis for reusable carrier-child operation in inspection, surveillance, and mobile-logistics applications.

</details>


### [122] [TraceFlow: Guiding Frozen Flow-Matching Robot Policies with Success and Failure Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20646)
*Jiaxuan Zhang,Ruizhe Liu,Yu Zhang,Yanchao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TraceFlow：一种无需更新权重、仅依赖终端成败位与检索轨迹（含失败轨迹）的测试时引导场，用于修正冻结的流匹配VLA策略动作生成；在真实机器人有序装箱任务中显著提升成功率并消除错序，仿真中增益具选择性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时方法（检索成功样本、学习评论器、验证器、动力学模型）均为冻结策略提供输入，但均未将机器人自身的失败轨迹作为负向证据，且只利用终端结果位这一最弱监督信号。论文旨在挖掘失败轨迹的负向价值以实现测试时策略改进。

Method: 提出TraceFlow——与进度对齐的引导场，将检索到的成功与失败轨迹的动作密度转化为对冻结流匹配动作专家的有界修正，仅用每条轨迹一个终端成败位作为标签，无需其他标注。构建TraceBank：存储按时间排序的"状态-动作"轨迹记录及终端标签，初始来自目标任务训练轨迹，后续接纳部署机器人自身的rollouts。

Result: 真实机器人有序装箱任务：基础策略50次试验中按序完成21次，TraceFlow达39次，在不更新任何权重的情况下某堆叠轮次达47次，错序回合从20降至0。仿真中增益具选择性：按套件选定配置时，RoboMemArena的Sequence任务成功率由78.92%升至91.50%，Transferring由54.41%升至62.00%（堆叠第2轮），26任务聚合指标不变，Counting与Occlusion分别下降1.12和1.42个百分点，LIBERO-Plus (Long)变化+1.27个百分点（p=0.0733）。堆叠增益有限，各分支均在第10轮前达到峰值，且轨迹库的成功/失败比对检索分配无预测能力。

Conclusion: 失败轨迹在仅凭终端成败位条件下可作为有效的负向测试时证据，TraceFlow在不更新权重的前提下改善冻结VLA策略，但增益具任务选择性且随堆叠轮次饱和；轨迹库成败比不能预测检索分配策略。

Abstract: A vision-language-action (VLA) policy with a flow-matching action expert generates each action chunk (a short command sequence) by integrating a learned velocity field; once its weights are fixed, the success or failure of an earlier rollout cannot change the chunk generated now. Concurrent test-time methods give a frozen policy such an input from retrieved successes, a learned critic, a verifier, or a dynamics model, but none uses the robot's own failed rollouts as negative evidence with nothing but a terminal outcome bit. We introduce TraceFlow, a progress-aligned guidance field that turns the action densities of retrieved successful and failed rollouts into a bounded correction to a frozen flow-matching action expert, using one terminal outcome bit per rollout and no other label. Its TraceBank stores traces, time-ordered state-action records with a terminal label, starts from the target-task training traces, and later admits the deployed robot's own rollouts. On an ordered real-robot packing task the base completes 21 of 50 trials in order, TraceFlow 39, and one stacking round without any weight update 47, with wrong-sequence episodes falling from 20 to 0. In simulation the gain is selective: with per-suite selected settings, TraceFlow raises RoboMemArena Sequence from 78.92\% to 91.50\% task success and Transferring from 54.41\% to 62.00\% at stacking round 2, leaves the 26-task aggregate unchanged, lowers Counting and Occlusion by 1.12 and 1.42 points, and changes LIBERO-Plus (Long) by +1.27 points (p = 0.0733). Stacking gains are finite, every branch peaking before round ten, and the bank's success-to-failure ratio predicts no retrieval allocation.

</details>


### [123] [SkipVLA: Skipping VLA Steps with Classical Planning for Fast Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20648)
*Kaivalya Agrawal,Md Ashiqur Rahman,Raymond A. Yeh,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkipVLA 将预训练 VLA 与经典运动规划器结合：规划器负责自由空间运动，仅对抓取、放置等接触密集技能查询 VLA，并复用冻结的 VLA 视觉-语言骨干预测目标位姿，实现最高 2.5 倍加速与更低能耗，且成功率不降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型虽能将图像与语言指令直接映射为机器人动作，但推理速度慢，长时程任务因需频繁查询策略而尤为明显。现有降延迟方法（小模型蒸馏、异步动作块重叠、搭配快速底层策略）仍需在整个任务中运行学习型策略；而经典运动规划器虽能快速求解无碰撞运动，却需要显式目标且缺乏语义理解。

Method: 提出 SkipVLA 混合策略：自由空间运动交由经典运动规划器处理，仅在抓取、放置等接触密集技能处查询 VLA。方法复用 VLA 冻结的视觉-语言骨干来预测每次规划运动的目标位姿，并利用大 VLA 已学到的知识训练该预测器，不引入额外演示数据。

Result: 在仿真中于 13 个 LIBERO 任务上使用三种 VLA 进行评估，并在物理 6-DoF YAM 机械臂上测试三个拾放任务。任务完成速度最高提升 2.5 倍，能耗显著降低，同时保持相同的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 将经典运动规划与 VLA 按技能分工结合的混合策略，可在不牺牲成功率的前提下有效降低 VLA 的推理延迟与能耗，且目标位姿预测器可零额外演示地从已有 VLA 知识中习得。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a class of generalist robot policies that map camera images and language instructions directly to robot actions. While promising, these models remain slow at test time, particularly for long-horizon tasks that require many queries to the policy. Recent efforts reduce VLA latency by distilling smaller models, overlapping asynchronous action chunks, or pairing the VLA with a fast low-level policy, but still run a learned policy for the entire task. In contrast to VLA, classical motion planners quickly find collision-free motions, but require an explicit goal and have no semantic understanding of the task. In this work, we present SkipVLA, a hybrid policy that combines a pretrained VLA with a classical motion planner, using the planner for free-space motion and querying the VLA only for contact-rich skills such as grasping and placing. SkipVLA reuses the frozen vision-language backbone of the VLA to predict a target pose for each planned motion, and learns this predictor without additional demonstrations introduced into the system by using what was already learnt by the large VLA. We evaluate SkipVLA with three VLAs on 13 LIBERO tasks in simulation and three pick-and-place tasks on a physical 6-DoF YAM arm, demonstrating up to 2.5x faster task completion and significantly lower energy consumption while achieving the same task success rate.

</details>


### [124] [DexTouch-WM: Learning Action-Conditioned Tactile World Models from Human Touch for Dexterous Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20649)
*Yan Qin,Yue Chen,Wenwei Lin,Shujia Liu,Chuqiao Lyu,Kailun Su,Chenze Yu,Ping Luo,Wenbo Ding,Tianxing Chen,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DexTouch-WM，一种以动作为条件的世界模型，利用可扩展的人类触觉数据联合预测未来RGB观测与双手触觉动态，并通过人-机共享触觉阵列与动作重定向实现人机接触动力学迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富灵巧操作需密集触觉交互数据，但真实机器人上采集成本高且受限于特定本体传感器，难以规模化；希望利用廉价可扩展的人类触觉交互来补充机器人数据。

Method: 核心洞见是人机在触觉观测与动作空间兼容时共享可迁移接触动力学。方法上在人类与灵巧机器人手上部署相同传感布局的柔性压阻阵列，并将人体运动重定向到机器人动作空间，使人类交互可监督同一动力学模型。模型将预训练视频专家与轻量触觉专家耦合，采用解剖学感知的触觉token与对齐的动作条件。

Result: 在人到机器人扩展实验中，固定5小时真实机器人监督，将人类交互从0增至100小时，尽管人机任务集不相交，机器域留出的视觉、几何与接触预测均显著提升。进一步将世界模型作为策略评估的替身环境及真实机器人策略学习的合成轨迹生成器。

Conclusion: 可扩展的人类交互为学习灵巧机器人世界模型提供了互补的数据维度，触觉与动作空间对齐是实现人机接触动力学迁移的关键。

Abstract: Learning predictive models of contact-rich dexterous manipulation requires dense tactile interaction, but such data are costly to scale on real robots and remain tied to embodiment-specific sensors. We introduce DexTouch-WM, an action-conditioned world model that learns from scalable human touch to jointly predict future RGB observations and bilateral tactile dynamics. Our insight is that human and robot manipulation share transferable contact dynamics when their tactile observations and action spaces are made compatible. We deploy flexible piezoresistive arrays with a shared sensing layout on both human and dexterous robot hands, and retarget human motion into the robot action space so that human interaction can supervise the same dynamics model used for real-robot prediction. DexTouch-WM couples a pretrained video expert with a lightweight tactile expert using anatomy-aware tactile tokens and aligned action conditioning. In human-to-robot scaling experiments, we keep five hours of real-robot supervision fixed while increasing human interaction from 0 to 100 hours, and observe substantial improvements in held-out robot-domain visual, geometric, and contact prediction despite disjoint human and robot task sets. Beyond prediction, we evaluate the world models as surrogate environments for policy evaluation and as generators of synthetic trajectories for real-robot policy learning, showing that scalable human interaction provides a complementary data axis for learning dexterous robot world models.

</details>


### [125] [HIL-UMI: Bringing Human-in-the-Loop Post-Training of Vision-Language-Action Models to Universal Manipulation Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20659)
*Zimu Han,Yiming Zeng,Jiyao Zhang,Zihao Zhao,Yuanfei Wang,Yixiang Jin,Shiqi Li,Shuangben Chen,Wei Huang,Ruodai Li,Hui Shen,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HIL-UMI：一种无需真机执行的手持式（UMI）人类在环VLA后训练框架，通过策略引导的数据采集与优势估计器精炼，实现在四个真实任务上稳定超越SFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型虽有强先验，但适配具体部署仍困难。任务示教上的监督微调面临两个问题：静态数据对分布外状态覆盖不足；标准模仿目标无法区分"有进展"的行为与低价值数据。而现有交互式后训练通常需要在物理机器人上重复执行策略并进行人工干预，成本高、难以扩展。

Method: HIL-UMI为策略引导的通用操作接口（UMI）框架，实现"无机器人"的人类在环VLA后训练。手持UMI示教时，在同一观测流上查询当前策略但不执行其预测；由Energy Score比较人类动作轨迹与策略推理结果，当差异表明处于分布外区域时触发数据采集。另一反馈回路利用在线优势预测值偏低来识别关键片段，用于精炼基于进展（progress）的优势估计器；更新后的估计器指导"优势条件行为克隆"，训练数据为基础示教与新增策略数据的均衡混合。该设计保留人类在环学习的迭代性与策略感知特性，同时将数据采集与机器人部署解耦。

Result: 在四个真实任务（涵盖长时序与精细操作）上，HIL-UMI相对SFT取得一致提升，并同时受益于定向采集与优势精炼。在Clean Up Table任务上，HIL-UMI优于HG-DAgger，且单帧采集时间更低。

Conclusion: HIL-UMI为VLA后训练提供了一条可跨操作者、跨地点扩展的路径：在脱离真机部署的前提下实现策略感知的交互式数据采集与优势估计精炼，持续提升下游任务性能。

Abstract: Large-scale vision-language-action (VLA) models provide powerful priors for robot manipulation, yet adapting them to a specific deployment remains challenging. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on task-specific demonstrations provides a step toward deployment, but faces two persistent limitations: static data provide limited coverage of out-of-distribution states, and standard imitation objectives do not distinguish progressing behavior from less useful data. Interactive post-training can address these limitations, but typically requires repeated policy execution and human intervention on a physical robot. We introduce HIL-UMI, a policy-guided Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) framework for robot-free human-in-the-loop VLA post-training. During handheld UMI demonstrations, HIL-UMI queries the current policy on the same observation stream without executing its predictions. The Energy Score compares the human action trajectory with policy inference and triggers collection when their discrepancy indicates an out-of-distribution region. In a separate feedback loop, low online advantage predictions identify essential segments for refining a progress-based advantage estimator. The updated estimator then guides advantage-conditioned behavioral cloning using a balanced mixture of base demonstrations and new policy data. This design preserves the iterative and policy-aware nature of human-in-the-loop learning while decoupling data collection from robot deployment. Experiments on four real-world tasks spanning long-horizon and precise manipulation show that HIL-UMI achieves consistent improvement over SFT and benefits from both targeted collection and advantage refinement. Moreover, HIL-UMI outperforms HG-DAgger on Clean Up Table with lower per-frame collection time, suggesting a scalable path for VLA post-training across operators and locations.

</details>


### [126] [Learning Foresight without Explicit Trajectories for 3D Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20669)
*Zhongbo Zhang,Zaibin Zhang,Yifan Wang,Changbo Yan,Lijun Wang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Movement Trend Guidance，通过从观测历史学习交互演化的紧凑隐表示来为3D扩散策略提供前瞻性，无需显式规划，仅增加3.52%参数即在多个基准上显著优于DP3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D扩散策略虽能基于当前观测生成几何上可行的动作，但成功操作不仅需要知道当下可行的运动，还需预判交互的走向。现有策略大多让这种前瞻性隐含地从动作学习中涌现，缺乏显式引导。

Method: 从短观测历史中学习交互演化的紧凑潜在表示；训练时用稀疏的未来夹爪状态监督该表示；推理时仅保留该潜在作为面向未来的条件，与当前观测一起使用。该潜在为动作生成提供全局条件，并额外在UNet瓶颈处引入门控FiLM分支。方法保留了原始密集动作与滚动时域公式，相对DP3仅增加3.52%参数。

Result: 在RoboTwin2.0、LIBERO-40和DexArt上持续优于DP3：RoboTwin2.0 50任务混合训练中62.8% vs 56.1%；LIBERO-40上71.93% vs 37.08%；五个真实机器人任务上72.0% vs 49.0%。

Conclusion: 扩散策略能从"知道交互走向向何处发展"中显著获益，而无需被明确告知具体移动位置，表明前瞻性引导是一种简单有效的增强手段。

Abstract: 3D diffusion policies are strong at generating geometrically grounded actions from current observations, but successful manipulation requires not only knowing what motion is feasible now, but also anticipating where the interaction is heading. Existing policies largely leave such foresight to emerge implicitly from action learning. We introduce Movement Trend Guidance, a simple but effective way to provide this foresight without introducing an explicit plan. From a short observation history, the policy learns a compact latent representation of interaction evolution. During training, sparse future gripper states supervise this representation; at inference, only the latent is retained as future-oriented conditioning alongside the current observation. The latent provides global conditioning for action generation, while an additional gated FiLM branch is used only at the UNet bottleneck. Despite adding only 3.52% more parameters to DP3, our method preserves the original dense-action and receding-horizon formulation and consistently improves upon DP3 across RoboTwin2.0, LIBERO-40, and DexArt. It reaches 62.8% vs. 56.1% in 50-task RoboTwin2.0 mixed training, 71.93% vs. 37.08% on LIBERO-40, and 72.0% vs. 49.0% on five real-robot tasks. These results show that a diffusion policy can benefit substantially from knowing where an interaction is heading, without being told exactly where to move.

</details>


### [127] [MAGNETAR: Multipath-Guided Spatial Posteriors for Transmitter Pose Inference in the Upper Mid-Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20670)
*Haozhe Lei,Ruibin Chen,Yuhan Jiang,Ali Rasteh,Aditya Dhananjay,Sundeep Rangan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAGNETAR从单次异步RF多径快照推断发射机的平面位置与朝向联合后验分布，采用按候选朝向条件化的共享2D U-Net在离散位置-朝向网格上联合归一化，联合网格后验优于参数化方法且融合联合后验优于融合位置边缘分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人定位无线电发射机时，仅给出点估计不足：在杂乱房间中，单次测量常与多个发射机位置一致；且由于中高频段天线具有方向性，还与多个朝向一致。因此需要推断位置与朝向的联合不确定性。

Method: MAGNETAR利用房间布局与接收机位姿，从单次异步射频多径快照（由到达角和信噪比估计表示）推断平面位置与朝向的联合后验。作者比较了五种神经打分器，最终采用共享2D U-Net，以每个候选朝向为条件，在离散化的位置-朝向网格上联合归一化分数。训练使用真机到仿真校准的10 GHz仿真数据以及少量实测数据子集。

Result: 基于网格的联合后验在留出仿真数据上优于参数化后验；朝向条件化打分器向机器人实测数据的迁移效果最佳；融合联合后验优于融合仅位置边缘分布。

Conclusion: 推断发射机的位置-朝向联合后验比点估计或位置边缘分布更有效，朝向条件化的U-Net打分与联合后验融合可提升机器人RF定位的鲁棒性与准确性。

Abstract: Robots that localize a radio transmitter need more than a point estimate: in cluttered rooms, one measurement is often consistent with several transmitter locations and, because upper-mid-band antennas are directional, several headings. We present MAGNETAR, which infers a joint posterior over planar transmitter position and heading from a single asynchronous radio-frequency (RF) multipath snapshot, represented by angle-of-arrival and signal-to-noise-ratio estimates, given the room layout and receiver pose. Among our five neural scorers, MAGNETAR adopts a shared 2D U-Net conditioned on each candidate heading, jointly normalizing scores over a discretized position-heading grid. Training uses real-to-sim-calibrated 10 GHz simulations and a small measured subset. Grid-based joint posteriors outperform parametric ones on held-out simulations, the heading-conditioned scorer transfers best to robotic measurements, and fusing joint posteriors improves on fusing position-only marginals.

</details>


### [128] [Towards Scaling Marine Perception with Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20680)
*Haoyu Ma,Onur Bagoren,Anja Sheppard,Elias Fandi,Ashrith Edukulla,Tanner Aslan,Natasha Sieh,Jingyu Song,Katherine A. Skinner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文扩展了基于IsaacSim的水下感知仿真器OceanSim，新增合成数据生成（SDG）流水线，可生成大规模、自动标注、照片级真实感的水下数据集，并在真实海胆检测任务上评估了仿真到现实（sim-to-real）的迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境中可扩展的机器学习受限于缺乏标注的真实世界训练数据，此类数据采集成本高、耗时长，难以大规模收集与整理。仿真数据有望弥合这一数据鸿沟，支撑各类水下感知学习任务。

Method: 在IsaacSim-based的OceanSim水下感知仿真器基础上，扩展一套合成数据生成流水线；该流水线支持用户生成大规模、自动标注、照片级真实的合成数据集，并可配置场景外观、结构与传感器设置。随后在真实海胆检测任务上进行评估，研究不同形式的合成场景变化对sim-to-real性能的影响。

Result: 在真实海胆检测任务上验证了流水线的有效性，并系统分析了不同合成场景变化对sim-to-real迁移性能的影响，同时总结了实验结果、当前流水线的主要局限。

Conclusion: 本文提出了面向水下感知的合成数据生成流水线，验证了其在真实检测任务中的可用性，并指出了未来改进方向，包括提升水下渲染保真度、增强场景多样性以及完善sim-to-real泛化能力的评估方法。开源代码已发布。

Abstract: Scalable machine learning in challenging underwater environments is strongly limited by the lack of labeled real-world training data. This data is often expensive and laborious to gather, making large-scale real-world data challenging to gather and curate. However, simulated data can help close the gap, enabling many learning-based tasks for underwater perception. In this work, we extend OceanSim, an IsaacSim-based underwater perception simulator, with a Synthetic Data Generation (SDG) pipeline for training models to be used in underwater scenarios. The proposed pipeline enables users to generate large, automatically labeled, photorealistic datasets with configurable scene appearance, structure, and sensor settings. We evaluate the pipeline on a real-world sea urchin detection task and study how different forms of synthetic scene variation affect sim-to-real performance. Based on these experiments, we discuss findings on our results, main limitations of the current pipeline and identify future directions for improving underwater rendering fidelity, scene diversity, and the evaluation of sim-to-real generalization. The open-source code can be found at https://github.com/umfieldrobotics/OceanSim.

</details>


### [129] [Custom PX4 firmware for autonomous hybrid aerial-marine missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20691)
*Andrea Capuozzo,Fabio Ruggiero,Vincenzo Lippiello*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文为水空两栖无人机提出PX4固件扩展，实现飞行与水面航行的统一任务规划与自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水环境测绘与监测需要能够在同一任务中兼顾飞行与水面航行的混合型无人机，而现有PX4固件缺乏对水面航行模式及两类航点统一调度的支持。

Method: 在PX4固件上进行扩展开发，引入手动与自主两种水面航行模式，并将其集成到标准PX4任务执行流程与QGroundControl地面站界面中；通过区分空中与水面航点，实现混合任务的统一规划与执行，同时保留原有飞行功能与安全机制。

Result: 仿真案例验证了该实现的有效性，在平静与波浪条件下均表现出稳定的水面航行性能。

Conclusion: 该固件扩展可在不破坏原有飞行能力与安全机制的前提下，支持水空两栖无人机的一体化任务规划与执行，为水环境测绘与监测提供可行平台方案。

Abstract: Mapping and monitoring aquatic environments can benefit from hybrid aerial-amphibious drones able to combine flight and water-surface navigation within the same mission. This paper presents a PX4 firmware extension for such platforms, introducing manual and autonomous marine navigation modes integrated with the standard PX4 mission pipeline and QGroundControl interface. The proposed framework preserves existing flight functionalities and safety mechanisms while enabling unified planning and execution of hybrid aerial-marine missions with differentiated aerial and marine waypoints. Simulated case studies validate the implementation and demonstrate stable surface navigation under calm and wavy conditions.

</details>


### [130] [HOPHY: A Hierarchical Hypergraph Representation for Off-Road Path and Mission Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20694)
*Pranay Meshram,Charuvahan Adhivarahan,Prithvi Poddar,Ehsan Tarkesh Esfahani,Chen Wang,Souma Chowdhury,Karthik Dantu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HOPHY：一种可复用的分层越野地形表示，基于几何连通语义区域、保连通粗区域与类型化超边，实现千米级地图上高成功率、低延迟的重复路径与任务规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害响应、搜救与战术UGV等任务级自主需要在 terrain、agent类型与目标频繁变化时反复进行路径与任务规划。像素网格搜索在千米尺度重复查询中代价高昂，而语义抽象方法必须在地形条件变化时仍保持代价有效性与连通性。

Method: 提出 HOPHY（Hierarchical Off-Road Planning using Hypergraphs）：将地图尺度地形组织为几何连通语义区域（GSNodes）、保连通的关键区域（Coarse Regions），以及编码地形、agent与天气上下文的类型化超边；通过超边交集选取受影响区域与关联边进行状态更新，无需重建整个层级结构。

Result: 在千米级真实越野地图上，HOPHY达到100%规划成功率，与oracle（像素A*）的中位代价偏差小于0.01%，查询与重规划延迟显著低于像素基线与抽象基线。用于MRTA问题时，总计算量较像素A*降低79倍、较最快抽象基线降低7.2倍，mission makespan与像素A*相当。在实体Clearpath Jackal上成功执行1.5公里、八任务的混合地表户外任务，并完成阻塞触发的重规划路线。

Conclusion: HOPHY以分层超图表示兼顾像素级代价精度与抽象级查询效率，支持增量式状态更新与多机器人任务分配，并具备真实平台上的任务级自主可行性。

Abstract: Mission-level autonomy for disaster response, search and rescue, and tactical UGV operations requires repeated path and mission planning as terrain conditions, agent types, and objectives change. Pixel-grid search is costly for repeated kilometer-scale queries, while semantic abstractions must maintain valid costs and connectivity as conditions change. We present HOPHY (Hierarchical Off-Road Planning using Hypergraphs), a reusable hierarchical terrain representation that organizes map-scale terrain into geometrically connected semantic regions (GSNodes), connectivity-preserving critical regions (Coarse Regions), and typed hyperedges for terrain, agent, and weather context. Hyperedge intersections select affected regions and incident edges for state updates without rebuilding the hierarchy. Across real off-road maps spanning kilometer-scale areas, HOPHY achieves 100% planning success and less than 0.01% median cost deviation from the oracle (pixel A*), with substantially lower query and replanning latency than the evaluated pixel and abstraction baselines. Applied to a multi-robot task-allocation (MRTA) problem, these gains reduce total computation by 79x over pixel A* and 7.2x over the fastest abstraction baseline, with mission makespan comparable to pixel A*. Finally, we demonstrate HOPHY on a physical Clearpath Jackal that successfully executes a 1.5-km, eight-task mission across mixed-surface outdoor terrain and a blockage-triggered replanned route.

</details>


### [131] [MoWAM: Explicit Future Motion Prediction for Efficient World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20709)
*Jiayu Wang,Bin Zhu,Yue Yu,Jingjing Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 MoWAM，用显式的未来运动预测替代未来视频生成，在保留未来动力学显式表征的同时去除推理期视频生成开销，提升分布外鲁棒性并支持高效的推理期扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World Action Models（WAMs）通过引入未来动力学来改进机器人策略学习，但在推理阶段显式生成未来视频会带来巨大的计算开销；若直接移除未来生成虽可提升效率，却使未来动力学仅隐式编码于观测特征中，在分布迁移下鲁棒性受限。因此需要一个既能保留未来显式表征、又避免高额推理成本的高效 WAM。

Method: 提出 MoWAM，以显式的未来运动预测取代未来视频生成。它不重建完整未来场景，而是将结构化机器人运动建模为未来的紧凑抽象，刻画机器人在当前场景与交互约束下的预期演化。采用 Mixture-of-Transformer 架构，在训练中学习未来视觉动力学，同时联合预测运动与动作，使推理期可完全移除视频生成，仍保留对未来的显式表示。紧凑的运动表示还支持高效的推理期扩展：采样多组运动—动作候选，并用运动感知的任务进度验证器进行筛选。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus 及真实世界操作任务上的实验表明，MoWAM 取得了较强的分布内性能、更优的分布外鲁棒性，以及高于代表性 WAM 基线的真实世界平均成功率。此外，随着探索候选数量增加，性能持续提升，说明显式未来运动为推理期扩展提供了有效且高效的基础。

Conclusion: 以紧凑的显式未来运动预测替代未来视频生成，是一种高效且有效的 WAM 设计范式：既降低了推理开销，又通过显式未来表征增强了鲁棒性，并天然支持推理期候选采样与验证式扩展。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) improve robot policy learning by incorporating future dynamics, yet explicitly generating future videos at inference introduces substantial computational overhead. Removing future generation improves efficiency, but leaves future dynamics only implicitly encoded in observation features, which can limit robustness under distribution shifts. We propose MoWAM, an efficient WAM that replaces future video generation with explicit future motion prediction. Instead of reconstructing the complete future scene, MoWAM models structured robot motion as a compact abstraction of the future, capturing how the robot is expected to evolve under the current scene and interaction constraints. A Mixture-of-Transformer architecture learns future visual dynamics during training while jointly predicting motion and action, allowing video generation to be removed entirely at inference while retaining an explicit representation of the future. The compact motion representation further enables efficient inference-time scaling by sampling multiple candidates of motion and action pairs and selecting among them with a motion-aware task-progress verifier. Experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that MoWAM achieves strong in-distribution performance, improved out-of-distribution robustness, and higher average real-world success than representative WAM baselines. In addition, performance improves as more candidates are explored, demonstrating that explicit future motion provides an effective and efficient basis for inference-time scaling.

</details>


### [132] [Underwater Visual Target Tracking with Target-Specific Depth Estimation and Adaptive Model-Fusion Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20731)
*Yuheng Zhou,Haiyang Cheng,Yanqi Feng,Pangkit Fong,Mei Xuan Lee,Marcus Gee,Chongrong Fang,Jianping He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于自主水下机器人的双目视觉伺服框架，通过目标深度掩码与卡尔曼滤波获取稳定3D相对状态，并将偏航调节与平移控制解耦，采用自适应模型融合MPC实现实时水下目标跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视觉的水下目标跟踪面临两大挑战：深度测量不可靠，以及目标运动模式未知。现有框架难以在实时性与跟踪精度之间取得良好平衡。

Method: 感知端：结合颜色、视差和时间线索构建目标深度掩码以筛选可靠目标像素，进行目标特定深度提取，并分别对深度测量与检测图像中心进行卡尔曼滤波，得到稳定的3D相对状态。控制端：将偏航调节与平移控制解耦，避免计算昂贵的耦合多自由度优化；平移控制器采用自适应模型融合预测控制，融合恒速与零速目标模型，依据历史预测误差更新模型权重，并在执行器、跟随距离和视场约束下计算平移指令。

Result: 通过仿真与真实世界实验验证了框架的有效性，并表明其性能优于现有框架。

Conclusion: 该双目视觉伺服框架在深度测量不可靠与目标运动未知条件下，能够实现鲁棒且实时的AUV水下目标跟踪。

Abstract: Vision-based underwater target tracking is challenged by unreliable depth measurements and unknown target motion. This paper proposes a stereo visual-servoing framework for an autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV). For perception, the framework derives a stable 3D relative state from stereo images through target-specific depth extraction and Kalman filtering. It constructs a target-depth mask from color, disparity, and temporal cues to select reliable target pixels, and then filters the resulting depth measurement and detected image center separately. For control, the framework decouples yaw regulation from translational control, avoiding computationally expensive coupled multi-DOF optimization and enabling real-time translational MPC. The translational controller employs adaptive model-fusion predictive control, combining constant-velocity and zero-velocity target models to accommodate different target-motion patterns. It updates the model weights using historical prediction errors and computes translational commands subject to actuation, following-distance, and field-of-view constraints. Through simulations and real-world experiments, we validate the effectiveness of the proposed framework and show it has better performance than existing frameworks.

</details>


### [133] [MILER: Semantic Mid-Level Representation for Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning in Unstructured Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20747)
*Thomas Steinecker,Denis Trescher,Alexander Bienemann,Thorsten Luettel,Mirko Maehlisch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MILER——一个具备零样本sim-to-real迁移能力的端到端策略框架，用于非结构化环境下的自动驾驶。离线阶段在自定义语义中层表征模拟器中用强化学习训练策略，部署时将相机与LiDAR经BEVFusion转为与模拟器一致的鸟瞰图表征，并通过轨迹对齐策略实现感知与控制的零样本迁移。在两辆车、3.0 km测试道上无人工干预行驶17.3 km验证有效性，全栈软件运行于Jetson AGX Orin。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习虽具超越人类性能与自学习策略的潜力，但在真实世界自动驾驶中应用稀少，尤其非结构化环境，主要瓶颈在于该类环境的sim-to-real迁移困难。

Method: 提出MILER端到端策略框架。离线训练：采用自定义语义中层表征（MLR）模拟器，以强化学习训练策略网络，其控制输出直接作用于自行车模型。实车部署：相机与LiDAR数据经BEVFusion生成与MLR模拟器一致的语义鸟瞰图表征；策略网络生成的动作用轨迹对齐策略而非直接施加于实车，从而实现感知与控制的零样本sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 在包含多种障碍物、发卡弯、最高33.6 km/h速度及越野路段的多样化测试道上广泛评估。两辆不同车辆在3.0 km测试道上无人工干预共行驶17.3 km，验证了方法有效性。整个软件栈运行于Jetson AGX Orin。

Conclusion: MILER通过语义中层表征模拟器与轨迹对齐策略，实现了非结构化环境下感知与控制的零样本sim-to-real迁移，并在实车长距离自主驾驶中得以验证，为强化学习在真实自动驾驶中的落地提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning constitutes a promising approach owing to its potential for superhuman performance and self-learned policies. However, its application to real-world autonomous driving remains scarce, particularly in unstructured environments, because of the challenges associated with sim-to-real transfer for unstructured environments. In this work, we present MILER, an end-to-end policy framework with zero-shot sim-to-real transfer. During offline training, we employ a custom semantic mid-level representation (MLR) simulator and train the policy network using reinforcement learning, with its control outputs applied directly to a bicycle model. During deployment on the real vehicle, camera and LiDAR data are processed by BEVFusion to generate a semantic bird's-eye-view representation consistent with that of the MLR simulator. The actions generated by the policy network are not applied directly to the real vehicle. Instead, we employ a trajectory-alignment strategy that enables zero-shot sim-to-real transfer of both perception and control. We extensively evaluate the proposed framework on a diverse test track comprising numerous challenges, including various obstacles, hairpin curves, velocities of up to 33.6 km/h, and off-road sections. In total, we drove 17.3 km with two different vehicles on a 3.0 km test track without human intervention, thereby demonstrating the effectiveness of our approach. Furthermore, the entire software stack runs on a Jetson AGX Orin.

</details>


### [134] [OPTED: On-Policy Fine-Tuning for End-to-End Driving using a Render-Free Teacher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20756)
*Damiano Da Col,Maximilian Igl,Peter Karkus,Kashyap Chitta,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone,Konrad Schindler,Christos Sakaridis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出OPTED：将强化学习从端到端驾驶策略的后训练中解耦——先用RL在向量化输入（HD地图与边界框）上训练特权教师模型，再由该教师在闭环后训练中监督预训练的学生模型。在AlpaSim中基于真实驾驶日志的3DGS神经重建对TransFuser与VaVAM微调，驾驶分数分别提升1.6倍与9.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单纯扩大预训练数据的收益递减，后训练在自动驾驶等物理AI领域日益重要。端到端驾驶策略通过行为克隆在人类示范上以开环方式预训练，但闭环部署中的误差累积会使车辆偏离训练分布，增加安全关键事故风险。闭环后训练可缓解该风险，但基于传感器的策略需依赖昂贵的仿真。

Method: OPTED（on-policy fine-tuning for end-to-end driving）将强化学习与端到端策略的后训练解耦：用RL在向量化输入（HD地图与边界框）上训练特权教师模型；该教师在闭环后训练中为预训练的学生模型提供监督。方法应用于两个相机模型TransFuser与VaVAM，在AlpaSim中利用真实驾驶日志的神经重建（3DGS）进行微调。

Result: 驾驶分数分别提升1.6倍（TransFuser）与9.5倍（VaVAM）。对照实验表明，OPTED达到与直接RL后训练相当的闭环性能，但仿真交互次数减少约三个数量级，同时更接近人类先验。

Conclusion: OPTED通过特权教师蒸馏实现了高效、低仿真成本的闭环后训练，在显著提升相机端到端驾驶策略性能的同时保持与人类示范的接近性，为传感器型策略的闭环微调提供了实用范式。

Abstract: As scaling pre-training data alone yields diminishing returns, post-training is becoming increasingly important across physical AI domains such as autonomous driving. End-to-end driving policies are pre-trained in open loop with behavior cloning on human demonstrations. However, compounding errors during closed-loop deployment can take the vehicle outside the training data distribution, increasing the risk of safety-critical incidents. Closed-loop post-training can mitigate this risk but requires costly simulation for sensor-based policies. We propose OPTED (on-policy fine-tuning for end-to-end driving) which decouples reinforcement learning from the post-training of the end-to-end policy: a privileged teacher is trained using RL on vectorized inputs (HD-map and bounding boxes). This teacher then provides supervision to the pre-trained student during closed-loop post-training. We apply OPTED to two camera-based models, TransFuser and VaVAM, and fine-tune them in AlpaSim, using neural reconstructions (3DGS) of real driving logs. Driving scores increase by factors of 1.6$\times$ and 9.5$\times$, respectively. In controlled experiments OPTED matches closed-loop performance with approximately three orders of magnitude fewer simulator interactions than direct RL post-training, while staying closer to the human prior. Project page: https://01dami23.github.io/opted/

</details>


### [135] [Agile-WAM: An Agile Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20761)
*Hanchu Zhou,Brendan Lynch,Raman Goyal,Dechen Gao,Begum Kasap,Boqi Zhao,Junshan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种敏捷的触觉世界动作模型（WAM），通过视觉-触觉共享潜空间与流匹配实现动作与未来多模态潜表示联合生成，并引入多时域多模态预测，在保持低推理延迟的同时显著提升接触丰富操作任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉世界动作模型依赖大规模预训练生成式骨干网络来建模接触丰富的物理动力学，导致推理效率低、部署灵活性差。作者希望在不牺牲接触动力学建模能力的前提下，实现敏捷、可高频控制的触觉WAM。

Method: 提出视觉-触觉共享潜空间编码，作为从视觉-触觉观测直接到动作的流匹配过程起点，联合生成动作块潜表示与未来视觉/触觉潜表示。核心设计为多时域多模态预测：利用视觉与触觉信号演化时间尺度不同的特性，对视觉潜表示在较大时间偏移处施加监督，而对触觉潜表示预测下一帧，以捕捉细粒度接触动力学。

Result: 在9个仿真与5个真实世界接触丰富操作任务上表现强劲且稳健，成功率超过最强基线，同时保持低推理延迟。五个真实世界实验中总体成功率相对提升29.4%，推理延迟仅为11.9 ms。

Conclusion: 多模态世界动作模型可通过敏捷架构实现，适用于精确且高频率的机器人控制，兼顾接触动力学建模与推理效率。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) advance beyond conventional visuomotor policies by jointly predicting future world states and robot actions, enabling the policy to learn physical dynamics that support effective control. However, recent tactile WAMs often rely on large-scale pretrained generative backbones to capture contact-rich physical dynamics, which limit their inference efficiency and flexible deployment. In this paper, we present \ABBR{}, an agile tactile World Action Model for contact-rich robot control. \ABBR{} encodes visual and tactile observations into a shared latent that serves as the source of a direct vision-tactile-to-action flow-matching process, which can jointly generate latent representations of action chunks and future visual/tactile latents. A key observation is that vision and tactile signals evolve at inherently different timescales: adjacent visual frames are often highly similar, whereas tactile signals can change abruptly upon contact. We therefore introduce multi-horizon multimodal prediction in \ABBR{}, which provides supervision for visual latent at a larger temporal offset while predicting the tactile latent in the next frame to capture fine-grained contact dynamics. Across nine simulated and five real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, \ABBR{} demonstrates strong and robust performance, outperforming the strongest baseline in success rate while maintaining low inference latency. In particular, in five real-world experiments, \ABBR{} yields a relative gain of $\textbf{29.4\%}$ in overall success rates while achieving inference latency of $\textbf{11.9 ms}$. These results demonstrate that multimodal WAM can be achieved with an agile architecture suitable for precise and high-frequency robot control. More details are available on our project page: https://hanchuzhou.github.io/TARO_project_page/.

</details>


### [136] [GeoAAC: Geometry-Based Adaptive Action Chunking from Denoising Trajectories in VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20776)
*Xin Chen,Sen Chen,Yujuan Ding,Jian Liu,Guoqing Wang,Wei Ye,Heng Tao Shen,Yi Bin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GeoAAC，一种基于几何信息的自适应动作分块方法，依据Flow Matching去噪轨迹的几何特性动态调整动作视界，无需额外训练即可提升VLA策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略普遍采用固定动作视界（Fixed Action Horizon），但任务各阶段对动作连续性、控制精度和闭环反馈的需求不同，固定视界无法适应变化的控制需求。

Method: 观察到Flow Matching去噪轨迹中动作前缀的几何变化与预测不确定性正相关，据此利用前缀级几何构建视界级几何轮廓（horizon-wise geometric profile），在单次生成中自适应确定动作视界，无需额外训练。

Result: 在GR00T N1.5与π0.5上，于LIBERO、LIBERO-Pro、RoboCasa365及真实世界操控任务实验，一致优于固定视界基线与现有自适应方法；仿真提升最高达8.7个百分点，真实世界平均成功率从53.3%提升至74.4%。

Conclusion: GeoAAC证明Flow Matching去噪轨迹几何可作为预测可靠性的过程级信号，实现高效、免训练的自适应动作分块，有效适配不同任务阶段的控制需求。

Abstract: Action chunking is widely used for action generation and execution in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies, yet existing approaches commonly use a fixed action horizon. During a rollout, different task stages may require different levels of action continuity, control precision, and closed-loop feedback, making a fixed horizon unable to accommodate changing control requirements. We propose \textbf{GeoAAC}, a geometry-based adaptive action chunking method for flow-based VLA policies that adjusts the action horizon according to the reliability of the current action prediction. We show that the geometry of Flow Matching denoising trajectories provides process-level information for characterizing prediction reliability, with geometric variation across action prefixes remaining positively correlated with predictive uncertainty. GeoAAC uses this prefix-wise geometry to construct a horizon-wise geometric profile and adaptively determine the action horizon from a single generation without additional training. Experiments with GR00T N1.5 and π0.5 on LIBERO, LIBERO-Pro, RoboCasa365, and real-world manipulation tasks show consistent improvements over fixed-action-horizon baselines and existing adaptive methods, including up to 8.7 percentage points in simulation and an increase in average real-world success rate from 53.3\% to 74.4\%.

</details>


### [137] [StageGuard: Learning Stage Transitions for Long-Horizon Robot Tasks via Agentic Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20791)
*Jinbang Huang,Yuanzhao Hu,Zhiyuan Li,Ran Qi,Yixin Xiao,Yangzheng Wu,Tengyue Ba,Zhanguang Zhang,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 StageGuard，一种 agentic 蒸馏框架，用于分层机器人规划中准确且高效的阶段切换决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层规划框架组合多种机器人控制策略执行长时域任务，需判定何时终止当前技能并切换至下一子任务。现有方法依赖预先设计的完成信号检查器，实际执行中难以获取；大规模 VLM 虽有强推理能力，但其决策边界与任务完成准则不一致，且云端部署与冗长推理带来显著延迟，难以实时监控。

Method: StageGuard 结合教师模型推理与示范轨迹，生成关于子任务完成与策略切换的结构化解释；轻量级学生 VLM 据此生成紧凑的自解释，并用于监督微调。

Result: 在两个基准的轨迹上评估阶段切换预测，并通过集成到 BEHAVIOR-1K 的分层机器人控制中评估闭环任务成功率，同时在真实机器人上进一步验证。结果显示阶段切换预测显著提升，并支持高效的在线监控。

Conclusion: StageGuard 通过 agentic 蒸馏实现准确、高效的阶段切换决策，兼顾性能与实时性，并在仿真基准与真实机器人上得到验证。

Abstract: Hierarchical planning frameworks combine skills from multiple robot control policies for long-horizon task execution, where determining when to terminate the current skill and advance to the next subtask is essential. Existing approaches often rely on pre-designed completion signal checkers that are hard to obtain in real-world execution. Large-scale vision-language models (VLMs) offer strong reasoning capabilities, but their decision boundaries are not inherently aligned with task completion criteria, while cloud deployment and lengthy reasoning introduce substantial latency, limiting real-time monitoring. We propose StageGuard, an agentic distillation framework for accurate and efficient stage-transition decisions. StageGuard combines teacher-model reasoning with demonstration trajectories to generate structured explanations of subtask completion and policy switching. A lightweight student VLM uses these explanations to generate compact self-explanations, which are used for supervised fine-tuning. We evaluate stage-transition prediction on trajectories from two benchmarks and assess closed-loop task success through integration into hierarchical robot control on BEHAVIOR-1K, with further validation on real robots. Results show substantial improvements in stage-transition prediction while supporting efficient online monitoring.

</details>


### [138] [Workspace Models: Lightweight Robotic Memory via Saliency-Driven Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20820)
*Nitish Dashora,Douglas Chen,Idan Shenfeld,John Marangola,Pulkit Agrawal,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"workspace token"：将昂贵的VLM查询移到训练阶段，蒸馏出一个轻量隐式记忆表示，部署时可直接替代观测，无需在环VLM推理即可解决记忆密集型操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序机器人操作需要记忆历史事件与动作。但直接以完整历史为条件会导致策略学习到虚假相关并降低性能；现有做法多依赖在环（in-the-loop）VLM查询压缩历史信息，计算开销大。

Method: 训练时而非部署时进行VLM计算：(1) 用VLM识别完成任务所需的当前与历史信息；(2) 通过集合重建解码器损失（set-reconstruction decoder loss）将这些信息蒸馏进workspace token。部署时该token作为观测的即插即用替代品。

Result: 在仿真与真机硬件上均验证，workspace token可替代观测供策略使用，无需在环VLM推理即可完成记忆密集型任务；且相比在环VLM方案更轻量，策略性能反而更好。

Conclusion: 将VLM的密集计算前移到训练阶段、以轻量隐式记忆替代在环推理，是解决机器人操作长时记忆问题的有效且更优路径。

Abstract: Complex robotic manipulation tasks frequently require a long-term memory of past events and actions. As conditioning on full histories renders policies prone to spurious correlations and degrades performance, many approaches to policy memory involve compressing historical information through expensive VLM queries in-the-loop to process only task-salient information. In this paper, we propose an alternative approach in which computationally intensive VLM queries are made during train-time to learn a lightweight latent memory that can be efficiently queried at deployment time. Our representation, which we call the \textbf{workspace token}, is trained by (1) using a VLM to identify current and historical information necessary for completing a task, then (2) distilling these into the workspace token using a set-reconstruction decoder loss. In both simulation and hardware, we show that the workspace token can be used as a drop-in replacement for observations during deployment, enabling policies to solve memory-intensive tasks without the need for VLM reasoning in-the-loop. Interestingly, we found that workspace tokens are not only more lightweight but also lead to better policy performance.

</details>


### [139] [Coding Agents with an Obstacle-Aware Harness for Safe Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20822)
*Bingxin Xu,Yuzhang Shang,Zhen Dong,Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次评估编码智能体在安全约束下的机器人操作能力，发现其忽略避障约束，并提出 SafeHarness 双模块框架以显式强化安全约束，显著提升任务成功率与避障率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体（由语言模型编写机器人控制器程序）已成为无需机器人专用训练即可操作机器人的范式，但其安全性尚未被研究。作者希望在"操作目标 + 不可触碰障碍物"的安全约束下检验该范式是否安全。

Method: 将操作分解为"路径阶段"与"接触阶段"以定位失败根源，并据此提出 SafeHarness：包含两个障碍感知模块——① 障碍感知路径规划（将物体以包围盒表征，在其上绘制候选路径为路径点序列，使智能体可提前规划、验证、必要时重规划再执行）；② 障碍感知接触执行（选择使接触本身避开障碍的接触位置）。

Result: SafeHarness 取得 71.9% 任务成功率与 87.5% 避障率，分别超过此前 SOTA 6.5% 与 27.0%，为无该框架同一智能体的 2.3 倍与 1.5 倍。

Conclusion: 失败不源于感知或指令（提示已禁止触碰、推理中也提及障碍），而源于规划层面安全约束未成为优先级：路径阶段缺乏"可通行路径"概念与不可行时重规划能力，接触阶段未意识到接触执行同样受该约束限制。显式引入障碍感知的规划与接触模块可有效弥补该缺陷。

Abstract: Coding agents have emerged as a promising paradigm for robot manipulation: a language model writes the robot controller as a program, and agents built in this way now operate robots without robot-specific training.Whether this paradigm is also safe, however, has not been asked. We evaluate coding agent under a safety constraint, where each task pairs a manipulation goal with an obstacle the robot must not touch. The agent pursues the goal but collides with the obstacle in most cases, treating task completion as its sole objective while neglecting safety. The agent reasons about the obstacle in its traces, and the prompt already forbids touching it, so neither perception nor instruction is at fault; the fault lies in the planning, where the stated constraint never becomes a priority. By decomposing manipulation into a route phase and a contact-rich moment, we locate the source of the failure. Along the route, the model cannot prioritize the safety constraint, having no notion of a clearing route and none of replanning once a chosen route becomes infeasible. At the contact, it is unaware that contact execution is bounded by the same constraint. To close this gap, we present SafeHarness, which equips the model with two obstacle-aware harnesses that enable it to prioritize the safety constraint. Obstacle-aware route planning grounds the objects as bounding boxes and draws candidate routes over them as sequences of waypoints. The agent then plans a route in advance, verifies it, replans when necessary, and only then executes it. Obstacle-aware contact execution instead selects the contact position so that the contact itself avoids the obstacle. SafeHarness attains 71.9% task success and 87.5% collision avoidance, surpassing the previous SOTA by 6.5% and 27.0%, respectively. These results are $2.3\times$ and $1.5\times$ those of the same agent without harnesses.

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [Identifying Damage Pathways Linking Sequence Composition to Storage Failure in DNA Data Storage via High-Dimensional Mediation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19822)
*Jingyi Li,Huaming Wu,Haixiang Zhang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出高维半参数生存中介分析框架，将GC含量作为暴露、损伤谱作为中介、存储失效作为结局，用深度神经网络拟合非线性效应，结合乘积系数筛选、SCAD惩罚与联合显著性检验进行中介选择，在电化学合成DNA老化实验中发现14个显著中介（均为单碱基缺失），揭示缺失型损伤是连接序列组成与存储可靠性下降的主要通路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNA数据存储具有极高信息密度与长期耐久性，但其可靠性受合成阶段引入、存储阶段累积的序列依赖型错误限制。目前尚不清楚序列组成如何通过特定分子损伤组分与存储失效相关联，缺乏机制导向的统计工具。

Method: 构建高维半参数生存中介框架：以GC含量为暴露，以高维基线损伤谱（按三核苷酸上下文与错误类型分层的每条读段损伤计数向量）为中介，以存储质量失效为结局。中介模型与生存模型中的非线性协变量效应用深度神经网络逼近。提出三步流程：乘积系数筛选、SCAD惩罚估计、联合显著性检验，用于中介变量选择与推断。

Result: 应用于电化学合成DNA的老化实验，方法识别出14个显著中介，全部对应单碱基缺失；估计的中介效应集中于以C结尾的三核苷酸上下文。

Conclusion: 缺失型损伤是连接序列组成与档案可靠性下降的主要通路，并提示可用于未来优化与错误控制策略的候选序列特征。该框架为理解和提升DNA数据存储可靠性提供了机制导向的统计方法。

Abstract: DNA data storage offers extraordinary information density and long-term durability, but its reliability is limited by sequence-dependent errors introduced during synthesis and accumulated during storage. It remains unclear how sequence composition is associated with storage failure through specific molecular damage components. We develop a high-dimensional semiparametric mediation framework for survival outcomes. GC content is treated as the exposure, a high-dimensional baseline damage spectrum (a vector of per-read damage counts stratified by trinucleotide context and error type) as the mediator, and storage-quality failure as the outcome. Nonlinear covariate effects in both the mediator and survival models are approximated using deep neural networks. A three-step procedure combining product-of-coefficients screening, Smoothly Clipped Absolute Deviation (SCAD) penalized estimation, and joint significance testing is developed for mediator selection and inference. Applied to an aging experiment on electrochemically synthesized DNA, the method identifies 14 significant mediators, all corresponding to single-base deletions, with estimated mediated effects concentrated in trinucleotide contexts ending in C. These results reveal deletion-type damage as a major pathway linking sequence composition to reduced archival reliability and suggest candidate sequence features for future optimization and error-control strategies. The proposed framework thus offers a mechanism-oriented statistical approach for understanding and improving the reliability of DNA data storage.

</details>


### [141] [Comparing statistical learning models in wastewater-based epidemiology: An application to norovirus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20038)
*Caelan McNamara,Fion Tan,Ella White,Elizaveta Semenova,Marta Blangiardo*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该研究以英格兰诺如病毒为案例，利用3,232份污水样本，比较了INLA-SPDE贝叶斯时空模型与Lasso、GAM、贝叶斯GAM、XGBoost、随机森林五种建模方法在污水流行病学（WBE）时空预测中的性能。随机森林综合表现最佳，INLA-SPDE在不确定性量化上表现良好，揭示了预测精度、不确定性量化与计算效率之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 污水流行病学（WBE）已成为传染病监测的重要工具，但目前缺乏对不同建模方法在时空维度上预测病原体浓度的直接比较，研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 以英格兰诺如病毒为案例，使用2021年5月至2022年3月期间来自152座污水处理厂的3,232份污水样本（源于英国环境健康保护监测计划）。以采用INLA与SPDE方法的贝叶斯时空模型为基准，对比Lasso回归、广义可加模型（GAM）、贝叶斯GAM、XGBoost和随机森林。采用10折空间块交叉验证，评估指标包括均方误差、偏差、相关性、95%预测区间的经验覆盖率、区间评分及计算成本。

Result: 随机森林为整体表现最佳模型，区间评分最优，经验覆盖率（95.3%）最接近名义值，同时点预测精度与XGBoost相当。INLA-SPDE模型同样表现良好，经验覆盖率接近名义值，区间评分位列第三，且在所有评估指标上偏差持续较低。两模型预测的时空趋势在空间格局上相似，但在不确定性估计上存在明显差异。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了预测精度、不确定性量化与计算效率之间的权衡。集成机器学习方法适合快速预测，而当概率性决策支持为公共卫生监测重点时，贝叶斯地质统计模型仍具重要价值。

Abstract: Wastewater-based epidemiology (WBE) is an increasingly important tool for infectious disease surveillance, but there has been limited direct comparison of modelling approaches for predicting pathogen concentrations across space and time. We compare the predictive performance of six modelling approaches using norovirus in England as a case study, considering 3,232 wastewater samples from 152 sewage treatment works collected between May 2021 and March 2022 as part of the UK Environmental Monitoring for Health Protection programme. The benchmark was a Bayesian spatio-temporal model using Integrated Nested Laplace Approximation (INLA) with the Stochastic Partial Differential Equation approach (SPDE), compared with Lasso regression, Generalised Additive Models (GAM), Bayesian GAM, Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), and Random Forest. Models were evaluated using 10-fold spatial-block cross-validation with metrics including mean squared error, bias, correlation, empirical coverage of 95% prediction intervals, interval score, and computational cost. Random Forest was the best overall performing model, achieving the best interval score and nearest to nominal empirical coverage (95.3%), while maintaining point prediction accuracy comparable to XGBoost. The INLA-SPDE model also performed well, with near-nominal empirical coverage, the third best interval score, and consistently low bias across all evaluated metrics. Comparing predicted spatio-temporal trends, both models yielded similar spatial patterns but notable differences in uncertainty estimation. Our findings highlight a trade-off between predictive accuracy, uncertainty quantification, and computational efficiency. Ensemble machine learning methods are well suited to rapid prediction, whereas Bayesian geostatistical models remain valuable when probabilistic decision support is a priority for public health surveillance

</details>


### [142] [Risk-Set Transported Synthetic Control with Difference-in-Differences Adjustment under Staggered Treatment Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20264)
*Mojtaba Eslami*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出RT-SC-DiD估计量，在交错处理采纳设计中对逐事件时间的合成控制权重施加以"运输参照"为目标的收缩正则，以利用逐渐退出供体池的暂时合格单位、抑制再优化误差传播，并通过供体支持诊断与仅供体安慰剂程序选取惩罚强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错采纳设计下，后处理单位仅在自身处理前可作为早处理队列的合格控制，可容许供体集合随事件时间收缩。若把供体池固定在最长视界会丢弃暂时合格供体；若在每个视界独立重估合成控制权重，则供体构成变动会导致反事实不稳定。

Method: 对每个队列与事件时间视界，在当前未处理供体上拟合权重，并向"运输参照"收缩——该参照把退出供体的权重重新分配给相似的存续供体；辅以DiD基线校正以消除持续性水平差异。理论上刻画逐视界再优化带来的扭曲，推导朴素删除并重归一化所引致的载荷变化，在显式假定的运输映射正则条件下给出误差传播的条件递推界。另提出供体支持诊断与仅用供体的安慰剂程序来选择运输惩罚。

Result: 在80次重复的试点比较及另一项每取值40次重复的敏感性分析中，中等强度的运输正则相对独立的逐视界估计与强锚定（强收缩）均降低了平均RMSE；该证据支持偏差—方差动机，但不构成已证明的保证。

Conclusion: RT-SC-DiD适用于后处理单位能提供有用短期信息、且供体支持随时间显著收缩的情境。据作者所知，现有交错合成控制与合成双重差分方法未显式地将队列内权重序列向运输参照正则化。

Abstract: In staggered treatment-adoption designs, later-treated units are valid controls for an earlier-treated cohort only until their own treatment begins, so the admissible donor set contracts with event time. Fixing the donor pool at the longest horizon discards temporarily eligible donors, whereas re-estimating synthetic-control weights independently at each horizon can make the counterfactual unstable as donor composition changes. We propose Risk-Set Transported Synthetic Control with Difference-in-Differences Adjustment (RT-SC-DiD). For each cohort and event-time horizon, the estimator fits weights on the currently untreated donors while shrinking them toward a transported reference that reallocates the weight of exiting donors to similar surviving donors. A DiD baseline correction removes persistent level differences. We characterize distortion from horizon-by-horizon reoptimization, derive the loading change induced by naive deletion and renormalization, and give a conditional recursive bound for error propagation under an explicitly assumed regularity condition on the transport map. We also introduce donor-support diagnostics and a donor-only placebo procedure for selecting the transport penalty. In an 80-replication pilot comparison and a separate 40-replication-per-value sensitivity analysis, intermediate transport regularization reduces average RMSE relative to independent horizon-specific estimation and strong anchoring. This evidence supports the method's bias-variance motivation but is not a proved guarantee. RT-SC-DiD is intended for settings where later-treated units provide useful short-horizon information and donor support contracts materially over time. Existing staggered synthetic-control and synthetic difference-in-differences methods do not, to our knowledge, explicitly regularize within-cohort weight sequences toward transported references as risk sets contract.

</details>


### [143] [A Hierarchical Bayesian Model for Selecting Relevant Schema Subgraphs,with an Application to Grounding Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20294)
*Robert Richardson*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文将模式链接建模为小属性图上的结构化子集选择，提出层次贝叶斯模型，用一元证据与自逻辑成对耦合联合推断候选表子图，并在 BIRD 与 Spider 上验证其校准性和预测集覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模式链接方法通常独立地为表或列打分，但目标对象往往是外键图上的连通子图；独立打分容易漏掉本身信号弱、却在连接其他相关表中起作用的表。

Method: 在小型模式图上构建层次贝叶斯模型：融合查询-表与查询-列的一元特征证据，以及外键边上的成对自逻辑耦合项；引入数据库级随机效应刻画基线纳入率和耦合强度的变化；利用图规模小实现条件似然与纳入概率的精确求值，并在参数后验的 Laplace 近似下做平均。

Result: 在 BIRD 和 Spider 上，正图耦合能召回独立打分遗漏的表，但会整体抬高边际纳入概率；联合估计截距与耦合可恢复校准，数据库级部分池化使结构效应适应不同模式。图耦合后验对精确相关子图赋予更高概率，预测集覆盖接近名义水平，而独立模型出现覆盖不足，模型还给出各数据库图权重强度的后验。

Conclusion: 耦合有助于表召回，但需截距补偿其均值漂移；方法更适合作为表子集的后验预测分布来评价，而非追求精确率最大化的选择器。

Abstract: Grounding a large language model on a relational database requires selecting the tables and joins relevant to a query. Existing schema-linking methods usually score tables or columns independently, although the target object is typically a connected subgraph of the foreign-key graph. We propose a hierarchical Bayesian model for schema linking as structured subset selection on small attributed graphs. Unary evidence from query-table and query-column features is combined with a pairwise autologistic coupling on foreign-key edges, allowing weakly-signalled tables to be selected when they connect other relevant tables. Database-level random effects model variation in the baseline inclusion rate and coupling strength, and the small size of the schema graphs permits exact evaluation of the conditional likelihood and inclusion probabilities, which we average over a Laplace approximation to the parameter posterior. On the benchmark datasets BIRD and Spider, positive graph coupling recovers tables missed by independent scoring but shifts the marginal inclusion probabilities upward. Jointly estimating the intercept and coupling restores calibration, and database-level partial pooling adapts the structural effect across schemas. The graph-coupled posterior assigns more probability to the exact relevant subgraph and produces informative predictive sets with near-nominal coverage, whereas predictive sets from the corresponding independent model undercover. The fitted model also reports a posterior over how strongly to weight the graph in each database. Stability and mean-shift results explain when coupling aids recovery and why the intercept must compensate for it. We therefore evaluate the method primarily as a posterior predictive distribution over table subsets rather than as a precision-maximizing selector.

</details>


### [144] [Emulation strategies for Bayesian inference of regional left ventricle material parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20372)
*Hongjin Ren,Vinny Davies,Hao Gao,Mu Niu,Benn Macdonald*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种贝叶斯代理建模框架，用于推断左心室五个生理分区的区域 Holzapfel-Ogden 材料参数；通过筛选八种模拟器策略，发现多输出变分高斯过程在点估计精度与不确定性量化间取得最佳平衡，并在合成与健康志愿者数据上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 左心室患者特异性生物力学模型可将心脏磁共振成像与区域心肌材料属性相关联，但现有基于模拟器的研究通常将心肌视为力学均匀介质，难以表征局部功能障碍。

Method: 将左心室按美国心脏协会17节段模型划分为五个生理分区，构建贝叶斯代理建模框架以推断区域 Holzapfel-Ogden 材料参数；筛选八种模拟器策略，涵盖单输出与多输出、局部与全局、高斯过程与神经网络架构；对保留的三种模型采用经验边际可信区间覆盖率与后验收缩进行评估；并开展合成局部/全局硬化场景及健康志愿者数据分析。

Result: 点估计精度相当的模型在不确定性表现上差异显著；多输出变分高斯过程在各评价标准间平衡最佳。合成硬化场景中，最大后验估计通常能区分硬化区与基线区，但非线性硬化参数比刚度幅值参数更难从舒张末期观测中辨识；健康志愿者分析表明在观测向量不完整且联合推断应变噪声尺度的情况下仍具可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架为区域左心室参数推断提供了一种计算可行且具有不确定性感知能力的方法。

Abstract: Patient-specific biomechanical models of the left ventricle can relate cardiac magnetic resonance imaging to regional myocardial material properties, but existing emulator-based studies typically treat the myocardium as mechanically homogeneous, limiting representation of localised dysfunction. We propose a Bayesian surrogate-modelling framework for inferring regional Holzapfel-Ogden material parameters in a left ventricle partitioned into five physiological zones derived from the American Heart Association 17-segment model. Eight emulator strategies spanning single- versus multi-output, local versus global, and Gaussian-process- versus neural-network-based architectures were screened using parameter point-estimation accuracy; the three retained models were evaluated using empirical marginal credible-interval coverage and posterior contraction. We found that models with comparable point accuracy nevertheless differed markedly in uncertainty. A multi-output variational Gaussian process provided the most favourable balance across these criteria and was retained for the subsequent analyses. In synthetic local and global stiffening scenarios, maximum a posteriori estimates generally distinguished stiffened from baseline zones, but the nonlinear-stiffening parameters were more difficult to identify from end-diastolic observations than the stiffness-magnitude parameters. A healthy-volunteer analysis demonstrates feasibility with an incomplete observation vector and jointly inferred strain-noise scales. These results suggest that the proposed framework provides a computationally feasible, uncertainty-aware approach to regional left ventricle parameter inference.

</details>

# q-bio.PE [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Self-Replicating Neural Cellular Automata: Quantifying Emergent Phenotypic and Genotypic Diversity in an OpenEnded Substrate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19902)
*Sanyam Jain,Felix Simon Reimers,Stefano Nichele*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该论文研究一种双通道元胞自动机基底，每个像素携带一个感知Moore邻域的微型神经网络（agent），细胞通过自我复制与权重变异而存续，形成共存、竞争与优势物种构成的生态系统；核心贡献是提出一套多尺度粗粒度多样性度量，并揭示表型多样性与基因型多样性之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象是由局部神经网络agent构成的in-silico基底，其生长过程会产生空间组织的物种生态。作者动机在于：这类表型完全由基因型（网络权重）驱动的系统缺乏可测量的多样性分析工具，难以在两尺度上量化其生长、共存与竞争动态。

Method: 方法基于双通道元胞自动机网格，每像素携带感知Moore邻域的微型神经网络；细胞仅通过自我复制存续，克隆存活的邻居并用均匀扰动变异其权重，使表型（细胞状态）完全由基因型（网络权重）决定。作者提出一组粗粒度多样性度量：四个基于细胞类型频率、熵与细胞方差的表型工具，以及两个基因型工具——分别以全权重向量的哈希和稀疏随机权重探针对每个agent着色。实验进行了五折扫描，包含1680次小规模运行与24次长运行（1000代，200×200）。

Result: 在24个长配置中有20个保持持续且自我维持；系统从少量种子创始体生长为空间组织的生态系统，呈现共存、竞争与优势物种。结果暴露了明确的表型—基因型多样性权衡：提高表型多样性会压低基因型多样性，反之亦然。全基因组哈希着色进一步揭示出随机权重探针系统性遗漏的谱系结构。

Conclusion: 该基底具有持续性与自我维持能力，其多样性可在表型与基因型两个尺度上被有效度量；表型与基因型多样性之间存在此消彼长的权衡关系，且全基因组哈希着色比稀疏随机探针更能捕捉谱系结构。代码、数据与动画作为补充材料发布。

Abstract: We study an in-silico substrate in which every pixel of a two-channel cellular-automata grid carries a tiny neural network (an agent) that senses its Moore neighborhood. A cell persists only by self-replication: a living neighbor is cloned and its weights are mutated by a uniform perturbation, so that phenotype (cell state) is driven entirely by genotype (network weights). From a handful of seeded founders the system grows into a spatially organized ecosystem of coexisting, competing and dominating species. Our main contribution is a battery of coarse-grained diversity metrics that make such growth measurable at two scales: four phenotypic tools based on cellular-type frequency, entropy and cell variance, and two genotypic tools that colour each agent by a hash of its full weight vector versus a sparse random-weight probe. Across a five-fold sweep of 1680 small runs and 24 long (1000-generation, 200 x 200) runs, the substrate is persistent and self-maintaining in 20 of the 24 long configurations and exposes a clear phenotype-genotype diversity trade-off: raising phenotypic diversity collapses genotypic diversity and vice versa. Full-genome hash colouring further reveals lineage structure that a random-weight probe systematically misses. Code, data and animations are released as supplementary material.

</details>
