### [146] [Deep Learning for Singular PDEs: A Weighted Neural Network Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19335)
*Badr Oulgiht*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种深度学习框架，用于求解带齐次Dirichlet边界条件和正性约束的弱奇异半线性椭圆方程，通过硬约束边界嵌入、Softplus正性保持和奇异性感知加权残差三项技术组合提升近似精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱奇异半线性椭圆方程中，非线性源项在正解靠近边界趋于零时变得无界，导致数值近似困难。标准残差方法难以处理这种近边界奇异性，且需同时满足边界条件与正性约束。

Method: 方法结合三个组件：(1) 通过硬约束形式将Dirichlet边界条件直接嵌入神经表示；(2) 采用Softplus变换在计算域内部保持正性，使奇异非线性项在训练中始终良定义；(3) 引入奇异性感知的加权残差损失，重点强化预测解较小、奇异行为更显著的区域。在弱奇异区域内使用数值参考解和受控验证测试进行评估，并与标准残差形式对比。

Result: 在弱奇异区域内通过数值参考解与受控验证测试完成评估；与标准残差形式对比，考察了近似精度与收敛行为。实验表明硬边界强制、正性保持和奇异性感知残差加权的组合具有潜力。

Conclusion: 将硬边界强制、正性保持与奇异性感知残差加权相结合，对弱奇异椭圆问题的深度学习求解具有应用潜力，可作为该类问题的有效近似策略。

Abstract: We propose a deep learning framework for the numerical approximation of weakly singular semilinear elliptic equations subject to homogeneous Dirichlet boundary conditions and positivity constraints. These problems are challenging because the nonlinear source term becomes unbounded as the positive solution approaches zero near the boundary.
  The proposed approach combines three components. First, the Dirichlet boundary conditions are embedded directly into the neural representation through a hard-constraint formulation. Second, a Softplus transformation preserves positivity in the interior of the computational domain, ensuring that the singular nonlinear term remains well-defined during training. Third, a singularity-aware weighted residual loss emphasizes regions where the predicted solution becomes small and the singular behavior is more pronounced.
  The method is evaluated in the weak singularity regime through numerical reference solutions and controlled verification tests. Comparisons with the standard residual formulation are used to assess approximation accuracy and convergence behavior. The numerical experiments illustrate the potential of combining hard boundary enforcement, positivity preservation, and singularity-aware residual weighting for weakly singular elliptic problems.

</details>


### [147] [Wideband Directional $\mathcal{H}^2$-Matrix Compression for the Electric Field Integral Equation with Geometry-Adaptive Cluster Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19431)
*Joshua M. Tetzner,Simon B. Adrian*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种适用于电场积分方程的宽带方向性 H² 矩阵高效构造方法，支持几何自适应聚类树，通过按簇电尺寸确定方向数、Spherical-Fibonacci 方向点与角向最近邻映射建立层级，并利用鲁棒化 tree-mimicry 主元策略的非完全自适应交叉近似构造嵌套方向表示，在保持精度与 O(N log N) 复杂度的同时显著降低存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有宽带方向性 H² 矩阵构造仅支持盒树（如八叉树），当几何或离散化与八叉树聚类不匹配时效率与存储表现不佳；因此需要一种能兼容几何自适应聚类树的构造方法。

Method: 1) 以每个簇的电尺寸（而非盒树层级）确定方向数；2) 使用 Spherical-Fibonacci 点生成方向；3) 通过角向最近邻映射在父簇与子簇方向集之间建立层级；4) 采用非完全自适应交叉近似（IACA）构造嵌套方向表示，并引入鲁棒化的 tree-mimicry 主元策略，防止特定几何与网格产生的块结构矩阵出现过早收敛。

Result: 数值实验表明：方法达到预期精度；存储量不高于基于八叉树的构造，且在几何或离散化与八叉树聚类匹配较差时存储显著更少；对高频问题呈现预期的 O(N log N) 缩放。

Conclusion: 该工作将宽带方向性 H² 矩阵构造从盒树推广到几何自适应聚类树，兼顾精度、存储效率与高频可扩展性，是该类快速算法构造方向上的有效改进。

Abstract: We present an efficient wideband construction of directional $\mathcal{H}^2$-matrices for the electrical field integral equation that, in contrast to existing constructions, supports not only box trees but also geometry-adaptive cluster trees. To accommodate geometry-adaptive cluster trees, we determine the number of directions from the electrical size of each cluster (instead of the level of a box tree), construct the directions using Spherical-Fibonacci points, and establish a hierarchy between the direction sets of a cluster and its children through an angular nearest-neighbor mapping. We construct the nested directional representation using the incomplete adaptive cross approximation, for which we introduce a robustified tree-mimicry pivoting strategy that prevents premature convergence for block-structured matrices arising from certain geometries and meshes. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves the desired accuracy, requires no more storage than the octree-based construction and substantially less when the geometry or discretization is poorly matched to octree clustering, and exhibits the expected $\mathcal{O}(N\log N)$ scaling for high-frequency problems.

</details>


### [148] [An interior penalty nonconforming finite element method for strain gradient elasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19567)
*Xuehai Huang,Zheqian Tang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文为应变梯度弹性(SGE)模型构造了一种内罚非协调有限元方法，采用由无散度面气泡富集的向量二次多项式构成的H¹非协调位移单元，在任意维数下建立了关于Lamé系数λ和尺寸参数ι一致鲁棒的最优误差估计，并由此导出二、三维非协调Stokes复形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应变梯度弹性模型包含高阶导数项，传统协调元要求C¹连续性，构造困难且单元复杂；同时模型含Lamé系数λ与内禀尺寸参数ι，如何在二者变化下保持数值方法的精度与鲁棒性是关键挑战。

Method: 采用内罚(interior penalty)框架处理非协调性，构造H¹非协调位移单元：以向量值二次多项式为基础，用无散度面气泡(divergence-free face bubbles)富集；利用位移单元的散度交换(divergence-commuting)结构进行误差分析，并据此构造二、三维非协调有限元Stokes复形。

Result: 证明了关于Lamé系数λ和尺寸参数ι均最优且鲁棒(一致)的误差估计；得到了二维、三维非协调有限元Stokes复形；数值实验验证了预测的收敛性与参数鲁棒性；单轴拉伸下的圆孔基准算例表明方法适用于多连通区域。

Conclusion: 所提内罚非协调有限元方法可有效、鲁棒地求解任意维数应变梯度弹性问题，兼具理论最优性与对多连通几何的适用性，并揭示了非协调Stokes复形的内在结构联系。

Abstract: An interior penalty nonconforming finite element method is developed for a strain gradient elasticity (SGE) model in arbitrary dimensions, using an $H^1$-nonconforming displacement element built from vector-valued quadratic polynomials enriched by divergence-free face bubbles. Rigorous analysis establishes optimal and robust error estimates with respect to both the Lamé coefficient $λ$ and the size parameter $ι$. The underlying divergence-commuting structure further leads to nonconforming finite element Stokes complexes in two and three dimensions. Numerical experiments support the predicted convergence behavior and parameter robustness, while a circular-hole benchmark under uniaxial tension illustrates the applicability of the method to a multiply connected domain.

</details>


### [149] [A Fictitious Domain Formulation with Bubble Enrichment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19836)
*Stefano Berrone,Lorenzo Neva,Stefano Scialò,Fabio Vicini*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文放宽了虚拟区域法中拉格朗日乘子边界网格的尺寸比约束，直接从背景三角剖分的迹构造边界网格，并通过加密气泡函数恢复一致离散inf-sup条件，进而证明稳定性、导出最优先验误差估计并以数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟区域法通过将复杂物理域嵌入简单的非匹配背景网格来规避复杂网格生成。现有采用拉格朗日乘子弱施加边界条件的方法，通常要求域网格与边界网格为两套独立的均匀网格，且网格尺寸须满足预设比例，这一限制在实际应用中不够灵活。本文旨在放宽该网格假设。

Method: 直接从背景三角剖分的迹（trace）构造边界网格，从而摆脱对独立均匀边界网格及其尺寸比的要求。为在较弱的网格假设下恢复一致离散inf-sup条件，在解空间中加入气泡函数进行加密。核心工作是构造一个合适的算子，用以证明该一致离散inf-sup条件。

Result: 通过所构造的算子证明了弱网格假设下的一致离散inf-sup条件，由此建立格式的稳定性，并导出最优先验误差估计；数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，无需两套独立均匀网格及固定尺寸比，直接在背景剖分迹上构造边界网格并结合气泡函数加密即可保证虚拟区域法弱边界施加格式的稳定性和最优收敛性。

Abstract: The fictitious domain method offers a powerful alternative to circumvent complex mesh generation process by embedding the complex physical domain into a simpler, non-matching background mesh. Among the various strategies to enforce the boundary condition on such a mesh, one possibility is to impose it weakly through a Lagrange multiplier. Existing approaches following this strategy typically require two independent uniform meshes for the domain and the boundary, whose mesh sizes are related by a prescribed ratio. In this work, we relax this requirement by constructing the boundary mesh directly from the trace of the background triangulation. To recover the uniform discrete inf-sup condition under this weaker mesh assumption, we enrich the discrete space of the solution with bubble functions. Our main result is the construction of a suitable operator that allows us to prove this uniform discrete inf-sup condition, thereby establishing the stability of the resulting scheme. As a consequence, we derive optimal a priori error estimates and provide a numerical experiment to validate the theoretical results.

</details>


### [150] [Entropy-Based Local Characteristic Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19838)
*Shaoshuai Chu,Michael Herty,Alexander Kurganov*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出基于熵的局部特征分解（ELCD），通过从界面相邻单元及相空间近邻候选态中选取局部熵最小态作为平均界面态，用于通量雅可比线性化，并将其嵌入二阶有限体积与五阶有限差分格式。二维Euler方程数值实验表明，相较算术平均，ELCD能更锐利地分辨复杂波结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部特征分解（LCD）广泛用于双曲守恒律的高阶数值方法以抑制虚假振荡。LCD实现需代表性平均界面态，通常由相邻网格值的算术平均或Roe型平均获得，但这类平均未必适合复杂波结构的分辨，因此需要更优的平均界面态选取策略。

Method: 提出熵基局部特征分解（ELCD）：将界面两侧相邻单元状态与相空间中若干近邻状态一并作为候选，从中选取使局部熵最小的状态作为平均界面态，用于线性化通量雅可比。将ELCD嵌入多种二阶有限体积格式与五阶有限差分格式。

Result: 针对二维气体动力学Euler方程的数值实验显示，采用ELCD的格式总体上比采用算术平均的对应格式更锐利地分辨复杂波结构。

Conclusion: 以局部熵最小化为准则选取平均界面态可有效改进LCD，从而提升高阶格式对复杂波结构的分辨能力，且该方法可推广至多种有限体积与有限差分框架。

Abstract: Local characteristic decomposition (LCD) is widely used in high-order numerical methods for hyperbolic systems of conservation laws to reduce spurious oscillations appearing in the computed solutions. The LCD implementation requires a representative average interface state, typically obtained using arithmetic or Roe-type averages of the nearly grid values. We propose an entropy-based LCD (ELCD), in which the states in the two cells adjacent to an interface are considered together with several nearby states in phase space. Among these candidates, we select the state that locally minimizes the entropy and use it as an average interface state for linearizing the flux Jacobian. We incorporate the ELCD into several second-order finite-volume and fifth-order finite-difference schemes. Numerical experiments for the two-dimensional Euler equations of gas dynamics show that the schemes, which utilize the ELCD procedure generally resolve complex wave structures more sharply than their counterparts, which use the arithmetic averages.

</details>


### [151] [Validating Hexahedra through their Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19926)
*Paul Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明了Knupp关于三线性六面体雅可比行列式正性的猜想：边界正性可保证整个单元内部的jacdet正性，并给出有限候选点算法实现高效验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限元分析中三线性六面体单元的仿真真实性要求其雅可比行列式（jacdet）在整个单元内保持正值。然而直接在整个单元体积上验证jacdet正性计算代价高，因此需要更高效的验证途径——这促使作者探索仅检查边界即可保证内部正性的可能性（Knupp猜想）。

Method: 理论证明与算法推导：1）证明Knupp猜想，即三线性六面体边界上的jacdet正性足以保证整个单元内部的正性；2）证明任意有效六面体的全局最小jacdet必位于边界上；3）证明边界上全局最小jacdet（无论单元是否有效）必在有限候选点集内取得，该候选点集可通过四次方程求根确定；4）综合上述结果构建算法验证方法。

Result: 证明了三个关键结论：(1) Knupp猜想成立，边界jacdet正性蕴含全局正性；(2) 有效六面体的全局最小jacdet位于边界；(3) 边界全局最小jacdet落在可由四次求根确定的有限候选点集中。据此，六面体有效性验证可简化为在有限边界点集上评估jacdet。

Conclusion: 通过边界有限点集上的jacdet评估即可算法化地验证三线性六面体单元的有效性，避免了全体积验证的高成本，为有限元网格质量保证提供了理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: Trilinear hexahedral mesh elements are used in finite element analysis to simulate volumetric phenomena. Realism of the simulation mandates that mesh elements through the trilinear map maintain positive volume, or mathematically, that the trilinear map maintains positive Jacobian determinant (jacdet). While jacdet positivity generally needs to be verified in the full element volume, we prove Knupp's conjecture which posits that for trilinear hexahedra, jacdet positivity on the boundary of the element is sufficient to guarantee positivity in the entire element. We further prove that for any valid hexahedron, its globally minimal jacdet must reside on the boundary. Lastly, we prove that the globally minimal jacdet on the boundary of a hexahedron, valid or not, must be achieved within a finite set of candidate points that can be determined by quartic root finding. Combining these results, we can algorithmically validate a hexahedron through evaluation of its jacdet on a finite set of boundary points.

</details>


### [152] [Accelerating structural optimization via EIFEM: a ROM-based preconditioner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20053)
*Raul Rubio,Alex Ferrer,Joaquin Alberto Hernandez,Pablo Antolin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出基于降阶模型与预处理技术的参数化优化框架，用EIFEM+DEIM构建局部参数相关降阶算子，以显式几何特征参数化设计空间，加速在线优化循环并提升可制造性与结构刚度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拓扑优化因反复评估高保真有限元模型而计算成本高，且常产生需大量后处理解释才能制造的复杂几何。

Method: 结合经验跨尺度有限元法（EIFEM）与离散经验插值法（DEIM）构建局部、参数相关的降阶算子；从优化角度将其视为使用非精确梯度的预处理策略以加速收敛；并以显式几何特征（如夹杂半径、点阵宽度）参数化设计空间。

Result: 以拓扑优化为基线，在三种单胞几何与三个基准问题上评估计算时间、设计拓扑和结构性能。结果显示该参数化ROM框架需离线训练阶段，但显著加速在线优化循环，且部分实验中可获得更刚硬的设计。

Conclusion: 参数化降阶建模与预处理相结合可有效降低拓扑优化在线计算成本，并通过几何特征参数化提高设计可制造性，在部分情形下还能改善结构性能。

Abstract: Topology optimization (TopOpt) is a standard tool for structural conceptual design, providing optimal structures with great design freedom. However, it has a high computational costs due to the repeated evaluation of high-fidelity finite element models and frequently produces complex geometries that require extensive interpretation for manufacturability. As an alternative, this work presents a parameterized optimization framework based on Reduced Order Modeling (ROM) and preconditioning techniques. We employ the Empirical Interscale Finite Element Method (EIFEM) coupled with the Discrete Empirical Interpolation Method (DEIM) to construct localized, parameter-dependent reduced operators. From an optimization perspective, this approach can be interpreted as a preconditioning strategy in which inexact gradients are used to accelerate convergence. Furthermore, parameterizing the design space in terms of explicit geometric features, such as inclusion radii or lattice widths, significantly improves the manu facturability of the resulting optimized designs. We assess the performance of the method in terms of computational time, design topology and structural performance with TopOpt as baseline for three differ ent unit cell geometries and three different benchmarks. While the parametric ROM framework requires an initial offline training phase, our comparative analysis demonstrates that it drastically accelerates the online optimization loop and that it can produce even stiffer design in some of our experiments.

</details>


### [153] [Exact fast factorizations of the AR(1) Karhunen-Loeve transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20221)
*Yuriy A. Reznik*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文推导了一阶自回归（AR(1)）源Karhunen-Loeve变换（KLT）的精确蝶形快速分解，将KLT分解为蝶形级、固定DCT-II/DCT-IV核心及两个正交修正因子，修正因子为对角加秩一矩阵的特征向量矩阵，并借助快速多极与层次化方法将总复杂度降至O(N log N)，实现大N下精度可控的精确KLT应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AR(1)源的KLT是经典最优变换，但其矩阵一般无快速算法，直接应用复杂度为O(N^2)，难以在大N场景下实现。作者希望找到类似DCT快速算法的精确（非近似）分解结构，以降低计算复杂度并保持精度可控。

Method: 对偶数阶N，将KLT矩阵分解为蝶形级B_N、固定半尺寸DCT-II与DCT-IV核心CII_{N/2}与CIV_{N/2}，以及两个正交修正因子Qs_{N/2}与Qa_{N/2}；这些修正因子是对角加秩一矩阵的特征向量矩阵。以N=4为特例给出闭式旋转角，并将修正因子视为分治特征求解器中的中心秩一更新特征问题，采用快速多极与层次化方法加速求解，给出整体O(N log N)的分解并绘制信号流图。

Result: 修正因子Qs与Qa为显式对角加秩一矩阵的特征向量矩阵；当rho -> 1时修正退化为单位阵，分解还原为经典的Chen-Smith-Fralick DCT-II分解；N=4时退化为一个蝶形加两个平面旋转，闭式角满足tan(2 psi_s) = -2/(1 - rho)与tan(2 psi_a) = 2/(1 + rho)；一般情形下得到总体复杂度O(N log N)、精度可控的精确KLT分解，并给出信号流图。

Conclusion: 该工作为AR(1)源的精确KLT提供了类蝶形快速分解，将修正结构统一为秩一更新特征问题，并借此获得O(N log N)复杂度，使大N下精度可控的精确KLT应用成为可能，同时与经典DCT快速分解建立了联系。

Abstract: We derive exact butterfly-style factorizations for the Karhunen-Loeve transform (KLT) of a first-order autoregressive (AR(1)) source with known correlation coefficient rho. For even order N, the KLT matrix factors into a butterfly stage B_N, the fixed half-size DCT-II and DCT-IV cores CII_{N/2} and CIV_{N/2}, and two orthogonal correction factors Qs_{N/2} and Qa_{N/2}. These corrections are the eigenvector matrices of explicit diagonal plus rank-one matrices. In the limiting case rho -> 1, the corrections become identities, and the factorization reduces to the classic Chen-Smith-Fralick factorization of the DCT-II. For N = 4, it reduces to one butterfly and two plane rotations with closed-form angles tan(2 psi_s) = -2/(1 - rho) and tan(2 psi_a) = 2/(1 + rho). In the general case, the correction factors are instances of the central rank-one-update eigenproblem in divide-and-conquer eigensolvers; applying fast-multipole and hierarchical methods to them, we derive a factorization with overall complexity O(N log N), giving accuracy-controlled application of the exact KLT at large N. Signal-flow graphs of the resulting algorithms are provided.

</details>


### [154] [Further Approaches of Dynamical Low-Rank Approximation for SDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20292)
*Yoshihito Kazashi,Fabio Nobile,Fabio Zoccolan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对随机微分方程（SDEs）提出两种新的DLRA型动力学，分别基于泛函极小化与（非正式的）Stratonovich表述，并讨论了SDE情形下DLRA的形式选择问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（arXiv:2308.11581）研究了SDE的一种DLRA型动力学，但其形式并非唯一。作者希望探索其他可能推导路径，以厘清哪种表述更适合SDE，以及"SDE的DLRA究竟是什么"这一根本问题。

Method: 提出两种推导方法：（1）通过泛函极小化得到一种动力学，其思路类似arXiv:1803.00499中的SDE系统DLRA；（2）由Stratonovich表述（非正式地）导出另一种动力学，在扩散项可微的前提下，利用Stratonovich形式在流形上书写随机过程，使漂移项中额外出现一个依赖流形几何的项。

Result: 泛函极小化方法得到的动力学与arXiv:1803.00499的SDE DLRA相似；Stratonovich方法在扩散可微时，所得漂移中多出一个几何依赖项。

Conclusion: 两种推导均可行但形式不同，说明SDE的DLRA缺乏唯一表述。这些结果引发关于何种形式更合适、以及SDE的DLRA应如何定义的讨论。

Abstract: In this article, we propose two other DLRA-type dynamics for stochastic differential equations (SDEs) than the one studied in arXiv:2308.11581, derived from a minimization of functionals and (informally) from a Stratonovich formulation, respectively. The former approach resembles the DLRA for SDE system proposed in arXiv:1803.00499. Providing the differentiability of the diffusion, the latter procedure registers an additional term in the drift. Indeed, its derivation exploits the Stratonovich formulation to write stochastic processes on manifold, and, hence, possesses a term that depends on the geometry of the manifold itself. These developments open the debate on which formalism is more suitable and what DLRA for SDEs really is.

</details>


### [155] [Existence of Dynamical Low-Rank Approximation for SDEs with Locally Lipschitz Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20307)
*Yoshihito Kazashi,Fabio Nobile,Fabio Zoccolan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 将动态低秩近似（DLRA）从已有设定扩展到具有局部Lipschitz漂移与扩散项（带线性增长界）的高维随机微分方程，并证明该设定下DLRA的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维随机微分方程在真实应用中计算时间与内存开销巨大，降阶方法可在低秩问题下实现快速且较精确的模拟；现有DLRA框架的适用条件受限。

Method: 在前期工作（arXiv:2308.11581）基础上，将DLRA框架推广至漂移项与扩散项为局部Lipschitz且满足线性增长界的SDE，并通过理论分析证明DLRA的存在性。

Result: 证明了在局部Lipschitz、线性增长界设定下DLRA的存在性，拓展了原框架的适用范围。

Conclusion: DLRA可推广至更一般（局部Lipschitz、线性增长）的SDE设定，从而扩大其在高维随机模拟中的可用性。

Abstract: Numerical simulations of high-dimensional stochastic differential equations (SDEs), which are increasingly employed in real-world applications, can be unaffordable in terms of computational time and memory. A possible solution is the deployment of reduced order methods (ROMs) that provide fast simulations with good accuracy when dealing with low-rank problems. In the context of SDEs, the Dynamical Low-Rank Approximation (DLRA) already showed remarkable results, in terms of approximation and computational efficiency of computational time because of being completely computed ``on-the-fly".
  In this article, we extend the framework of DLRA for SDEs proposed in our primary work arXiv:2308.11581 by considering locally Lipschitz drift and diffusion with linear-growth bound, by showing the existence of DLRA for this setting.

</details>


### [156] [Numerical Methods for Dynamical Low-Rank Approximations of Stochastic Differential Equations -- Part II: Stochastic discretization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20317)
*Yoshihito Kazashi,Fabio Nobile,Fabio Zoccolan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文（第二部分）从随机离散化角度分析了第一部分提出的随机微分方程（SDE）动力低秩近似（DLRA）数值算法，采用M样本蒙特卡洛方法近似随机空间，针对椭圆扩散情形给出了DLR投影分裂算法的收敛性结果，并讨论了DLR欧拉-丸山等算法的困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一部分（arXiv:2601.21428）引入了SDE的动力低秩近似算法，但其数值算法的误差分析在随机离散化视角下并不标准，因为算法产生的是一类带噪的相互作用粒子系统。因此需要专门的收敛性分析来刻画随机离散化对DLRA算法精度的影响。

Method: 采用含M个样本的蒙特卡洛方法近似随机空间及相关量，得到带噪相互作用粒子系统。在初始条件具有高阶矩和次高斯尾的假设下，针对椭圆扩散情形，对第一部分提出的SDE的DLR投影分裂算法进行收敛性分析。区分三种处理Gramian矩阵的情形：完全满秩、每步正则化、仅在最小奇异值低于给定阈值时正则化。

Result: 在完全离散Gramian在整个时间演化中满秩时，对大M观察到接近标准蒙特卡洛的收敛率O(1/√M)；若每步对Gramian进行正则化，收敛率接近O(1/∛M)；若仅在最小奇异值低于预设正阈值时才正则化，则随机收敛率介于上述两者之间。对一般扩散情形，提供了一个易于实现的收敛算法。对DLR欧拉-丸山和DLR投影分裂用于EM的结果未能直接导出，文中简要讨论了其困难性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了SDE的DLRA算法在蒙特卡洛随机离散化下的收敛性理论，量化了Gramian处理策略对收敛率的影响，并为一般扩散提供了实用算法；同时指出DLR-EM相关算法的收敛分析仍是开放且具有挑战性的问题，后续将以数值模拟加以补充验证。

Abstract: In this second article (Part II), we analyze the numerical algorithms for the Dynamical Low-Rank Approximation (DLRA) of Stochastic Differential Equations (SDEs) introduced in Part I arXiv:2601.21428 under the perspective of the stochastic discretization. Specifically, we employ a Monte Carlo method with $M$ samples to approximate the stochastic space and all the related quantities of interest. Consequently, these algorithms produce noisy interacting particle systems whose error analysis is not standard. Assuming high moments and subgaussian tails of the initial condition, in the case of elliptic diffusion, we provide convergence results for the DLR Projector Splitting for SDEs presented in Part I. When the fully discretized Gramian is of full rank for all the time evolution, then one observes a convergence rate close to the usual Monte Carlo one, i.e. $O(\frac{1}{\sqrt{M}})$, for large $M$. If a regularization of this matrix is employed at each time-step, the rate is close to $O(\frac{1}{\sqrt[3]{M}})$ for large $M$. On the other hand, in case the regularization occurs only when the smallest singular value of the Gramian is smaller than a prescribed positive threshold, the stochastic convergence rate is located between the aforementioned results. Furthermore, in the case of general diffusion, we provide an easy-to-implement convergent algorithm. Concerning the DLR Euler-Maruyama and the DLR Projector Splitting for Euler-Maruyama (EM), these results are not straightforward to derive and a brief discussion on why this is a challenging task is provided, too. Numerical simulations will complete this analysis.

</details>


### [157] [An optimal order fractional backward collocation method for adjoint Volterra integro-differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20356)
*Mahmoud A. Zaky*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对具有弱奇异核的伴随Volterra积分微分方程，提出并分析了一种最优阶分数阶后向配置方法。通过刻画解在终点处的奇异行为，构造终点分级网格与适配奇性的分数阶局部逼近空间，导出了解及其导数的最优收敛与超收敛估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后向Volterra结构与弱奇异核共同导致解在终点端点出现分数幂奇异性，降低了精确解的正则性，使标准多项式配置方法出现阶数退化。因此需要一种能适配终点奇异性、恢复最优收敛阶的数值方法。

Method: 首先建立正则性结果，刻画解在终点处的奇异行为（解连续可微，但二阶导数在终点可能具有弱奇异性）；基于该正则结构引入终点分级网格，构造局部逼近空间适配端点奇异性的分数阶后向配置格式；随后对解及其导数严格推导收敛性与超收敛性估计，并给出分数阶参数与网格分级指数的选取策略以实现最优收敛阶。

Result: 证明了所提格式对解及其导数的收敛与超收敛估计；在适当选取分数阶参数与网格分级指数时，方法达到由局部逼近阶决定的最优收敛阶；数值实验验证了理论预测，表明方法对伴随弱奇异Volterra积分微分方程具有精度与有效性。

Conclusion: 通过终点分级网格与分数阶局部逼近空间的设计，可有效克服弱奇异核引起的终点奇异性所导致的阶数退化问题，实现最优阶收敛，为伴随弱奇异Volterra积分微分方程提供了一种精度高、理论保证完善的数值方法。

Abstract: This paper develops and analyzes an optimal-order fractional backward collocation method for adjoint Volterra integro-differential equations with weakly singular kernels. The backward Volterra structure, together with the weakly singular kernel, induces fractional-power singularities at the terminal endpoint, thereby reducing the classical regularity of the exact solution and causing order deterioration in standard polynomial collocation methods. We first establish a regularity result that characterizes the terminal singular behavior of the solution, showing that the solution is continuously differentiable, whereas its second derivative may exhibit a weak singularity at the terminal endpoint. Motivated by this regularity structure, we introduce a terminally graded mesh and construct a fractional backward collocation scheme whose local approximation space is adapted to the endpoint singularity. Rigorous convergence and superconvergence estimates are derived for both the solution and its derivative. With suitable choices of the fractional parameter and the mesh-grading exponent, the proposed method attains the optimal convergence orders dictated by the local approximation degree. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical predictions and demonstrate the accuracy and effectiveness of the method for adjoint weakly singular Volterra integro-differential equations.

</details>


### [158] [Adaptive Sparse-grid Discontinuous Galerkin Approximations the Bhatnagar--Gross--Krook Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20395)
*Stefan Schnake,Miroslav Stoyanov,Eirik Endeve,Cory Hauck*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究BGK动理学方程的自适应稀疏网格间断Galerkin离散方法，通过混合插值策略保证守恒性，可将活跃自由度降低数倍至几个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BGK模型定义在四维和六维相空间，标准DG方法受维数灾难限制而不切实际，需要能随时间自适应的压缩表示；同时，结构保持的BGK模拟中高效且不破坏守恒的Maxwellian求值是核心障碍。

Method: 采用自适应稀疏网格DG方法求解BGK模型；提出一种混合插值策略，利用速度可分离性恢复正确的离散碰撞不变量，并证明自适应稀疏网格上离散碰撞算子的守恒性；通过比较自适应自由度与全网格DG方法量化精度与压缩效果。

Result: 在流体与稀薄两种区域下，该方法能给出准确且物理相关的解，并可分辨陡峭梯度；活跃自由度降低数倍至数个数量级，六维算例中降低幅度最大。测试算例包括弛豫问题、多维Sod激波管以及剪切/膨胀流动。

Conclusion: 自适应稀疏网格策略在保持离散守恒性的同时显著压缩自由度，适用于高维BGK模拟；所有计算均基于开源ASGarD自适应稀疏网格DG库实现。

Abstract: This work studies adaptive sparse-grid discontinuous Galerkin (DG) discretizations for the Bhatnagar--Gross--Krook (BGK) model, a kinetic equation posed in four- and six-dimensional phase-space. Standard DG methods are rendered impractical for the BGK model by the curse of dimensionality, motivating compressed representations that adapt to the solution in time. Using the adaptive sparse-grid DG method, we quantify accuracy and compression by comparing the adaptive degrees of freedom to full-grid DG methods and by assessing the resulting kinetic and fluid quantities in both fluid and rarefied regimes. Test cases include a relaxation problem, a multidimensional Sod shock tube, and shear/expansion flows used in prior low-rank BGK studies. To build an efficient Maxwellian evaluation without violating conservation, a central obstacle for structure-perserving BGK simulations, we introduce a hybrid interpolation strategy that exploits velocity separability to recover the correct discrete collision invariants and prove conservation of the resulting discrete collision operator on adaptive sparse grids. Our results show that the adaptive sparse-grid strategy can recover accurate and physically relevant solutions with sharp gradients, and the method reduces the active degrees of freedom by factors ranging from several-fold to several orders of magnitude, with the largest reductions occurring in the six-dimensional examples. All computations are performed with the open-source ASGarD adaptive sparse-grid DG library.

</details>


### [159] [A Hybrid High-Order Method for the Elasticity Problem with Linear Slip Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20444)
*Erik Burman,Peiqi Huang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种用于含线性滑移界面（弹簧型界面条件）线弹性问题的混合高阶（HHO）方法，采用多项式未知量、局部对称应变重构与界面稳定化，实现了对全范围柔度参数的统一处理，并证明方法具有最优收敛率、参数无关性及无锁死特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线弹性问题中界面条件常为弹簧型（位移跳跃与牵引力通过柔度张量成正比），涵盖从完全粘合到无牵引的整个柔度范围。经典方法往往需针对不同柔度区间分别处理，或需附加跳跃未知量，且易受锁死现象影响。因此需要一个统一、稳健且无锁死的离散格式。

Method: 设计混合高阶（HHO）方法：网格贴合界面，允许一般多面体单元；离散未知量为面片上次数 k≥1 与单元内次数 k+1 的多项式。两项创新为：融入界面条件的局部对称应变重构，以及基于正则化界面刚度 S_h、借鉴 Hansbo 思想构造的界面稳定化。单套格式即可覆盖从完全粘合（α=β=0，界面稳定化起 Nitsche 型罚作用）到无牵引（α,β→+∞）的全部柔度范围，且无需附加跳跃未知量。

Result: 证明了离散双线性形式的强制性；能量范数下误差收敛阶为 h^{k+1}，L² 范数下为 h^{k+2}，且常数独立于柔度参数与 Lamé 系数 λ，因而方法无锁死。数值算例与对比验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该 HHO 方法为含线性滑移界面的线弹性问题提供了统一、无锁死且最优收敛的离散框架，界面条件自然融入应变重构与稳定化中，鲁棒性和精度均由理论与数值实验共同证实。

Abstract: We design and analyse a hybrid high-order (HHO) method for the linear elasticity problem with a linear slip interface, i.e.\ with interface conditions of spring type, in which the jump of the displacement across the interface is proportional to the traction through a compliancy tensor $\bK=α\bI+(β-α)\udl{n}\otimes\udl{n}$. The mesh is fitted to the interface, the discrete unknowns are polynomials of degree $k\ge 1$ on the mesh faces and of degree $k+1$ in the mesh cells, and general polytopal cells are allowed. The two novelties of the method lie in the local symmetric strain reconstruction, which incorporates the interface condition, and in the interface stabilisation, which is built from a regularised interface stiffness $\bS_h$ in the spirit of Hansbo and Hansbo \cite{HH04}. As a consequence, a single formulation covers the whole range of compliancies, from the perfectly bonded interface $α=β=0$, where the interface stabilisation acts as a Nitsche-type penalty, to the traction-free interface obtained as $α,β\to+\infty$, and no unknown is attached to the jump. We prove that the discrete bilinear form is coercive and that the errors converge as $h^{k+1}$ in the energy norm and as $h^{k+2}$ in the $L^2$ norm, with constants that are independent of the compliancy parameters and of the Lamé coefficient $λ$, so that the method is also locking free. Various numerical examples and comparisons are provided to confirm the theoretical results.

</details>


### [160] [Incremental Column Subset Selection via Conditional Determinantal Point Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20556)
*Laura Grigori,Zhipeng Xue*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文从行列式点过程（DPP）视角研究列子集选择问题，提出条件DPP与多阶段ARP（MSARP）算法，并给出Frobenius范数逼近误差的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 列子集选择的目标是选取少量代表性列以精确逼近给定矩阵。现有算法（如ARP）缺乏增量式选择能力，作者希望借助DPP与现有算法的联系，支持增量列选择并保持精度。

Method: 引入条件DPP，可适用于任意列选择策略；将其与自适应随机旋转（ARP）结合，提出多阶段ARP（MSARP）算法；并设计一种自适应确定所选列数的定精度ARP变体。

Result: 建立了这些方法在期望Frobenius范数逼近误差上的理论保证。在列子集选择与Nyström逼近的数值实验中，MSARP达到与标准ARP相当的精度，同时支持增量选择。

Conclusion: DPP与条件DPP为列子集选择提供了统一视角，MSARP在保持ARP精度的同时实现增量式列选择，定精度变体可自适应确定列数。

Abstract: Column subset selection aims to seek a small set of representative columns that accurately approximates a given matrix. We study this problem from the perspective of determinantal point processes (DPPs) and their relation to several existing algorithms. To support incremental column selection, we introduce conditional DPPs, which can be applied to any column selection strategy. In particular, we combine it with adaptive randomized pivoting(ARP), and develop a multi-stage ARP (MSARP) algorithm. We establish theoretical guarantees on the expected Frobenius-norm approximation errors for these methods. In addition, we propose a fixed-precision variant of ARP that adaptively determines the number of selected columns. Numerical experiments on column subset selection and Nyström approximation show that MSARP achieves accuracy comparable to standard ARP while enabling incremental selection.

</details>


### [161] [Beyond PINNs: A Unified Gauss--Newton and Petrov--Galerkin Framework for Neural and Hybrid PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20641)
*Nilo Schwencke,Roland Maier*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将物理信息神经网络（PINN）与有限元方法（FEM）的数值逼近纳入同一视角：通过有限族线性测量对泛函Gauss–Newton问题进行离散化，并借助适当的对偶配对将线性测量表示为测试函数，从而得到Petrov–Galerkin离散。该框架可恢复逐点配点与自然梯度构造作为特例，并将测试函数的选择显式化为算法设计选择。针对椭圆问题，可自然导出弱残差格式及混合有限元–神经构造，数值实验验证了框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN通常以逐点强残差最小化训练，而FEM基于弱变分表述及离散后的有限维系统，二者范式不同、缺乏统一理论联系。作者希望建立一个共同框架，阐明两者的内在关系，并把测试函数的选择从隐含做法提升为显式的算法设计维度。

Method: 以有限族线性测量对泛函Gauss–Newton问题进行离散化；通过适当的对偶配对，将线性测量表示为测试函数，使所得Gauss–Newton系统恰好成为线性化泛函问题的Petrov–Galerkin离散。该视角统一了逐点配点与自然梯度构造，并将其作为特例纳入。随后将框架专门化到椭圆问题，导出弱残差格式以及作用于互补逼近空间的混合有限元–神经构造。

Result: 理论分析表明，逐点配点和自然梯度构造是该框架的特定情形，测试函数选择成为显式设计选项。在椭圆问题上，框架自然给出弱残差表述与混合有限元–神经近似。数值实验支持该框架，并展示弱Gauss–Newton格式与混合有限元–神经近似的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为PINN与FEM提供了一个基于线性测量离散化与Petrov–Galerkin表述的统一理论基础，把测试函数选择显式化，并在椭圆问题上给出弱残差与混合有限元–神经构造，数值实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks and finite element methods provide two different paradigms for the numerical approximation of partial differential equations: the former are commonly trained by minimizing pointwise strong residuals, whereas the latter are naturally built from weak variational formulations and the finite-dimensional systems obtained after discretization. In this work, we introduce a common framework based on the discretization of functional Gauss--Newton problems by finite families of linear measurements. We show that, through an appropriate duality pairing, the linear measurements can be represented by test functions. The resulting Gauss--Newton system is then precisely a Petrov--Galerkin discretization of the linearized functional problem. This perspective recovers pointwise collocation and natural-gradient constructions as particular cases, while making the choice of test functions an explicit algorithmic design choice. We specialize this framework to elliptic problems, where it naturally leads to weak residual formulations and to a hybrid finite element--neural construction acting on complementary approximation spaces. Numerical experiments support the proposed framework and demonstrate the effectiveness of weak Gauss--Newton formulations and hybrid finite element--neural approximations.

</details>


### [162] [A Unified Framework for Wasserstein Convergence of ULMC Methods beyond Log-Concavity: Old and New](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20713)
*Wanjie Lyu,Xiaojie Wang,Bin Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"通用"预测-校正框架，统一了Euler型、UBU型与随机化欠阻尼Langevin蒙特卡洛(ULMC)格式，并构造了两类新低代价积分器(LC-RIs与LC-UBUIs)及其无指数变体，同时建立长时间误差分析框架，给出各格式的非渐近Wasserstein误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维概率分布采样在计算统计、科学计算与机器学习中日益重要，基于欠阻尼Langevin动力学(ULD)的ULMC方法是一类高效算法。现有Euler型、UBU型与随机化方案通常需要较多梯度评估或高斯采样，计算代价高，且缺乏统一的理论分析框架与长时间误差界。因此作者希望统一这些格式并降低单步代价。

Method: 提出"通用"预测-校正公式，通过选择不同方法参数统一Euler型、UBU型与随机化方案；由此导出两类低代价积分器LC-RIs与LC-UBUIs，以及基于多项式与有理逼近的无指数变体；建立概率度量下一般离散格式的长时间误差分析框架，用于推导各格式的非渐近Wasserstein误差界。

Result: 新UBU型与随机化方案每次迭代仅需一次梯度评估和两个高斯采样，显著低于现有对应方法。在特定光滑性与非对数凹条件下，得到非渐近W1误差界，收敛阶为：Euler型O(d^{1/2}h)、UBU型O(d h^2)、随机化型O(d^{1/2}h^{3/2})；在强凸设定下，同样的误差界可在W2距离下复现。数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该工作通过统一的预测-校正视角整合了多种ULMC离散格式，并给出低代价新算法及统一的长时间误差分析，理论上揭示了不同格式的收敛阶差异，实验与理论一致。

Abstract: As a fundamental task across computational statistics, scientific computing and machine learning, sampling from high-dimensional probability distributions has received increasing attention in recent years. Numerous sampling algorithms have been proposed, among which underdamped Langevin Monte Carlo (ULMC) methods based on underdamped Langevin dynamics (ULD) have emerged as a class of efficient ones. In this work, we introduce a ``universal" predictor-corrector formulation that bridges Euler-type, UBU-type and randomized schemes through different choices of method parameters. Notably, the ``universal" integrator induces two novel classes of low-cost integrators, termed low-cost randomized integrators (LC-RIs) and low-cost UBU integrators (LC-UBUIs), as well as their exponential-free variants based on polynomial and rational approximations. The resulting new UBU-type and randomized schemes require only one gradient evaluation and two Gaussians per iteration, considerably reducing the number of gradient evaluations or Gaussians per iteration required by existing counterparts. Further, a general framework of long-time error analysis is developed for general discretization schemes in a probability metric. Under certain smoothness and non-log-concavity conditions, we rely on the unified framework to establish non-asymptotic $\mathcal{W}_1$-error bounds of both old and new schemes, revealing convergence rates of order $\mathcal{O}(d^{\frac{1}{2}}h)$ for Euler-type schemes, order $\mathcal{O}(d h^2)$ for UBU-type ones and order $\mathcal{O}(d^{\frac{1}{2}}h^{\frac{3}{2}})$ for randomized ones. In the strongly convex setting, the same non-asymptotic error bounds can be recovered in $\mathcal{W}_2$-distance. Numerical experiments corroborate the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [163] [Sensitivity Calculus and its Numerical Implementation for Multi-D Hyperbolic Balance Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20770)
*Olivia Dreßen,Michael Herty,Adrian Kolb,Siegfried Müller*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 研究多维标量平衡律解对初值扰动的敏感性，用"法向位移"表征间断超曲面扰动，推导广义切向量演化方程并设计数值方法，二维实验验证一阶精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度优化与双曲型平衡律约束反问题都依赖可靠的一阶敏感性信息；但初值扰动会同时改变解的平滑部分与激波位置，导致解算子的经典差商即使在1D也一般不在L¹收敛；而1D的广义切向量方法难以直接推广到多维，因为需要间断超曲面的几何描述。

Method: 以法向位移表示间断超曲面的扰动，并计入由位移引起的法方向变化，由此得到广义切向量各分量的演化方程；基于该演算框架，构造用于计算解关于初值一阶变分的数值方法。

Result: 二维数值实验证实所提近似达到预期的一阶精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的几何化广义切向量框架可将一维敏感性分析推广到多维平衡律，为双曲约束下的一阶灵敏度计算提供了可行且精度可靠的工具。

Abstract: We investigate the sensitivity of solutions to multi-dimensional scalar balance laws with respect to perturbations of the initial data analytically and numerically. Reliable first-order sensitivity information is essential in gradient-based optimization and inverse problems constrained by hyperbolic balance laws. In hyperbolic problems, such perturbations affect both the smooth components of the solution and the locations of shocks. Consequently, classical difference quotients of the solution operator generally fail to converge in $L^1$, even in one space dimension. Although generalized tangent-vector techniques have been developed for one-dimensional problems, extending these ideas to multiple space dimensions is considerably more challenging because it requires a geometric description of hypersurfaces of discontinuity.
  We represent perturbations of hypersurfaces of discontinuity by normal displacements and account for the induced variation of the normal direction. This representation yields evolution equations for the components of a generalized tangent vector. Based on this calculus, we develop a numerical method for computing first-order variations with respect to the initial data. Numerical experiments in two space dimensions confirm the expected first-order accuracy of the resulting approximation.

</details>

### [164] [Recent benchmarks in the Analysis Grand Challenge and integration with Combine (and HS3)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19753)
*Peter Fackeldey,Massimiliano Galli,Hannah Havel,Alexander Held,Iason Krommydas,Oksana Shadura,Nick Smith*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: AGC以现代Python包实现HEP分析流程，本文报告数据处理包性能优化基准、Combine与PyHEP生态（coffea-casa）集成，以及HS3在Combine中的集成计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGC作为高能物理（HEP）分析的参考实现，其主流程依赖Python生态；同时CMS超90%的已发表分析使用Combine，而其不属于PyHEP生态。为统一工作流、复用生态并支持语言无关的似然表示，需将Combine与AGC/PyHEP集成并引入HS3标准。

Method: 以AGC为范例，利用更新后的数据处理包（coffea、uproot、awkward-array）开展性能基准测试；构建Combine在专用基础设施coffea-casa上的集成与运行示例；规划并示例化HS3在Combine中的集成。

Result: 给出了覆盖数据处理包近期优化收益的性能基准结果；提供了Combine在coffea-casa中集成运行的示例；提出了HS3与Combine的集成示例与方案。

Conclusion: 通过将Combine纳入PyHEP生态并以AGC为参考，可实现端到端的Python化HEP分析链，并借助HS3实现语言无关的似然表示与框架互换，提升性能与互操作性。

Abstract: The Analysis Grand Challenge (AGC) showcases an example of HEP analysis. Its reference implementation uses modern Python packages to realize the main steps, from data access to statistical model building and fitting. The packages used for data handling and processing (coffea, uproot, awkward-array) have recently undergone a series of performance optimizations. While not being part of the HEP Python (PyHEP) ecosystem, the Combine tool is a pillar of CMS analyses, covering more than 90% of the analyses published in the last few years. As such, it is necessary to have Combine integrated in the PyHEP ecosystem, using the AGC as example. This project also includes, in the long-term, providing support and integration for the High Energy Physics Statistics Serialization Standard (HS3), as a way to have a language-independent way of representing the likelihood and use different frameworks interchangeably. In these proceedings we cover part of the recent work performed on the AGC and Combine, including: performance benchmarks, covering benefits introduced by the recent improvements in the data processing packages; examples of how Combine can be integrated and run in a dedicated infrastructure (coffea-casa); and examples and plans to integrate HS3 in Combine.

</details>


### [165] [Status and Prospects of the HEP Statistical Inference Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19761)
*Massimiliano Galli*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文总结了LHC主要实验（CMS、ATLAS）统计工具现状与未来规划，重点关注RooFit自动微分、与JAX等现代库的互操作性以及通过HS3实现跨框架通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理（HEP）统计分析传统上依赖RooFit和RooStats，但如今面临统计模型复杂度激增（涉及数百个感兴趣参数和数千个干扰参数）、大规模似然最小化需要可扩展性能，以及跨日益多样化的工具、计算硬件、框架与库（尤其机器学习领域）互操作性需求等前所未有的挑战。

Method: 综述LHC主要实验（CMS、ATLAS）所使用统计工具的现状与未来规划，重点涵盖ROOT领域改进（RooFit自动微分）、与现代库（JAX）的互操作性，以及通过HS3实现跨框架通信。

Result: 作为一篇现状与规划综述，本文梳理了上述统计工具在应对复杂度、可扩展性与互操作性挑战方面的进展方向，具体围绕RooFit自动微分、JAX互操作和HS3框架通信展开。

Conclusion: 面对统计模型复杂度增长、大规模似然最小化性能需求及生态互操作性挑战，LHC实验的统计工具正朝RooFit自动微分、现代库（JAX）集成与HS3跨框架通信方向发展。

Abstract: Statistical inference is a crucial part of HEP analyses. Historically based on RooFit and RooStats, the statistical tools used by the experiments are now facing unprecedented challenges, such as the rapidly growing complexity of statistical models (involving hundreds of parameters of interest and thousands of nuisance parameters), the need for scalable performance in large likelihood minimizations, and the demand for interoperability across an increasingly diverse ecosystem of tools, computational hardware, and frameworks and libraries, especially the ones developed and used within the machine learning world. This contribution summarizes status and future plans for the statistical tools used by some of the main LHC experiments (CMS, ATLAS), with a focus on improvements coming from the ROOT world (RooFit automatic differentiation), interoperability with modern libraries (JAX) and communication across frameworks (HS3).

</details>


### [166] [Constraints on the Higgs boson total width from on-shell signal-background interference in the $H\toγγ$ decay channel with $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19948)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用ATLAS探测器140 fb$^{-1}$的13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据，通过共振$H\to\gamma\gamma$振幅与连续双光子本底的干涉效应，对希格斯玻色子总衰变宽度$\Gamma_H$进行直接壳上测量，在95%置信水平给出$\Gamma_H<307$ (258) MeV的观测（预期）上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希格斯玻色子总衰变宽度$\Gamma_H$编码了其全部衰变模式；任何超出标准模型（SM）的新粒子衰变都会使$\Gamma_H$高于SM预言的4.1 MeV。现有对$\Gamma_H$的间接离壳约束依赖于关于希格斯耦合的假设，需要一种不依赖此类假设的、对潜在新粒子衰变敏感的直接探测手段。

Method: 利用共振$H\to\gamma\gamma$振幅与连续双光子本底之间的干涉效应，该干涉会以使双光子不变质量分布发生畸变、且畸变形式依赖$\Gamma_H$，从而无需对希格斯耦合做假设。使用ATLAS探测器在LHC Run 2期间采集的$\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV质子-质子对撞数据，总量140 fb$^{-1}$，进行直接的壳上测量。

Result: 在95%置信水平下，给出观测（预期）上限$\Gamma_H<307$ (258) MeV，约为SM预言值的75 (63)倍。

Conclusion: 与间接离壳约束不同，该测量不依赖关于希格斯玻色子耦合的假设，为探测希格斯玻色子可能衰变为新粒子提供了一种新颖且互补的探针。

Abstract: The total decay width of the Higgs boson, $Γ_H$, encodes all of its decay modes; any decay into particles beyond the Standard Model (SM) would increase $Γ_H$ above the SM prediction of 4.1 MeV. In this Letter, a direct, on-shell measurement of $Γ_H$ is reported, exploiting the interference between the resonant $H \to γγ$ amplitude and the continuum diphoton background, which distorts the diphoton invariant mass distribution in a way that depends on $Γ_H$ without requiring assumptions on the Higgs boson couplings. Using 140 fb$^{-1}$ of proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV recorded by the ATLAS detector during Run 2 of the LHC, an observed (expected) upper limit of $Γ_H < 307$ (258) MeV is set at 95% confidence level, approximately 75 (63) times the SM prediction. Unlike indirect off-shell constraints on $Γ_H$, this measurement does not rely on assumptions about the Higgs boson couplings, providing a novel complementary probe sensitive to potential Higgs boson decays into new particles.

</details>


### [167] [IRIS-HEP 2026 Statistical Ecosystem Blueprint White Paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19992)
*Matthew Feickert,Massimiliano Galli*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该白皮书综述高能物理（HEP）统计工具生态的现状，并基于2026年2月IRIS-HEP统计生态蓝图研讨会收集的意见，总结未来研发方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理统计工具生态分散且演进迅速，社区需要一份对现状的共识性梳理以及对未来R&D方向的集体判断，以指导后续工具开发与资源投入。

Method: 以白皮书形式，汇总2026年2月IRIS-HEP统计生态蓝图（blueprint）活动中收集的社区观点，对现有统计工具生态进行现状描述与方向归纳。

Result: 给出了高能物理统计工具生态的当前状态描述，并整理出社区对未来研发方向的若干观点。

Conclusion: 该白皮书为HEP统计工具的后续研发提供了社区层面的方向性参考，但其结论基于研讨会意见汇总，属于观点性总结而非实证研究。

Abstract: This white paper presents the current status of the ecosystem of statistical tools used in High Energy Physics (HEP), and attempts to summarize the views on the future R&D directions. These views have been collected during the latest IRIS-HEP statistical ecosystem blueprint, which took place in February 2026.

</details>


### [168] [Coverage Is Not Ordering: Ancillary Leakage and Representation Dependence in Covariance-Based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20007)
*Tommaso Dorigo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 协方差矩阵近似代替真实统计模型会改变似然比排序，引入本应被精确推断忽略的辅助性残差信息；在10%相对不确定度下约7.6%实验的置信决策发生改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协方差矩阵常被用作测量统计模型的紧凑替代，但既有研究多关注对拟合值或其不确定度的影响，忽略了它是否会改变实验本身的似然比排序（即推断结论的顺序结构）。作者希望厘清该替代在参数推断中引入的额外信息，并将其与Peelle Pertinent Puzzle及辅助统计量经典文献联系起来。

Method: 构建可解析的相关测量模型，将数据分解为信息成分与辅助性残差分歧；用等校准接受域对称差的概率质量量化排序差异，即重复实验中关于被测参数值的置信决策发生改变的实验比例；并在小不确定度区域进行阶数分析，区分排序差异与常规、辅助条件覆盖缺陷的阶数。进一步推广到不等统计不确定度及多于两个测量的情形。

Result: 精确似然推断不使用辅助残差分歧，而数据依赖协方差矩阵及非线性变换后的高斯重构会将其重新引入参数推断。小不确定度下排序差异对总相对不确定度为一级，而常规与辅助条件覆盖缺陷为二级；在“覆盖盲”点，主导覆盖差异消失但排序差异仍非零。代表性盲例中总相对不确定度10%时，尽管两种程序均精确校准，约7.6%实验改变置信决策。

Conclusion: 协方差近似可为真实模型中辅助的拟合优度统计量制造推断相关性，从而改变同一信息内容所报告的置信区间；该机制将Peelle Pertinent Puzzle与辅助性及相关子集经典文献统一起来，并推广至不等不确定度与多测量情形。

Abstract: A covariance matrix is often used as a compact surrogate for the statistical model of a measurement. We show that this replacement can change not only a fitted value or its uncertainty, but also the likelihood-ratio ordering of the experiment itself. In a tractable correlated-measurement model, the data separate into an informative component and an ancillary residual disagreement. Exact likelihood inference therefore does not use that disagreement, whereas data-dependent covariance matrices and Gaussian reconstructions after nonlinear transformations can reintroduce it into parameter inference.
  We quantify the resulting change by the probability mass of the symmetric difference of equally calibrated acceptance regions - the fraction of repeated experiments for which the confidence decision about a tested parameter value changes. In the small-uncertainty regime the ordering discrepancy is generically first order in the total relative uncertainty, whereas conventional and ancillary-conditioned coverage defects begin at second order. At 'coverage-blind' points the leading coverage difference vanishes while the ordering discrepancy remains nonzero. In a representative blind case with 10% total relative uncertainty, about 7.6% of experiments change their confidence decision despite exact calibration of both procedures.
  The same mechanism connects Peelle's Pertinent Puzzle to the classical literature on ancillarity and relevant subsets: a covariance approximation can manufacture inferential relevance for a goodness-of-fit statistic that is ancillary in the true model. We show directly how this changes the confidence interval reported for the same informative content, and extend the analysis beyond equal statistical uncertainties and beyond the two-measurement case.

</details>


### [169] [A heterogeneous and vectorized sequence for the HL-LHC full tracking reconstruction of the CMS experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20135)
*Emmanouil Vourliotis*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CMS实验Phase-2在线事件重建及离线跟踪主迭代的新基线跟踪策略，通过结合GPU优化的Patatrack、LST算法与CPU向量化的mkFit算法，应对HL-LHC高堆积带来的计算挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HL-LHC预期每次束团交叉将产生空前数量的同时碰撞（高堆积），给CMS实验的在线与离线径迹重建带来前所未有的计算挑战，需要新的跟踪策略以在有限计算资源下维持物理性能。

Method: 提出一种组合式跟踪序列：将面向GPU并行执行优化的Patatrack与LST算法，与面向CPU高效性能向量化的mkFit算法相结合，构成Phase-2在线事件重建及离线跟踪主迭代的新基线策略，并引入位移径迹（displaced tracking）与机器学习技术。

Result: 该组合方案降低了计算资源需求，同时通过纳入位移径迹跟踪并日益利用机器学习技术，提升了物理覆盖范围（physics reach）。

Conclusion: 该组合跟踪策略为应对HL-LHC极端计算挑战提供了有效解决方案，兼顾计算效率与物理性能，成为CMS Phase-2跟踪的新基线。

Abstract: This contribution presents the new baseline strategy for the Phase-2 tracking of the CMS experiment for online event reconstruction, and for the main iteration of offline tracking. This tracking sequence takes advantage of the combination of cutting-edge tracking algorithms that are either optimized for parallel execution on GPUs (Patatrack and LST), or vectorized for efficient CPU performance (mkFit). Such a combined approach offers an effective solution to deal with the unprecedented computational challenges caused by the large number of simultaneous collisions per bunch crossing expected at the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider (HL-LHC). The proposed combination not only reduces the computational resource requirements but also enhances the physics reach by incorporating displaced tracking and increasingly leveraging machine learning techniques.

</details>
