Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 在BESIII实验的e⁺e⁻连续区数据中，首次观测到显著的double-s s̄产生，比值σ(φ s s̄+anything)/σ(φ+anything)=(40.4±1.7±1.5)%，与Belle和BaBar报道的double-c c̄产生水平相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双夸克对（如double-c c̄）产生是检验QCD和夸克强子化机制的重要过程。Belle和BaBar已报道了double-c c̄产生，但其速率高于理论预言，机制尚不清楚。作者希望通过测量双奇异夸克对（double-s s̄）产生来提供新的实验约束，从而加深对夸克强子化和QCD的理解。

Method: 利用BESIII实验在√s=3.08 GeV采集的e⁺e⁻对撞数据，通过探测与含s夸克或s s̄对的强子伴随产生的瞬发φ介子，测量e⁺e⁻→φ+X(s s̄)过程，其中X(s s̄)包括η、η′介子以及奇异介子对K⁺K⁻、K⁺K*⁻、K⁻K*⁺、K⁰K̄⁰和K⁰K̄*⁰+c.c.。由此确定比值σ(e⁺e⁻→φ s s̄+anything)/σ(e⁺e⁻→φ+anything)。

Result: 测得比值为(40.4±1.7_stat.±1.5_syst.)%，表明在e⁺e⁻连续区存在显著的双s s̄产生。该双s s̄产生水平与Belle和BaBar报道的双c c̄产生水平相当，而后者的理论计算预言速率偏低。

Conclusion: BESIII对双s s̄产生的实验测量为理解夸克强子化和QCD提供了重要信息，并与double-c c̄产生中理论与实验的差异相呼应，有望推动相关理论模型的发展。

Abstract: We report the observation of significant double-$s\bar{s}$ production in the $e^+e^-$ continuum, based on the measurement of prompt $φ$ mesons produced in association with hadrons containing an $s$ quark or an $s\bar{s}$ pair. In an analysis of $e^+e^-$ collision data collected by the BESIII experiment at $\sqrt{s}=3.08~\textrm{GeV}$, the ratio $σ(e^+e^- \to φ s\bar{s}+\textrm{anything}) / σ(e^+e^-\rightarrowφ+\textrm{anything})$ is determined to be $(40.4\pm1.7_{\rm stat.}\pm1.5_{\rm syst.})\%$ by detecting and measuring $e^+e^-\toφ+ X(s\bar{s})$, where $X(s\bar{s})$ denotes an $η$ meson, an $η^{\prime}$ meson, or one of the strange-meson pairs $K^+K^-$, $K^+K^{*-}$, $K^-K^{*+}$, $K^0\bar{K}^{0}$, and $K^0\bar{K}^{*0}+\textrm{c.c.}$. The level of double-$s\bar{s}$ production is in line with the double-$c\bar{c}$ production reported by the Belle and \babar\ collaborations, for which theoretical calculations predict lower rates. The experimental measurement of double $s\bar{s}$ production at BESIII can shed light on the understanding of quark hadronization and QCD.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Three-Dimensional Coulomb Discrete Spectrum via Symplectic Geometry and Phase-Space Constraints: A Geometric Framework for Condensed-Matter Coulomb-Like Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19159)
*Gang Zheng,Peng Chen,Mengli Wang,Wenqi Xue,Benniu Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种几何相空间框架，利用辛流形的群复合律构造经典积分核，并结合三条几何约束，在不做算子对角化的情况下精确恢复三维库仑束缚态的离散能级与壳层简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统获取库仑型束缚态离散能级的方法（薛定谔方程直接对角化）依赖显式算子计算，而半经典近似在低能区（如基态附近）失效。作者希望寻找一条既能给出精确谱结构、又具有对称性透明性、且能规避半经典误差的替代路径。

Method: 采用 KS（Kustaanheimo–Stiefel）正则化，将负能开普勒轨道映射为四维各向同性谐振子。利用辛流的群复合律，由经典两点作用量与 Van Vleck 振幅构造积分核；其无穷小生成元满足精确线性演化方程（作者称之为 quadratic closure）。随后施加三条几何约束——无穷远衰减、原点正则性、Hopf 纤维化下的纤维不变性——求解谱结构。

Result: 在该框架下精确复现了 E_n/E_1=1/n^2 的能标关系与 g_n=n^2 的简并度；相位尺度 α 仅设定作用量单位，所有谱结构特征与其取值无关。对二次哈密顿量，经典核即使在基态能量下也零半经典误差，几何约束可直接识别对称性保护的简并。

Conclusion: 作者将该方法应用于浅杂质态、莫尔超晶格与半经典输运，展示了几何筛选规则可简化凝聚态环境中的谱分析，为谱数据提供了一条对称性透明、无需对角化的获取途径。

Abstract: Discrete energy-level structures in Coulomb-like localized bound states are conventionally obtained by direct diagonalization of the Schrödinger equation or by semiclassical approximations that fail at low energies. Here we develop a geometric phase-space framework that recovers the exact discrete energy levels and shell degeneracy of three-dimensional Coulomb bound states from classical symplectic geometry and global boundary constraints, bypassing explicit operator diagonalization. Using Kustaanheimo--Stiefel regularization, we map negative-energy Kepler orbits to four-dimensional isotropic harmonic oscillators. The group composition law of the symplectic flow determines an integral kernel built from the classical two-point action and Van Vleck amplitude, whose infinitesimal generator satisfies an exact linear evolution equation---a property we term \textit{quadratic closure}. Imposing three geometric constraints---decay at infinity, regularity at the origin, and fiber invariance under the Hopf fibration---we recover the exact $E_n/E_1=1/n^2$ scaling and $g_n=n^2$ degeneracy. The phase scale $α$ sets the action unit; all spectral structural features are independent of its value. This framework offers a symmetry-transparent route to spectral data: for quadratic Hamiltonians, the classical kernel incurs zero semiclassical error even at ground-state energies, and the geometric constraints directly identify symmetry-protected degeneracies without diagonalization. We apply the method to shallow impurity states, moiré superlattices, and semiclassical transport, demonstrating how geometric screening rules simplify spectral analysis in condensed-matter environments.

</details>


### [172] [The spectral $ζ$-function of Sturm--Liouville operators with $N$ generalized point potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19322)
*Guglielmo Fucci,Jonathan Stanfill*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究带 $N$ 个广义点势的正则与奇异 Sturm–Liouville 算子的谱 zeta 函数，借助特征函数的围道积分表示并给出解析延拓与 $\zeta$-正则化行列式的算例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点相互作用可刻画为定义在 $N+1$ 个相邻区间链上的 Sturm–Liouville 自伴扩张，此类模型在谱理论中具有重要性，需要系统构造其谱 zeta 函数并处理其在原点的解析延拓问题。

Method: 将点相互作用表述为 $SL(2,\mathbb{R})$ 匹配条件联系相邻区间的广义边界值；以此构造谱 zeta 函数，并表示为含适当特征函数的围道积分；通过具体例子演示向原点邻域的解析延拓方法。

Result: 给出了带 $N$ 个广义点势的 Sturm–Liouville 算子谱 zeta 函数的围道积分表达式；在特定例子中显式完成了向原点邻域的解析延拓，并计算了 $\zeta$-正则化泛函行列式。

Conclusion: 该工作为含广义点势的 Sturm–Liouville 算子提供了谱 zeta 函数的统一构造框架，并通过实例验证了解析延拓与 $\zeta$-正则化行列式的可行性。

Abstract: This work analyzes the spectral zeta function associated with regular and singular Sturm--Liouville operators endowed with $N$ generalized point potentials. The point interactions are characterized as a particular class of self-adjoint extensions of Sturm--Liouville operators defined on a chain of $N+1$ adjacent intervals. Each interaction is described by $SL(2,\R)$ matching conditions relating generalized boundary values across neighboring intervals. We construct the spectral zeta function associated with Sturm--Liouville operators endowed with $N$ generalized point potentials in terms of a contour integral involving an appropriate characteristic function. The process of analytic continuation of the spectral zeta function to a neighborhood of the origin is illustrated by means of specific examples where the $ζ$-regularized functional determinant is also explicitly computed.

</details>


### [173] [Site-scale, Multi-resolution WRF Wind Modeling with Observational Nudging for Methane Emission Monitoring in the Permian Basin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19442)
*Ehsan Kharazmi,Erin Menezes,George Karniadakis,Anantha Sundaram,Arash Fathi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该研究构建了针对二叠纪盆地油气设施的多尺度WRF降尺度框架（3 km→1 km→200 m→40 m），内层采用LES模式并同化现场风速观测，以生成用于甲烷源定位与排放速率估算的高分辨率风场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲烷源定位与排放速率估算需要能解析站点尺度时空变化的风场，而单个风速计或粗分辨率业务气象产品无法提供这类信息。

Method: 将逐小时3 km HRRR场通过四重嵌套（3 km、1 km、200 m、40 m）动态降尺度；外层两域使用PBL参数化，内层两域运行于LES模式；在最内层对两台现场风速计的1分钟观测进行仅风场的观测松弛（observational nudging），并考察嵌套反馈与湍流闭合方案选择的影响。

Result: 基准模拟能再现观测风事件的总体演变，但在弱风时段出现漂移；观测松弛结合单向与双向嵌套使风速绝对偏差分别降低65%与87%，均方根误差分别降低33%与44%；松弛还提升了高频能量与可分辨空间梯度，但湍流统计量的改善并不一致。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一条实用的基于物理的途径来生成用于甲烷羽流建模的高分辨率风场，同时强调需要独立的空间观测来验证远离同化传感器处的精度。

Abstract: Accurate methane source localization and emission-rate estimation at oil and gas facilities require wind fields that resolve site-scale spatial and temporal variability, which cannot be characterized by a single anemometer or coarse operational weather products. We develop a multiscale Weather Research and Forecasting (WRF) framework for a Permian Basin facility that dynamically downscales hourly, 3 km High-Resolution Rapid Refresh (HRRR) fields through four nested domains with grid spacings of 3 km, 1 km, 200 m, and 40 m. The two outer domains use planetary-boundary-layer parameterization, whereas the two inner domains operate in large-eddy-simulation mode. One-minute wind observations from two on-site anemometers are assimilated through wind-only observational nudging in the innermost domain, and the effects of nesting feedback and turbulence-closure choices are examined. Simulations for winter and summer 2025 periods are evaluated using near-surface wind-speed time series, power spectra, spatial fields, and statistical metrics. The baseline simulation reproduces the broad evolution of observed wind events but drifts during weak-wind periods. Observational nudging with one- and two-way nesting reduces the absolute bias of wind-speed by %65 at one sensor and %87 at the other, while reducing the corresponding root-mean-square errors by %33 and %44. Nudging also increases high-frequency energy and resolved spatial gradients, although improvements in turbulence statistics are not uniform. These results demonstrate a practical physics-based pathway for generating high-resolution wind fields for methane-plume modeling while emphasizing the need for independent spatial observations to validate accuracy away from assimilated sensors.

</details>


### [174] [Boundary parity in the mirror model on the Manhattan lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19498)
*Jian Gu,Qin Hao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 在Manhattan格点mirror模型上研究"边界-边界轨迹穿过标记连接件的次数为偶数"这一有限域事件，给出有限尺度confinement判据：若某偶数两方格矩形内该事件概率达到普适阈值，则平面上几乎每条轨迹都是周期的。构造精确归一化的三色张量表示，利用有向延续的带符号部分置换结构证明近似压缩的确定性界，从而把数值评估转为严格的有限体积结论。在p=2/5时证明边界奇偶概率随矩形宽度先降后升（非单调），且仍低于充分阈值，未能判定该密度下的平面局域化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: mirror模型中的平面局域化（所有轨迹周期）通常难以严格判定。作者希望把纯数值的有限体积测量提升为严格的有限尺度判据：找到一个局部的、有限可算的事件概率阈值，一旦达到即可推出全局周期行为，从而为平面局域化提供可验证的充分条件。

Method: 定义边界到边界轨迹穿越标记连接件为偶数次的有限域事件；构造精确归一化的三色张量表示，结合有向延续的带符号部分置换结构，给出近似张量压缩的确定性误差界，将数值收缩计算严格化为有限体积陈述；在Manhattan格点上对偶数两方格矩形做张量网络收缩数值实验，并在p=2/5处分析边界奇偶概率随宽度的变化。

Result: 建立有限尺度confinement判据：若某偶数两方格矩形内边界奇偶概率达到普适阈值，则几乎必然平面上所有轨迹周期。在p=2/5处证明边界奇偶概率随矩形宽度非单调，先下降后出现可认证的上升；这些值始终低于充分阈值，故未解决该密度下的平面局域化。实验还刻画了有限尺寸最小值随密度的变化，比较了匹配型与逐元素误差界，并展示了局部mirror改动的端点敏感与协同响应。

Conclusion: 三色张量表示与带符号部分置换误差界构成了把数值证据转为严格有限尺度结论的工具链，并给出一个可验证的周期性充分判据。但该判据的阈值未被满足，平面局域化在p=2/5仍为开放问题。

Abstract: We study a finite-domain event for the mirror model on the Manhattan lattice: every boundary-to-boundary trajectory crosses a marked connector an even number of times, while internal cycles are unrestricted. Our main theorem gives a finite-scale confinement criterion: if this event's probability reaches a universal threshold in one even two-square rectangle, then almost surely every trajectory in the plane is periodic. We construct an exactly normalized three-color tensor representation and prove deterministic bounds for approximate contractions using the signed partial-permutation structure of directed continuations. These tools turn numerical evaluations into rigorous finite-volume statements. At mirror density \(p=2/5\), we prove that the boundary-parity probability is nonmonotone in the rectangle width, with a decrease followed by a certified increase. These values remain below the sufficient confinement threshold and do not resolve planar localization at this density. Further experiments track how the measured finite-size minimum varies with density, compare matching-based and entrywise error bounds, and exhibit endpoint-sensitive and cooperative responses to local mirror changes.

</details>


### [175] [Eberlein convolution as an inner product and quantitative Bombieri--Taylor results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19501)
*Daniel Lenz,Nicolae Strungaru*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Eberlein卷积视为内积，由此得到Cauchy–Schwarz型不等式，从而给出计算衍射理论中Bragg峰的非常一般的Bombieri–Taylor型结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在衍射理论中，Bragg峰的严格计算通常依赖Bombieri–Taylor型定理。作者希望以统一的抽象框架给出一个更一般的结果，揭示这类定理背后的核心机制。

Method: 将Eberlein卷积重新解释为抽象内积，并据此建立一个抽象的Cauchy–Schwarz型不等式，由该不等式导出Bombieri–Taylor型结论及若干推广。

Result: 论证简洁且高度一般，所建立的Cauchy–Schwarz型不等式不仅蕴含经典Bombieri–Taylor型结果，还给出其多种推广形式。

Conclusion: Eberlein卷积的内积视角为Bragg峰的极限计算提供了统一、简洁的抽象基础。

Abstract: We present a simple argument providing a very general Bombieri--Taylor-type result to compute Bragg peaks in diffraction theory as limits. At its abstract core the argument relies on considering the Eberlein convolution as an inner product. This inner product gives an abstract version of a Cauchy--Schwarz type inequality which implies the Bombieri--Taylor-type result as well as generalizations.

</details>


### [176] [A c-transform optimal transport method for high-contrast freeform reflector design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19663)
*Gang Bao,Yixuan Zhang,Jiaqi Yu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于最优传输对偶性的快速c-transform方法，用于远场高对比度自由曲面反射器设计，无需人工背景正则化即可稳定求解含零强度区域的Monge-Ampère型方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高对比度自由曲面反射器设计中，目标强度存在零强度区域会导致Monge-Ampère型方程退化。常用做法是在目标强度上添加正的人工背景以改善方程正则性，但这会不可避免地降低可实现照明对比度，并在本应保持黑暗的区域引入非零强度。

Method: 提出基于最优传输对偶性的快速c-transform方法，将重复的离散c-transform集成到基于测度的对偶优化框架中，在迭代全过程中保持反射器势函数的c-凹性，从而维持迭代点的可容许性。为高效计算离散c-transform，利用其接触结构开发了一种局部化搜索算法。

Result: 证明了局部化搜索算法的精确性与线性复杂度；数值实验表明，该方法能精确实现零背景的高对比度照明目标。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了无人工背景正则化的高对比度自由曲面反射器设计，保持迭代可容许性并在含零强度区域时仍稳定收敛。

Abstract: Design of high-contrast freeform reflectors is challenging, as the presence of zero-intensity regions leads to degeneracy in the associated Monge-Ampere-type equation. A common remedy is to add a positive artificial background to the target intensity, which improves the regularity of the equation. However, this regularization inevitably reduces the achievable illumination contrast and introduces nonzero intensity into regions that are intended to remain dark. We propose a fast c-transform method based on optimal transport duality for high-contrast freeform reflector design in the far field without artificial background regularization. The method integrates repeated discrete c-transforms into a measure-based dual optimization scheme to maintain the c-concavity of the reflector potential throughout the iteration. This preserves the admissibility of the iterates and enables stable convergence for targets with zero-intensity regions. To efficiently compute the discrete c-transforms, we develop a localized search algorithm that exploits their contact structure and prove its exactness and linear complexity. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed method accurately realizes high-contrast illumination targets with zero background.

</details>


### [177] [Contact Large Deviations of Stochastic Vector Bundles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19679)
*D. Y. Zhong*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"接触大偏差理论"，在随机向量丛上用接触1-形式统一导出速率函数、标度累积生成函数与Gallavotti–Cohen型涨落对偶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大偏差理论的速率函数与涨落对称性缺乏几何基础，作者希望用接触几何为其提供统一框架。

Method: 在随机向量丛上构建接触大偏差理论：以约束函数为广义拉格朗日量，由最小约束原理导出动力学；从主方程逐阶构造接触势，得到由不变密度、漂移与涨落张量支配的Hamilton–Jacobi–输运耦合系统；引入时间反演对合J与反向漂移。

Result: 接触路径测度满足大偏差原理，速率函数由约束作用量给出；标度累积生成函数满足平稳本征方程并具Donsker–Varadhan变分刻画；熵产生率为物理可观测量；获得GC型对偶λ_fwd(q)=λ_rev(q+1)+λ_fwd(-1)，可逆情形Ag=0退化为经典一维GC对称。

Conclusion: 接触几何为大偏差速率函数与涨落对称性提供了几何基础，统一了速率函数、SCGF与GC对偶，并推广了经典GC对称性。

Abstract: Large deviation theory lacks a geometric foundation for its rate functions and fluctuation symmetries. This paper develops a contact large deviation theory on stochastic vector bundles, in which the rate function, the scaled cumulant generating function, and a Gallavotti--Cohen-type fluctuation duality all follow from the contact 1-form. The constraint function acts as a generalized Lagrangian; the least constraint principle yields the dynamics, and the contact potential is built order by order from the master equation, producing a coupled Hamilton--Jacobi--transport system governed by the invariant density, drift, and fluctuation tensor. The contact path measure satisfies a large deviation principle with rate function given by the constraint action; the scaled cumulant generating function obeys a stationary eigenvalue equation with a Donsker--Varadhan variational characterization. The entropy production rate, the fluctuation--dissipation combination $e=\tfrac12 g^T Ag-σ$, is the physical observable; the time-reversal involution $J:(t,y,φ)\mapsto(t,y,-φ-\nabla\lnρ)$ with reversed drift $v^{\mathrm{rev}}=-v+Ag$ yields a Gallavotti--Cohen-type duality $λ_{\mathrm{fwd}}(q)=λ_{\mathrm{rev}}(q+1)+λ_{\mathrm{fwd}}(-1)$. The classical one-dimensional GC symmetry is recovered in the reversible case $Ag=0$.

</details>


### [178] [Reconstructability of Inverse Problems under Symmetry: Separating Structural, Effective, and Physical Upper Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19697)
*Isshin Arai*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在既有"重构维数"概念基础上，提出结构、有效、物理重构维数（SRD、ERD、PRD）三级上界体系，用两个对比系统说明重构性在哪个层级丧失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作中"重构维数"仅由对称性决定，是重构自由度的上界；但真实物理系统状态量由因果$O$诱发的物理过程$P_o$生成，状态空间常退化，表示论结构给出的上界往往达不到。因此需要区分"纯对称性决定的上界"与"物理过程实现后的上界"。

Method: 引入三级上界结构：结构重构维数（SRD）由中间空间的表示论结构决定，有效重构维数（ERD）由重构映射的具体设计决定，物理重构维数（PRD）由物理过程$P_o$下状态空间的退化程度决定；对每个不可约分量构成层级$\mathrm{SRD}\ge\mathrm{ERD}\ge\mathrm{PRD}$，以两个间隙定位重构性丧失的层级。通过两个对比系统加以说明：片状颗粒取向动力学（因果为速度梯度）与二体问题（因果本身由对称性定义）。

Result: 建立了$\mathrm{SRD}\ge\mathrm{ERD}\ge\mathrm{PRD}$的分量级层级，两个间隙分别指示重构性在表示论、重构映射设计、物理退化哪一环节流失；两个示例展示了该层级在具体物理逆问题中的适用性。

Conclusion: 该工作为物理逆问题中的重构提供了一个具体框架，将纯对称性上界与实际可实现的重构能力区分开来。

Abstract: In our previous work we introduced the Reconstruction Dimension, an upper bound on the reconstructable degrees of freedom determined solely by the symmetry. In actual physical systems, however, the state quantities are generated through physical processes $P_o$ induced by the cause $O$. The state space is therefore often degenerate, and the upper bound determined by the representation-theoretic structure is not reached. This calls for a distinction between the upper bound determined solely by symmetry and the one realized after the physical process. We therefore introduce a three-level structure of upper bounds: the Structural, Effective, and Physical Reconstruction Dimensions (SRD, ERD, PRD), determined respectively by the representation-theoretic structure of the intermediate space, by the concrete design of the reconstruction map, and by the degeneracy of the state space under the physical process $P_o$. For each irreducible component they form the hierarchy $\mathrm{SRD}\ge\mathrm{ERD}\ge\mathrm{PRD}$, so that the two gaps identify at which level reconstructability is lost. The framework is illustrated through two contrasting systems: the orientation dynamics of flake-like particles, where the cause is a velocity gradinet, and the two-body problem, where the cause itself is defined through the symmetry. This paper provides a concrete framework for reconstruction in physical inverse problems.

</details>


### [179] [Classification of pairs of second-order Hamiltonian operators and hydrodynamic type systems in six components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19785)
*Giorgio Gubbiotti,Lambertus Van Geemen,Pierandrea Vergallo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在射影空间上用交错三形式刻画具有二阶哈密顿结构的流体力学系统，并完成6分量系统的完整分类，得到5个标准形，另对其中一类给出任意维数的推广结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作以射影空间上的交错三形式来刻画具有二阶哈密顿结构的流体力学系统，本文旨在基于该刻画推进6分量系统的分类问题。

Method: 利用非平凡群作用技术，对6分量系统进行等价性约化，得到5个标准形；并对其中一个情形给出适用于任意维数的一般性结果，其几何结构与辛标准形密切相关。

Result: 完成了6分量系统的完整分类，获得5个标准形；并对其中一类情形获得了适用于任意维数的推广结果。

Conclusion: 在射影空间交错三形式框架下可系统地对二阶哈密顿流体力学系统进行分类，所得标准形为后续研究提供了规范形式基础，其中一类的几何结构紧密关联辛标准形。

Abstract: Following a recent characterisation of hydrodynamic systems with second-order Hamiltonian structure in terms of alternating three-forms on the projective space, we present a complete classification of systems in 6 components. We use non-trivial group action techniques to obtain 5 canonical forms. A general result for every dimension is also obtained for one of these cases, whose geometric structure is strictly related to the symplectic canonical form.

</details>


### [180] [A Cumulative Framework for Solid Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19852)
*Lev Steinberg*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出固体变形的累积变分框架，将相容Cosserat分量、分布不相容分量与缺陷源分量递归组合为统一材料状态，并给出算例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固体变形理论往往将相容变形、不相容（缺陷）与缺陷源作为分离的边值问题或按固定时间顺序处理，缺乏对三者耦合与累积响应的统一描述。本文旨在建立一个能刻画组合材料状态对外部条件变化累积响应的统一变分框架。

Method: 提出累积变分框架：以一个累积能量及相应场方程耦合三个结构分量（相容Cosserat分量、分布不相容分量、缺陷源分量），在共同边界与初始条件下求解，不作分离边值问题、不预设时间顺序。给出一个一维特例，并作空间推广，引入缺陷与源梯度项。

Result: 一维特例展示了结构分叉、保留结构导致的残余变形与残余应力、对初始组合状态的依赖、反向加载下的方向记忆、参数化累积滞回，以及与结构稳定化相关的可观测切线刚度变化。空间推广引入梯度项后得到尺寸相关的结构稳定性算子；对所采用的稳定四次能量，梯度项能正则化非均匀扰动，但本身不能在已稳定的三分量分支上产生有限波长局域化失稳。

Conclusion: 该框架通过累积结构与能量区分了哪些现象由框架本身导出，哪些需要额外的本构演化律。初始完美体失稳后出现缺陷再源参与的分支序列，是所选初始状态与本构能量的分叉属性，而非框架的固定层级。

Abstract: A cumulative variational framework for solid deformation is proposed. Its central organizing principle is the recursive combination of three structural components of one material body: a compatible Cosserat component, a distributed-incompatibility component, and a defect-source component. At any prescribed external condition these components constitute a combined material state. They are governed under common physical boundary and initial conditions and are coupled through one cumulative energy and the associated field equations. They are not separate boundary-value problems and need not appear in a prescribed temporal sequence. The cumulative character of the framework concerns the response of the combined state to variation of externally prescribed conditions. For the special case of an initially perfect body, loss of stability may produce successive branches with defect and then source participation. Such a sequence is a bifurcation property of the selected initial state and constitutive energy, rather than a defining hierarchy of the framework. A one-dimensional specialization illustrates structural bifurcation, residual deformation and residual stress produced by retained structure, dependence on the initial combined state, directional memory under reverse loading, parametric cumulative hysteresis, and changes of observable tangent stiffness associated with structural stabilization. A spatial extension introduces defect and source gradient terms and yields a size-dependent structural stability operator. For the stable quartic energy considered, the gradient terms regularize nonuniform perturbations but do not by themselves generate a finite-wavelength localization instability on an already stable three-component branch. The examples thereby distinguish consequences of the cumulative structure and energy from phenomena requiring additional constitutive evolution laws.

</details>


### [181] [Asymmetric phase transitions in random noncommutative geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19898)
*Benedek Bukor,Masoud Khalkhali,Samuel Kováčik,Adam Kubiś,Katarína Magdolenová,Nathan Pagliaroli,Juraj Tekel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过Riemann-Hilbert方法、正性自举法与Hamiltonian Monte Carlo模拟三种手段，研究四次型(0,1)与(1,0) Dirac系综的非对称相，并给出本征值分布与自由能的显式表达，重建相结构，三者在大矩阵尺寸下高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四次型(0,1)与(1,0) Dirac系综的Schwinger-Dyson方程与鞍点方程的非对称解求解空间极其复杂，亟待从解析与数值多角度刻画其相结构与本征值分布。

Method: 采用三种方法：Riemann-Hilbert方法用于求解非对称解并导出本征值分布与自由能的显式公式；正性自举法从自举矩重建本征值分布；Hamiltonian Monte Carlo模拟用于重建相结构。

Result: Riemann-Hilbert方法给出多种解的本征值分布与自由能显式公式；HMC模拟重建了相结构；正性自举法由自举矩重建了本征值分布。三种方法在大矩阵尺寸下表现出极好的一致性。

Conclusion: 多方法交叉验证表明，对四次型Dirac系综非对称相的解析与数值刻画相互吻合，为该类模型的相结构研究提供了可靠且互补的工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the asymmetric phases of the quartic type (0, 1) and (1, 0) Dirac en- sembles via three approaches: the Riemann-Hilbert approach, bootstrapping with positivity, and Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (HMC) simulations. The focus of this work is on asymmetric solutions to the Schwinger-Dyson and saddle point equations of these models, whose solution spaces prove deeply intricate. Via the Riemann-Hilbert approach, we are able to give explicit formulae for the eigenvalue distributions and free energy of various solutions. Using Hamiltonian Monte Carlo simulations, we are able to reconstruct the phase structure. Lastly, using bootstrapping with positivity, we are able to reconstruct the eigenvalue distribution of these models from their bootstrapped moments. All three methods show excellent agreement for a large matrix size.

</details>


### [182] [Heterogeneous Anisotropic Kac Limits on Rectifiable Resolved Cut Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20215)
*Sai Peng*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文用测度论方法解决"环境收敛无法确定切割体上非局部能量"的问题，证明对应尖锐界面Gamma极限：极限为分辨的各向异性变分加外边界接触项及各岸系数为1的接触项，裂纹接近边界处无需强制低维项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境收敛无法确定切割体上的非局部能量：切割体积为零，但微观键仍保留其路径穿越哪一标注岸的信息。需直接以测度论方式处理这一信息，并去掉闭合性、正可达、有限图册、曲率界、角度条件及分支集正则性等假设。

Method: 对由一致张成族（可为非对易流）生成的异构Kac相互作用，采用测度论直接处理切割信息。切割可取有限或可数族Borel、可数可求长超曲面，具有限总表面质量、可测近似法向与可测二值岸数据。证明结合流的精确时空面积公式、一维分裂延拓下界与有序流紧性，并去除紧径向支撑。

Result: 极限为分辨的各向异性变分，加上外边界接触项与每岸一个系数为1的接触项。Gamma泛函在裂纹接近边界处不含强制低维项，常值恢复不产生集中。有限一阶矩给出相同的局部Griffith泛函；在幂律阈值处极限分裂为局部、对数重整化局部与真正分数三种regime。光滑缝与结探测器作为直接穿越规则的均匀等价实现被恢复。

Conclusion: 建立了无正则性假设下切割体非局部能量的尖锐界面Gamma极限，给出避免边界浓度与裂纹低维项的精确刻画，并揭示局部、对数重整化局部与分数regime的阈值分界。

Abstract: Ambient convergence does not determine a nonlocal energy on a cut body: a cut has zero volume, but a microscopic bond retains which labelled bank its path crosses. We give a direct measure-theoretic resolution of this information and prove the corresponding sharp-interface Gamma-limit. The cut may be a finite or countable family of Borel, countably rectifiable hypersurfaces with finite total surface mass, measurable approximate normals, and measurable binary bank data. No closedness, positive reach, finite atlas, curvature bound, angle condition, or regularity of the branch set is assumed. For heterogeneous Kac interactions generated by a uniformly spanning family of possibly noncommuting flows, the limit is the resolved anisotropic variation plus the outer boundary contact and one coefficient-one contact on each bank. The Gamma-functional contains no compulsory lower-dimensional term where a crack approaches the boundary; constant recovery creates no concentration there. The proof combines an exact spacetime area formula for flow crossings, a one-dimensional split-extension lower bound, and ordered-flow compactness. We also remove compact radial support. A finite first moment yields the same local Griffith functional; at the power-law threshold the limits separate into local, logarithmically renormalized local, and genuinely fractional regimes. Smooth slit and junction detectors are recovered as uniformly equivalent realizations of the direct crossing rule.

</details>


### [183] [Tangential Surface Pressures and Boundary Localization in Disordered Edwards--Anderson Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20266)
*Hexiang Wang,Keheng Zhu,Mauris Chueng*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究无序Edwards–Anderson Ising模型的边界自由能，证明了有限宽度矩形条带上"自由-固定"边界修正（除以切向长度）在任意有限逆温度及零温下收敛，并给出边界局域化、切向压强定理、自平均性及低温局域化判据等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序自旋系统中，边界自由能及其自平均性是理解表面效应与相变的核心问题；作者希望在没有无穷体积Gibbs态性质假设的前提下，建立边界自由能收敛、边界局域化与低温局域化的严格结果。

Method: 对固定宽度矩形条带，结合一致几乎可加性估计与乘积测度集中，得到沿切向区间的自平均性；推导精确的有限体积Gaussian插值恒等式；证明一致指数边界混合条件蕴含边界局域化；将切向压强定理推广到任意维数与有限一阶矩的对称耦合分布；采用乘积集中假设下的切向立方自平均；提出亚临界开放键判据。

Result: 证明了期望的"自由-固定"边界修正除以切向长度在任意有限逆温度和零温下均收敛；给出显式指数速率的边界局域化；切向压强定理推广到所有维数；在乘积集中假设下获得沿切向立方体的自平均；对稀疏带符号耦合显式验证了亚临界开放键判据，从而得到低温局域化。

Conclusion: 该工作为无序Ising模型的边界自由能提供了统一且严格的框架，融合几乎可加性、集中不等式与Gaussian插值技术，在较弱假设下建立了边界压强收敛、自平均与低温边界局域化，且不依赖无穷体积Gibbs态性质。

Abstract: We study boundary free energies in disordered Edwards--Anderson Ising models. For rectangular strips of fixed width, we prove that the expected free-to-fixed boundary correction, divided by the tangential length, converges for every finite inverse temperature and at zero temperature. The proof combines a uniform almost-additivity estimate with product-measure concentration, yielding self-averaging along tangential intervals. We derive an exact finite-volume Gaussian interpolation identity and prove that a uniform exponential boundary-mixing condition implies boundary localization with an explicit exponential rate. The tangential pressure theorem extends to all dimensions and symmetric coupling laws with finite first moment; under a product-concentration hypothesis, self-averaging is obtained along tangential cubes. Finally, a subcritical open-bond criterion, verified explicitly for sparse signed couplings, gives low-temperature localization without assuming an infinite-volume Gibbs-state property.

</details>


### [184] [An Electrodynamic Lifting of the Toda Monopoles in Curved 3+1 Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20272)
*Hanwen Liu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究双曲Toda方程的解与经典阿贝尔单极子的对应关系，确定了何时其联络可视为弯曲Lorentzian 3+1时空（带类空Killing场）上的无源电磁势；一般分支由Maxwell方程通过Toda场的一个特征量限定Killing纤维尺度与水平twist，分离Liouville分支给出纯磁场，球面解给出收缩屏上的单位电荷单极子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望厘清Toda方程解所诱导的Abelian单极子联络在何种条件下同时构成弯曲Lorentzian时空中带Killing场的无源电磁势，并刻画其可容许的几何结构。

Method: 利用(2+1)维Einstein–Weyl空间上的Toda解构造经典Abelian单极子，检验其联络是否满足Maxwell方程；分别分析一般分支与分离Liouville分支，用Toda场的一个特征量刻画Killing纤维尺度与水平twist；考察带类空Killing场的3+1维Lorentzian背景（不假定Einstein或自对偶）。

Result: 一般分支上，Maxwell方程通过Toda场的一个特征量分类可容许的Killing纤维尺度与水平twist；分离Liouville分支产生纯磁场并允许更自由的纤维几何；球面解给出收缩屏上带守恒通量的单位电荷单极子。

Conclusion: Toda方程与Abelian单极子／无源电磁势之间的联系可在不假定背景为Einstein或自对偶的一般弯曲Lorentzian时空中建立，且解的分支结构对应不同的电磁与几何性质。

Abstract: Every solution of the hyperbolic Toda equation determines a classical Abelian monopole on a (2+1)-dimensional Einstein--Weyl space. We determine when its connection is also a source-free electromagnetic potential on a curved Lorentzian 3+1 spacetime with a spacelike Killing field. On the generic branch, Maxwell's equation classifies the admissible Killing-fibre scale and horizontal twist through one characteristic of the Toda field. A separated Liouville branch gives purely magnetic fields with considerably freer fibre geometry. The round spherical solution yields a monopole of unit charge with conserved flux on a contracting screen. The background metric here is neither assumed to be Einstein nor self-dual.

</details>


### [185] [Factorization method for a clamped obstacle from near-field measurements via a far-field transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20528)
*General Ozochiawaeze,Isaac Harris*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究双调和Helmholtz方程下不可穿透夹紧障碍物的逆形状问题，通过引入与障碍无关的远场变换，使近场算子获得对称分解，从而用分解法完整刻画障碍物并给出重构算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对双调和Helmholtz方程的逆形状问题，近场算子不直接满足分解法所需的对称分解条件，因此需要一种方法来弥补这一缺陷，实现对夹紧障碍物的严格重构。

Method: 利用近场点源与偶极子测量数据（障碍物周围闭合测量曲线上的散射场及其法向导数），引入一个独立于障碍物的远场变换，将近场算子扩展为满足对称分解的远场算子，进而应用分解法理论进行完整刻画，并基于变换算子的谱数据设计实用重构算法；数值实验中合成近场数据由基本解方法生成。

Result: 理论方面，远场变换使近场算子获得对称分解，从而可由分解法给出障碍物的严格完整刻画；数值实验验证了所提方法的有效性，并进一步展示了仅使用点源产生的散射场测量数据也能实现重构，表明可减少测量数据量。

Conclusion: 远场变换为近场算子的分解法应用提供了可行途径，所提方法对二维双调和Helmholtz方程中不可穿透夹紧障碍物的重构有效，且具备减少测量数据量的潜力。

Abstract: This paper considers an inverse shape problem for recovering an unknown impenetrable clamped obstacle in two dimensions from near-field point source and dipole measurements for the biharmonic Helmholtz equation in the frequency domain. The measured data consist of the scattered field and its normal derivative on a closed measurement curve surrounding the obstacle. Since the associated near-field operator does not directly admit the symmetric factorization required by the factorization method, we introduce a far-field transformation. This transformation is defined independently of the obstacle and augments the near-field operator into the associated far-field operator which does admit a symmetric factorization. This yields a rigorous complete characterization of the obstacle by the factorization method theory and leads to a practical reconstruction algorithm based on the spectral data of the transformed operator. Numerical experiments are presented to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method, with synthetic near-field data generated using the method of fundamental solutions. We also consider reconstructions using only scattered-field measurements generated by point sources, demonstrating the potential for reduced the amount of measured data.

</details>


### [186] [Phase transitions in non-Hermitian spherical integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20685)
*Pierre Bousseyroux,Marc Potters*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究非厄米随机矩阵中带约束球面积分的大N渐近，揭示离域与局域相变机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受厄米球面积分机制与库仑气体图像的启发，探索非厄米随机矩阵中带约束球面积分的渐近行为，特别是其向局域化的相变现象。

Method: 对两向量范数及相互标积固定的约束球面积分进行大N渐近分析；在鞍点层面用非厄米变换 R1 与 R2 刻画离域区的渐近，并结合移位矩阵的最大奇异值及其左右奇异向量的重叠度刻画局域区。

Result: 在离域区，渐近行为由非厄米变换 R1、R2 主导；鞍点层面出现向局域区的相变，该局域区由移位矩阵的最大奇异值及对应左右奇异向量的重叠度控制。

Conclusion: 以数学物理风格提出关于单特征值大偏差与边界涨落的猜想，为非厄米带约束积分提供相变图像与后续研究方向。

Abstract: We study the large-$N$ asymptotics of a constrained spherical integral for non-Hermitian random matrices, in which the norms and mutual scalar product of two vectors are fixed. In the delocalized regime, the asymptotics are governed by the non-Hermitian transforms $\mathcal R_1$ and $\mathcal R_2$. At saddle-point level, the constrained integral exhibits a transition to a localized regime controlled by the largest singular value of a shifted matrix and by the overlap of its associated left and right singular vectors. Motivated by the Hermitian spherical-integral mechanism and by the Coulomb-gas picture, we formulate conjectures for one-eigenvalue large deviations and boundary fluctuations. Throughout, our analysis is carried out in the spirit of mathematical physics.

</details>


### [187] [Disorder on the hyperbolic square lattice I: Anderson delocalization and absolutely continuous spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20798)
*Simon Becker,Izak Oltman*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究双曲格点上的Anderson模型，证明了弱无序下正测度确定性集合上绝对连续谱的存在与奇异谱的缺失，并在特定条件下（含截断Cauchy分布及受控扰动）得到区间上纯绝对连续谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲格点上Anderson模型的谱性质（绝对连续谱存在性与奇异谱缺失）此前缺乏在正测度确定性集合上的系统结果，本文旨在填补这一空白并刻画弱无序下的谱类型。

Method: 考虑笛卡尔积图：欧氏格点与每个顶点含五个正方形的正则双曲正方形格点的乘积；结合解析、谱测度与Green函数等工具分析该图上Anderson模型的谱。

Result: 证明了弱无序下在正测度的确定性集合上绝对连续谱存在且无奇异谱；当单点密度在[-1,1]上下有界时，对0<λ≤2亦成立；对截断Cauchy分布及受控扰动，得到区间上的纯绝对连续谱。

Conclusion: 双曲格点上的Anderson模型在弱无序情形下具有显著的绝对连续谱行为，并在特定分布与扰动下呈现区间上的纯绝对连续谱。

Abstract: We study the Anderson model on hyperbolic lattices. The graph is the Cartesian product of the Euclidean lattice with the regular hyperbolic square lattice having five squares at each vertex. We prove the existence of absolutely continuous spectrum and the absence of singular spectrum on a deterministic set of positive measure at weak disorder, and for $0<λ\le2$ when the single-site density is bounded below on $[-1,1]$. Truncated Cauchy distributions and controlled perturbations give purely absolutely continuous spectrum on intervals.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Tachikawa's second conjecture implies the Auslander-Reiten conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19172)
*Haruhisa Enomoto*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明立川第二猜想蕴含Artin代数上的Auslander-Reiten猜想，从而统一了多个同调猜想之间的等价关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Auslander-Reiten猜想、广义Nakayama猜想、Auslander-Gorenstein猜想、Nakayama猜想、Gorenstein-投射猜想与立川第二猜想长期被视为相互关联但未完全统一的同调猜想，作者希望厘清它们之间的逻辑蕴含关系。

Method: 通过构造代数的双重平凡扩张（two-fold trivial extension），证明立川第二猜想蕴含交换Artin环上Artin代数的Auslander-Reiten猜想，并结合已知蕴含关系进行推导。

Result: 证明立川第二猜想蕴含Auslander-Reiten猜想，进而得到Auslander-Reiten猜想、广义Nakayama猜想、Auslander-Gorenstein猜想、Nakayama猜想、Gorenstein-投射猜想与立川第二猜想全部等价，并且立川第二猜想蕴含立川第一猜想。

Conclusion: 上述六个同调猜想在等价意义下可归约为同一猜想，且立川第二猜想强于其第一猜想，为同调猜想研究提供了统一的逻辑框架。

Abstract: We prove that Tachikawa's second conjecture implies the Auslander-Reiten conjecture for artin algebras over a commutative artinian ring. The proof uses the two-fold trivial extension of an algebra. Together with known implications, it follows that the Auslander-Reiten conjecture, the generalized Nakayama conjecture, the Auslander-Gorenstein conjecture, the Nakayama conjecture, the Gorenstein-projective conjecture, and Tachikawa's second conjecture are all equivalent, and that Tachikawa's second conjecture implies his first.

</details>


### [189] [The Arthur-Packet Support Equality for Real Reductive Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19197)
*Jiawei Yang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文证明了 Arthur 参数 ψ 的 microlocal packet 正好等于两步 Jordan 归纳包的支持，从而完成了 AIMV 所猜想的等式（即证明反向包含）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adams–Ionov–Mason-Brown–Vogan 已证明 ψ 的 microlocal packet 包含于其 unipotent 参数 ψ₂ 的包的两步 Jordan 归纳的支持中，并猜想等式成立；本文旨在补足反向包含以证明该猜想。

Method: 先将参数平移到足够正的 translate，使 ABV 空间相关连通分支共享统一的 flag-variety 模型；用 perverse devissage 将问题归约为奇异支撑的蕴含关系，该归约由 AIMV microstalk 比较背后的 incidence 计算得到；随后对单个 packet 成员使用 coherent continuation 把结论拉回原参数。

Result: 证明了反向包含成立，即 microlocal packet 等于两步 Jordan 归纳包的支持，完成了 AIMV 猜想的等式。

Conclusion: microlocal packet 与两步 Jordan 归纳的支持完全一致，AIMV 的等式猜想成立。

Abstract: Let $ψ$ be a real Arthur parameter and let $ψ_2$ be the unipotent parameter in a fixed Jordan decomposition. Adams, Ionov, Mason-Brown, and Vogan proved that the microlocal packet of $ψ$ is contained in the support of the two-step Jordan induction of the packet of $ψ_2$, and conjectured equality. We prove the reverse inclusion. The argument first passes to a sufficiently positive translate, where the relevant connected components of the ABV spaces have a common flag-variety model. Perverse devissage reduces the problem to a singular-support implication, which follows from the incidence calculation underlying the AIMV microstalk comparison. Coherent continuation for individual packet members then returns the result to the original parameter.

</details>


### [190] [On the Hiraga-Ichino-Ikeda conjecture on formal degrees for $\mathrm{G_2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19430)
*Yugo Takanashi*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明triality $\mathrm{PGSO}_8$与内窥群$\mathrm{G}_2$之间的扭内窥特征恒等式蕴含$\mathrm{G}_2$的Hiraga-Ichino-Ikeda形式度猜想，并借此建立$\mathrm{PGSO}_8$（带triality）表示论的基本结果，核心工具为$E_6$型伴随群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hiraga-Ichino-Ikeda猜想关于形式度的预言在$\mathrm{G}_2$情形尚缺证明；作者希望借助triality内窥关系与$E_6$型伴随群的几何结构，为这一猜想提供新的证明途径。

Method: 利用triality $\mathrm{PGSO}_8$与内窥群$\mathrm{G}_2$之间的扭内窥特征恒等式，并应用$E_6$型伴随群，建立$\mathrm{PGSO}_8$（带triality）表示论的基本结果，进而推导形式度猜想。

Result: 证明了$\mathrm{G}_2$的Hiraga-Ichino-Ikeda形式度猜想；同时得到$\mathrm{PGSO}_8$（带triality）表示论的一系列基本结果。

Conclusion: triality内窥特征恒等式可推出$\mathrm{G}_2$的形式度猜想，$E_6$型伴随群是该方法的关键，所建立的$\mathrm{PGSO}_8$表示论结果具有独立价值。

Abstract: We prove that the twisted endoscopic character identities between triality $\mathrm{PGSO}_8$ and the endoscopic group $\mathrm{G}_2$ imply the Hiraga-Ichino-Ikeda conjecture on formal degrees for $\mathrm{G}_2$. In the course of proving the main result, we also establish some fundamental results on representation theory of $\mathrm{PGSO}_8$ with triality. A key feature of our approach is an application of the adjoint group of type $E_6$.

</details>


### [191] [A Comparison Theorem for Parahoric Character Sheaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19860)
*Zhihang Yu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文建立了仿射仿抛物子群上特征层特征函数的 Jordan 分解公式，将其与正深度 Deligne–Lusztig 特征标联系起来，并证明了广义深水平 Green 函数与特征层函数的正交性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 Lusztig 关于深度零情形（\cite{Lu90}）的刻画推广到正深度，并为限制特征标的用重数提供层论表达。

Method: 对 \cite{INY25} 中定义的仿抛物子群上的特征层，建立其特征函数的 Jordan 分解；在 q 充分大时，将这些函数等同于深水平 Deligne–Lusztig 特征标的 $(-1)^{\dim G}$ 倍。

Result: 得到 Jordan 分解公式；证明这些特征函数（q 足够大时）等于 $(-1)^{\dim G}$ 倍的对应深水平 Deligne–Lusztig 特征标；并证明广义深水平 Green 函数与特征层函数的正交性。

Conclusion: 作为应用，给出了 $G^{F^2}$ 特征标限制到 $G^F$ 时重数的层论表达式，将 \cite{Lu90} 的结果推广至正深度。

Abstract: We establish a Jordan decomposition formula for the characteristic functions of the character sheaves on parahoric subgroups defined in \cite{INY25}. For a sufficiently large $q$, these functions are $(-1)^{\dim G}$ times the corresponding deep level Deligne--Lusztig characters, extending \cite{Lu90} to positive depth. We also prove the orthogonality for the generalized deep level Green functions and the character sheaves functions. As an application, we present a sheaf-theoretic expression to the multiplicity of the $G^{F^2}$ character restricted to $G^{F}$.

</details>


### [192] [A refined multiplication formula in 2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius extriangulated categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20049)
*Ming Ding,Fan Xu,Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 在 Hom-有限 2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius 外三角范畴中证明了 Wang-Wei-Zhang 丛特征（cluster character）的乘法公式，推广了已有结果，并给出冻结项的 Auslander-Reiten 网表达及主系数网关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wang-Wei-Zhang 的乘法公式此前仅在一维情形得到，Keller-Plamondon-Qin 的结果则针对（稳定）2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius 或三角范畴。本文旨在将乘法公式推广到更一般的 Hom-有限 2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius 外三角范畴，统一并扩展这些结果。

Method: 设 $\mathcal C$ 为带丛倾斜对象的 Hom-有限 2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius 外三角范畴，且其稳定范畴关于诱导的丛倾斜对象具有可构造锥（constructible cones）。在此假设下证明 Wang-Wei-Zhang 丛特征的乘法公式；进而利用该公式刻画 Auslander-Reiten 网中冻结项；对带主系数的无环箭图，在 Higgs 范畴中计算冻结重数并导出显式主系数网关系。

Result: 得到 Wang-Wei-Zhang 丛特征的乘法公式，推广一维情形及 Keller-Plamondon-Qin 的（稳定）2-Calabi-Yau 结果；用极小余极限（minimal cosyzygy）中间项的特征表达 Auslander-Reiten 网中的冻结项；对带主系数的无环箭图，在 Higgs 范畴中计算冻结重数并给出显式主系数网关系。

Conclusion: 乘法公式在更一般的 Hom-有限 2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius 外三角范畴上成立，为冻结项与主系数网关系提供了统一框架与显式计算。

Abstract: We prove a multiplication formula for the Wang-Wei-Zhang cluster character on a Hom-finite $2$-Calabi--Yau Frobenius extriangulated category $\mathcal C$ with a cluster-tilting object under the assumption that its stable category has constructible cones with respect to the induced cluster-tilting object. This formula generalizes those obtained by Wang-Wei-Zhang for $\mathcal C$ in the one-dimensional case and by Keller-Plamondon-Qin for (stably) $2$-Calabi-Yau Frobenius or triangulated categories. As a consequence, we express the frozen term in an Auslander-Reiten mesh with the character of a minimal cosyzygy middle term. For acyclic quivers with principal coefficients, we compute these frozen multiplicities in Higgs categories and obtain explicit principal coefficient mesh relations.

</details>


### [193] [Unitary Branching for $\mathfrak{sl}(m\vert n), \mathfrak{osp}(m\vert 2n)$ and $F(4)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20332)
*Steffen Schmidt*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文利用Dirac不等式（结合Huang-Pandžić-Dirac算子）与Freudenthal递推的超类比，确定了基本典型李超代数sl(m|n)、osp(m|2n)与F(4)限制到其偶子代数时的不可约酉表示的支化规律，给出有限递推程序及闭合公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究基本典型李超代数在偶子代数上的限制支化，是李超代数表示论的核心问题之一；但正系统未必适配相关实形式，导致经典Dirac方法难以直接应用，亟需推广。

Method: 将Huang-Pandžić-Dirac算子D_{g,g_0}的Dirac不等式与由Dirac形式导出的Freudenthal递推超类比相结合；通过奇反射将Dirac不等式搬运至不适配实形式的正系统，并确定相应的修正项。

Result: 得到确定偶成分及其支化重数的有限递推程序，并给出对非典型度一致成立的支化重数闭合公式；对sl(m|n)，修正后的Dirac不等式还给出了所有与酉表示相关的正系统的酉性刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作将Dirac方法从适配正系统推广到一般正系统，为基本典型李超代数的酉表示支化提供了统一且非典型度一致的算法与显式公式，并附带sl(m|n)的酉性判据。

Abstract: We determine the branching of irreducible unitary representations of the basic classical Lie superalgebras $\mathfrak{sl}(m|n)$, $\mathfrak{osp}(m|2n)$, and $F(4)$ under restriction to their even subalgebras. The proof combines Dirac inequalities for the Huang-Pandžić-Dirac operator $\operatorname{D}_{\mathfrak{g},\mathfrak{g}_{\bar{0}}}$ with a super analogue of Freudenthal's recursion derived from the Dirac formalism. We extend the Dirac method to positive systems that are not adapted to the relevant real form by transporting the Dirac inequality through odd reflections and determining the resulting correction terms. This yields finite recursive procedures that determine the occurring even constituents and their branching multiplicities, together with closed formulas for the latter, uniformly in the degree of atypicality. For $\mathfrak{sl}(m|n)$, the corrected Dirac inequalities also give a characterization of unitarity for all positive systems relevant to unitary representations.

</details>


### [194] [Representations of formal Lie groups and Lie pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20547)
*Fulin Chen,Binyong Sun,Chuyun Wang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文建立形式Lie群与Lie对的表示论基础，构造形式流形函数空间上的Lie对模，并证明形式Lie群的表示范畴同构于对应Lie对的模范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式Lie群是微分几何中Lie群的形式化推广，类比于代数几何中的形式群概型。作者在前作中已建立形式Lie群的基本理论（包括将其与Lie对等同的形式Lie理论定理），但表示论方面尚待发展，故本文旨在奠定形式Lie群与Lie对表示论的基础。

Method: 在形式Lie群与Lie对框架下发展表示论：在形式流形上的函数空间上构造Lie对的模，并研究形式Lie群表示范畴与相应Lie对模范畴之间的关系。

Result: 成功构造了形式流形函数空间上的Lie对模，并证明了形式Lie群的表示范畴与相应Lie对的模范畴同构。

Conclusion: 形式Lie群的表示论可完全由相应Lie对的模论刻画，表示范畴的等价（同构）为形式Lie群的表示理论提供了代数化工具，延续并深化了形式Lie理论与形式群概型的类比。

Abstract: Formal Lie groups, which generalize Lie groups in differential geometry, are analogous to formal group schemes in algebraic geometry. In a previous paper, we established the basic theory of formal Lie groups, including a formal Lie theory theorem that identifies formal Lie groups with Lie pairs. In this paper, we develop the foundations of the representation theory of formal Lie groups and Lie pairs. In particular, we construct modules of Lie pairs on function spaces over formal manifolds and prove that the category of representations of a formal Lie group is isomorphic to the category of modules of the corresponding Lie pair.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [Asymptotic counting of integers with prime factors $p_{r^a s^b}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19434)
*Mehdi Golafshan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了素因子均形如 $p_{r^a s^b}$（其中 $r,s\ge2$ 为固定的乘法独立整数）的整数的计数问题，给出了相对误差为 $o(1)$ 且乘法常数明确的渐近公式。对于 $(r,s)=(2,3)$，这类整数正是序数 $\omega^{\omega^2}$ 以下序数的 prime codes，从而解决了 Vernaeve、Vindas 和 Weiermann 计数问题中的该情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始计数问题（尤其是序数 prime codes 的计数）此前缺乏显式渐近结果，作者希望给出一个相对误差为 $o(1)$、且乘法常数明确的渐近公式，以推进对 Vernaeve–Vindas–Weiermann 计数问题的理解。

Method: 设 $p_n$ 为第 $n$ 个素数，$r,s\ge2$ 为固定的乘法独立整数。统计不超过 $x$ 且其所有素因子都形如 $p_{r^a s^b}$（$a,b\ge0$ 为整数）的整数个数，并通过解析数论/筛法手段推导其渐近公式，且使乘法常数显式化。

Result: 得到了该计数的渐近公式，相对误差为 $o(1)$，且误差中的乘法常数是显式的。特别地，当 $(r,s)=(2,3)$ 时，这些整数对应于序数 $\omega^{\omega^2}$ 以下的 prime codes，由此确定了该情形下的计数结果。

Conclusion: 论文给出了素因子限定为 $p_{r^a s^b}$ 的整数计数的显式渐近公式，并作为应用解决了 Vernaeve–Vindas–Weiermann 序数 prime codes 计数问题中 $(r,s)=(2,3)$ 的情形。

Abstract: Let $p_n$ be the $n$th prime, and let $r,s\ge2$ be fixed multiplicatively independent integers. We count the integers up to $x$ whose prime factors all have the form $p_{r^a s^b}$ with integers $a,b\ge0$. Our asymptotic formula for this count has relative error $o(1)$ and is explicit down to the multiplicative constant. For $(r,s)=(2,3)$ these integers are the prime codes of the ordinals below $ω^{ω^2}$, so the formula settles that case of the counting problem of Vernaeve, Vindas and Weiermann.

</details>


### [196] [Endoscopic description of the local Langlands correspondence for $\mathrm{G}_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19439)
*Yugo Takanashi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了Gan-Savin构造的p进G₂的L-包满足内视特征恒等式，并由此导出相关整体重数公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gan与Savin已构造出p进G₂的L-包，但其是否满足内视（endoscopic）特征恒等式尚未得到验证，这一问题对于该L-包的合理性及其在Langlands纲领中的定位至关重要。

Method: 通过证明从G₂到具有triality的PGSO₈的函子性提升的twisted内视特征恒等式，进而推出G₂的L-包的内视特征恒等式。

Result: 所构造的p进G₂的L-包满足内视特征恒等式；同时建立了G₂到PGSO₈（带triality）函子性提升的twisted内视特征恒等式，并得到G₂离散自守谱（来自PGL₃尖点自守谱）的整体重数公式。

Conclusion: Gan-Savin的p进G₂的L-包被证明具有内视性质，其证明途经PGSO₈的twisted内视特征恒等式，并附带给出了一个由PGL₃尖点谱产生的G₂离散自守谱的整体重数公式。

Abstract: We prove that the $L$-packets for $p$-adic $\mathrm{G}_2$ constructed by Gan and Savin satisfy the endoscopic character identities. In the course of the proof, we also prove twisted endoscopic character identities for functorial lifts from $\mathrm{G}_2$ to $\mathrm{PGSO}_8$ with triality. As a byproduct, we also prove a global multiplicity formula for the discrete automorphic spectrum of $\mathrm{G}_2$ coming from the cuspidal automorphic spectrum of $\mathrm{PGL}_3$.

</details>


### [197] [A uniform proof approach for congruences modulo 3 for partitions with $k$-colored odd parts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19440)
*Marie Jameson,James A. Sellers*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文为着色分拆函数 $a_k(n)$ 的模 3 同余提供了一个统一研究框架，并由此证明了一族无穷多组非嵌套的模 3 同余式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于 $a_k(n)$ 模 3 同余的研究多采用零散、特设（ad hoc）的方法，缺乏系统性，本文旨在建立统一框架来系统研究其模 3 同余。

Method: 定义 $a_k(n)$ 为偶数部分只有一种颜色、奇数部分可用 $k$ 种颜色着色的 $n$ 的分拆数；在此基础上构造统一框架来研究算术级数中的模 3 同余。

Result: 在该统一框架下，证明了无穷多个无穷族（infinite family of infinite families）的非嵌套（non-nested）模 3 同余式。

Conclusion: 该框架将此前零散的模 3 同余结果系统化，并给出更一般、更强的同余族结论。

Abstract: In recent work, Hirschhorn and the second author defined $a_k(n)$ to be the number of partitions of $n$ wherein the even parts come in only one color, while the odd parts may be ``colored'' with one of $k$ colors for fixed $k\geq 1$. This function generalizes the classical partition function and has been of significant interest because it satisfies a number of congruences. Although prior work studying $a_k(n)$ has resulted in congruences in arithmetic progressions in a somewhat ad hoc manner, this work gives a uniform framework for studying congruences modulo 3. This allows us to prove an infinite family of infinite families of non-nested congruences modulo 3.

</details>


### [198] [A new family of maximal curves not covered by the Hermitian curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19546)
*Liming Ma,Yipeng Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文构造了一类新的 $\mathbb{F}_{q^{2n}}$-极大曲线，证明其不被 Hermitian 曲线覆盖，并给出 Beelen–Montanucci 曲线的 Kummer 模型、亏格计算、极大性的统一证明以及自同构子群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限域上极大曲线及其是否被 Hermitian 曲线覆盖是有限域代数几何与编码理论中的核心问题；Beelen–Montanucci 曲线是一类重要例子，其覆盖性质、亏格与自同构结构仍有待完善。

Method: 对每个素数幂 $q>2$ 与偶数 $n\ge4$，显式构造定义在 $\mathbb{F}_{q^{2n}}$ 上的曲线；当 $n\ge3$ 为奇数时，给出 Beelen–Montanucci 曲线的 Kummer 模型；分别对奇、偶 $n$ 计算亏格，并通过计数有理点位置给出极大性的统一证明；进一步证明 $q>2$、奇 $n\ge5$ 时 Beelen–Montanucci 曲线不被 Hermitian 曲线覆盖。

Result: 构造出亏格为 $(q^2-1)q^n/2$ 的 $\mathbb{F}_{q^{2n}}$-极大曲线且不被 $\mathbb{F}_{q^{2n}}$ 上的 Hermitian 曲线覆盖；获得 Beelen–Montanucci 曲线的 Kummer 模型与奇偶 $n$ 的亏格；证明 $q>2$、奇 $n\ge5$ 时 Beelen–Montanucci 曲线不被 Hermitian 曲线覆盖，推广了 Galois 覆盖情形；并对任意素数幂 $q$ 与 $n\ge4$ 给出阶为 $(q^n+1)q(q^2-1)$ 的显式自同构子群。

Conclusion: 该工作统一并推广了极大曲线与 Beelen–Montanucci 曲线的相关结果，构造了新的大亏格非 Hermitian 覆盖极大曲线，并揭示了其自同构结构。

Abstract: For every prime power $q>2$ and every even integer $n\ge4$, we construct an $\mathbb{F}_{q^{2n}}$-maximal curve of genus $(q^2-1)q^n/2$ that is not covered by the Hermitian curve over $\mathbb{F}_{q^{2n}}$. The defining equation also gives a Kummer model for the Beelen--Montanucci curves when $n\ge3$ is odd. We compute the genus for both odd and even $n$ and give a uniform proof of maximality by counting rational places. For $q>2$ and odd $n\ge5$, we also prove that the Beelen--Montanucci curves are not covered by the Hermitian curve, extending the known result for Galois coverings. For every prime power $q$ and $n\ge4$, we also give an explicit automorphism subgroup of order $(q^n+1)q(q^2-1)$.

</details>


### [199] [Simultaneous nonvanishing of quadratic twists via Rankin-Cohen brackets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19649)
*Ramin Takloo-Bighash*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明迹化的对角 Rankin–Cohen 括号线性无关，推出至少 r 个 Hecke 特征形式满足中心 L 值非零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 Hecke 特征形式中心 L 值非零问题（非零中心 L 值的重数下界），推广到有 nebentypus 的级数与 twist 情形。

Method: 对奇数基本判别式 D 与固定 r≥1，构造权 ℓ 的 Eisenstein 级数 G_{k,D}（级 |D|，特征 χ_D）的对角 Rankin–Cohen 括号，取前 r 个迹化 Tr_1^{|D|}[G,G]_{2e}，证明其在 S_{2ℓ}(SL_2(Z)) 中线性无关；结合 Kayath–Lane–Neifeld–Ni–Xue 的 Petersson 公式。

Result: 对充分大的 ℓ（满足 (-1)^ℓ D>0），前 r 个迹化对角 RC 括号在 S_{2ℓ}(SL_2(Z)) 中线性无关；从而至少 r 个归一化 Hecke 特征形式 f∈S_{2ℓ}(SL_2(Z)) 满足 L(f⊗χ_D, ℓ)≠0。

Conclusion: 当 D=1 时，对每个固定 r 和充分大的 K≡0 (mod 4)，至少存在 r 个权 K 的一级 Hecke 特征形式，其中心 L 值非零。

Abstract: Let $D$ be an odd fundamental discriminant, with $D=1$ permitted, and let $r\geq 1$ be fixed. We prove that, for every sufficiently large integer $\ell$ satisfying $(-1)^\ell D>0$, the first $r$ traced diagonal Rankin--Cohen brackets \[
  \mathrm{Tr}_1^{|D|}[G_{\ell-2e,D},G_{\ell-2e,D}]_{2e},
  \qquad 1\leq e\leq r, \] are linearly independent in $S_{2\ell}(SL_2(\mathbb Z))$. Here $G_{k,D}$ is the Eisenstein series of weight $k$, level $|D|$, and nebentypus $χ_D$. The Petersson formula of Kayath, Lane, Neifeld, Ni, and Xue then implies that at least $r$ normalized Hecke eigenforms $f\in S_{2\ell}(SL_2(\mathbb Z))$ satisfy $L(f\otimesχ_D,\ell)\neq 0$. For $D=1$, this gives, for every fixed $r$ and every sufficiently large $K\equiv 0\pmod 4$, at least $r$ level-one eigenforms of weight $K$ with nonzero central value.

</details>


### [200] [Finite subgroups of outer automorphism groups of the absolute Galois groups of mixed-characteristic local fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19696)
*Yu Nishio*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文从单远阿贝尔几何视角研究混合特征局部域绝对伽罗瓦群外自同构群的有限子群，证明存在非拟几何有限子群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然同态给出的自同构群像给出拟几何子群的典型例子，本文旨在探究拟几何子群集合是否恰好等于所有有限子群。

Method: 采用单远阿贝尔几何（mono-anabelian geometry）的观点，定义外自同构群的拟几何有限子群：其自同构群中的原像同构于某混合特征局部域的绝对伽罗瓦群。

Result: 证明了混合特征局部域绝对伽罗瓦群的外自同构群中存在非拟几何的有限子群。

Conclusion: 拟几何子群集合一般不能刻画为外自同构群的所有有限子群集合。

Abstract: In the present paper, we study finite subgroups of the outer automorphism groups of the absolute Galois groups of mixed-characteristic local fields from the point of view of mono-anabelian geometry. We shall refer to a finite subgroup of the outer automorphism group of the absolute Galois group of a mixed-characteristic local field as quasi-geometric if its inverse image in the automorphism group of the corresponding absolute Galois group is isomorphic to the absolute Galois group of some mixed-characteristic local field. It is well-known that the image of the natural injective homomorphism from the automorphism group of a mixed-characteristic local field to the outer automorphism group of the associated absolute Galois group yields a typical example of a quasi-geometric subgroup. The main result of the present paper asserts the existence of non-quasi-geometric finite subgroups of the outer automorphism groups of the absolute Galois groups of mixed-characteristic local fields. This result shows that the set of quasi-geometric subgroups of the outer automorphism group of the absolute Galois group of a mixed-characteristic local field cannot be characterized, in general, as the set of all finite subgroups of the outer automorphism group.

</details>


### [201] [Norms of Schneider--Teitelbaum Polynomials in $p$-adic Fourier Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20002)
*Yu Katagiri*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文显式计算了Schneider–Teitelbaum p进Fourier理论中多项式 $P_n(T)$ 在固定阶局部 $F$ 解析函数Banach空间上于 $x\Omega$ 处取值的范数，并据此给出Bannai–Kobayashi基的显式归一化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schneider–Teitelbaum的p进Fourier理论中，多项式 $P_n(T)$ 是核心对象，但其在局部解析函数Banach空间上的范数行为尚不明确。Bannai–Kobayashi构造的基虽然重要，却缺乏显式的范数信息，需要归一化以便应用。作者利用Ardakov–Berger关于 $P_n(\Omega)$ 取值的新估值公式，来填补这一空白。

Method: 设 $F$ 为 $\mathbb{Q}_p$ 的非分歧二次扩域，$\Omega$ 为Lubin–Tate周期。基于Ardakov–Berger关于 $P_n(\Omega)$ 取值的最新估值公式，显式计算函数 $P_n(x\Omega)$ 在固定阶局部 $F$ 解析函数Banach空间上的范数。

Result: 显式确定了 $P_n(x\Omega)$ 在固定阶局部 $F$ 解析函数Banach空间上的范数。作为应用，得到了Bannai–Kobayashi所构造基的显式归一化，使其所有元素范数均为1。

Conclusion: 通过Ardakov–Berger估值公式，作者完成了 $P_n(x\Omega)$ 范数的显式计算，并给出Bannai–Kobayashi基的显式归一化，使其成为范数为1的基，从而完善了该p进Fourier理论中基的范数理论。

Abstract: Let $F$ be the unramified quadratic extension of $\mathbb{Q}_p$, and let $P_n(T)$ denote the polynomials arising in the $p$-adic Fourier theory of Schneider and Teitelbaum. We determine explicitly the norms of the functions $P_n(xΩ)$ on the Banach spaces of locally $F$-analytic functions of fixed order, where $Ω$ is a Lubin--Tate period. Our computation is based on a recent valuation formula of Ardakov and Berger for the values $P_n(Ω)$. As an application, we obtain an explicit normalization of the basis constructed by Bannai and Kobayashi, whose elements all have norm one.

</details>


### [202] [Sampling and Moments of Reciprocal Quadratic Mellin Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20201)
*K. Srinivasa Raghava*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究倒数二次有理函数相关的对数Mellin积分，对完全变形函数的正整数样本给出代数生成函数，并确定主半权例子的极小四次式、分支轨迹、微分方程、多项式递推与系数渐近；同时建立双曲公式、全正性与矩序列性质及对角渐近的π极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对数Mellin积分及其正整数样本的生成函数结构，特别是当有理核具有代数系数时的代数性现象，并希望精确刻画主半权例子的代数、微分方程与渐近性质，同时理解实参数下矩序列的确定性与正性。

Method: 对数Mellin积分与倒数二次有理核；整个变形的正整数采样与除以π后的生成函数；主半权例子的极小四次式与完全有限分支轨迹分析；极小微分方程与多项式递推；鞍点区域c>1, -2√c<b<0内的双曲公式与实a∈(0,1)分析；系数渐近与最大化半径/高对数矩分析；固定临界轨迹上的两项对角渐近与修正因子1+γ/M。

Result: 当有理核具有代数系数时，整个变形的正整数样本生成函数除以π后对每个a∈Q, 0<|a|<1均为代数函数。对主半权例子，确定了极小四次式、完全有限分支轨迹、极小微分方程、多项式递推和系数渐近。在鞍点区域c>1, -2√c<b<0，实0<a<1的双曲公式给出确定Hausdorff矩序列、严格全正性和单调商极限。对实|a|<1，同一区域的一般系数渐近表明一个最大值同时控制采样半径和高阶对数矩。在固定临界轨迹上，两项对角渐近给出趋于π的归一化乘积，除以修正1+γ/M后得到O(M^{-2})近似。

Conclusion: 该工作将倒数二次有理核的对数Mellin积分与代数生成函数、极小微分方程和递推联系起来，并在鞍点区域获得双曲公式、全正性与矩序列结果；固定临界轨迹上的对角渐近给出趋于π的归一化乘积及O(M^{-2})修正逼近。

Abstract: We study logarithmic Mellin integrals attached to a reciprocal quadratic rational function. When the rational kernel has algebraic coefficients, positive-integer samples of an entire deformation have a generating function which, after division by $π$, is algebraic for every $a\in\mathbb Q$ with $0<\vert{}a\vert{}<1$. For the principal half-weight example we determine the minimal quartic, complete finite branch locus, minimal differential equation, polynomial recurrence, and coefficient asymptotics. Throughout the saddle region $c>1$, $-2\sqrt c<b<0$, positive hyperbolic formulas for real $0<a<1$ give determinate Hausdorff moment sequences, strict total positivity, and monotone quotient limits. For real $\vert{}a\vert{}<1$, a general coefficient asymptotic in the same region shows that one maximum controls both the sampling radius and the high logarithmic moments. On the fixed-critical locus, two-term diagonal asymptotics give a normalized product tending to $π$; division by the displayed correction $1+γ/M$ gives an $O(M^{-2})$ approximation.

</details>


### [203] [On a question of Ruzsa about densities of sumsets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20206)
*Paolo Leonetti*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文否定了Ruzsa提出的关于和集渐近密度之间是否存在插值型不等式的问题，证明不存在这样的常数使该不等式对所有具渐近密度的集合成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ruzsa曾提出：对具渐近密度的集合A，其和集A+A的下渐近密度是否能被上渐近密度与A的密度的插值幂次控制。本文旨在判定该问题的真伪。

Method: 通过构造反例：对任意给定的常数0<ν<1与c>0，构造一个具有渐近密度的集合A，使得不等式d*(A+A)的上界与其d(A)^ν及d*(A+A)^{1-ν}的乘积关系不成立，从而说明不存在满足条件的常数。

Result: 证明了不存在常数0<ν<1与c>0使得该不等式对所有具渐近密度的集合A成立。

Conclusion: Ruzsa的问题答案是负面的；和集的上下渐近密度与集合密度之间不存在此类插值型幂次不等式。

Abstract: Let $\mathsf{d}$, $\mathsf{d}^{\ast}$, and $\mathsf{d}_{\ast}$ denote, respectively, the asymptotic density, the upper asymptotic density, and the lower asymptotic density on $\mathbb N$. We show that there are no constants $0<ν<1$ and $c>0$ such that $$ \mathsf{d}_{\ast}(A+A)\ge c\bigl(\mathsf{d}^{\ast}(A+A)\bigr)^{1-ν}\mathsf{d}(A)^ν $$ for every set $A\subseteq \mathbb{N}$ which admits asymptotic density. This answers in the negative a question of I.Z. Ruzsa.

</details>


### [204] [Rogers--Ramanujan identities from the geometry of $X^a=Y^b$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20567)
*Yifeng Huang,Kenny Lau,Ken Ono*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了Huang–Jiang–Oblomkov猜想：对互素 $1<a<b$ 的环面纽结奇点，计数、HJO $q$-级数与无穷乘积三者相等，并给出更强的有限恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Rogers–Ramanujan与Andrews–Gordon恒等式几何推广至环面纽结奇点，验证HJO猜想。

Method: 结合Bergeron–Garsia–Leven–Xin与Mellit的有理洗牌定理、斜率算子的乘性定理，以及有界柱状分拆的行列式模型，并通过共同的 $q$-差分方程联系。

Result: 建立三元等式：归一化计数、HJO $q$-级数 $Z_{a,b}$ 与显式无穷乘积 $P_{a,b}$ 相等；更强地，秩 $N$ 的HJO和等于 $(q;q)_N$ 乘以有界柱状分拆生成函数，$N\to\infty$ 即得猜想。

Conclusion: 有限恒等式与HJO猜想已在Lean中由AxiomProver形式化，依赖两项已声明的文献输入。

Abstract: We prove the conjecture of Huang, Jiang, and Oblomkov (HJO) giving a geometric extension of the Rogers--Ramanujan and Andrews--Gordon identities for every torus-knot singularity $X^a=Y^b$ with coprime $1<a<b.$ For a prime power $q$, let $\mathcal{NC}_n^{a,b}(\mathbb F_q)$ denote the set of pairs of commuting nilpotent $n\times n$ matrices $(A,B)$ over $\mathbb F_q$ satisfying $A^a=B^b$. We establish the threefold equality between their normalized counts, the HJO $q$-series $Z_{a,b}$, and the explicit infinite product $P_{a,b}$: \[ \underbrace{\vphantom{\Bigg|} \prod_{m\geq1}(1-q^{-m}) \Biggl(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty} \frac{\lvert\mathcal{NC}_n^{a,b}(\mathbb F_q)\rvert} {\lvert\operatorname{GL}_n(\mathbb F_q)\rvert}\Biggr) }_{\text{point count}} = \underbrace{\vphantom{\Bigg|}Z_{a,b}(q^{-1}) }_{\text{\(q\)-series}} = \underbrace{\vphantom{\Bigg|}P_{a,b}(q^{-1}) }_{\text{infinite product}}. \] Our main result is a stronger finite identity: the rank $N$ HJO sum equals $(q;q)_N$ times the generating function for balanced cylindric partitions with entries bounded by $N$. Taking $N\to\infty$ yields the HJO conjecture. The proof combines the compositional rational shuffle theorem of Bergeron--Garsia--Leven--Xin and Mellit with a multiplicativity theorem for slope operators and a determinantal model for bounded cylindric partitions, linked by a common $q$-difference equation. The finite identity and the HJO conjecture have been formalized in Lean by AxiomProver, conditional on two stated literature inputs.

</details>


### [205] [A characterization of Sophie Germain primes - II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20208)
*Paolo Leonetti*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了：当偶数 n≥4 时，集合 {1^{σ(1)}, 2^{σ(2)}, …, n^{σ(n)}} 能构成模 n 的完全剩余系，当且仅当 p=n/2 为素数且 p−1 无平方因子，从而补全了奇数情形的对应结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献对奇数给出了类似刻画，本文旨在解决偶数情形，为这一问题提供完整的充要条件。

Method: 通过对偶数的结构分析与排列 σ 的构造/排除，将问题归结为 p=n/2 的素数性与 p−1 的无平方因子性。

Result: 证明了对偶数 n≥4，存在排列 σ 使 {1^{σ(1)},…,n^{σ(n)}} 为模 n 的完全剩余系，当且仅当 n/2 为素数且该素数减一无平方因子。

Conclusion: 偶数情形与既有奇数情形相呼应，共同给出了该完全剩余系问题的完整判别准则。

Abstract: Let $n\ge 4$ be an even integer. We show that $\{1^{σ(1)},2^{σ(2)},\ldots,n^{σ(n)}\}$ is a complete residue system modulo $n$ for some permutation $σ$ of $\{1,2,\ldots,n\}$ if and only if $p:=n/2$ is prime and $p-1$ is squarefree. This complements the results in [Int. J. Number Theory \textbf{14} (2018), 653--660], where an analogue characterization has been proved for odd integers.

</details>


### [206] [The Three Gates: A Rooted-Operator Approach to Weil Positivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20367)
*Marco Desogus*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文通过三道闸门（Gate I–III）的局部化根算子论证证明实奇对数通道中的Weil正性，最终由受限奇Weil判据推出黎曼假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实奇对数通道中建立Weil正性需要处理继承父响应、算术失配与折叠标量借方的一致性表述问题，为此引入局部化根算子论证并保留极秩一项。

Method: Gate I建立严格胞腔/壳层符号定理与区间算术界；Gate II结合Mellin单元分解、除子偏等距平方、仿射平移、全形式Cauchy–Carleman输运、多源相干短路、增广标量根与真度量下的Schur几何，将继承父响应置于同一post-old-core/common-cut原始形式；Gate III使用压缩、闭包与受限奇Weil判据。

Result: 给出显式不等式 g^{M+}_k=1/2+log k−(439/250 log2)Σ_m V_{k,m}−(5/2)W_k>0（k≥7），并证明同时common-cut定理保持父基与横向预算分离、折叠恒等式余项非负。

Conclusion: 由严格认证端点Y=7出发，Schur归纳得到整数端点正性，零延拓给出任意有限支撑半径的非负性，结合闭包与受限奇Weil判据推出黎曼假设。

Abstract: We present a localized rooted-operator argument for Weil positivity in the real odd logarithmic channel, retaining the polar rank-one term throughout. Gate I establishes a strict cellular/shell sign theorem with rigorous interval-arithmetic bounds. Gate II combines the Mellin unit-cell decomposition, divisor partial-isometry squares, affine translations, full-form Cauchy--Carleman transport, coherent multi-source shorting, the augmented scalar root, and Schur geometry in the true metric. Gate III uses compression, closure, and the restricted odd Weil criterion. A central point in Gate II is to place the inherited parent response in the same post-old-core/common-cut primal form in which the arithmetic mismatch and folded scalar debit are charged. If $P^{\rm ex}_{k,m}$ is the surviving inherited pivot and $a_m$ the inherited coordinate of the Schur minimizer, then $P^{\rm ex}_{k,m}a_m=ω^{\rm ex}_{k,m}$, so the inherited source-coupling contribution is the negative metric energy of that response. The aligned forcing is retained explicitly, and an outward-rounded finite computation together with the analytic tail gives $g^{M+}_k=\frac12+\log k-\frac{439}{250\log 2}\sum_m V_{k,m}-\frac52 W_k>0$ for $k\ge7$. The simultaneous common-cut theorem keeps the parent-ground and transverse budgets separate before the common infimum, while the fold identity leaves a non-negative remainder. Starting from the rigorously certified endpoint $Y=7$, Schur induction yields positivity at the integer endpoints, zero-extension gives non-negativity at every finite support radius, and closure together with the restricted odd Weil criterion yields the Riemann hypothesis. Short fantasy interludes provide only an expository map and may be omitted without changing any definition, lemma, theorem, or proof.

</details>


### [207] [A problem of Yang and Chen on weighted representation functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20385)
*Shuang-Shuang Li,Ya-Ting Xu,Xiao-Hui Yan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究集合 A⊆ℕ 上表示函数 R_{1,k}(A,n) 与其补集表示函数相等的集合个数 f_k(t)，证明了 f_k(t) ≍_k 2^t/t^{k/2}，从而否定地回答了 Yang 和 Chen 的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yang 和 Chen 定义了 f_k(t)，即满足对所有 n≥t 有 R_{1,k}(A,n)=R_{1,k}(ℕ∖A,n) 的集合 A⊆ℕ 的个数，并提问：对任意整数 k,l>1，f_k(t) 与 f_l(t) 是否最终相等。本文旨在解决这一公开问题。

Method: 通过研究线性型表示函数 R_{1,k}(A,n)（即 n=a_1+ka_2，a_1,a_2∈A 的解数）与其补集表示函数相等的集合结构，对 f_k(t) 进行渐近计数估计，得到其上界与下界同阶。

Result: 证明了 f_k(t) ≍_k 2^t/t^{k/2}，即 f_k(t) 与 2^t/t^{k/2} 在常数因子（依赖 k）意义下同阶。

Conclusion: 由于渐近主项 2^t/t^{k/2} 明显依赖 k，故对不同的 k,l>1，f_k(t) 与 f_l(t) 不会最终相等，从而给出了 Yang 和 Chen 问题的否定回答。

Abstract: Let $\mathbb N$ denote the set of nonnegative integers. For an integer $k>1$ and a set $A\subseteq\mathbb N$, let $R_{1,k}(A,n)$ denote the number of solutions of $n=a_1+ka_2$ with $a_1,a_2\in A$. For integers $k>1$ and $t\ge1$, Yang and Chen defined $f_k(t)$ to be the number of sets $A\subseteq\mathbb N$ for which \[ R_{1,k}(A,n)=R_{1,k}(\mathbb N\setminus A,n) \] for all integers $n\ge t$, and asked whether $f_k(t)$ and $f_l(t)$ are eventually equal for any integers $k,l>1$. We prove that \[ f_k(t)\asymp_k \frac{2^t}{t^{k/2}}, \] which gives a negative answer to their problem.

</details>


### [208] [Low moments of automorphic random multiplicative function sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20460)
*Sun-Kai Leung*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文确定了有界次数的随机乘性函数部分和低阶矩的阶，其中局部酉参数满足素平方抵消条件，推广了Harper的经典工作，并应用于酉型或辛型的不可约紧群表示（含SU(d)与对称幂模型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Harper关于随机乘性函数部分和低阶矩的工作具有开创性，但限于特定模型。作者希望将这一框架推广到更广泛的Euler乘积族，以覆盖高秩Sato-Tate模型等更一般的算术对象。

Method: 对有界次数的Euler乘积所关联的随机乘性函数，施加素平方处局部酉参数的抵消条件，据此计算其部分和低阶矩的精确阶。

Result: 证明了在素平方抵消条件下低阶矩的阶，推广了Harper的结果，并适用于酉型或辛型的不可约紧群表示，包括高阶Sato-Tate模型SU(d)与奇对称幂Sato-Tate模型Sym^m SU(2)。

Conclusion: 该结果扩展了随机乘性函数矩估计的适用范围，将Harper的结论统一至更一般的紧群表示框架。

Abstract: We determine the order of the low moments of partial sums of random multiplicative functions associated with Euler products of bounded degree whose unitary local parameters satisfy a prime-square cancellation condition, thereby generalizing Harper's seminal work. The result applies to irreducible compact-group representations of unitary or symplectic type, including the higher-rank Sato--Tate model $SU(d)$ and the odd symmetric-power Sato--Tate model $\operatorname{Sym}^{m}SU(2)$.

</details>


### [209] [Finding New Limit Points of Mahler Measure by Methods of Missing Data Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20468)
*Jean-Marc Sac-Épée,Souad El Otmani,Armand Maul,Georges Rhin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过引入缺失数据恢复方法，进一步扩展了单变量多项式Mahler测度的已知极限点列表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: D. Boyd与M. Mossinghoff建立的单变量多项式Mahler测度极限点列表已被基于遗传算法的方法扩展，作者希望在此基础之上继续扩展该列表。

Method: 采用一种缺失数据恢复（missing data restoration）方法，对已有极限点列表进行适配与扩展。

Result: 成功扩展了已知的单变量多项式Mahler测度极限点列表。

Conclusion: 缺失数据恢复方法可有效用于发现Mahler测度的新极限点，为极限点列表的扩展提供了新的技术途径。

Abstract: It is well known that the set of Mahler measures of single variable polynomial has limit points of which a list established by D. Boyd and M. Mossinghoff has been extended through approaches based on genetic algorithms. In this paper, we wish to further extend the list of known limit points by adapting a method of missing data restoration.

</details>


### [210] [Computational results on sums of a prime with squares or cubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20505)
*Kenny Applegate,Kyle Pratt*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文验证了关于"一个奇素数加两个正平方和"的猜想在 $14<n<10^{26}$ 范围内成立，并证明 $308<n<10^{16}$ 的整数可表示为一个奇素数加两个正立方和，方法依赖若干椭圆曲线上的整点计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一个自然数论猜想断言所有大于14的整数均可写成一个奇素数加两个正平方之和。该猜想涉及素数分布与二次型/加性表示的交汇，直接对全体整数证明极困难，因此作者希望先在尽可能大的有限范围内给出验证，并探索相关的加性表示问题（如素数加两个立方）。

Method: 采用两阶段的"bootstrap"策略：先研究"一个素数加一个正平方"的和的表示，再将结果提升到"一个素数加两个正平方"。对于素数加两个正立方的情形，同样通过该类表示进行分析。关键技术环节是确定若干椭圆曲线上的整点（integral points），以排除或确认例外情形。

Result: 证明了所有满足 $14<n<10^{26}$ 的整数 $n$ 都能表示为一个奇素数加两个正平方之和；同时证明所有介于 $308$ 与 $10^{16}$ 之间的整数可表示为一个奇素数加两个正立方之和。相关归约过程中所需的椭圆曲线整点被完整确定。

Conclusion: 该工作为"奇素数加两个正平方"猜想提供了迄今最大范围（至 $10^{26}$）的验证，并将其方法推广到素数加两个正立方的表示问题，显示椭圆曲线整点计算是处理此类加性素数表示问题的有效工具。

Abstract: One conjectures that all integers greater than 14 are the sum of an odd prime and two positive squares. We prove that all integers $n$ with $14<n<10^{26}$ can be represented in this manner. Along the way, we investigate sums of a prime and one square and then ``bootstrap'' to get our results on sums of a prime and two squares. We also show that all integers between $308$ and $10^{16}$ can be represented as the sum of an odd prime and two positive cubes. Our methods require the determination of the integral points on various elliptic curves.

</details>


### [211] [An ergodic approach to equations of the form $x+y=\lfloorα(n)\rfloor$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20727)
*Vitaly Bergelson,Hao Pan,Saúl Rodríguez Martín*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文引入遍历论方法研究形如 x+y=⌊α(n)⌋ 的加性方程解的存在性，完整刻画了多项式增长 Hardy 域函数下密度问题与划分问题可解的条件，并推广到双集合、k x+l y 形式及正相对上密度素数子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 Erdős、Sárközy、Sós (1989) 以及 Khalfalah、Szemerédi (2006) 的问题与结果启发，作者希望为关于 x+y=⌊α(n)⌋ 这类加性方程的求解问题提供统一的遍历论框架，并刻画使密度问题与划分问题始终可解的 α 之范围。

Method: 采用遍历论方法（ergodic approach）处理加性方程问题。α(t) 取足够正则的函数，如多项式或多项式增长的对数-指数函数（例如 α(t)=t^{5/2}+t^2 log t）。研究对象 A 为具有正上密度的给定集合，或 N 的某个给定有限划分的胞元。进一步推广至双集合情形：寻找 x∈A_1, y∈A_2，其中 A_1, A_2 为两个正密度集合，并借此处理 k x+l y=⌊α(n)⌋（k,l∈Z）。部分结果对具有正相对上密度的素数子集亦成立。

Result: 对多项式增长的 Hardy 域函数给出了完整刻画，明确了哪些 α 使得相应的划分问题与密度问题总是可解；遍历论方法成功推广到双集合问题及 kx+ly=⌊α(n)⌋ 形式的方程；部分结果在正相对上密度的素数子集上同样成立。

Conclusion: 遍历论方法为研究关于 ⌊α(n)⌋ 的加性方程解的存在性提供了有效且统一的工具，能够完整刻画多项式增长 Hardy 域函数下的可解性，并自然推广至双集合、整数系数线性组合以及素数子集等更一般的情形。

Abstract: Motivated by questions and results of Erdős, Sárközy and Sós (1989), and Khalfalah and Szemerédi (2006), we introduce an ergodic approach to additive problems concerning solutions of equations of the form \[ x+y=\lfloorα(n)\rfloor,\quad x,y\in A,n\in\mathbb{N} \] where $α(t)$ is a sufficiently regular function, such as a polynomial or a logarithmico-exponential function of polynomial growth (e.g. $α(t)=t^{5/2}+t^2\log t$), and $A$ is either a given set of positive upper density, or a cell of a prescribed finite partition of $\mathbb{N}$. We give a complete description of the Hardy field functions of polynomial growth for which these partition and density problems are always solvable.
  The ergodic approach also applies to the more general problem of finding $x\in A_1,y\in A_2$, where $A_1,A_2$ are two given sets of positive density. This in turn allows us to treat equations of the form $kx+ly=\lfloorα(n)\rfloor$, where $k,l\in\mathbb{Z}$.
  Some of our results also hold true when $A$ is a subset of the primes with positive relative upper density.

</details>
