### [212] [A Significant Dust Reservoir Uncovered with JWST in the Type Ic SN 1983V More Than 40 Years Post-Explosion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19263)
*Sophia S. Kressy,Thomas Moore,Ori D. Fox,Nathan Smith,Raphael Baer-Way,Luc Dessart,Michael J. Dulude,W. V. Jacobson-Galán,Melissa Shahbandeh,Tea Temim,Chris Ashall,David A. Coulter,Alexei V. Filippenko,Sebastian Gomez,Jacob Jencson,Kyle Medler,Daniel Patnaude,Tamás Szalai,Schuyler D. Van Dyk,Aiden Agostinelli,Sarah Healy,Zachary G. Lane,Dan Milisavljevic,Shazrene Mohamed,Benjamin Radmore,Armin Rest,Arkaprabha Sarangi,Yossef Zenati,Noah Zimmer*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过JWST在爆炸40余年后探测到Type Ic SN 1983V位置上的点源，认为其为含尘超新星遗迹，尘埃约7.7×10⁻³ M☉并呈环状分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超新星晚期辐射搜寻是活跃领域，红外波段可探测尘埃热辐射，用于约束超新星对宇宙尘埃预算的贡献及前身星质量损失历史。超新星越老，尘埃越可能冷却至多数观测设备（含JWST）探测阈值以下，因此数十年高龄超新星的IR观测极为罕见。

Method: 利用JWST对Type Ic SN 1983V位置进行偶然/意外IR观测，探测到点源；与邻近HII区对比排除其HII区性质；结合档案HST数据寻找可能的Hα对应体，并与持续的超新星激波相互作用导致的尘埃碰撞加热情景进行一致性分析。

Result: 在爆炸40余年后于SN 1983V位置探测到点源，其性质不同于邻近HII区，很可能为含尘超新星；档案HST数据显示存在可能的Hα对应体，与持续激波相互作用碰撞加热尘埃的情景相符。尘埃质量约7.7×10⁻³ M☉，对剥离包层超新星而言偏大；探测年龄仅次于SN 1980K，为JWST探测到的第二老超新星。

Conclusion: 尘埃分布更符合双星相互作用导致的环面（torus）结构，而非球形星风；尘埃可能并非新形成，但表明此类系统可贡献尘埃产生，尤其在大质量恒星与双星系统更普遍的早期宇宙中。最终需光谱观测确认该源的超新星性质。

Abstract: Searching for late-time emission from supernovae (SNe) is an active field. Infrared (IR) wavelengths are sensitive to thermal emission from dust, which can be used to probe SN contributions to the cosmic dust budget and progenitor mass-loss histories. The older an SN, the more likely it is for any existing dust to have cooled below the detection threshold of most observatories, even JWST. Decades-old IR observations of SNe are therefore exceedingly rare. Here we present fortuitous and serendipitous JWST IR observations that detect a point source at the position of the Type Ic SN 1983V more than 40 yr post-explosion. We demonstrate that the source is unlike nearby HII regions and likely to be the dusty SN. We further show evidence from archival HST data of a plausible H/alpha counterpart associated with ongoing SN shock interaction that collisionally heats the dust. In this scenario, the dust is distributed in a torus, more consistent with mass loss from binary interaction than a spherical wind. While not the oldest SN detected by JWST (SN 1980K), SN 1983V is a close second. Moreover, it has a relatively large dust mass (~7.7 x 10^-3 M_solar), particularly for a stripped-envelope SN. Although the dust is not likely newly formed, it does suggest such systems may contribute to dust production, particularly in the early Universe where massive stars and binary systems were more common. Spectroscopic observations can ultimately confirm the SN nature of this source.

</details>


### [213] [Hydrodynamic Simulations of Eccentric Black Hole Encounters in AGN Discs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19281)
*Andrew Imai,Henry Whitehead,Bence Kocsis*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过483组二维粘性流体力学模拟，研究了活动星系核盘中恒星质量黑洞近距交会时的双黑洞捕获问题，重点考察初始偏心率的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活动星系核气盘中嵌入式黑洞可能通过气体内摩擦耗散轨道能量而形成双黑洞，但以往模拟多假设初始轨道为圆形，非圆轨道初始条件对捕获过程的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 在剪切盒框架下使用欧拉网格代码Athena++进行483组二维绝热粘性流体力学模拟，将两个嵌入式黑洞与气体共同演化；改变初始径向间距b、其中一个恒星质量黑洞绕中心超大质量黑洞的偏心率e（0至0.1）及偏心相位角φ_e，并与局部盘厚度比h=H/R0≈0.005作比较。

Result: 小偏心率会使捕获窗口在参数空间中发生位移；当e~h至2h时可实现圆轨道模型无法实现的成功捕获，而e远大于h时系统在交会前保有更少气体、捕获率更低。首次近心点距离可预测直接捕获，判据r_p<0.1r_H对希尔球交会的分类正确率达92.7%。希尔球内气体质量有助于识别"贫气型"失败，但在固定初始密度、温度和粘性系数条件下未给出额外清晰的捕获边界。

Conclusion: 交会前的偏心率通过对首次交会几何形态和可供轨道能量耗散的气体储量的共同作用，调控气体辅助的双黑洞形成过程。

Abstract: We investigate close encounters between stellar-mass black holes (BHs) in the gaseous discs of active galactic nuclei (AGNs), during which binary black holes (BBHs) may form. We perform a suite of 483 2D adiabatic viscous hydrodynamic simulations within a shearing box prescription using the Eulerian grid code Athena++. We co-evolve the two embedded BHs with the gas. To probe the dependence of capture on non-circular initial conditions, we vary the initial radial separation $b$, the eccentricity $e$ of one of the stellar BHs around the central supermassive black hole, and the eccentric phase angle $φ_e$. We consider eccentricities from $e=0$ to $e=0.1$ and compare them with the local disc aspect ratio $h=H/R_0\simeq0.005$. We find that small eccentricities shift the capture window in the parameter space. Eccentricities of order the disc aspect ratio, in particular $e\sim h$--$2h$, produce successful captures at initial separations that do not capture in the circular models. By contrast, systems with $e \gg h$ retain less gas before encounter and have lower capture fractions across the sampled parameter grid. We find that the first periapsis distance is a useful predictor of direct capture. Predicting direct capture for $r_{\rm p}<0.1r_H$ correctly classifies $92.7\%$ of Hill sphere encounters. The Hill sphere gas mass helps identify gas-poor failures, but provides no clear additional capture boundary within this suite, in which the initial disc density, temperature, and viscosity parameter are held fixed. Pre-encounter eccentricity modifies gas-assisted BBH formation through its combined effects on the first-encounter geometry and the gas reservoir available for orbital energy dissipation.

</details>


### [214] [Lepto-hadronic modeling of blazars associated with well-reconstructed high-energy neutrino events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19477)
*Leonard Pfeiffer,Margot Boughelilba,Simone Garrappa,Sara Buson,Massimiliano Lincetto,Annette Bremer*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过多波段SED与轻子-强子模型分析6个与IceCube高能中微子事件空间一致的耀变体，发现其预测中微子率与单个观测事件相容，可能是单个事件的可能对应体，但难以显著贡献弥散中微子通量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活动星系核（AGN）与高能中微子的关联在族群和单源分析中均有研究，但这些关联究竟反映真实物理过程还是偶然符合尚不明确。耀变体（视线方向对齐相对论喷流的AGN）与IceCube高能事件空间一致，为检验该问题提供了天然框架。

Method: 选取6个与IceCAT-1高能事件（并考虑IceCAT-2改进定位）具有单一伽马射线对应体的耀变体，汇编其多波段SED。在轻子-强子框架下建模，探索参数空间以复现观测辐射，并偏好最大化中微子产生的解。

Result: SED可用靠近宽线区的致密发射区与中等多普勒因子复现。辐射总体以轻子主导，强子级联贡献次之，仅一个源伽马射线由强子主导。4个源中相对论粒子能量密度接近与磁场均分。预测中微子探测率最高约5×10⁻²/年，在泊松统计下多与一个观测到的IceCube事件相容。

Conclusion: 这些耀变体不太可能显著贡献弥散中微子通量，但其性质与作为单个中微子事件的可能对应体相容，这由外部光子场对强子相互作用率的贡献驱动，即便对这些场此前被认为不显著的源也是如此。

Abstract: The association of active galactic nuclei (AGN) with high-energy neutrinos has been studied through population and individual source analyzes, but whether these associations reflect physical processes or chance coincidences remains open. Blazars, AGN with relativistic jets aligned with the line of sight, spatially consistent with IceCube high-energy events, offer a natural framework to address this question. Combining multifrequency observations with physically motivated emission models allows us to assess whether these sources can plausibly account for the detected neutrinos. We study six blazars with single gamma-ray counterparts to well-reconstructed IceCAT-1 high-energy events, accounting for the improved localization expected from IceCAT-2, and compile their multifrequency spectral energy distributions (SEDs). We model these sources within a lepto-hadronic framework, exploring their parameter space to reproduce the observed emission while favoring solutions that maximize neutrino production. The SEDs can be reproduced with compact emission regions close to the broad-line region with moderate Doppler factors. The emission is generally leptonic-dominated, with subdominant contributions from hadronic cascades, except for one source where the gamma-rays are hadronic-dominated. For four, the energy density of the relativistic particles is close to equipartition with the magnetic field. The predicted neutrino detection rates reach up to $~5x10^{-2}$ per year, mostly compatible with one observed IceCube event under Poisson statistics. While these blazars are unlikely to significantly contribute to the diffuse neutrino flux, their properties are consistent with being possible counterparts to individual neutrino events, driven by contributions of external photon fields to the hadronic interaction rate, even for sources where these fields have previously been considered as non-significant.

</details>


### [215] [Polarization of Synchrotron Emission from Rotation Powered Pulsars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19741)
*Seima Saeki,Shota Kisaka,Jumpei Takata*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文在外间隙模型中同时考虑向外与向内（朝转轴）发射，研究旋转动力脉冲星的X射线偏振特性并与IXPE观测比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2021年IXPE升空后获得了多颗脉冲星的X射线偏振数据，为充分利用这些数据，需要理解偏振特性与磁层结构的关系；而此前模型未考虑向内发射对偏振的贡献。

Method: 在外间隙模型中引入向外发射和朝转轴方向的向内发射两类辐射，计算偏振位置角（PA）与偏振度的变化，并将模型与IXPE观测对比。

Result: 偏振位置角在强度峰值处发生变化，通常伴随高偏振度（与向外发射情形一致）；当向外与向内发射的强度峰重叠时，PA变化往往超过50°，大于仅有向外发射的情形。对PSR B1509-58的对比显示，观测PA摆动更倾向于来自南半球的视线方向。

Conclusion: 纳入向内发射可显著改变偏振位置角的摆动特征，有助于用IXPE观测约束外间隙模型的磁层几何，如PSR B1509-58的视线方向。

Abstract: With the launch of the Imaging X-ray Polarimeter Explorer in 2021, recent observations have provided X-ray polarization data for several rotation-powered pulsars. To fully exploit these data, it is important to better understand the relationship between polarization properties and the magnetospheric structure. In this paper, we investigate polarization properties in the outer gap model, incorporating both outward emission and inward emission toward the rotation axis, which has not been considered previously. We find that the polarization position angle varies at the intensity peaks, typically accompanied by a high polarization degree, as is in the case for outward emission. Moreover, the variation in the polarization position angle tends to exceed 50 degrees when the intensity peaks of outward and inward emissions overlap, compared to cases with only outward emission. We compare the polarization model including inward emission with IXPE observations. For example, for PSR B1509-58 where inward emission is proposed to be dominant in the outer gap model, we find that observed PA swing prefers a line of sight from the southern hemisphere rather than the northern hemisphere.

</details>


### [216] [The rise and decline of a transient obscuration event in a moderately distant type-I quasar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19910)
*A. Akylas,A. Georgakakis,E. Pouliasis,I. Georgantopoulos,C. Ricci,M. Chira,A. Ruiz,E. M. Xilouris*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对红移z=0.35的I型类星体2MASS J14302580+4159572开展了跨22年的多波段观测，首次捕捉到一个瞬变电离吸收体（温吸收体）的完整出现与消退过程，并揭示其与内层窄线区电离状态变化同步，提供了类星体温吸收体长期演化的罕见案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电离物质外流（温吸收体，WAs）在近邻赛弗特星系中很常见，但在更遥远、能量更高的类星体（QSOs）中却十分稀少；同时温吸收体的长期演化及其对核周环境的影响缺乏直接观测，因此需要借助长时间基线、多历元的多波段观测来研究这一现象。

Method: 利用XMM-Newton（2002–2005年发现观测）及XMM-Newton、NuSTAR、Swift/XRT联合的22年多历元X射线观测，结合光学观测，对2MASS J14302580+4159572进行光谱分析；拟合吸收体的柱密度与电离参数，追踪其随时间变化；并联合高电离[O III] λ4363发射线变化进行对比验证。

Result: 前两次观测的X射线谱为典型无遮蔽I型类星体谱，2005年观测在约3 keV以下出现显著吸收凹陷，拟合得到中等电离吸收体柱密度NH≈5×10^22 cm^-2、电离参数log ξ≈1.4；该吸收体在近18年内保持稳定，直至2023–2024年Swift/XRT数据显示其减弱；同期内禀3–10 keV连续谱极为稳定，说明变光由视线方向电离吸收变化驱动而非内禀光度波动。高电离[O III] λ4363发射线与X射线吸收同步变化。

Conclusion: 该事件被解释为位于宽线区（BLR）之外、亚秒差距至秒差距尺度、密度约10^6 cm^-3的热驱动电离外流；外流在十年尺度上逐步改变内层窄线区（NLR）的电离状态。该观测为类星体温吸收体的长期演化及其对核周环境的影响提供了罕见样本。

Abstract: Outflows of ionised material, often dubbed warm absorbers (WAs), are common in nearby Seyferts but sparse in distant and more energetic quasars (QSOs). We present multi-epoch X-ray and optical observations of the serendipitously identified type-I QSO 2MASS J14302580+4159572 at a redshift of $z=0.35$, revealing the rare rise and decline of a transient ionised absorber over a 22-year timescale.
  The discovery observations were obtained with XMM-Newton between 2002 and 2005. The first two observations show unremarkable X-ray spectra typical of unobscured type-I QSOs, whereas the 2005 observation reveals a pronounced suppression of the spectrum below $\sim3\,\mathrm{keV}$. Spectral analysis shows that this event is produced by a moderately ionised absorber with column density $N_{\rm H}\simeq5\times10^{22}\,\mathrm{cm^{-2}}$ and ionisation parameter $\logξ\simeq1.4$. Joint analysis of XMM-Newton, NuSTAR, and Swift/XRT observations spanning 22 years indicates that the absorber remained stable for nearly 18 years before weakening in the 2023--2024 Swift/XRT data. Throughout this period, the intrinsic 3--10 keV continuum remained remarkably stable, indicating that the observed variability is driven by changes in line-of-sight ionised absorption rather than intrinsic luminosity fluctuations.
  We interpret the event as a thermally driven ionised outflow located outside the broad-line region (BLR), at sub-parsec to parsec scales, with a density of order $10^{6}\,\mathrm{cm^{-3}}$. Supporting this scenario, the high-ionisation [O\,III] $\lambda4363$ emission line varies in tandem with the X-ray absorption, suggesting that the outflow progressively modifies the ionisation state of the inner narrow-line region (NLR) on decadal timescales. These observations provide a rare view of the long-term evolution of a quasar warm absorber and its impact on the circumnuclear environment.

</details>


### [217] [Investigating the influence of the full-kinematics treatment for charged-current processes in binary neutron star mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19922)
*Ramon Jaeger,Henrique Gieg,Tim Dietrich*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 比较双中子星并合模拟中三种带电弱相互作用速率处理方案（弹性近似、NuLib、完整运动学），评估其对抛射物、中微子光度、引力波谱及核合成产额的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱相互作用在双中子星并合中至关重要（冷却、改变物质成分、中微子驱动风），中微子输运已成为数值相对论模拟的标准配置；其中带电弱相互作用速率的弹性近似被普遍采用，但其准确性缺乏系统比较。

Method: 在截断双矩（M1）输运方案下，采用隐式-显式时间积分器，对同一双中子星并合系统分别使用三种带电弱相互作用速率方案（弹性近似、公开的NuLib库、完整运动学方法）进行并合模拟对比。

Result: 弹性近似略微高估动力学抛射物质量，但低估其中的高电子分数物质；早期中微子光度被高估。完整运动学与NuLib速率差异在模拟不确定度内可忽略。三种方案的并合后引力波谱在数值不确定度内无法区分。核网络计算的核合成产额大体一致，惟在A∈[90,125]质量区间弹性近似给出更高丰度。不同相互作用速率还影响残余吸积盘性质。

Conclusion: 弹性近似对带电弱相互作用速率的处理存在系统性偏差，而完整运动学与NuLib结果基本等价；更精确的相互作用速率对跨越长期演化时标的模拟尤为重要。

Abstract: Weak interactions are fundamental in the merger of binary neutron stars (BNS). They provide cooling, change the composition of matter outflows, and can lead to significant secular outflows through neutrino-driven winds. Hence, the inclusion of neutrino transport has become standard in numerical-relativity simulations of mergers involving neutron stars. In more detail, the interactions of neutrinos with matter in a truncated two-moments scheme (M1) are described through interaction rates, calculated from a set of standard interactions. Among those are charged-current reactions, for which the elastic approximation is commonly assumed. More sophisticated alternatives are provided by, e.g., the publicly available NuLib library, or rates computed within a full kinematics approach. In this work, we compare merger simulations of a BNS systems employing each of these three prescriptions for the computation of charged-current weak rates, included in the M1 equations with an implicit-explicit time integrator. We find that the elastic approximation slightly overestimates the dynamical ejecta mass, while underestimating the amount of high electron fraction material therein. Additionally, it overestimates the neutrino luminosities at early times. The effects of full kinematics in comparison to the NuLib rates appear negligible within the uncertainties of the simulation data. Similarly, the post-merger gravitational-wave spectra for the three prescriptions were found to be indistinguishable within the numerical uncertainties. Nucleosynthetic yields, computed with a nuclear network, are broadly consistent, except in the atomic mass range $A\in[90,125]$ where the elastic approximation shows larger abundances. Finally, the different interaction rates seem to affect the remnant disk properties, hinting at the importance of more accurate interaction rates for simulations spanning secular timescales.

</details>


### [218] [A 32-day Quasi-periodic Modulation in the Post-peak Light Curve of the Superluminous Supernova SN 2018bsz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19984)
*Aiswarya Sankar. K,Ting-Wan Chen,Seán J. Brennan,Morgan Fraser,Keiichi Maeda,Thomas Moore,Sheng Yang,Amar Aryan,Dietrich Baade,Ping Chen,Chow-Choong Ngeow*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 对近邻贫氢超亮超新星SN 2018bsz进行多波段光度再分析，在十个波段中发现约31.6天的准周期残余调制，可能源于中心引擎、前身星双星或结构化星周介质，但观测尚不能唯一确定物理起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超新星光变曲线中的周期性结构可能揭示中心引擎动力学、双星轨道运动或结构化星周介质（CSM）等物理时钟，因此需要寻找并刻画此类周期性信号。

Method: 利用Swift/UVOT与GROND对SN 2018bsz（z=0.0267）在峰值后约110天静止系内的多波段观测；对各波段平滑衰减成分独立建模并扣除，采用广义Lomb-Scargle周期图分析残差，并进行联合多波段分析、去趋势稳健性检验、比较星与月周期检验。

Result: 在u至Ks十个波段中发现相位大致一致的周期性残余变化；g、r、i、z、J单波段最强探测周期为31.5–31.8天，联合分析给出静止系周期P=31.61±0.03天；信号跨越约三个周期，故为准周期；对去趋势方法稳健，比较星与月周期检验不支持观测系统性；从光学到近红外相位大体相干，振幅向短波增强，低显著性波段可能存在随波长变化的周期偏移。

Conclusion: SN 2018bsz是SLSN-I中相干、月尺度光度调制最清晰的案例之一；当前观测无法唯一判定物理起源，Lense-Thirring进动、爆炸前结构化CSM相互作用以及超新星后与幸存伴星的相互作用均为可能机制。

Abstract: Recurrent structure in supernova (SN) light curves may reveal physical clocks associated with central-engine dynamics, binary orbital motion, or interaction with structured circumstellar material (CSM). We present a multiband photometric reanalysis of the nearby hydrogen-poor superluminous SN (SLSN-I) 2018bsz (z=0.0267), using Swift/UVOT and GROND observations spanning approximately 110 rest-frame days after maximum light. After independently modelling and subtracting the smooth decline in each band, Generalized Lomb-Scargle periodograms reveal recurrent residual variations at broadly consistent phases across ten bands from u to Ks. The strongest individual-band detections, in the g, r, i, z, and J bands, yield periods of 31.5-31.8 days, while the joint multiband analysis gives a rest-frame period of P=31.61 (+/-0.03) days. The modulation is traced over approximately three cycles and is therefore described as quasi-periodic. The signal is robust to the adopted detrending procedure, while comparison-star and lunar-cycle tests disfavour an observational systematic. The modulation is broadly phase-coherent from the optical to the near-infrared, but its amplitude increases towards shorter wavelengths; lower-significance bands also show possible wavelength-dependent shifts in the best-fitting period. The present observations do not uniquely determine the physical origin. Lense-Thirring precession, interaction with CSM structured before the explosion, and post-SN interaction with a surviving companion remain possible. SN 2018bsz provides one of the clearest examples of coherent, month-scale photometric modulation in an SLSN-I.

</details>


### [219] [The 2025.6 X-ray Minimum of Eta Carinae](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20046)
*Amit Kashi,Noam Soker*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用NICER、Swift、XRISM和Chandra对η Carinae 2025.6 X射线极小期及其恢复过程进行多波段观测，发现极小期提前约10天，光谱软化且光度短时标变化，表明X射线衰减并非由视向吸收（如食）主导，而是由双星风的不稳定相互作用、碰撞风区破坏以及主星风物质吸积到次星所致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究η Carinae周期性别距点附近的X射线极小（光谱事件）成因，特别是检验"增强视向吸收（如食）"假说是否成立，并理解该现象与双星风相互作用和吸积过程的关系。

Method: 使用NICER/XTI、Swift/XRT、XRISM/Xtend和Chandra/HRC-I对2025.6 X射线极小期及恢复期进行观测；构建软（0.5–2 keV）和硬（2–10 keV）能段光变曲线，并计算硬度比。

Result: X射线极小期比前五个周期提前约10天出现；与以往事件类似，光谱在下降期变软，同时X射线光度在短时标上存在变化。

Conclusion: 观测强化了"增强视向吸收（食）在产生光谱事件中不起主要作用"的结论；X射线衰减由双星风及其相互作用的随机变化与不稳定性、碰撞风区破坏以及主星风物质吸积到次星引起。近星点处深度X射线极小的主导机制是主星物质被次星吸积。该吸积过程在十九世纪大爆发和次大爆发期间（主星质量损失率更高）应以更高吸积率发生，并可能驱动喷流。更广泛的启示是：双星系统中的吸积事件（可能伴随喷流）为其他亮蓝变星光变巨爆发和中等光度光学暂现源提供能量。

Abstract: We present observations of the recent 2025.6 X-ray minimum and recovery of $η$~Carinae, obtained with NICER/XTI, Swift/XRT, XRISM/Xtend, and Chandra/HRC-I. We construct light curves in the soft (0.5--2 keV) and hard (2--10 keV) bands and derive hardness ratios. The X-ray emission reached its minimum about 10 days earlier than in the previous five cycles. As in previous events, the spectrum softened during the decline, while the X-ray luminosity varied on short timescales. These findings strengthen the conclusion that enhanced line-of-sight absorption, such as that expected during an eclipse, does not have a significant role in producing the spectroscopic event. Instead, they indicate that the X-ray decline is caused by stochastic variations and instabilities in the two stellar winds and their interaction, destruction of the colliding winds region, and accretion of primary wind onto the secondary. The main process behind the deep X-ray minimum near periastron is the accretion of mass lost by the primary star onto the secondary star. Accretion at periastron passages in $η$ Carinae implies that accretion occurred at much higher rates during the nineteenth-century Great and Lesser Eruptions, when the primary star lost mass at much higher rates. Such accretion likely launches jets. The broader implication is that accretion events in binary systems, possibly with jet launching, power other luminous blue variable giant eruptions and intermediate-luminosity optical transients.

</details>


### [220] [Evolution and mergers of eccentric white dwarf binaries encountering intermediate-mass black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20338)
*Adarsh Pandey,Aryabrat Mahapatra,Tapobrata Sarkar*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用三维SPH模拟研究了偏心白矮星双星在绕中等质量黑洞近距离掠过后的演化，发现IMBH会显著加速并改变双星并合过程，且结果与接近强度非单调相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究近距离掠过中等质量黑洞（IMBH）如何改变具有内部近心点Roche瓣溢出的偏心白矮星（WD）双星的演化。

Method: 采用三维光滑粒子流体动力学（SPH）模拟，沿绕Schwarzschild IMBH的抛物线、逆行轨道演化偏心率$e_{\rm in}=0.6$、质量比$q=0.2$--$0.7$的双星，在两种接近强度$β^b=0.5$与$1.5$下进行，并与孤立演化情形对比。

Result: IMBH大幅加速并合：所有受扰双星均在第二次内部近心点通过时或稍后并合，时间短于孤立情形。并合结果与接近强度非单调：$q=0.2$双星在$β^b=1.5$时保留两个束缚核，而在$β^b=0.5$时并合；深掠使双星进入偏心率更低的轨道，表明遭遇时机相对于内轨道很重要。深掠对$q=0.2$--$0.4$产生内部轨道角动量的瞬时反转，吸积体束缚物质获得比供体更多的自旋角动量。双星质心绕IMBH的比轨道角动量仅变化初始值的几个$×10^{-4}$。还识别出约$\sim15$--$17\%$（$q=0.7$）氦来源物质可在动力学时标上燃烧氦。

Conclusion: IMBH近距离遭遇能够从定量上改变偏心白矮星双星的演化。

Abstract: We study how a close passage past an intermediate-mass black hole (IMBH) changes the evolution of an eccentric white dwarf (WD) binary that has nominal Roche lobe overflow at internal pericenter. Using three-dimensional smoothed particle hydrodynamics, we follow binaries with eccentricity $e_{\rm in}=0.6$ and mass ratios $q=0.2$ -- $0.7$ on parabolic, retrograde orbits around a Schwarzschild IMBH at two encounter strengths, $β^b=0.5$ and $1.5$. We compare them with the same binaries evolved in isolation. The IMBH greatly speeds up the merger. Every IMBH-perturbed binary that merges does so during or shortly after its second internal pericenter passage, which is always less than the time taken to merge in isolation. The merger outcome is not monotonic with encounter strength. The $q=0.2$ binary retains two bound cores for $β^b=1.5$ but merges at $β^b=0.5$. In the deeper encounter it settles onto a less eccentric post-encounter orbit, showing that the timing of the encounter relative to the inner orbit plays an important role. Deep encounters also produce transient reversals of the internal orbital angular momentum for $q=0.2$ -- $0.4$, while the material bound to the accretor gains more spin angular momentum than that bound to the donor. The specific orbital angular momentum of the binary CM around the IMBH changes by only a few $\times10^{-4}$ of its initial value. We also identify He-origin material that can burn helium on a dynamical timescale, amounting to $\sim15$ -- $17\%$ of the He-origin mass for $q=0.7$. IMBH encounters can therefore quantitatively change the evolution of eccentric WD binaries.

</details>


### [221] [Low Frequency Radio Imaging Study of PSR J1818-1607](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20745)
*A. Yadav,M. P. Surnis,B. C. Joshi,M. Bagchi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用uGMRT在300–500 MHz和550–750 MHz对磁星PSR J1818−1607开展低频射电观测，发现流量密度与谱指数变化，并识别出两次可能的射电耀发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究射电强磁星的辐射机制，尤其是谱指数变化对潜在发射机制的限制。

Method: 使用升级版巨型米波射电望远镜（uGMRT）在band 3（300–500 MHz）和band 4（550–750 MHz）对磁星PSR J1818−1607进行低频射电观测，并检索多个档案射电成像巡天数据。

Result: 识别出与该磁星相关的连续谱源；观测到流量密度变化及其谱指数变化；流量密度时间序列显示两次潜在射电耀发，且具有不同谱特性；档案巡天中未探测到连续谱源，也无相关弥漫发射的直接证据。

Conclusion: 谱指数变化对射电强磁星的潜在发射机制具有重要约束意义，需进一步观测以厘清其辐射本质。

Abstract: We report on the low-frequency radio observations of magnetar PSR J1818$-$1607 carried out with the upgraded Giant Metrewave Radio Telescope at band 3 (300$-$500 MHz) and band 4 (550-750 MHz). We have identified the continuum source associated with the magnetar and report variations in flux density and its spectral index. The flux density timeseries for the magnetar reveals the presence of two potential radio flaring episodes with varying spectral properties. We discuss the implications of the spectral index changes on the potential emission mechanisms for radio-loud magnetars. We also report non-detections of the continuum source as well as no direct indication for an associated diffuse emission from multiple archival radio imaging surveys.

</details>


### [222] [A systematic search for long GRBs without supernovae: global properties and rate of events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20787)
*Yu-Han Yang,Eleonora Troja,Nicolas Monsalves,Massine El Kabir,Narjes Shahamat Dehsorkh,Hira Waseem*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用Swift二十年观测系统搜寻近距无超新星长伽马暴，将候选样本翻倍，测得体积速率约0.5 Gpc⁻³ yr⁻¹，与短暴及有超新星长暴相当，可能与致密双星并合通道相符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分长伽马暴（LGRB）未伴随明亮超新星，说明其爆发机制不同于传统的坍缩星（collapsar）通道。此类无超新星LGRB极为稀少，它们究竟是偶发离群事件还是GRB族群中的重要组成部分尚不清楚，因此需要系统性地搜寻并统计其性质与发生率。

Method: 使用Swift卫星二十年的观测数据，系统搜寻红移z≲0.35的近距、且无已确认超新星关联的LGRB。分析这些事件的高能特性、余辉以及寄主星系环境，筛选出具有严格超新星上限和可靠距离尺度的样本，并据此推导体积速率（未做喷流束流校正）。

Result: 揭示出一个异质族群：部分与恒星形成或尘埃环境相关，部分位于宁静星系中。新样本使候选无超新星事件数量翻倍以上。由基准样本得到体积速率R=(0.5±0.2) Gpc⁻³ yr⁻¹，与短暴及光度L≳10⁵⁰ erg s⁻¹的有超新星标准长暴的表观速率相当。在合理束流因子下，该速率与多种致密双星并合通道一致，但若整个族群归因于单一前身星通道，允许的参数空间已相当狭窄。

Conclusion: 由于候选事件数量少且距离尺度存在不确定性，样本无法约束低光度尾部。要判定无超新星LGRB是已知前身星通道的暗弱尾部，还是存在通往相对论性爆发的独立途径，仍需更大样本。

Abstract: Some long duration gamma-ray bursts (LGRBs) are not associated with bright supernovae (SNe), indicating that their explosion mechanism differs from the traditional collapsar channel. SN-less LGRBs are rare, thus it remains unclear whether they are sporadic outliers or a substantial component of the GRB population. In this work, we use two decades of Swift observations to conduct a systematic search for nearby ($z\lesssim$0.35) LGRBs without a confirmed SN association. Our analysis reveals a heterogeneous population linked, in part, to star-forming or dusty environments and, in part, to quiescent galaxies. Through the analysis of their high-energy properties, afterglows, and environments, we identify a sample of LGRBs with robust SN limits and distance scales, more than doubling the number of candidate SN-less events. From our fiducial sample, we derive a volumetric rate, not corrected for beaming, of $R = (0.5 \pm 0.2) {\rm~Gpc^{-3}\ yr^{-1}}$, comparable to the apparent rate of short duration GRBs (SGRBs) and standard LGRBs with SNe with luminosities $L \gtrsim\,10^{50}\,{\rm erg\,s^{-1}}$. For plausible beaming factors, the inferred rate of SN-less LGRBs is consistent with several compact binary merger channels, although the allowed parameter space is already narrow if the entire population is attributed to a single progenitor channel. Owing to the small number of candidate events and the uncertainties in their distance scale, our sample leaves the low-luminosity tail unconstrained. A larger sample will be needed to determine whether they are the faint tail of known progenitor channels or evidence of a distinct route to relativistic explosions.

</details>


### [223] [Systematic Effects of Hydrogen and Helium Atmosphere Mismatch on Radius Inference in PSR~J0740+6620-like Synthetic NICER Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20795)
*Isiah M. Holt,M. Coleman Miller,Alexander J. Dittmann,Frederick K. Lamb*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文用基于PSR J0740+6620的合成NICER脉冲波形数据，检验大气成分假设错误对中子星半径推断的影响，发现强聚束比下会出现显著偏差且拟合残差仍可接受，但贝叶斯证据能正确识别大气模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星半径约束可揭示其内部冷致密物质性质。已有合成NICER数据研究表明半径推断对多类建模系统误差稳健，但错误的非均匀大气成分假设（氢/氦）带来的影响尚不明确，需评估其对半径估计的偏差及模型选择可靠性。

Method: 以约2.1倍太阳质量脉冲星PSR J0740+6620观测为基础生成合成NICER脉冲波形数据。在合成数据采用氦（或氢）大气的前提下，拟合时故意假定氢（或氦）大气，比较推断半径的偏差。测试两种条件：一是与真实PSR J0740+6620数据相同的斑点到背景比；二是将斑点到背景比提高约20倍，同时将总计数固定为观测的约5.5×10^5。并利用贝叶斯证据进行模型比较。

Result: 在PSR J0740+6620实际的斑点到背景比下，氢/氦大气成分错配对推断半径几乎无偏。当斑点到背景比提高约20倍且总计数固定时，成分错配可产生显著偏置的半径估计，而拟合仍可给出统计上可接受的残差。即便在这些情况下，贝叶斯证据始终能正确识别出正确的大气模型。

Conclusion: 大气成分错配对半径推断的影响依赖于斑点到背景比；在低聚束比下可忽略，但在高聚束比下可产生隐蔽的显著偏差。仅凭可接受的拟合残差不足以排除系统误差，应重视使用贝叶斯证据进行模型比较，并结合拟合优度检验。

Abstract: Constraints on neutron star radii provide insight into the properties of the cold, dense matter in their interiors. Previous studies using synthetic Neutron star Interior Composition Explorer (NICER) pulse waveform data have demonstrated that radius inferences derived therefrom are robust against several classes of modeling systematics. Here we explore the consequences of assuming the wrong atmospheric composition, using synthetic data based on the $\sim 2.1~M_\odot$ pulsar PSR~J0740$+$6620. We find that the assumption of a hydrogen atmosphere when the synthetic data assumed a helium atmosphere, or vice versa, produces little bias in the inferred radius at the spot-to-background ratio of the actual PSR~J0740$+$6620 data. However, when we increase the spot-to-background ratio by a factor of $\sim20$ while keeping the total number of counts fixed at the observed $\sim 5.5\times 10^5$, we find that composition mismatch can produce significantly biased radius estimates while remaining hidden inside a fit with statistically acceptable residuals. Even in these cases, the Bayesian evidence consistently identifies the correct atmospheric model. Our findings reinforce the importance of using the Bayesian evidence for model comparison and goodness-of-fit tests.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [224] [Bad metallicity in the semi-quantum regime of the Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19264)
*Evyatar Tulipman,Vadim Oganesyan,Thomas P. Devereaux,Steven A. Kivelson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在无限U哈伯德模型中，通过精确对角化发现小空穴密度下存在一个“半量子区”式的中间温区：压缩率近似与温度无关，而电阻率呈T线性且超过Ioffe-Regel极限，其微观机制是掺杂空穴周围形成的准局域铁磁“自旋笼”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 坏金属在超过Ioffe-Regel极限后仍表现出近似T线性的直流电阻率，且这一现象出现在基态迥异的多种体系中，暗示它是强局域关联的普适表现。作者希望用一个极简模型检验这一假设，并寻找该反常输运的微观起源。

Method: 采用无限U哈伯德模型，取小空穴密度，利用精确对角化方法对有限尺寸团簇计算热力学与输运性质。

Result: 降温过程中出现一个中间温区：量子效应使压缩率近似与温度无关，但电阻率呈T线性并超过Ioffe-Regel极限。进入该温区伴随掺杂空穴周围形成准局域铁磁“自旋笼”，支撑局域量子运动嵌入涨落自旋背景之中。尽管模型极简，其坏金属行为与多种材料平台所观测到的现象相似。

Conclusion: 该中间温区可类比液氦的“半量子区”，表明远超Ioffe-Regel极限的T线性电阻率是强局域关联的普适结果，而准局域铁磁自旋笼可能是不相干输运的关键机制。

Abstract: Bad metals exhibit approximately $T$-linear dc resistivity beyond the Ioffe--Regel limit. That this behavior occurs in systems with radically different ground states suggests it is a generic manifestation of strong local correlations. We test this hypothesis in the infinite-$U$ Hubbard model with small hole densities using exact diagonalization to compute thermodynamic and transport properties of finite clusters. Upon cooling, we find an intermediate temperature range, an electronic analogue of the ``semi-quantum regime'' of liquid helium, in which quantum effects produce a roughly $T$-independent compressibility, yet the resistivity is $T$-linear and exceeds the Ioffe--Regel limit. Entry into this regime is accompanied by the formation of quasi-local ferromagnetic ``spin cages'' around doped holes, regions that facilitate local quantum motion embedded in a fluctuating spin background, analogous to the transient crystalline cages thought to control incoherent transport in semi-quantum liquid helium. Remarkably, despite the simplicity of the model, the bad metal behavior found here resembles that seen in various material platforms.

</details>


### [225] [Non-Abelian Anyon Condensation: a Path-Integral Monte Carlo Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19282)
*Rafael Flores-Calderón,Frank Pollmann,Michael Knap*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出无符号问题的路径积分框架，用于研究Kitaev量子双模型 $\mathcal D(G)$ 中非阿贝尔拓扑序的相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔拓扑序的相变难以用可在宏观尺度保持可计算性的微观数值方法描述，缺乏统一且数值可行的研究框架。

Method: 将单键微扰按非可逆电/磁1-形式对称性组织；对任意有限群 $G$ 构造精确的"物质化"等距映射，引入顶点自由度，将仅含键的模型映射为 $G$ 规范-希格斯理论；利用不动点的1-形式对称性定义广义 Fredenhagen-Marcu 序参量，并对 $G=S_3$ 进行量子蒙特卡洛模拟。

Result: 对 $G=S_3$，增殖非阿贝尔电任意子驱动一级相变，所有非平凡电任意子凝聚；纯磁极限下模型退化为 (2+1) 维纯 $G$ 规范理论，$G=S_3$ 时呈现一级禁闭相变，由 Wilson 圈面积律的出现和涌现磁1-形式对称性的恢复所诊断。广义 Fredenhagen-Marcu 序参量在对应任意子凝聚时变为有限值。

Conclusion: 该工作为非阿贝尔任意子凝聚、禁闭及广义对称性破缺提供了统一的数值研究框架。

Abstract: Transitions out of non-Abelian topological order are difficult to describe in microscopic quantum models with numerical methods that remain tractable at large scales. We develop a sign-free path-integral framework for Kitaev quantum doubles $\mathcal D(G)$ by organizing single-link perturbations in terms of non-invertible electric and magnetic 1-form symmetries that proliferate distinct anyon species. For any finite group $G$, an exact $\textit{matterization}$ isometry introduces vertex degrees of freedom and maps the link-only model onto a $G$ gauge--Higgs theory. Furthermore, the 1-form symmetries of the fixed point allow us to define generalized Fredenhagen--Marcu order parameters that become finite when the corresponding anyons condense. For $G = S_3$, quantum Monte Carlo simulations show that proliferating a non-Abelian electric anyon drives a first-order transition in which all nontrivial electric anyons condense. In the purely magnetic limit, the model reduces to a $(2+1)$D pure $G$ gauge theory; for $G=S_3$, it exhibits a first-order confinement transition, diagnosed by the onset of a Wilson-loop area law and the restoration of an emergent magnetic 1-form symmetry. These results provide a unified numerical framework for non-Abelian anyon condensation, confinement, and generalized symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [226] [Valley-Helical Superconductivity Driven by Repulsion and Quantum Geometry: Applications to Time-Reversal Symmetric Rhombohedral Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19283)
*Julian May-Mann,Trithep Devakul,Gal Shavit*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在无谷极化、无时间反演破缺的体系中，过屏蔽排斥（Kohn-Luttinger机制）可诱导各谷内具有相反手性的配对，形成谷螺旋超导态，并在中等耦合下压过常规谷间配对态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期在谷极化石墨烯及过渡金属硫族化合物体系中观测到手性超导的实验迹象，作者希望探究在不依赖谷极化或时间反演对称性破缺的情况下是否也能实现手性配对。

Method: 针对两谷具有相反Berry曲率的体系，分析过屏蔽排斥（Kohn-Luttinger机制）诱导的配对；并采用时间反演对称的菱面体石墨烯实际模型进行计算。

Result: Kohn-Luttinger机制可在每个谷内诱导出相反手性的配对，形成谷螺旋态；该态在中等耦合下能胜过仅具弱耦合不稳定性的常规谷间配对态。在4≤N≤8的菱面体石墨烯模型中，发现存在大范围的填充与位移场区域实现主导的谷螺旋超导。

Conclusion: 谷螺旋超导态具有拓扑投影边缘模、异常磁响应以及交织的Kekulé型键序等特征，可用于将其与更常规的超导体区分开。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent experimental signatures of chiral superconductivity in valley-polarized graphene- and transition-metal-dichalcogenide-based systems, we investigate the possibility of chiral pairing in the absence of valley polarization or time-reversal symmetry breaking. For systems with opposite Berry curvature in the two valleys, we find that overscreened repulsion (i.e., the Kohn-Luttinger mechanism) can indeed induce pairing of opposite chiralities in each valley, producing a valley-helical state. Moreover, the valley-helical state can outcompete more conventional intervalley paired states at intermediate coupling, despite only the latter having a weak-coupling instability. Using a realistic model of time-reversal-symmetric rhombohedral graphene, we find extended ranges of fillings and displacement fields with dominant valley-helical superconductivity. This occurs for all layer numbers considered here, $4\leq N\leq 8$. We show that the valley-helical states can display topologically projected edge modes, unusual magnetic responses, and intertwined Kekulé-like bond-order; features that can be used to distinguish them from more conventional superconductors.

</details>


### [227] [Energy partitioning of wave packets in one-dimensional systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19450)
*Flavia B. Ramos,Imke Schneider,Sebastian Eggert,Rodrigo G. Pereira*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文用tDMRG研究一维临界费米子体系中高斯波包的非平衡动力学，发现相互作用导致激发分数化为左右传播模式，并用Luttinger液体理论解析预测其能量分配，指出能量分配非普适、依赖波包宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究一维临界费米子体系中波包激发的非平衡动力学，特别是能量如何在涌现激发之间分配，并与电荷分数化进行对比。

Method: 在基态上注入高斯波包，采用时间依赖密度矩阵重整化群（tDMRG）追踪实时演化；并用Luttinger液体理论推导涌现模式所携带能量的解析预测。

Result: 观察到相互作用导致初始激发分数化为反向传播的左右移动模式，其能量可在实空间分辨；低能区场论预测与数值模拟吻合良好；与由Luttinger液体参数完全决定的电荷分数化不同，能量分配是非普适的，依赖波包宽度等细节。

Conclusion: 研究提供了能量分配在实空间中的表征，并建立了非平衡数值模拟与有效场论描述之间的定量比较。

Abstract: We investigate the non-equilibrium dynamics of wave packets in a one-dimensional critical fermionic system and analyze the resulting partitioning of energy between emergent excitations. Starting from a Gaussian wave packet injected on top of the many-body ground state, we follow its real-time evolution using the time-dependent density-matrix renormalization group. We observe that interactions lead to fractionalization of the initial excitation into counter-propagating left- and right-moving modes, whose energies can be resolved in real space. To interpret these results, we employ Luttinger liquid theory, which allows us to derive analytical predictions for the energy carried by the emergent modes. We find good agreement between field-theoretical predictions and numerical simulations in the low-energy regime. In contrast to charge fractionalization, which is completely determined by the Luttinger liquid parameter, we show that energy partitioning is non-universal and depends on details of the injected wave packet, such as its width. Our results provide a real-space characterization of energy partitioning in one-dimensional systems and establish a quantitative comparison between non-equilibrium numerical simulations and the effective field-theory description.

</details>


### [228] [Universality of the $1/9$ Magnetization Plateau and Quantum-Disordered States in the Kagome Family $\mathrm{Cs_8AB_3Ti_{12}F_{48}}$ ($A=\mathrm{Rb},\mathrm{Li}$; $B=\mathrm{K},\mathrm{Na}$)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19572)
*Prena Chaudhary,Asiri Thennakoon,Tommy Park,Hanru Wang,Leshan Zhao,Laurel Winter,Neil Herrison,Christina Hoffmann,Junghong H. He,Harald O. Jeschke,Hiroyuki Nojiri,Akira Matsuo,Koichi Kindo,Miwako Takahashi,Yukio Noda,Taku J. Sato,Shiyan Li,Hiroaki Ueda,Gia-Wei Chern,Seung-Hun Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 化学压力调控钛基kagome反铁磁体Cs8AB3Ti12F48中分数磁化平台的层级，发现膨胀晶格稳定1/9平台且抑制1/3平台，压缩晶格则破坏阻挫网络并诱导磁有序，表明1/9与1/3平台微观起源不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: spin-1/2 kagome反铁磁体中低场1/9磁化平台的微观起源长期未解，需要明确其与更稳健的1/3平台是否共享同一微观机制，并探索其在阻挫kagome磁性中的普适性。

Method: 对钛基kagome家族Cs8AB3Ti12F48（A=Rb,Li；B=K,Na）施加化学压力（改变A、B离子尺寸），进行高达60 T的高场磁化测量，并结合比热测量与第一性原理计算，分析晶格膨胀/压缩对交换网络和基态性质的影响。

Result: 晶格膨胀的Cs8RbK3Ti12F48和Cs8LiK3Ti12F48在缺失常规更稳健1/3平台的情况下仍表现出稳健的1/9平台-like相；压缩的Cs8LiNa3Ti12F48既无1/9平台-like相也无量子无序基态。比热与第一性原理计算显示，晶格膨胀保持阻挫、全连通kagome交换网络及无能隙量子无序基态，而压缩将交换网络重组为弱耦合准一维子系统并诱导连续磁相变。

Conclusion: 1/9与1/3平台不必共享同一微观起源，1/9平台可能是阻挫spin-1/2 kagome磁性的更普适特征。

Abstract: The microscopic origin of the low-field $1/9$ magnetization plateau in spin-$1/2$ kagome antiferromagnets remains unresolved. Here, we show that chemical pressure reshapes the hierarchy of fractional magnetization plateaus in the titanium-based kagome family $\mathrm{Cs_8AB_3Ti_{12}F_{48}}$ ($A=\mathrm{Rb},\mathrm{Li}$; $B=\mathrm{K},\mathrm{Na}$). High-field magnetization measurements up to 60 T reveal a robust $1/9$ plateau-like phase in the expanded $\mathrm{Cs_8RbK_3Ti_{12}F_{48}}$ and $\mathrm{Cs_8LiK_3Ti_{12}F_{48}}$ compounds, despite the absence of the conventionally more robust $1/3$ plateau. In contrast, compressed $\mathrm{Cs_8LiNa_3Ti_{12}F_{48}}$ exhibits neither the $1/9$ plateau-like phase nor a quantum-disordered ground state. Specific-heat measurements and first-principles calculations show that lattice expansion preserves a frustrated, fully connected kagome exchange network and gapless quantum-disordered ground states, whereas compression reorganizes the exchange network into weakly coupled quasi-one-dimensional subsystems and induces successive magnetic transitions. These results demonstrate that the $1/9$ and $1/3$ plateaus need not share a common microscopic origin and suggest that the $1/9$ plateau may represent a more universal feature of frustrated spin-$1/2$ kagome magnetism.

</details>


### [229] [Vestigial chirality from fluctuating loop currents on the kagome lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19618)
*Yin Shi,Xuande Bu,Sheng Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在kagome晶格三-Q环流序中，蒙特卡洛模拟揭示直接融化与两步融化两种机制；两步机制下平移畴壁先增殖，形成保留时间反演奇序但M点关联短程的中间相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多分量序可能分阶段融化，在初级分量变为短程后留下复合序。作者希望研究这种可能性，并为kagome金属在常规电荷密度波临界温度之上观测到的时间反演奇响应提供热力学框架。

Method: 在有效固定振幅符号模型上进行大规模团簇与并行回火蒙特卡洛模拟；对稳定振幅分辨的Ginzburg-Landau理论做有限尺寸标度分析。

Result: 发现直接与两步融化两种机制。两步机制中平移畴壁先于手性改变壁增殖，恢复晶格平移对称性同时保留长程时间反演奇序，初级M点关联呈短程；当最低能手性改变壁仅比同手性平移壁贵约12-13%时进入两步区。有限尺寸标度表明该相在振幅弛豫下仍稳定存在。

Conclusion: 结果确立了残余环流序的定量畴壁判据，以及一条无需长程环流布拉格序即可破缺时间反演对称性的涨落路径，为kagome金属中高于常规电荷密度波临界温度的时间反演奇响应提供可能的热力学解释。

Abstract: Multicomponent order can melt in stages, leaving a composite order after its primary constituents become short ranged. We study this possibility for commensurate three-$Q$ loop-current order on the kagome lattice. Large-scale cluster and parallel-tempering Monte Carlo simulations reveal direct and two-stage melting regimes in an effective fixed-amplitude sign model. In the latter regime, translation-sector domain walls proliferate before chirality-changing walls, restoring lattice translational symmetry while preserving long-range time-reversal-odd order. The primary $M$-point correlations are short ranged in this intermediate phase. The two-stage regime begins when the lowest-energy chirality-changing wall is only about $12$--$13\%$ more costly than a same-chirality translation wall. Finite-size scaling of a stable amplitude-resolved Ginzburg--Landau theory shows that the phase survives amplitude relaxation. Our results establish a quantitative domain-wall criterion for vestigial loop-current order and a fluctuation route to time-reversal symmetry breaking without long-range loop-current Bragg order. This separation provides a possible thermodynamic framework for time-reversal-odd responses recently reported above the critical temperature for conventional charge-density-wave ordering in kagome metals.

</details>


### [230] [Fractional quantum spin Hall crystals from hidden density-wave order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19638)
*Matthew Shammami,Sudip Chakravarty*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出对称破缺与拓扑之间存在生成关系：混合单-三重态d密度波通过隐藏键序打破平移对称性并产生时间反演成对的陈能带，在分数填充下经排斥相互作用可转变为分数量子自旋霍尔液体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称破缺与拓扑通常被视为描述物质自组织的两种不同框架。作者希望探究二者之间是否存在内在联系，即一种描述能否派生出另一种。

Method: 提出混合单-三重态d密度波（粒子-空穴凝聚体）模型，分析其隐藏键序、平移对称性破缺与陈能带生成；在奇分母分数填充下采用有限尺寸精确对角化研究排斥相互作用效应。

Result: 该对称破缺拓扑态在相互作用下可转变为分数量子自旋霍尔液体，其相反手性产生电荷流输运自旋、自旋流输运电荷的交叉响应；强自旋间耦合与稀释填充倾向电荷序并使残余能带色散起决定作用；半填充自旋解耦极限给出均匀关联液体，其征象与复合费米液体物理一致。

Conclusion: 对称破缺可生成拓扑，隐藏键序驱动的d密度波为分数量子自旋霍尔液体及复合费米液体等拓扑相提供了统一框架，并揭示了这些相的边界条件。

Abstract: Broken symmetry and topology are distinct descriptions for how matter organizes itself. Here, we propose one gives rise to the other. A mixed singlet-triplet $d$-density wave, which is a particle-hole condensate, has a hidden bond order that breaks translation symmetry and generates a time-reversed pair of Chern bands, without producing conventional charge or spin density order. At fractional fillings with odd denominators, finite size exact diagonalization studies show how repulsive interactions can transform this symmetry-broken topological state into a fractional quantum spin Hall liquid. Its opposite chiralities produce a crossed response in which charge flux transports spin, and spin flux transports charge. The same setting also reveals what borders such a phase. Strong interspin coupling and increasingly dilute fillings favor charge order and make residual band dispersion decisive, while at half filling, the spin-decoupled limit gives a homogeneous correlated liquid with signatures consistent with the physics of a composite Fermi liquid.

</details>


### [231] [Pressure induced structural phase transition and magneto-elastic coupling in Eu doped LaCrO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19795)
*Asish Kumar Mishra,Mrinmay Sahu,Bhagyashri Giri,Bidisha Mukherjee,Suvashree Mukherjee,Harekrishna Bhunia,Tamalkanti Mukherjee,Sujoy Ghosh,Partha Mitra,Peter Liermann,Goutam Dev Mukherjee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过高压XRD、拉曼光谱与低温磁化测量，揭示了5% Eu掺杂LaCrO₃在约10.6 GPa发生正交-菱方结构相变及约4.5 GPa的异常，并预测压力诱导的磁基态转变为反铁磁有序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为关联5% Eu掺杂LaCrO₃（ELCO）在压力下的结构与磁性质，并厘清其与未掺杂LaCrO₃（LCO）行为的异同，作者开展高压综合研究以探明结构相变与磁基态演化机制。

Method: 采用同步辐射X射线衍射（XRD）、微区拉曼光谱和低温磁化测量，对5% Eu掺杂LaCrO₃进行高压研究，并将ELCO的XRD与拉曼异常同LCO的相似结果进行对比分析。

Result: 高压XRD显示约10.6 GPa发生正交到菱方结构相变，高压拉曼数据予以佐证，并表明低频软模相关的晶格不稳定性在驱动该相变中起关键作用；此外，多个拉曼模的位移与积分强度在约4.5 GPa出现明显异常。

Conclusion: 结合低温磁化测量与XRD、拉曼异常，并与LCO类似结果对比，预测ELCO存在压力诱导的磁基态转变，转变为反铁磁有序。

Abstract: In this study, we have carried out a detailed high pressure investigation on 5$\%$ Eu doped LaCrO$_3$ (ELCO) using synchrotron X-ray diffraction (XRD), micro Raman spectroscopy, and low-temperature magnetization measurements to correlate the structural and magnetic properties under pressure. The high pressure XRD reveals the orthorhombic to rhombohedral structural phase transition around 10.6 GPa. The high pressure Raman data corroborate this result and indicate that the lattice instabilities associated with the low-frequency soft modes play a crucial role in driving this transition. In addition, a pronounced anomaly in the Raman shift and integrated intensity of several Raman modes is observed around 4.5 GPa. By combining the low-temperature magnetization measurements with the anomalies observed in the XRD and Raman data and by comparing with the similar results in LaCrO$_3$ (LCO), a pressure-induced change in the magnetic ground state to antiferromagnetic ordering is predicted.

</details>


### [232] [Tunable flat band on the surface of a rhombohedral kagome ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19857)
*Xinglu Que,Qingyu He,Lihui Zhou,Lilian Prodan,Vladimir Tsurkan,István Kézsmárki,Hidenori Takagi,Dennis Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在层状kagome铁磁体Fe₃Sn₂中，STM观测到近费米能的平带，但第一性原理计算表明该平带并非源于层内破坏性跃迁，而是由菱面体堆垛kagome层间的层间跃迁所致，且局限于表面层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索kagome材料的一个核心目标是实现具有meV量级带宽且靠近费米能的平带。普遍假设认为平带源于kagome晶格上的破坏性跃迁过程（层内跳跃的破坏性干涉）。作者旨在验证Fe₃Sn₂是否具备此类平带，并厘清其平带形成的真实物理机制。

Method: 对层状kagome铁磁体Fe₃Sn₂进行扫描隧道显微镜（STM）测量，观测微分隧道电导中的平带特征；并结合第一性原理平板（slab）计算，分析平带的来源与空间分布。

Result: STM证实Fe₃Sn₂在费米能附近存在平带，表现为微分电导中的尖锐峰。第一性原理计算表明，该平带并非由层内跃迁的破坏性干涉压平，而是源于菱面体堆垛kagome层之间的层间跃迁，并局限于表面层。该表面带形成于布里渊区角点K̄和K̄'附近，表现出丰富的磁场依赖：包括旋转Fe磁矩导致谷对称性破缺所产生的精细结构，以及与轨道磁矩相关的持续抗磁位移，均被计算重现。

Conclusion: 研究强调了层堆垛方式对kagome磁体能带结构的关键作用，并在实验上展示了一种在拓扑磁体原子级薄层体积中生成磁可调平带的替代机制。

Abstract: A central goal in the exploration of kagome-based materials is the realization of a flat band that has meV bandwidth and lies close to the Fermi energy. The prevailing assumption is that band flattening originates from destructive hopping processes on the kagome lattice. We perform scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) on the layered kagome ferromagnet Fe$_3$Sn$_2$ and show that it indeed hosts a flat band near the Fermi energy, which is manifested as a sharp peak in the differential tunneling conductance. First-principles slab calculations reveal, however, that this band is flattened not by the destructive interference of intralayer hopping, but by interlayer hopping between rhombohedral-stacked kagome planes in Fe$_3$Sn$_2$, and is confined to the surface layer. This surface band, forming in the vicinity of the Brillouin zone corners $\bar{K}$ and $\bar{K'}$, exhibits rich magnetic-field dependence, including fine structure due to valley-symmetry breaking by rotated Fe moments, as well as a persistent diamagnetic shift associated with orbital magnetic moments, all reproduced by our calculations. Our results, highlighting the crucial role of layer stacking on the band structure of kagome magnets, demonstrate experimentally an alternative mechanism of generating magnetically tunable flat bands in atomically thin volumes of topological magnets.

</details>


### [233] [Corner entanglement scaling with projected entangled pair states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20020)
*Chloé Van Bastelaere,Rui-Zhen Huang,Laurens Vanderstraeten*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文章提出一种从投影纠缠对态（PEPS）直接提取角纠缠熵的方法，并将其用于二维量子临界点的诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角纠缠熵是探测二维量子临界点普适性质的强有力工具，但如何从张量网络态（如PEPS）中直接提取该量尚缺乏有效方法。

Method: 发展了一种在热力学极限下从PEPS直接提取角纠缠熵的方法。

Result: 应用于量子临界点模型时，角贡献随有效关联长度呈现标度行为，符合有限纠缠标度假设；得到的角系数与其他方法一致。

Conclusion: 该方法能高效诊断二维强关联量子临界点。

Abstract: Entanglement scaling provides a powerful probe of universal properties at quantum critical points. In two dimensions, contributions originating from a corner-shaped bipartition exhibit a universal scaling, which is determined by the underlying conformal field theory. We develop a method to extract this corner entanglement entropy from projected entangled pair states directly in the thermodynamic limit. When applied to models at a quantum critical point, we show that the corner contribution exhibits scaling with the effective correlation length, in agreement with the hypothesis of finite-entanglement scaling. Our results for the corner coefficients are consistent with other methods, demonstrating the efficiency of our method for diagnosing strongly-correlated quantum critical points in two dimensions.

</details>


### [234] [When Is Kramers-Wannier Duality Invertible?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20090)
*Akash Sinha,Pramod Padmanabhan,Vladimir Korepin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究量子Ising链的Kramers-Wannier对偶何时能在有限Hilbert空间上实现为可逆酉变换，用Ising键代数表示的两个中心元给出充要条件，并构造了新的可逆对偶实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Ising链的Kramers-Wannier对偶在有限周期链上自然表现为非可逆变换，只有在引入对称性扭曲扇区后才能提升为可逆酉变换。已有工作缺乏对"何时存在可逆实现"的系统判据，本文旨在给出统一的分类标准。

Method: 作者将Kramers-Wannier对偶实现问题归结为Ising键代数在有限Hilbert空间上的表示问题，利用该代数的两个中心元刻画表示结构，从而导出可逆实现的表示论判据（充要条件）。

Result: 建立了可逆Kramers-Wannier对偶存在的充要条件，该条件由Ising键代数表示的两个中心元决定；判据同时统一了传统非可逆实现与含对称性扭曲扇区的可逆构造，并适用于具有相同底层键代数的其他模型。

Conclusion: Ising键代数表示是否允许可逆Kramers-Wannier对偶完全由中心元决定。作为示例，作者在一个非平凡Ising键代数实现中发现了可逆Kramers-Wannier对偶对应的序-无序对偶。

Abstract: {\it Kramers-Wannier} duality of the quantum Ising chain is naturally realized as a noninvertible transformation on a finite periodic chain. Upon incorporating appropriate symmetry-twisted sectors, however, the duality can be promoted to an invertible unitary transformation. In this Letter, we classify when the Kramers-Wannier duality admits an invertible realization on a finite Hilbert space. We show that this is determined by the representation of the Ising bond algebra and establish a necessary and sufficient condition in terms of its two central elements. This representation-theoretic criterion unifies the conventional noninvertible realization with invertible constructions involving symmetry-twisted sectors and applies equally to other models with the same underlying bond algebra. As an explicit example, we find an order-disorder duality in a non-trivial realization of the Ising bond algebra that admits an invertible Kramers-Wannier duality.

</details>


### [235] [Pressure-induced electronic and structural evolution of EuIrGe3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20376)
*N. S. Dhami,V. Baledent,I. Batistic,C. M. N. Kumar,D. Kaczorowski,L. Nataf,J. M. Ablett,J. -P. Rueff,J. P. Itie,P. Fertey,S. R. Shieh,P. Popcevic,Y. Utsumi Boucher*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文通过X射线吸收谱、高压X射线衍射结合DFT计算及电阻率测量，研究了非中心对称BaNiSn₃型反铁磁体EuIrGe₃在压力下的电子与晶体结构演化，揭示了其区别于EuCoGe₃和EuRhGe₃的独特压力响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究EuTGe₃家族中过渡金属d电子态在压力诱导的电子与结构演化中的作用，理解EuIrGe₃与同族化合物EuCoGe₃、EuRhGe₃压力响应的差异来源。

Method: 采用X射线吸收谱（Eu L₃边）表征Eu价态演化，同步辐射高压X射线衍射确定晶格压缩与结构相变，辅以密度泛函理论计算获得晶格与状态方程参数，并结合电阻率测量追踪反铁磁有序温度随压力的变化。

Result: Eu L₃边谱显示Eu平均价态随压缩连续升高，同时Ge和Ir电子态发生变化；高压XRD表明四方相（I4mm）呈各向异性压缩，并在38 GPa以上出现结构相变；实验晶格与状态方程参数与DFT计算吻合良好；电阻率测量显示反铁磁有序温度在18 GPa以内单调升高，说明尽管非磁性Eu³⁺组态贡献增加，反铁磁基态仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: EuIrGe₃的压力响应与EuCoGe₃、EuRhGe₃不同，凸显了过渡金属d电子态在EuTGe₃家族压力诱导电子与结构演化中的重要作用。

Abstract: We investigated the pressure-induced evolution of the electronic and crystal structure of the noncentrosymmetric BaNiSn3-type antiferromagnet EuIrGe3 using x-ray absorption spectroscopy, synchrotron x-ray diffraction complemented by density functional theory calculations, and electrical resistivity measurements. The Eu L3-edge spectra reveal a continuous increase in the mean Eu valence under compression, accompanied by modifications of the Ge and Ir electronic states. High- pressure x-ray diffraction shows anisotropic lattice compression in the tetragonal (I4mm) phase and provides evidence for a structural phase transition above 38 GPa. The experimentally deter- mined lattice and equation-of-state parameters are in good agreement with the DFT calculations. Electrical resistivity measurements reveal a monotonic increase in the antiferromagnetic ordering temperatures up to 18 GPa, indicating that the antiferromagnetic ground state remains robust despite the increasing contribution of the nonmagnetic Eu3+ configuration to the intermediate va- lence state. These results demonstrate that EuIrGe3 exhibits a pressure response distinct from EuCoGe3 and EuRhGe3, highlighting the important role of the transition metal d-electron states in the pressure-induced electronic and structural evolution of the EuT Ge3 family

</details>


### [236] [Emergent Surface Kondo Flat Band Driven by Competing Interactions in a Topological Ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20432)
*Nazar Zaremba,Susmita Changdar,Haojie Guo,Iñigo Robredo,Daniel Lozano-Gomez,Andreas Leithe-Jasper,Rui Lou,Yurii Prots,Mitja Krnel,Markus König,Konstantin Semeniuk,Berit H. Goodge,Yuri Grin,Andrii Kuibarov,Oleksandr Suvorov,Alexander Fedorov,Adam Pikul,Ivan Soldatov,Rudolf Schäfer,Bernd Büchner,Oksana Kvitnitskaya,Yurii Naidyuk,Louis Lamm,Meike Pfeiffer,Elena Hassinger,Matthias Vojta,Sergey Borisenko,Miguel M. Ugeda,Jeroen van den Brink,Eteri Svanidze,Maia G. Vergniory*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作报道了UAsS晶体中一种新现象：磁性可驱动不同强关联电子态在同一材料的不同区域共存，即关联量子相的自发空间分离，为关联拓扑体系研究开辟了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代凝聚态物理的核心目标之一是揭示强电子关联、磁性与能带拓扑交织所产生的新奇量子态。重费米子相是电子关联最显著的表现之一，拓扑重费米子态已在若干非磁性材料中被确认，但其与磁有序竞争后的后果基本未被探索。

Method: 结合体敏感探针、第一性原理计算、角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）以及表面敏感的ARPES与扫描隧道显微/谱学（STM/STS）对UAsS晶体进行综合表征。

Result: 体敏感测量表明UAsS体态为金属性铁磁体，仅具中等程度的关联驱动能带重整化；第一性原理计算揭示其拓扑电子结构同时具有节线（nodal lines）与Weyl点，ARPES测量与之一致，分辨出节线和Weyl交叉。与此形成鲜明对比的是，表面敏感的ARPES与STM/STS观测到钉扎在费米能级处的显著平带及尖锐共振，这是第一性原理计算无法捕捉的、涌现出的强关联Kondo态的标志。

Conclusion: 磁性可驱动不同的强关联电子态在同一材料的不同区域共存，建立了组织量子物质的一种此前未知的机制，为关联拓扑系统的研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: A central goal of modern condensed matter physics is to uncover new quantum states of matter arising from the intertwined effects of strong electron correlations, magnetism, and band topology. Heavy-fermion phases, generated by Kondo interactions, represent one of the most remarkable manifestations of electronic correlations, and topological heavy-fermion states have been identified in several non-magnetic materials. Yet, the consequences of their competition with magnetic order have remained largely unexplored. Here, we reveal a new phenomenon: the spontaneous spatial separation of correlated quantum phases. By showing that magnetism can drive distinct strongly correlated electronic states to coexist in different regions of a single material, our work establishes a previously unknown mechanism for organizing quantum matter and opens a new direction in the study of correlated topological systems. Using \emph{bulk-sensitive} probes, we show that UAsS crystals are, in the bulk, metallic ferromagnets with only moderate correlation-driven band renormalizations. First-principles calculations reveal a topological electronic structure hosting both nodal lines and Weyl points, pointing to a rich underlying topology. Angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) measurements are consistent with these predictions, resolving the nodal lines and Weyl crossings. In striking contrast, \emph{surface-sensitive} ARPES and scanning tunneling microscopy/spectroscopy (STM/STS) measurements reveal a pronounced flat band pinned at the Fermi level, accompanied by a sharp resonance -- hallmarks of an emergent, strongly correlated Kondo state not captured by first-principles calculations.

</details>


### [237] [Non-Thermal Effects in Fermionic Atoms Coupled to Open Cavities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20434)
*Jules Sueiro,Marco Schiro*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究Fermi-Hubbard模型与开放耗散单腔模耦合，用Keldysh图技术推导并求解费米子量子动理学方程，揭示非平衡稳态、热流为零及相互作用诱导的向平衡分布过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔量子电动力学与强关联电子体系的交叉是量子多体物理的重要前沿，但开放耗散腔模作为非平衡噪声与耗散源如何影响费米子的动理学与相变尚不清楚，尤其是非平衡稳态的热力学性质及其对超辐射相变的影响需要系统研究。

Method: 采用Keldysh非平衡格林函数/图方法，将玻色腔模视为非平衡噪声与耗散源，推导费米子的量子动理学方程；先处理无Hubbard相互作用情形，再在弱耦合区引入Hubbard相互作用。

Result: 无相互作用时费米子达到非平衡稳态，具有非热分布函数，且该分布恰好使费米子与腔模间热流为零；识别出出现低频有效温度描述的参数区间，并讨论了涨落对超辐射相变平均场相图的影响；弱耦合Hubbard相互作用导致向完全平衡分布的过渡（crossover）。

Conclusion: 开放耗散腔模可驱动费米子进入具有零热流的非平衡稳态，其非热分布与有效温度描述受参数控制，而Hubbard相互作用在弱耦合下将系统推向平衡，为腔-关联电子体系的非平衡相变研究提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We study a Fermi-Hubbard model coupled to a open dissipative single cavity mode. Using Keldysh diagrammatics we derive and solve the quantum kinetic equations for the fermions, taking the bosonic cavity mode as a source of non-equilibrium noise and dissipation. In absence of Hubbard interactions we show that the fermions reach generically a non-equilibrium steady-state, characterized by a non-thermal distribution function. Quite interestingly we demonstrate that the latter exactly nullifies the heat-current between fermions and cavity mode. We discuss the regimes of parameters where a low-frequency effective temperature description emerges and how the fluctuations affect the mean-field phase diagram for the superradiance phase transition. Finally we include Hubbard interaction in the weak-coupling regime and show that it leads to a crossover towards a full equilibrium distribution.

</details>


### [238] [Collective Charge-\(2e\) Bosonic Excitations in Charge-Ordered Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20472)
*Ping Tang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在有效电荷-赝自旋模型中，将电荷序基态表示为交错赝自旋序，通过Holstein-Primakoff变换量子化涨落，揭示出两支携带相反量子化电荷±2e的简并、有隙玻色准粒子——"双荷子"（bichargons）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电荷序通常以电子密度的静态调制来刻画，其集体激发从准粒子角度的发展远不如自旋有序系统成熟；作者希望借鉴磁振子（magnon）在自旋序系统中的准粒子范式，将其推广到电荷范畴。

Method: 构建有效电荷-赝自旋模型，用交错赝自旋序表示电荷序基态；对有序态周围的涨落做Holstein-Primakoff变换进行量子化，得到两支玻色准粒子；随后分析温度梯度、化学势调控、静电场与交流电场下的输运响应。

Result: 发现两支简并、有隙的玻色色散支，携带相反的量子化电荷±2e（双荷子）；温度梯度驱动热激发的双荷子扩散，当通过调节化学势偏离电荷中性点解除两支简并时产生电荷Seebeck响应；热双荷子气体在静电场下仍为电绝缘，因准粒子数不守恒；交流电场可参数化地生成相干的正负双荷子对，并在偏置电场下支持有限的直流漂移电流，类似于半导体中光激发电子-空穴对介导的光电导。

Conclusion: 双荷子被确立为电荷序系统中一类新的集体玻色电荷载流子，将自旋序系统中磁振子的准粒子范式推广到电荷部门，以电荷取代自旋角动量作为被输运的量。

Abstract: Charge order is conventionally characterized by a static modulation of the electronic density, while its collective excitations remain less developed from a quasiparticle perspective than those of spin-ordered systems. Here, we formulate the collective excititations of charge order within an effective charge-pseudospin model, in which the charge-ordered ground state is represented by staggered pseudospin order. Quantizing fluctuations around this ordered state via a Holstein--Primakoff transformation, we reveal two branches of bosonic quasiparticles with degenerate, gapped dispersions that carry opposite quantized electric charges $\pm 2e$. We therefore term these charge-$2e$ bosons ``\textit{bichargons},'' closely paralleling the two magnon branches of a bipartite antiferromagnet that carry opposite spin angular momenta. We show that a temperature gradient drives the diffusion of thermally excited bichargons, generating a charge Seebeck response when the degeneracy between the oppositely charged branches is lifted by tuning the chemical potential away from the charge-neutrality point. In contrast, despite carrying finite electric charges, a thermal bichargon gas remains electrically insulating under a static electric field because bichargon quasiparticle number is not conserved. An ac electric field, however, can parametrically generate coherent pairs of oppositely charged bichargons that support a finite dc drift current in the presence of a bias electric field, offering a bosonic analogue of photoconductivity mediated by photoexcited electron--hole pairs in semiconductors. Our results establish bichargons as a new class of collective bosonic charge carriers in charge-ordered systems and extend the quasiparticle paradigm of magnons in spin-ordered systems to the charge sector, with electric charge replacing spin angular momentum as the transported quantity.

</details>


### [239] [Unconstrained compact lattice QED$_{2+1}$ coupled to phonons: Gauss sectors, orthogonal semimetal, and deconfined criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20502)
*João C. Inácio,Fakher F. Assaad*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过无约束紧致格点QED$_{2+1}$耦合键声子的量子蒙特卡洛研究，揭示物理高斯扇区的动力学生成与退禁闭的不稳定性，确立了OSM、AFM与VBS相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范理论对2D量子磁体的描述通常依赖于在受限物理希尔伯特空间上施加高斯定律约束；本文旨在探究在不预先固定高斯算符本征值的情形下，物理高斯扇区如何成为能量上有利，以及这一过程与禁闭和对称破缺的联系。

Method: 采用精确辅助场量子蒙特卡洛（AFQMC）方法，在$N_f=2$下研究无约束紧致格点QED$_{2+1}$耦合键声子系统，其中局域高斯算符守恒但本征值未先验固定；并对比显式施加高斯定律后的行为。

Result: 识别出正交半金属（OSM）相，其中费米子剥离规范荷形成狄拉克液体，该相混合不同高斯扇区并在显式施加高斯定律时失稳；施加高斯定律后，紧致性允许携带AFM和VBS量子数的单极子激发，规范场涨落与自旋子-声子耦合调控单极子竞争，生成包含OSM、AFM和VBS的相图；OSM相变对应高斯定律的动力学生成，AFM与VBS带电单极子竞争产生与退禁闭量子临界一致的转变。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在投影到物理希尔伯特空间后，紧致格点QED$_{2+1}$中的退禁闭总是不稳定地趋向AFM或VBS序。

Abstract: Gauge theoretic descriptions of 2D quantum magnets are defined on a restricted physical Hilbert space by imposing Gauss's law. In this work, we study an unconstrained compact lattice QED$_{2+1}$ coupled to bond phonons, in which local Gauss operators are conserved, but their eigenvalues are not a priori fixed. Using exact auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo simulations at $N_f = 2$, we investigate how the physical Gauss sector becomes energetically favourable, and how this process is connected to confinement and symmetry breaking. We identify an orthogonal semimetallic (OSM) phase, in which fermions striped off their gauge charge and form a Dirac liquid. This phase mixes various Gauss sectors, and becomes unstable when Gauss's law is explicitly enforced. Upon the imposition of Gauss's law, compactness allows for monopole excitations carrying antiferromagnetic (AFM) and valence-bond-solid (VBS) quantum numbers. Gauge field fluctuations and spinon-phonon coupling tune between the competition of such monopoles and generate a phase diagram with OSM, AFM and VBS orders. The transitions out of the OSM phase coincide with the dynamical generation of Gauss's law, while the competition between AFM and VBS charged monopoles produces a transition consistent with deconfined quantum criticality. Thus our results establish that upon the projection to the physical Hilbert space, deconfinement in compact lattice QED$_{2+1}$ is always unstable towards either AFM or VBS order.

</details>


### [240] [Multiconfigurational Analysis of Local Electronic Structure of $\mathrm{RuO_2}$ Using Relativistic Embedded Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20526)
*Zhosan I. A.,Lomachuk Yu. V.,Maltsev D. A.,Moiseev I. A.,Andreev O. Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 采用多组态相对论嵌入簇方法研究RuO₂局域电子结构，发现4d轨道谱更接近四方对称、轨道准简并显著，抑制了交错磁自旋劈裂所需的xy四极序，解释了块体RuO₂无交错磁性的实验现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RuO₂是交错磁候选材料，但块体实验未观测到交错磁性，而应变薄膜却有稳健信号，这一矛盾需要从局域电子结构层面给出解释。作者希望检验独立粒子模型中依赖正交对称性降低的xy四极序在真实多组态、含自旋轨道耦合情形下是否成立。

Method: 构建逐级逼近的嵌入簇模型：自由Ru离子→Ru+Q₆静电模型→裸[RuO₆]⁸⁻配体模型→高精度RuO₆@CTEP嵌入簇（再现晶体环境）。所有体系均在SA-CASSCF与NEVPT2+SOC水平处理，以同等精度同时刻画强电子关联和自旋轨道耦合。

Result: 尽管RuO₂中Ru位点的形式局域对称性为正交（D₂h），计算得到的4d轨道能谱及其劈裂模式更接近更高的四方对称性（D₄h），相关4d轨道间保持强准简并。

Conclusion: 交错磁自旋劈裂所依赖的局域xy四极序源于对称性降低，而轨道准简并会抑制该序。这为块体RuO₂实验中未观测到交错磁性、而应变薄膜却有稳健交错磁信号提供了自然的解释。

Abstract: We present a multiconfigurational, relativistic embedded-cluster study of the local electronic structure of ruthenium dioxide ($\mathrm{RuO_2}$), a candidate altermagnetic material. Starting from free $\mathrm{Ru}$ ions, we progressively build up the local environment through a $\mathrm{Ru+Q_6}$ electrostatic model, a bare $\mathrm{[RuO_6]^{8-}}$ ligand model, and finally a high-accuracy $\mathrm{RuO_6}$@CTEP embedded cluster that reproduces the crystalline surroundings. All systems are treated at the SA-CASSCF and NEVPT2+SOC levels of theory to capture strong electron correlation and spin-orbit coupling on an equal footing. While the formal local site symmetry of the $\mathrm{Ru}$ sites in $\mathrm{RuO_2}$ is orthorhombic ($D_{2h}$), we find that the calculated $4d$-orbital energy spectrum and its splitting pattern behave much closer to the higher tetragonal ($D_{4h}$) symmetry, preserving a strong quasi-degeneracy among the relevant $4d$ orbitals. Since the local $xy$ quadrupolar order responsible for altermagnetic spin splitting in independent-particle models relies on this symmetry reduction, its suppression by orbital quasi-degeneracy offers a natural explanation for why altermagnetism is not observed in bulk $\mathrm{RuO_2}$ experiments, in contrast to the robust altermagnetic signatures reported in strained thin films.

</details>


### [241] [Lyapunov-controlled thermalization: an exact real-time example](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20597)
*Jonas Loy,Jan C. Louw*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: KMS关系的验证不足以证明系统达到平衡。在大N大q的SYK淬火协议中，尽管淬火后费米子关联函数完全热化，第二次淬火揭示了初始态的隐藏记忆，其衰减率为Lyapunov指数λ_L。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为满足KMS关系即表明系统达到热平衡。本文旨在检验这一判据是否充分，并研究多体系统中真正的热化过程。

Method: 采用大N、大q极限下的Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev（SYK）模型淬火协议：先进行一次淬火，待其关联函数呈现热化特征后，再进行一次回到初始哈密顿量的反向淬火，并考察连接第一次淬火前时间的关联函数。

Result: 所有淬火后的费米子关联函数精确呈现热性质，但第二次淬火揭示出初始态的隐藏记忆。该记忆是一个广延的非平衡（NEQ）见证量，其衰减率恰为Lyapunov指数λ_L。在完整的大N、大q极限下，可以得到精确的实时间演化。

Conclusion: KMS关系的满足不能作为平衡的充分证据。λ_L决定了量子态在何种速率下变得与Gibbs态实际不可区分，该体系为解析研究热化提供了理想平台。

Abstract: A verified Kubo-Martin-Schwinger (KMS) relation, after a drive has ceased, is not sufficient evidence to prove equilibrium. We demonstrate this in a large-$N$ large-$q$ Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) quench protocol. Although all post-quench fermion correlators are exactly thermal, a second quench back to the initial Hamiltonian reveals hidden memory of the initial state. It is encoded in correlators connecting to times before the first quench. This memory is an extensive nonequilibrium (NEQ) witness with its decay rate being the Lyapunov exponent $λ_L$. Thus $λ_L$ sets the rate at which the state becomes effectively indistinguishable from a Gibbs state. Despite being a unitary interacting many-body system, the complete NEQ real-time evolution is obtained exactly in the large-$N$, large-$q$ limit, making the setup ideal for analytically studying thermalization.

</details>


### [242] [Engineering Weak Universality with Quantum Dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20755)
*Warre Missiaen,Michael Wimmer,Natalia Chepiga*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出用量子点架构实现量子Ashkin-Teller与XYZ/八顶点模型，通过共振腔介导和直接库仑相互作用调控临界指数，并证明短链即可分辨连续变化的临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有连续变化临界指数的量子临界理论在实验上难以实现，缺乏可微观调控参数的平台。

Method: 提出量子点架构，分别利用共振腔介导相互作用和直接库仑相互作用实现量子Ashkin-Teller模型与XYZ/八顶点模型；聚焦由非局域对偶性联系的两条临界线，其中局域序参量对应拓扑弦算符。

Result: 基于库仑的实现可实现更强相互作用区域；短链中已能分辨连续变化的临界行为；在实验可行参数下，Ashkin-Teller模型临界指数变化约10%，八顶点模型变化更大，且均远高于测量不确定度。

Conclusion: 该量子点平台可在实验上微观调控临界指数，为研究连续变化临界行为的量子临界理论提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Quantum critical theories with continuously varying critical exponents remain challenging to access experimentally. Here, we propose quantum-dot architectures for realizing the quantum Ashkin-Teller and XYZ/eight-vertex models using resonator-mediated and direct Coulomb interactions, respectively. We focus on the Ashkin-Teller and eight-vertex critical lines, which are connected by a non-local duality relating local order parameters to topological string operators. The resulting platforms provide microscopic control over the parameters explicitly controlling critical exponents, with the Coulomb-based implementation enabling stronger interaction regimes. We demonstrate that continuously varying critical behavior can already be resolved in short chains. For experimentally realistic parameters, the accessible interaction range is expected to produce changes in the critical exponents of order $10\%$ for the Ashkin-Teller model and substantially larger changes for the eight-vertex model. In both cases, the predicted variations lie well above the estimated measurement uncertainties.

</details>


### [243] [Spacetime Dynamics of Altermagnetic Magnons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20767)
*Ali Emami Kopaei,Karthik Subramaniam Eswaran,Krzysztof Wohlfeld*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过实空间局域自旋激发传播，可在棋盘格晶格中辨识交变磁性序：有限J₂产生方向依赖的磁振子群速度与两支分裂，外波前近似圆形而内部呈十字形各向异性图案；J₂反号进入非阻挫区后，特征速度增大且各向异性图案旋转π/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分交变磁性与常规铁磁、反铁磁序通常依赖动量空间探测手段，缺乏实空间动力学判据。本文旨在探索局域自旋激发的实空间扩散能否作为交变磁序的独特动力学指纹，从而丰富探测手段。

Method: 在二维棋盘格晶格上采用线性自旋波理论，引入最近邻耦合J₁与次近邻耦合J₂，计算磁振子能带、群速度及局域自旋激发的时空传播行为，并对比铁磁与反铁磁情形，同时考察J₂符号改变（J₂<0，非阻挫区）对传播图案的影响。

Result: 有限J₂导致方向依赖的磁振子群速度并分裂两支磁振子；传播整体更接近反铁磁而非铁磁，保留近似圆形外波前，但内部出现明显十字形空间结构。将J₂改为负值进入非阻挫区后，特征传播速度增大，增强传播的对角方向发生交换，对应各向异性空间图案旋转π/2。

Conclusion: 实空间时空动力学可作为交变磁磁振子的互补探针，为在固态与合成量子系统中通过实空间传播图案识别非常规磁序提供途径。

Abstract: Distinguishing altermagnetism from conventional ferromagnetism and antiferromagnetism typically relies on momentum-space probes. Here, we show that the real-space spreading of a localized spin excitation provides a distinctive dynamical fingerprint of altermagnetic order. Using linear spin-wave theory on a two-dimensional checkerboard lattice with nearest-neighbor and next-nearest-neighbor couplings J_1 and J_2, we demonstrate that finite J_2 produces direction-dependent magnon group velocities and splits the two magnon branches. The resulting propagation remains closer to that of an antiferromagnet than a ferromagnet, retaining an approximately circular outer wavefront, while the direction-dependent magnon velocities produce a pronounced cross-like spatial structure inside this front. We further show that changing the sign of J_2 to enter the unfrustrated regime (J_2<0) increases the characteristic propagation velocities and interchanges the diagonal directions of enhanced propagation, corresponding to a pi/2 rotation of the anisotropic spatial pattern. These results establish spacetime dynamics as a complementary probe of altermagnetic magnons, providing a route to identifying unconventional magnetic order through real-space propagation patterns in solid-state and synthetic quantum systems.

</details>


### [244] [Statistics, 't Hooft Anomaly, and the Else-Nayak Index: a careful comparison of concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20813)
*Hanyu Xue*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文澄清了广义对称性/对称性反常与拓扑激发统计之间的关联，指出“对称性patch算符”方案的缺陷，提出以“跳跃算符对易（对称）”为核心判据，并在适当假设下建立统计与反常的一一对应；通过高一维DW规范场耦合从规范化视角解释该关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中普遍认为广义对称性、拓扑激发统计与对称性反常相互关联，但这一关联的建立方式存在陷阱：把对称变换截断到有限区域得到的patch算符虽能在边界产生对称缺陷，几何上类似在支撑边界产生拓扑激发的跳跃算符，却无法给出良定义的统计，也不保证与对称性反常一致。作者希望澄清这些潜在误解并给出更可靠的比较框架。

Method: 首先批判性分析“对称变换的格点截断/patch算符”方案，指出其与跳跃算符的几何类比不足以定义统计或保证与反常一致。随后提出更自然稳健的条件：拓扑激发的跳跃算符应是对称的，即与对称变换对易。在适当假设下证明该条件给出统计与反常的一一对应。进一步引入高一维的DW规范场并与边界物质耦合，从规范化（gauging）角度解释：跳跃算符本质上是规范化后作用在物理自由度上的规范不变算符；固定规范场背景后，全局对称性来自保持该背景的规范变换，而跳跃的对称条件恰是剩余的规范不变性。

Result: 在适当假设下，跳跃算符的对称性条件给出拓扑激发统计与对称性反常之间的一一对应；规范化图像表明该对称条件等同于固定背景下的剩余规范不变性，从而解释了统计—反常关系的来源。

Conclusion: 对称性patch算符的几何类比不足以确立统计与反常的联系；应以“跳跃算符与对称变换对易”作为核心判据。该框架澄清了文献中的潜在误解，并为比较对称性与拓扑激发提供了更可靠的基础。

Abstract: Generalized symmetries and topological excitations, as well as symmetry anomalies and the statistics of topological excitations, are widely believed to be related. There are, however, pitfalls in how this relation is established. A lattice truncation of a symmetry transformation to a finite patch gives a symmetry patch operator that creates symmetry defects at its boundary. This geometric picture resembles a hopping operator creating topological excitations at the boundary of its support, but does not provide well-defined statistics, let alone guarantee agreement with the symmetry anomaly. A more natural and robust relation is that the hopping operators of topological excitations are symmetric: they commute with symmetry transformations. Under suitable assumptions, this condition yields a one-to-one correspondence between statistics and anomalies. We further couple boundary matter to a DW gauge field in one higher dimension to explain this relation from the perspective of gauging. A hopping operator is, in essence, a gauge-invariant operator acting on the physical degrees of freedom after gauging. Once the gauge-field background is fixed, global symmetry comes from gauge transformations that preserve that background, while the symmetric condition on hopping is precisely the remaining gauge invariance. This distinction clarifies potential misconceptions in the literature and provides a more reliable framework for comparing symmetries and topological excitations.

</details>

### [245] [$H\mathbb{Z}/4$ is not an $\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom Spectrum over the $2$-Complete Sphere Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19446)
*Mattie Ji*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Hℤ/4不是2-complete球谱上的E₂-Thom谱，从而解决了Kitchloo结果中遗留的(p,k)=(2,2)情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Hopkins-Mahowald定理指出Hℤ/p是p-complete球谱上的E₂-Thom谱，Kitchloo将其推广至Hℤ/p^k（p-local球谱上），但遗留了(p,k)=(2,2)这一唯一未决情形。本文旨在补全该缺口。

Method: 通过反证构造：假设Hℤ/4是2-complete球谱上的E₂-Thom谱，导出矛盾；由于2-complete情形不成立，2-local情形也随之不成立。本文声明在GPT-5.6 Sol与GPT-6 Astra的协助下完成，初稿经Claude Fable 5.1审阅。

Result: Hℤ/4不是2-complete球谱上的E₂-Thom谱，因此也不是2-local球谱上的E₂-Thom谱。这填补了(p,k)=(2,2)的空白，说明Kitchloo定理的例外情形确实为真实反例。

Conclusion: Hopkins-Mahowald型定理在(p,k)=(2,2)时失效，Hℤ/4不具E₂-Thom谱结构，从而完整刻画了Hℤ/p^k成为E₂-Thom谱的例外情形。

Abstract: For any prime number $p$, the classic Hopkins-Mahowald theorem asserts that $H\mathbb{Z}/p$ is an $\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom spectrum over the $p$-complete sphere spectrum. Kitchloo extended this result to show that $H\mathbb{Z}/p^k$ is an $\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom spectrum over the $p$-local sphere spectrum, except for the case when $(p, k) = (2, 2)$. We solve the remaining case by showing that $H\mathbb{Z}/4$ is not an $\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom spectrum over the $2$-complete sphere spectrum, and consequently the $2$-local sphere spectrum. This work was developed with substantial assistance from GPT-5.6 Sol and GPT-6 Astra, and an early draft was subsequently reviewed using Claude Fable 5.1.

</details>


### [246] [New families of moment-angle manifolds diffeomorphic to connected sums of products of spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19945)
*Victoria Kovyrshina,Taras Panov*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在特定三维星状（尤其多面体）单纯球面上矩角流形可微同构于球面乘积的连通和，并给出奇数维邻接星状球面的分解及非多面体Barnette与Brückner球面矩角流形的显式描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究矩角流形的微分拓扑结构，特别是在星状或多面体单纯球面情形下的连通和分解，揭示其与球面乘积的关系。

Method: 通过构造可微同构，分析三维星状单纯球面（恰有两个不相邻缺失边）的矩角流形，推广到奇数维邻接星状单纯球面，并对Barnette与Brückner球面给出显式描述。

Result: 对于三维星状（含多面体）单纯球面且恰有两个不相邻缺失边，矩角流形可微同构于球面乘积的连通和，其中一个和项为三个球面的乘积；奇数维邻接星状单纯球面的矩角流形同构于若干两球面乘积的连通和；并显式描述了非多面体Barnette与Brückner球面对应的矩角流形。

Conclusion: 矩角流形在星状单纯球面条件下具有良好的连通和分解结构，分解为球面乘积的连通和，为相关拓扑分类提供了新结果。

Abstract: We prove that the moment-angle manifold $\mathcal Z_{\mathcal K}$ is diffeomorphic to a connected sum of products of spheres when $\mathcal K$ is a starshaped (in particular, polytopal) 3-dimensional simplicial sphere with exactly two missing edges that are not adjacent to each other. One of the summands of the connected sum is a product of three spheres.
  For neighbourly starshaped simplicial spheres $\mathcal K$ of odd dimension, we prove the diffeomorphism $\mathcal Z_{\mathcal K} \cong M_1\#\cdots\# M_k$, where each $M_i$ is a product of two spheres. We give an explicit description of the moment-angle manifolds corresponding to non-polytopal Barnette and Brückner spheres.

</details>


### [247] [Representation stability of string links and manifold links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20470)
*Filipp Buryak*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了弦链与流形链空间有理上同调随分量数增长的表示稳定性，并给出了各阶生成元数目的显式上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究链条空间（嵌入空间）上同调随分量数增加时的稳定性，将 FI#-模框架推广到弦链和流形链，并给出定量的生成界。

Method: 结合结构映射的几何构造、嵌入模浸入的 hairy graph complex 模型，以及一个允许在忘记对称群作用后建立生成界的函子性框架。

Result: 对 m≥1、d≥m+3 的弦链以及 d≥3 的流形链，其有理上同调群在各阶均构成有限生成 FI#-模，第 n 阶生成元数至多为 n(d-2)/(d-m-2)；同时得到有理同伦群的显式生成界。

Conclusion: 弦链与流形链的有理上同调及同伦具有表示稳定性，且生成界可定量控制。

Abstract: We prove representation stability for rational cohomology of spaces of string links and manifold links in Euclidean space as the number of links grows. For string links we consider the component of the standard embedding of disjoint copies of $\mathbb{R}^{m}$ into $\mathbb{R}^{d}$, where $m \geq 1$ and $d \geq m+3$. For manifold links we consider the component determined by disjoint copies of a fixed embedding of a closed smooth $m$-manifold into $\mathbb{R}^{d-2}$, where $d \geq 3$. In both cases, the rational cohomology groups form finitely generated $\mathrm{FI}^{\#}$-modules in each degree with generation arity at most $n\frac{d-2}{d-m-2}$ in degree $n$. We also obtain explicit generation bounds for the rational homotopy groups. The proofs combine geometric constructions of structure maps, hairy graph complex models for embeddings modulo immersions and a functorial framework that allows generation bounds to be established after forgetting the symmetric group actions.

</details>
